State.disableAnimationsEnabled = false
State.disabledAnimMode = "Both"
State._animPatches = {melee = {}, gun = {}, running = false}

function applyDisableAnimations(mode)
    if not mode then mode = State.disabledAnimMode end
    State._animPatches.running = true

    if mode == "Melee" or mode == "Both" then
        local ok, Melee = pcall(function()
            return require(Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Melee)
        end)
        if ok and Melee and Melee.StartShooting then
            if not State._animPatches.melee.start then
                State._animPatches.melee.start = Melee.StartShooting
            end
            Melee.StartShooting = function(self, p1, p2)
                local vm = self.ViewModel; local savedPlay
                if vm then savedPlay = vm.PlayAnimation; vm.PlayAnimation = function() end end
                local result = { State._animPatches.melee.start(self, p1, p2) }
                if vm and savedPlay then
                    vm.PlayAnimation = savedPlay
                    pcall(function()
                        for _, name in ipairs({"Attack", "Attack1", "Attack2", "Swing", "Slash", "Combo1", "Combo2", "Combo3"}) do
                            vm:StopAnimation(name)
                        end
                    end)
                end
                return table.unpack(result)
            end
        end
    end

    if mode == "Gun" or mode == "Both" then
        local ok, Gun = pcall(function()
            return require(Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
        end)
        if ok and Gun then
            if Gun.StartShooting and not State._animPatches.gun.start then
                State._animPatches.gun.start = Gun.StartShooting
                Gun.StartShooting = function(self, p1, p2)
                    local vm = self.ViewModel; local savedPlay
                    if vm then savedPlay = vm.PlayAnimation; vm.PlayAnimation = function() end end
                    local result = { State._animPatches.gun.start(self, p1, p2) }
                    if vm and savedPlay then
                        vm.PlayAnimation = savedPlay
                        pcall(function()
                            for _, name in ipairs({"Shoot","Fire","ShootBurst","Shoot_ADS","ShootHip","ShootEmpty","ShootADS","Burst"}) do vm:StopAnimation(name) end
                        end)
                    end
                    return table.unpack(result)
                end
            end

            local function silenceShootAnim(self, fn, ...)
                if not self.ViewModel then return fn(self, ...) end
                local vm = self.ViewModel; local oldPlay = vm.PlayAnimation
                vm.PlayAnimation = function() end
                local ok, res = pcall(fn, self, ...)
                vm.PlayAnimation = oldPlay
                pcall(function() for _, name in ipairs({"Shoot","Fire","ShootBurst","Shoot_ADS","Shoot_Hip","ShootEmpty","ShootADS","Burst"}) do vm:StopAnimation(name) end end)
                if ok then return res end
            end

            if Gun.FinishShooting and not State._animPatches.gun.finish then
                State._animPatches.gun.finish = Gun.FinishShooting
                Gun.FinishShooting = function(self, ...) return silenceShootAnim(self, State._animPatches.gun.finish, ...) end
            end
            if Gun.ShootBurst and not State._animPatches.gun.burst then
                State._animPatches.gun.burst = Gun.ShootBurst
                Gun.ShootBurst = function(self, ...) return silenceShootAnim(self, State._animPatches.gun.burst, ...) end
            end
        end
    end
end

function restoreDisableAnimations()
    State._animPatches.running = false

    pcall(function()
        local ok, Melee = pcall(function()
            return require(Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Melee)
        end)
        if ok and Melee and State._animPatches.melee.start then
            Melee.StartShooting = State._animPatches.melee.start
            State._animPatches.melee.start = nil
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        local ok, Gun = pcall(function()
            return require(Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
        end)
        if ok and Gun then
            if State._animPatches.gun.start then
                Gun.StartShooting = State._animPatches.gun.start
                State._animPatches.gun.start = nil
            end
            if State._animPatches.gun.finish then
                Gun.FinishShooting = State._animPatches.gun.finish
                State._animPatches.gun.finish = nil
            end
            if State._animPatches.gun.burst then
                Gun.ShootBurst = State._animPatches.gun.burst
                State._animPatches.gun.burst = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end

function State.normalizeTargetHUDFields(selection)
    local fields = {}
    if type(selection) == "string" then
        fields[selection] = true
    elseif type(selection) == "table" then
        for _, fieldName in ipairs(selection) do
            if type(fieldName) == "string" then
                fields[fieldName] = true
            end
        end
    end
    if next(fields) == nil then
        for _, fieldName in ipairs(State.targetHUDInfoFields) do
            fields[fieldName] = true
        end
    end
    return fields
end

function State.getTargetHUDThumbnail(player)
    if not player or not player.UserId then
        return nil
    end

    local userId = player.UserId
    if State.targetHUDThumbnailCache[userId] then
        return State.targetHUDThumbnailCache[userId]
    end

    if State.targetHUDThumbnailRequests[userId] then
        return nil
    end

    State.targetHUDThumbnailRequests[userId] = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, image = pcall(function()
            return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(userId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size60x60)
        end)
        if ok and type(image) == "string" and image ~= "" then
            State.targetHUDThumbnailCache[userId] = image
        end
        State.targetHUDThumbnailRequests[userId] = nil
    end)

    return nil
end

function State.getTargetHUDInfo(player)
    if not player then
        return {}
    end

    local info = {}
    info.Name = player.DisplayName ~= "" and player.DisplayName or player.Name or ""
    info.Weapon = ""
    if type(ESPUtil) == "table" and type(ESPUtil.getWeaponNameForPlayer) == "function" then
        info.Weapon = ESPUtil.getWeaponNameForPlayer(player) or ""
    end

    local humanoid = player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if humanoid then
        info.Health = tostring(math.floor(humanoid.Health or 0))
    else
        info.Health = ""
    end

    local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("CustomLeaderstats")
    local levelValue = player:GetAttribute("Level")
    local streakValue = player:GetAttribute("StatisticDuelsWinStreak")
    local rankValue = player:GetAttribute("Rank") or player:GetAttribute("DisplayELO") or player:GetAttribute("DisplayRank")

    if not levelValue and leaderstats then
        local levelObject = leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Level")
        if levelObject and levelObject.Value ~= nil then
            levelValue = levelObject.Value
        end
    end
    if not streakValue and leaderstats then
        local streakObject = leaderstats:FindFirstChild("Win Streak")
        if streakObject and streakObject.Value ~= nil then
            streakValue = streakObject.Value
        end
    end

    info.Level = levelValue and tostring(levelValue) or ""
    info.Streak = streakValue and tostring(streakValue) or ""
    info.Rank = rankValue and tostring(rankValue) or ""

    return info
end

function State.registerTargetHUDStyle(instance, properties)
    if not instance or not Library or type(Library.AddToRegistry) ~= "function" then
        return
    end

    Library:AddToRegistry(instance, properties, true)
    State.targetHUDStyleInstances = State.targetHUDStyleInstances or {}
    table.insert(State.targetHUDStyleInstances, instance)
end

function State.clearTargetHUDStyleRegistry()
    if not State.targetHUDStyleInstances then
        return
    end

    if Library and type(Library.RemoveFromRegistry) == "function" then
        for _, instance in ipairs(State.targetHUDStyleInstances) do
            pcall(function()
                Library:RemoveFromRegistry(instance)
            end)
        end
    end

    State.targetHUDStyleInstances = nil
end

function State.createTargetHUD()
    if State.targetHUDScreenGui and State.targetHUDScreenGui.Parent then
        return State.targetHUDScreenGui
    end
    State.clearTargetHUDStyleRegistry()

    local function getPlayerGui()
        local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not lp then
            return nil
        end
        local gui = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if gui then
            return gui
        end
        local ok, found = pcall(function()
            return lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5)
        end)
        return ok and found or nil
    end

    local playerGui = getPlayerGui()
    if not playerGui then
        return nil
    end

    local function createGui(className, properties)
        if Library and type(Library.Create) == "function" then
            return Library:Create(className, properties)
        end

        local instance = Instance.new(className)
        for property, value in pairs(properties) do
            instance[property] = value
        end
        return instance
    end

    local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    screenGui.Name = "PhantomRivalsTargetHUDGui"
    screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
    screenGui.DisplayOrder = 20
    screenGui.Parent = playerGui
    screenGui.Enabled = State.targetHUDEnabled

    local rootFrame = createGui("Frame", {
        Name = "TargetHUDRoot",
        AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.65, 0),
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
        BackgroundTransparency = 0,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        ClipsDescendants = true,
        ZIndex = 20,
        Parent = screenGui,
    })
    State.registerTargetHUDStyle(rootFrame, {
        BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor",
    })

    local rootStroke = createGui("UIStroke", {
        Color = Library.OutlineColor,
        Thickness = 1,
        Transparency = 0.25,
        ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
        Parent = rootFrame,
    })
    State.registerTargetHUDStyle(rootStroke, {
        Color = "OutlineColor",
    })

    local rootLayout = createGui("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
        Parent = rootFrame,
    })

    local rootAccent = createGui("Frame", {
        Name = "Accent",
        BackgroundColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
        BorderSizePixel = 0,
        LayoutOrder = 1,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
        ZIndex = 21,
        Parent = rootFrame,
    })
    State.registerTargetHUDStyle(rootAccent, {
        BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor",
    })

    local contentFrame = createGui("Frame", {
        Name = "Content",
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        LayoutOrder = 2,
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
        AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
        ZIndex = 21,
        Parent = rootFrame,
    })

    local padding = createGui("UIPadding", {
        PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8),
        PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
        Parent = contentFrame,
    })

    local listLayout = createGui("UIListLayout", {
        SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
        Padding = UDim.new(0, 6),
        Parent = contentFrame,
    })

    local function createSection(title)
        local section = createGui("Frame", {
            Name = title:gsub("%s+", "") .. "Section",
            BackgroundColor3 = Library.BackgroundColor,
            BorderColor3 = Library.OutlineColor,
            BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Inset,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ZIndex = 22,
            Parent = contentFrame,
        })
        State.registerTargetHUDStyle(section, {
            BackgroundColor3 = "BackgroundColor",
            BorderColor3 = "OutlineColor",
        })

        local sectionLayout = createGui("UIListLayout", {
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Vertical,
            Parent = section,
        })

        local sectionAccent = createGui("Frame", {
            Name = "Accent",
            BackgroundColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            LayoutOrder = 1,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 2),
            ZIndex = 23,
            Parent = section,
        })
        State.registerTargetHUDStyle(sectionAccent, {
            BackgroundColor3 = "AccentColor",
        })

        local sectionInner = createGui("Frame", {
            Name = "Inner",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            LayoutOrder = 2,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ZIndex = 23,
            Parent = section,
        })

        local sectionPadding = createGui("UIPadding", {
            PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6),
            PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 7),
            PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8),
            PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8),
            Parent = sectionInner,
        })

        local sectionInnerLayout = createGui("UIListLayout", {
            SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder,
            Padding = UDim.new(0, 4),
            Parent = sectionInner,
        })

        local titleLabel = createGui("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Title",
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Font = Library.Font,
            TextSize = 14,
            TextColor3 = Library.AccentColor,
            TextStrokeTransparency = Library.TextStrokeTransparency or 1,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center,
            Text = title,
            LayoutOrder = 1,
            ZIndex = 24,
            Parent = sectionInner,
        })
        State.registerTargetHUDStyle(titleLabel, {
            TextColor3 = "AccentColor",
        })

        local contentRow = createGui("Frame", {
            Name = "Content",
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            LayoutOrder = 2,
            Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44),
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            ZIndex = 24,
            Parent = sectionInner,
        })

        local bodyLabel = createGui("TextLabel", {
            Name = "Body",
            Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 0),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0),
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Font = Library.Font,
            TextSize = 13,
            TextColor3 = Library.FontColor,
            TextStrokeTransparency = Library.TextStrokeTransparency or 1,
            TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left,
            TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top,
            TextWrapped = true,
            AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,
            Text = "",
            ZIndex = 25,
            Parent = contentRow,
        })
        State.registerTargetHUDStyle(bodyLabel, {
            TextColor3 = "FontColor",
        })

        local imageLabel = createGui("ImageLabel", {
            Name = "ProfilePicture",
            Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 44),
            Position = UDim2.new(1, -44, 0, 0),
            AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0),
            BackgroundColor3 = Library.MainColor,
            BackgroundTransparency = 0,
            BorderSizePixel = 0,
            Image = "",
            Visible = false,
            ZIndex = 25,
            Parent = contentRow,
        })
        State.registerTargetHUDStyle(imageLabel, {
            BackgroundColor3 = "MainColor",
        })

        local imageStroke = createGui("UIStroke", {
            Color = Library.OutlineColor,
            Thickness = 1,
            Transparency = 0.15,
            ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border,
            Parent = imageLabel,
        })
        State.registerTargetHUDStyle(imageStroke, {
            Color = "OutlineColor",
        })

        local imageCorner = createGui("UICorner", {
            CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0),
            Parent = imageLabel,
        })

        return {
            Frame = section,
            Title = titleLabel,
            Body = bodyLabel,
            Content = contentRow,
            ProfilePicture = imageLabel,
        }
    end

    State.targetHUDSections = {
        Ragebot = createSection("Ragebot Target"),
        SilentAim = createSection("Silent Aim Target"),
        Aimbot = createSection("Aimbot Target"),
    }

    State.targetHUDScreenGui = screenGui
    return screenGui
end

function State.destroyTargetHUD()
    State.clearTargetHUDStyleRegistry()
    if State.targetHUDScreenGui then
        pcall(function()
            State.targetHUDScreenGui:Destroy()
        end)
        State.targetHUDScreenGui = nil
        State.targetHUDSections = nil
    end
end

function State.setTargetHUDEnabled(enabled)
    State.targetHUDEnabled = enabled
    if enabled then
        State.createTargetHUD()
    end
    if State.targetHUDScreenGui then
        State.targetHUDScreenGui.Enabled = enabled
    end
end

function State.updateTargetHUDSection(section, player, fields)
    if not section or not section.Frame then
        return
    end

    local label = section.Title
    local body = section.Body
    local imageLabel = section.ProfilePicture
    local showProfile = fields and fields["Profile Picture"]
    local profileVisible = false

    if not fields or next(fields) == nil then
        section.Frame.Visible = false
        return
    end

    section.Frame.Visible = true
    if body then
        body.Size = showProfile and UDim2.new(1, -56, 0, 0) or UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
    end

    local titleText = label and label.Text or "Target"
    if label then
        label.Text = titleText
    end

    if not player or not player.Character then
        if body then
            body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
            body.Text = "No target"
        end
        if imageLabel then
            imageLabel.Visible = false
        end
        return
    end

    local info = State.getTargetHUDInfo(player)
    local lines = {}

    if fields["Name"] then
        table.insert(lines, "Name: " .. (info.Name ~= "" and info.Name or "Unknown"))
    end
    if fields["Weapon"] then
        table.insert(lines, "Weapon: " .. (info.Weapon ~= "" and info.Weapon or "Unknown"))
    end
    if fields["Health"] then
        table.insert(lines, "Health: " .. (info.Health ~= "" and info.Health or "Unknown"))
    end
    if fields["Rank"] then
        table.insert(lines, "Rank: " .. (info.Rank ~= "" and info.Rank or "Unknown"))
    end
    if fields["Level"] then
        table.insert(lines, "Level: " .. (info.Level ~= "" and info.Level or "Unknown"))
    end
    if fields["Streak"] then
        table.insert(lines, "Streak: " .. (info.Streak ~= "" and info.Streak or "Unknown"))
    end

    if body then
        body.Text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
    end

    if imageLabel then
        if showProfile then
            local thumbnail = State.getTargetHUDThumbnail(player)
            if thumbnail then
                imageLabel.Image = thumbnail
                profileVisible = true
            else
                profileVisible = false
            end
        else
            profileVisible = false
        end
        imageLabel.Visible = profileVisible
    end
end

function State.updateTargetHUD()
    if not State.targetHUDEnabled then
        return
    end

    if not State.targetHUDScreenGui or not State.targetHUDScreenGui.Parent then
        State.createTargetHUD()
    end
    if not State.targetHUDScreenGui then
        return
    end

    local rageTarget = State.rageBotCurrentTarget
    local silentTarget = State.getClosestSilent()
    local aimbotTarget = State.getClosest()

    State.updateTargetHUDSection(State.targetHUDSections.Ragebot, rageTarget, State.targetHUDFieldsRagebot)
    State.updateTargetHUDSection(State.targetHUDSections.SilentAim, silentTarget, State.targetHUDFieldsSilentAim)
    State.updateTargetHUDSection(State.targetHUDSections.Aimbot, aimbotTarget, State.targetHUDFieldsAimbot)
end

local function destroyReal3DText()
    if State.real3DTextConnection then
        State.real3DTextConnection:Disconnect()
        State.real3DTextConnection = nil
    end

    if State.real3DTextGui then
        State.real3DTextGui:Destroy()
        State.real3DTextGui = nil
    end

    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = player and player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local old = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("Real3DTextGui")
    if old then
        old:Destroy()
    end
end

function State.disableReal3DText()
    destroyReal3DText()
end

function State.enableReal3DText()
    destroyReal3DText()

    local text = "PHANTOM"
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local FONT = {
        A = {"01110", "10001", "10001", "11111", "10001", "10001", "10001"},
        B = {"11110", "10001", "10001", "11110", "10001", "10001", "11110"},
        C = {"01111", "10000", "10000", "10000", "10000", "10000", "01111"},
        D = {"11110", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "11110"},
        E = {"11111", "10000", "10000", "11110", "10000", "10000", "11111"},
        F = {"11111", "10000", "10000", "11110", "10000", "10000", "10000"},
        G = {"01111", "10000", "10000", "10111", "10001", "10001", "01111"},
        H = {"10001", "10001", "10001", "11111", "10001", "10001", "10001"},
        I = {"11111", "00100", "00100", "00100", "00100", "00100", "11111"},
        J = {"00111", "00010", "00010", "00010", "10010", "10010", "01100"},
        K = {"10001", "10010", "10100", "11000", "10100", "10010", "10001"},
        L = {"10000", "10000", "10000", "10000", "10000", "10000", "11111"},
        M = {"10001", "11011", "10101", "10101", "10001", "10001", "10001"},
        N = {"10001", "11001", "10101", "10011", "10001", "10001", "10001"},
        O = {"01110", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "01110"},
        P = {"11110", "10001", "10001", "11110", "10000", "10000", "10000"},
        Q = {"01110", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10101", "10010", "01101"},
        R = {"11110", "10001", "10001", "11110", "10100", "10010", "10001"},
        S = {"01111", "10000", "10000", "01110", "00001", "00001", "11110"},
        T = {"11111", "00100", "00100", "00100", "00100", "00100", "00100"},
        U = {"10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "01110"},
        V = {"10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "10001", "01010", "00100"},
        W = {"10001", "10001", "10001", "10101", "10101", "10101", "01010"},
        X = {"10001", "10001", "01010", "00100", "01010", "10001", "10001"},
        Y = {"10001", "10001", "01010", "00100", "00100", "00100", "00100"},
        Z = {"11111", "00001", "00010", "00100", "01000", "10000", "11111"},
        ["0"] = {"01110", "10001", "10011", "10101", "11001", "10001", "01110"},
        ["1"] = {"00100", "01100", "00100", "00100", "00100", "00100", "01110"},
        ["2"] = {"01110", "10001", "00001", "00010", "00100", "01000", "11111"},
        ["3"] = {"11110", "00001", "00001", "01110", "00001", "00001", "11110"},
        ["4"] = {"10010", "10010", "10010", "11111", "00010", "00010", "00010"},
        ["5"] = {"11111", "10000", "10000", "11110", "00001", "00001", "11110"},
        ["6"] = {"01111", "10000", "10000", "11110", "10001", "10001", "01110"},
        ["7"] = {"11111", "00001", "00010", "00100", "01000", "01000", "01000"},
        ["8"] = {"01110", "10001", "10001", "01110", "10001", "10001", "01110"},
        ["9"] = {"01110", "10001", "10001", "01111", "00001", "00001", "11110"},
    }

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "Real3DTextGui"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.Parent = playerGui
    State.real3DTextGui = gui

    local viewport = Instance.new("ViewportFrame")
    viewport.Name = "TextViewport"
    viewport.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    viewport.Position = UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5)
    viewport.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(700, 260)
    viewport.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    viewport.Parent = gui

    local viewportCamera = Instance.new("Camera")
    viewportCamera.FieldOfView = 35
    viewportCamera.CFrame = CFrame.new(0, 0, 12)
    viewportCamera.Parent = viewport
    viewport.CurrentCamera = viewportCamera

    local world = Instance.new("WorldModel")
    world.Parent = viewport

    local model = Instance.new("Model")
    model.Name = "Real3DTextModel"
    model.Parent = world

    local blockSize = 0.28
    local overlap = 0.04
    local pixelStep = blockSize - overlap
    local letterGap = 0.035
    local spaceGap = 0.5
    local depth = 0.45
    local parts = {}

    local function createRun(x, y, width)
        local part = Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name = "TextBlock"
        part.Size = Vector3.new(width + overlap * 2, blockSize + overlap * 2, depth)
        part.Anchored = true
        part.CanCollide = false
        part.CanTouch = false
        part.CanQuery = false
        part.CastShadow = false
        part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        part.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        part.Position = Vector3.new(x, y, 0)
        part.Parent = model
        table.insert(parts, part)
    end

    local cursorX = 0
    local upperText = string.upper(text)

    for i = 1, #upperText do
        local char = string.sub(upperText, i, i)

        if char == " " then
	            cursorX = cursorX + spaceGap
        else
            local pattern = FONT[char]

            if pattern then
                for row = 1, #pattern do
                    local line = pattern[row]
                    local col = 1

                    while col <= #line do
                        if string.sub(line, col, col) == "1" then
                            local startCol = col

                            while col <= #line and string.sub(line, col, col) == "1" do
									col = col + 1
							end
                            local endCol = col - 1
                            local runLength = endCol - startCol + 1
                            local width = blockSize + (runLength - 1) * pixelStep
                            local x = cursorX + (startCol - 1) * pixelStep + ((runLength - 1) * pixelStep) / 2
                            local y = -(row - 1) * pixelStep

                            createRun(x, y, width)
                        else
							col = col + 1
                        end
                    end
                end

	                cursorX = cursorX + 5 * pixelStep + letterGap
            else
				cursorX = cursorX + 5 * pixelStep + letterGap
            end
        end
    end

    local boxCFrame = model:GetBoundingBox()
    local center = boxCFrame.Position

    for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
	            part.Position = part.Position - center

	end

	model:PivotTo(CFrame.new(0, 0, 0))

    local rotateSpeed = math.rad(90)
    local start = os.clock()

    State.real3DTextConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not State.real3DTextGui or not State.real3DTextGui.Parent then
            destroyReal3DText()
            return
        end

        local t = os.clock() - start
        model:PivotTo(CFrame.Angles(0, -t * rotateSpeed, 0))
    end)
end

function isRageKnifeMode()
    return tostring(State.rageWeaponType or "Gun") == "Knife"
end

function isRageMeleeMode()
    local weaponType = tostring(State.rageWeaponType or "Gun")
    return weaponType == "Melee" or weaponType == "Knife"
end

function getRageMeleeWeaponName()
    if isRageKnifeMode() then
        return State.rageKnifeWeapon or "Knife"
    end
    return State.autoLoadoutWeapon3
end

local RivalsGameOptionModules = {}

local function requireRivalsGameOptionModule(moduleName)
    if RivalsGameOptionModules[moduleName] then
        return RivalsGameOptionModules[moduleName]
    end

    local modules = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Modules")
    local moduleScript = modules and modules:FindFirstChild(moduleName)
    if not moduleScript then
        return nil
    end

    local ok, result = pcall(require, moduleScript)
    if ok and type(result) == "table" then
        RivalsGameOptionModules[moduleName] = result
        return result
    end

    return nil
end

local function appendUniqueOption(options, seen, value)
    if type(value) ~= "string" or value == "" or seen[value] then
        return
    end

    seen[value] = true
    table.insert(options, value)
end

local function getRivalsWeaponOptions(className, fallbackValues)
    local itemLibrary = requireRivalsGameOptionModule("ItemLibrary")
    local shopLibrary = requireRivalsGameOptionModule("ShopLibrary")
    local options = {}
    local seen = {}
    local releasedWeapons = {}

    if shopLibrary and type(shopLibrary.GetReleasedOwnableWeapons) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            releasedWeapons = shopLibrary:GetReleasedOwnableWeapons(nil, shopLibrary.OwnableWeaponsAlphabetized) or {}
        end)
    end

    local sources = {}
    if #releasedWeapons > 0 then
        table.insert(sources, releasedWeapons)
    elseif shopLibrary then
        table.insert(sources, shopLibrary.OwnableWeaponsAlphabetized)
        table.insert(sources, shopLibrary.OwnableWeapons)
    end

    if #sources == 0 and itemLibrary then
        table.insert(sources, itemLibrary.ItemsAlphabetized)
    end

    for _, source in ipairs(sources) do
        if type(source) == "table" then
            for key, value in pairs(source) do
                local weaponName = type(value) == "string" and value or (type(key) == "string" and key or nil)
                local itemInfo = weaponName and itemLibrary and itemLibrary.Items and itemLibrary.Items[weaponName]
                if weaponName and (not className or (itemInfo and itemInfo.Class == className)) then
                    appendUniqueOption(options, seen, weaponName)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if itemLibrary and type(itemLibrary.ItemsAlphabetized) == "table" and #options == 0 then
        for _, weaponName in ipairs(itemLibrary.ItemsAlphabetized) do
            local itemInfo = itemLibrary.Items and itemLibrary.Items[weaponName]
            if not className or (itemInfo and itemInfo.Class == className) then
                appendUniqueOption(options, seen, weaponName)
            end
        end
    end

    for _, fallback in ipairs(fallbackValues or {}) do
        local itemInfo = itemLibrary and itemLibrary.Items and itemLibrary.Items[fallback]
        if not className or not itemInfo or itemInfo.Class == className then
            appendUniqueOption(options, seen, fallback)
        end
    end

    table.sort(options, function(a, b)
        return a:lower() < b:lower()
    end)
    return options
end

local function getRivalsMapOptions()
    local duelLibrary = requireRivalsGameOptionModule("DuelLibrary")
    local options = {}
    local seen = {}

    if duelLibrary then
        for _, mapName in ipairs(duelLibrary.MapOrder or {}) do
            appendUniqueOption(options, seen, mapName)
        end
        for _, mapName in ipairs(duelLibrary.HiddenMapOrder or {}) do
            appendUniqueOption(options, seen, mapName)
        end
        for mapName in pairs(duelLibrary.Maps or {}) do
            appendUniqueOption(options, seen, mapName)
        end
    end

    if #options == 0 then
        for _, mapName in ipairs(DEFAULT_RIVALS_MAPS) do
            appendUniqueOption(options, seen, mapName)
        end
    end

    return options
end

local function getRivalsQueueOptions()
    local duelLibrary = requireRivalsGameOptionModule("DuelLibrary")
    local options = {}
    local seen = {}

    for _, queueName in ipairs((duelLibrary and duelLibrary.MatchmakingQueueOrder) or DEFAULT_RIVALS_QUEUES) do
        appendUniqueOption(options, seen, queueName)
    end

    if #options == 0 then
        for _, queueName in ipairs(DEFAULT_RIVALS_QUEUES) do
            appendUniqueOption(options, seen, queueName)
        end
    end

    return options
end

State.randomDuelMaps = getRivalsMapOptions()
if not table.find(State.randomDuelMaps, State.selectedDuelMap) then
    State.selectedDuelMap = State.randomDuelMaps[1]
end
State.selectedQueueMode = "1v1"
State.autoMatchmakingEnabled = false
State.autoMatchmakingInterval = 5
State.autoMatchmakingThread = nil
State.modDetectorEnabled = false
State.modDetectorMode = "Notify"
State.modDetectorAutoLoad = false
State.modDetectorCache = {}
State.modDetectorConnection = nil
State.maulSlamMultiplierEnabled = false
State.maulSlamRangeMultiplier = 1.5
State.maulSlamOriginalProperties = {}
State.maulSlamConnection = nil
State.maulSlamBackups = {}
State.cosmeticLibrary = nil
State.playerDataController = nil

function findManagedSkybox()
    return Lighting:FindFirstChild(State.managedSkyboxName)
end

function clearSkybox()
    local skybox = findManagedSkybox()
    if skybox then
        skybox:Destroy()
    end
end

function createOrUpdateSkybox(def)
    if type(def) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local skybox = findManagedSkybox()
    if not skybox then
        skybox = Instance.new("Sky")
        skybox.Name = State.managedSkyboxName
        skybox.Parent = Lighting
    end

    skybox.SkyboxFt = def.Front or ""
    skybox.SkyboxBk = def.Back or ""
    skybox.SkyboxLf = def.Left or ""
    skybox.SkyboxRt = def.Right or ""
    skybox.SkyboxUp = def.Up or ""
    skybox.SkyboxDn = def.Down or ""
end

function applySkyboxPreset(name)
    if name == nil or name == "None" or name == "" then
        clearSkybox()
        return
    end

    if name == "Custom" then
        createOrUpdateSkybox(State.skyboxCustom)
        return
    end

    local preset = State.skyboxPresets[name]
    if preset then
        createOrUpdateSkybox(preset)
    else
        clearSkybox()
    end
end

function getKeyPickerActive(KeyPicker, Fallback)
    if KeyPicker and typeof(KeyPicker.GetState) == "function" then
        return KeyPicker:GetState()
    end
    if typeof(Fallback) == "boolean" then
        return Fallback
    end
    return false
end

function getKeyPickerValue(KeyPicker, Fallback)
    if KeyPicker and KeyPicker.Value then
        if KeyPicker.Value == "None" then
            return nil
        end

        if KeyPicker.Value ~= "Unknown" then
            return KeyPicker.Value
        end
    end
    if typeof(Fallback) == "string" then
        return Fallback
    end
    return nil
end

function firePickWeaponsLoadout(w1, w2, w3, w4)
    local ok, ReplicatedStorage = pcall(function() return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end)
    if not ok or not ReplicatedStorage then return false end
    local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    if not remotes then return false end
    local replication = remotes:FindFirstChild("Replication")
    if not replication then return false end
    local fighter = replication:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
    if not fighter then return false end
    local pick = fighter:FindFirstChild("PickWeapons")
    if not pick or not pick.FireServer then return false end
    local args = {{ w1 or "", w2 or "", w3 or "", w4 or "" }}
    local success, err = pcall(function() pick:FireServer(unpack(args)) end)
    return success
end

function applyRageLoadoutSelection()
    if isRageMeleeMode() then
        return firePickWeaponsLoadout(
            State.autoLoadoutWeapon1,
            State.autoLoadoutWeapon2,
            getRageMeleeWeaponName(),
            State.autoLoadoutWeapon4
        )
    end

    local preferred = State.ragePreferredWeapon or "Primary"
    if preferred == "Secondary" then
        return firePickWeaponsLoadout(
            State.autoLoadoutWeapon2,
            State.autoLoadoutWeapon1,
            State.autoLoadoutWeapon3,
            State.autoLoadoutWeapon4
        )
    end

    return firePickWeaponsLoadout(
        State.autoLoadoutWeapon1,
        State.autoLoadoutWeapon2,
        State.autoLoadoutWeapon3,
        State.autoLoadoutWeapon4
    )
end

State.viewModelWireframePartTransparency = 1
State.viewModelWireframeMaxFaces = 1200
State.viewModelWireframeMaxEdges = 3600
State.viewModelWireframeMeshCache = {}
State.viewModelWireframeMeshFailures = {}
State.viewModelArmContainerNames = {
    LeftArm = true,

function getViewModelsFirstPerson()
    local viewModels = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    local firstPerson = viewModels and viewModels:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
    local workspaceFallback = firstPerson
    if firstPerson and #firstPerson:GetDescendants() > 0 then
        return firstPerson
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        local cameraViewModels = camera:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
        firstPerson = cameraViewModels and cameraViewModels:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
        if firstPerson and #firstPerson:GetDescendants() > 0 then
            return firstPerson
        end

        firstPerson = camera:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
        if firstPerson and #firstPerson:GetDescendants() > 0 then
            return firstPerson
        end
    end

    return workspaceFallback or firstPerson
end

function isViewModelWireframeMaterial(name)
    return name == "Wire Frame" or name == "Wireframe"
end

function getMaterialByName(name)
    if type(name) ~= "string" or isViewModelWireframeMaterial(name) then
        return Enum.Material.Neon
    end
    local material = Enum.Material[name]
    return material or Enum.Material.Neon
end

function isViewModelMeshVisual(part)
    return part and part:IsA("MeshPart")
end

function getViewModelWireframeAssetService()
    if State.viewModelWireframeAssetService ~= nil then
        return State.viewModelWireframeAssetService or nil
    end

    local ok, assetService = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("AssetService")
    end)

    if ok and assetService then
        State.viewModelWireframeAssetService = assetService
        return assetService
    end

    State.viewModelWireframeAssetService = false
    return nil
end

function getViewModelMeshContent(part)
    if not part or not part:IsA("MeshPart") then
        return nil
    end

    local ok, content = pcall(function()
        return part.MeshContent
    end)
    if ok and content then
        return content
    end

    local meshId
    ok, meshId = pcall(function()
        return part.MeshId
    end)
    if ok and type(meshId) == "string" and meshId ~= "" and Content and Content.fromUri then
        local contentOk, uriContent = pcall(function()
            return Content.fromUri(meshId)
        end)
        if contentOk then
            return uriContent
        end
    end

    return nil
end

function getViewModelMeshCacheKey(part)
    local content = getViewModelMeshContent(part)
    if content then
        return tostring(content)
            .. "|"
            .. string.format("%.4f,%.4f,%.4f", part.Size.X, part.Size.Y, part.Size.Z),
            content
    end

    return nil, nil
end

function scaleViewModelMeshPoint(point, center, scale)
    return Vector3.new(
        (point.X - center.X) * scale.X,
        (point.Y - center.Y) * scale.Y,
        (point.Z - center.Z) * scale.Z
    )
end

function getViewModelWireframeMeshData(part)
    local key, content = getViewModelMeshCacheKey(part)
    if not key or not content then
        return nil
    end

    local cached = State.viewModelWireframeMeshCache[key]
    if cached ~= nil then
        return cached or nil
    end
    if State.viewModelWireframeMeshFailures[key] then
        return nil
    end

    local assetService = getViewModelWireframeAssetService()
    if not assetService or type(assetService.CreateEditableMeshAsync) ~= "function" then
        State.viewModelWireframeMeshFailures[key] = true
        return nil
    end

    local ok, editableMesh = pcall(function()
        return assetService:CreateEditableMeshAsync(content, {FixedSize = true})
    end)
    if not ok or not editableMesh then
        State.viewModelWireframeMeshFailures[key] = true
        return nil
    end

    local faces
    ok, faces = pcall(function()
        return editableMesh:GetFaces()
    end)
    if not ok or type(faces) ~= "table" or #faces == 0 then
        pcall(function() editableMesh:Destroy() end)
        State.viewModelWireframeMeshFailures[key] = true
        return nil
    end

    local meshSize = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    pcall(function()
        meshSize = editableMesh:GetSize()
    end)

    local center = Vector3.zero
    pcall(function()
        center = editableMesh:GetCenter()
    end)

    local scale = Vector3.new(
        part.Size.X / math.max(meshSize.X, 0.001),
        part.Size.Y / math.max(meshSize.Y, 0.001),
        part.Size.Z / math.max(meshSize.Z, 0.001)
    )
    local faceStride = math.max(math.ceil(#faces / State.viewModelWireframeMaxFaces), 1)
    local positions = {}
    local edgeKeys = {}
    local edges = {}

    for faceIndex, faceId in ipairs(faces) do
        if ((faceIndex - 1) % faceStride) == 0 then
            local vertices
            ok, vertices = pcall(function()
                return editableMesh:GetFaceVertices(faceId)
            end)

            if ok and type(vertices) == "table" and #vertices >= 2 then
                for vertexIndex = 1, #vertices do
                    local a = vertices[vertexIndex]
                    local b = vertices[(vertexIndex % #vertices) + 1]
                    local aKey = tostring(a)
                    local bKey = tostring(b)
                    local edgeKey = aKey < bKey and (aKey .. "|" .. bKey) or (bKey .. "|" .. aKey)

                    if not edgeKeys[edgeKey] then
                        edgeKeys[edgeKey] = true

                        if not positions[aKey] then
                            local positionOk, position = pcall(function()
                                return editableMesh:GetPosition(a)
                            end)
                            if positionOk and typeof(position) == "Vector3" then
                                positions[aKey] = scaleViewModelMeshPoint(position, center, scale)
                            end
                        end

                        if not positions[bKey] then
                            local positionOk, position = pcall(function()
                                return editableMesh:GetPosition(b)
                            end)
                            if positionOk and typeof(position) == "Vector3" then
                                positions[bKey] = scaleViewModelMeshPoint(position, center, scale)
                            end
                        end

                        if positions[aKey] and positions[bKey] then
                            table.insert(edges, {positions[aKey], positions[bKey]})
                            if #edges >= State.viewModelWireframeMaxEdges then
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if #edges >= State.viewModelWireframeMaxEdges then
            break
        end
    end

    pcall(function() editableMesh:Destroy() end)

    if #edges == 0 then
        State.viewModelWireframeMeshFailures[key] = true
        return nil
    end

    cached = {
        Edges = edges,
        Key = key,
    }
    State.viewModelWireframeMeshCache[key] = cached
    return cached
end

function shouldUseViewModelCharmPart(part, charmType)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then
        return false
    end

    local materialName
    if charmType == "Gun" then
        materialName = State.viewModelGunMaterial
    elseif charmType == "Arm" then
        materialName = State.viewModelArmMaterial
    end

    if isViewModelWireframeMaterial(materialName) then
        return isViewModelMeshVisual(part)
    end

    return true
end

function rememberViewModelMeshAppearance(part, original)
    if not part or not original then return end

    if part:IsA("MeshPart") and original.MeshPartTextureID == nil then
        local ok, textureId = pcall(function()
            return part.TextureID
        end)
        if ok then
            original.MeshPartTextureID = textureId
        end
    end

    original.SpecialMeshes = original.SpecialMeshes or {}
    original.SurfaceAppearances = original.SurfaceAppearances or {}

    for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") and original.SpecialMeshes[child] == nil then
            local ok, textureId = pcall(function()
                return child.TextureId
            end)
            original.SpecialMeshes[child] = ok and textureId or ""
        elseif child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") and original.SurfaceAppearances[child] == nil then
            original.SurfaceAppearances[child] = child.Parent
        end
    end
end

function backupViewModelAppearance(part)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") or State.originalViewModelAppearance[part] then
        return
    end

    local original = {
        Material = part.Material,
        Color = part.Color,
    }

    pcall(function()
        original.Transparency = part.Transparency
        original.LocalTransparencyModifier = part.LocalTransparencyModifier
    end)

    rememberViewModelMeshAppearance(part, original)
    State.originalViewModelAppearance[part] = original
end

function restoreViewModelAppearance(part)
    local original = part and State.originalViewModelAppearance[part]
    if not original then return end

    if part.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            part.Material = original.Material
            part.Color = original.Color
            if original.Transparency ~= nil then
                part.Transparency = original.Transparency
            end
            if original.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= nil then
                part.LocalTransparencyModifier = original.LocalTransparencyModifier
            end
            if original.MeshPartTextureID ~= nil and part:IsA("MeshPart") then
                part.TextureID = original.MeshPartTextureID
            end
        end)

        if original.SpecialMeshes then
            for mesh, textureId in pairs(original.SpecialMeshes) do
                pcall(function()
                    if mesh.Parent then
                        mesh.TextureId = textureId
                    end
                end)
            end
        end

        if original.SurfaceAppearances then
            for surfaceAppearance, parent in pairs(original.SurfaceAppearances) do
                pcall(function()
                    if parent and part.Parent and surfaceAppearance.Parent ~= parent then
                        surfaceAppearance.Parent = parent
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end
    State.originalViewModelAppearance[part] = nil
end

function clearViewModelMeshTextures(part)
    local original = State.originalViewModelAppearance[part]
    rememberViewModelMeshAppearance(part, original)

    pcall(function()
        if part:IsA("MeshPart") and part.TextureID ~= "" then
            part.TextureID = ""
        end
    end)

    for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
            pcall(function()
                if child.TextureId ~= "" then
                    child.TextureId = ""
                end
            end)
        elseif child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            pcall(function()
                child.Parent = nil
            end)
        end
    end
end

function applyViewModelCharmAppearance(part, materialName, color)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return end

    backupViewModelAppearance(part)
    local original = State.originalViewModelAppearance[part]
    local material = getMaterialByName(materialName)

    pcall(function()
        if part.Material ~= material then
            part.Material = material
        end
        if part.Color ~= color then
            part.Color = color
        end
        if original and original.Transparency ~= nil then
            part.Transparency = original.Transparency
        end
        if original and original.LocalTransparencyModifier ~= nil then
            part.LocalTransparencyModifier = original.LocalTransparencyModifier
        end
    end)

    clearViewModelMeshTextures(part)
end

function applyViewModelWireframeAppearance(part, color)
    if not part or not part:IsA("BasePart") then return false end

    if not isViewModelMeshVisual(part) then
        destroyViewModelWireframe(part)
        restoreViewModelAppearance(part)
        applyViewModelCharmAppearance(part, "Neon", color)
        return false
    end

    backupViewModelAppearance(part)
    if not ensureViewModelWireframe(part, color) then
        restoreViewModelAppearance(part)
        applyViewModelCharmAppearance(part, "Neon", color)
        return false
    end

    pcall(function()
        part.Transparency = State.viewModelWireframePartTransparency
        part.LocalTransparencyModifier = State.viewModelWireframePartTransparency
    end)
    return true
end

function destroyViewModelWireframe(part)
    local entry = part and State.viewModelWireframes[part]
    if entry and entry.Adornment then
        pcall(function()
            entry.Adornment:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    if part then
        State.viewModelWireframes[part] = nil
        for _, child in ipairs(part:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == State.viewModelWireframeName and child:IsA("WireframeHandleAdornment") then
                pcall(function()
                    child:Destroy()
                end)
            end
        end
    end
end

function clearViewModelWireframes()
    local parts = {}
    for part in pairs(State.viewModelWireframes) do
        table.insert(parts, part)
    end
    for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
        destroyViewModelWireframe(part)
    end

    local firstPerson = getViewModelsFirstPerson()
    if firstPerson then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(firstPerson:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                destroyViewModelWireframe(descendant)
            end
        end
    end
end

function ensureViewModelWireframe(part, color)
    if not part or not part:IsA("MeshPart") then return false end

    local data = getViewModelWireframeMeshData(part)
    if not data then
        return false
    end

    local existing = State.viewModelWireframes[part]
    if existing and existing.Adornment and existing.Adornment.Parent and existing.Size == part.Size and existing.MeshKey == data.Key then
        existing.Adornment.Color3 = color
        existing.Adornment.Visible = true
        return true
    end

    destroyViewModelWireframe(part)

    local wireframe
    local ok = pcall(function()
        wireframe = Instance.new("WireframeHandleAdornment")
        wireframe.Name = State.viewModelWireframeName
        wireframe.Adornee = part
        wireframe.Color3 = color
        wireframe.Transparency = 0
        pcall(function() wireframe.AlwaysOnTop = true end)
        pcall(function() wireframe.ZIndex = 10 end)
        wireframe.Parent = part

        for _, edge in ipairs(data.Edges) do
            wireframe:AddLine(edge[1], edge[2])
        end
    end)

    if not ok then
        if wireframe then
            pcall(function()
                wireframe:Destroy()
            end)
        end
        return false
    end

    State.viewModelWireframes[part] = {
        Adornment = wireframe,
        MeshKey = data.Key,
        Size = part.Size,
    }
    return true
end

function addViewModelParts(container, charmType, parts)
    if not container then return end
    if shouldUseViewModelCharmPart(container, charmType) then
        parts[container] = charmType
    end

    for _, descendant in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
        if shouldUseViewModelCharmPart(descendant, charmType) then
            parts[descendant] = charmType
        end
    end
end

function isViewModelCharmContainer(instance)
    return instance
        and (instance:IsA("Model") or instance:IsA("Folder") or instance:IsA("BasePart"))
end

function isViewModelGunCharmContainer(instance)
    return isViewModelCharmContainer(instance) and instance.Name == "ItemVisual"
end

function isViewModelArmCharmContainer(instance)
    if not isViewModelCharmContainer(instance) then
        return false
    end

    local name = instance.Name
    if State.viewModelArmContainerNames[name] then
        return true
    end

    local lowerName = string.lower(name)
    for _, blocked in ipairs(State.viewModelArmNameBlocklist) do
        if string.find(lowerName, blocked, 1, true) then
            return false
        end
    end

    for _, keyword in ipairs(State.viewModelArmNameKeywords) do
        if string.find(lowerName, keyword, 1, true) then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

function isInsideViewModelGunCharm(instance, firstPerson)
    local current = instance
    while current and current ~= firstPerson do
        if isViewModelGunCharmContainer(current) then
            return true
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end
    return false
end

function collectViewModelCharmParts()
    local parts = {}
    local firstPerson = getViewModelsFirstPerson()
    if not firstPerson then return parts end

    local descendants = firstPerson:GetDescendants()
    if State.viewModelCharmGunEnabled then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(descendants) do
            if isViewModelGunCharmContainer(descendant) then
                addViewModelParts(descendant, "Gun", parts)
            end
        end
    end

    if State.viewModelCharmArmEnabled then
        local namedArmParts = {}
        for _, descendant in ipairs(descendants) do
            if isViewModelArmCharmContainer(descendant) then
                addViewModelParts(descendant, "Arm", namedArmParts)
            end
        end
        for part in pairs(namedArmParts) do
            if not parts[part] and not isInsideViewModelGunCharm(part, firstPerson) then
                parts[part] = "Arm"
            end
        end

        for _, descendant in ipairs(descendants) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart")
                and not parts[descendant]
                and not isInsideViewModelGunCharm(descendant, firstPerson)
            then
                parts[descendant] = "Arm"
            end
        end
    end

    return parts
end

function getViewModelCharmMaterial(charmType)
    if charmType == "Gun" then
        return State.viewModelGunMaterial
    elseif charmType == "Arm" then
        return State.viewModelArmMaterial
    end
    return nil
end

function getViewModelCharmColor(charmType)
    if charmType == "Gun" then
        return State.viewModelGunColor
    elseif charmType == "Arm" then
        return State.viewModelArmColor
    end
    return Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
end

function syncViewModelCharm()
    local desiredParts = collectViewModelCharmParts()

    local partsToRestore = {}
    for part in pairs(State.originalViewModelAppearance) do
        if not desiredParts[part] then
            table.insert(partsToRestore, part)
        end
    end
    for _, part in ipairs(partsToRestore) do
        restoreViewModelAppearance(part)
    end

    local wireframesToDestroy = {}
    for part in pairs(State.viewModelWireframes) do
        local charmType = desiredParts[part]
        local materialName = getViewModelCharmMaterial(charmType)
        if not part.Parent or not charmType or not isViewModelWireframeMaterial(materialName) then
            table.insert(wireframesToDestroy, part)
        end
    end
    for _, part in ipairs(wireframesToDestroy) do
        destroyViewModelWireframe(part)
    end

    for part, charmType in pairs(desiredParts) do
        local materialName = getViewModelCharmMaterial(charmType)
        local color = getViewModelCharmColor(charmType)

        if isViewModelWireframeMaterial(materialName) then
            applyViewModelWireframeAppearance(part, color)
        else
            destroyViewModelWireframe(part)
            applyViewModelCharmAppearance(part, materialName, color)
        end
    end
end

function refreshViewModelCharm()
    clearViewModelWireframes()
    syncViewModelCharm()

    local enabled = State.viewModelCharmGunEnabled or State.viewModelCharmArmEnabled
    if enabled and not State.viewModelCharmConnection then
        State.lastViewModelCharmSync = 0
        State.viewModelCharmConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local now = os.clock()
            if now - State.lastViewModelCharmSync < State.viewModelCharmSyncInterval then return end
            State.lastViewModelCharmSync = now
            syncViewModelCharm()
        end)
    elseif not enabled and State.viewModelCharmConnection then
        State.viewModelCharmConnection:Disconnect()
        State.viewModelCharmConnection = nil
    end
end

function disableViewModelCharm()
    State.viewModelCharmGunEnabled = false
    State.viewModelCharmArmEnabled = false
    refreshViewModelCharm()
    clearViewModelWireframes()
end

FOVFillCircle = nil
FOVCircle = nil
SilentFOVFillCircle = nil
SilentFOVCircle = nil
FlickbotFOVCircle = nil
CustomCrosshair = nil
CustomCrosshairOutline = nil
fighterReq = nil
RageOrbitVisualLines = {}
RageOrbitTargetDot = nil
FOVFillGui = nil
FOVFillFrame = nil
FOVFillGradient = nil
SilentFOVFillGui = nil
SilentFOVFillFrame = nil
SilentFOVFillGradient = nil

local function getPlayerGui()
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not lp then return nil end
    local gui = lp:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if gui then return gui end
    local ok, found = pcall(function() return lp:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 5) end)
    return ok and found or nil
end

local function createFOVFillUI(isSilent)
    local playerGui = getPlayerGui()
    if not playerGui then return nil end

    local guiName = isSilent and "AspectRivalsSilentFOVFillGui" or "AspectRivalsFOVFillGui"
    local frameName = isSilent and "SilentFOVFillFrame" or "FOVFillFrame"

    local existing = playerGui:FindFirstChild(guiName)
    if existing then
        existing.DisplayOrder = 50
        existing.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        local frame = existing:FindFirstChild(frameName)
        if frame then
            frame.ClipsDescendants = true
            frame.ZIndex = 1
        end
        local gradient = frame and frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        if isSilent then
            SilentFOVFillGui = existing
            SilentFOVFillFrame = frame
            SilentFOVFillGradient = gradient
        else
            FOVFillGui = existing
            FOVFillFrame = frame
            FOVFillGradient = gradient
        end
        return existing, frame
    end

    local screenGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    screenGui.Name = guiName
    screenGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    screenGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    screenGui.DisplayOrder = 50
    screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    screenGui.Parent = playerGui

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = frameName
    frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.ClipsDescendants = true
    frame.ZIndex = 1
    frame.Visible = false
    frame.Parent = screenGui

    local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    corner.Parent = frame

    local gradient = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, State.fovCircleFillStartColor),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, State.fovCircleFillMiddleColor),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, State.fovCircleFillEndColor),
    })
    gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1 - State.fovCircleTransparency),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1 - State.fovCircleTransparency),
    })
    gradient.Rotation = State.fovRotationAngle or 0
    gradient.Parent = frame

    if isSilent then
        SilentFOVFillGui = screenGui
        SilentFOVFillFrame = frame
        SilentFOVFillGradient = gradient
    else
        FOVFillGui = screenGui
        FOVFillFrame = frame
        FOVFillGradient = gradient
    end

    return screenGui, frame
end

local function getFOVFillFrame(isSilent)
    local cachedFrame = isSilent and SilentFOVFillFrame or FOVFillFrame
    if cachedFrame and cachedFrame.Parent then
        return cachedFrame
    end

    local playerGui = getPlayerGui()
    if not playerGui then return nil end
    local guiName = isSilent and "AspectRivalsSilentFOVFillGui" or "AspectRivalsFOVFillGui"
    local frameName = isSilent and "SilentFOVFillFrame" or "FOVFillFrame"
    local screenGui = playerGui:FindFirstChild(guiName)
    if not screenGui or not screenGui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
        return nil
    end
    screenGui.DisplayOrder = 50
    screenGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
    local frame = screenGui:FindFirstChild(frameName)
    if frame then
        frame.ClipsDescendants = true
        frame.ZIndex = 1
    end
    local gradient = frame and frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
    if isSilent then
        SilentFOVFillGui = screenGui
        SilentFOVFillFrame = frame
        SilentFOVFillGradient = gradient
    else
        FOVFillGui = screenGui
        FOVFillFrame = frame
        FOVFillGradient = gradient
    end
    return frame
end

local function hideFOVFillUI(isSilent)
    local frame = isSilent and SilentFOVFillFrame or FOVFillFrame
    if frame then
        frame.Visible = false
    end
end

local function updateFOVFillUI(isSilent, center, radius, shouldShow, startColor, middleColor, endColor, transparency, rotationAngle)
    local frame = getFOVFillFrame(isSilent)
    if not frame and shouldShow then
        createFOVFillUI(isSilent)
        frame = getFOVFillFrame(isSilent)
    end
    if not frame then
        return
    end

    frame.Visible = shouldShow
    if not shouldShow then
        return
    end

    startColor = startColor or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    middleColor = middleColor or startColor
    endColor = endColor or startColor
    transparency = tonumber(transparency) or 0.7
    radius = math.max(0, tonumber(radius) or 0)

    frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, radius * 2, 0, radius * 2)
    frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, center.X, 0, center.Y)

    local gradient = isSilent and SilentFOVFillGradient or FOVFillGradient
    if not gradient or gradient.Parent ~= frame then
        gradient = frame:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient")
        if isSilent then
            SilentFOVFillGradient = gradient
        else
            FOVFillGradient = gradient
        end
    end
    if gradient then
        gradient.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, startColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, middleColor),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, endColor),
        })
        gradient.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1 - transparency),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1 - transparency),
        })
        gradient.Rotation = rotationAngle or 0
    end

    return frame.Visible
end

do
    local okFill, fillCircle = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Circle") end)
    if okFill and fillCircle then
        FOVFillCircle = fillCircle
        FOVFillCircle.Filled = true
        FOVFillCircle.Color = State.fovCircleFillMiddleColor or State.fovCircleColor
        FOVFillCircle.Radius = State.fov
        FOVFillCircle.Transparency = math.clamp(State.fovCircleTransparency or 0.7, 0, 1)
        FOVFillCircle.NumSides = 64
        FOVFillCircle.ZIndex = 0
        FOVFillCircle.Visible = false
    end

    local ok, circle = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Circle") end)
    if ok and circle then
        FOVCircle = circle
        FOVCircle.Thickness = 2
        FOVCircle.Transparency = 0.7
        FOVCircle.Color = State.fovCircleColor
        FOVCircle.Radius = State.fov
        FOVCircle.Filled = false
        FOVCircle.Transparency = State.fovCircleTransparency
        FOVCircle.Thickness = State.fovCircleThickness
        FOVCircle.NumSides = 64
        FOVCircle.ZIndex = 1
        FOVCircle.Visible = false
    end

    local okSilentFill, silentFillCircle = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Circle") end)
    if okSilentFill and silentFillCircle then
        SilentFOVFillCircle = silentFillCircle
        SilentFOVFillCircle.Filled = true
        SilentFOVFillCircle.Color = State.SilentAimState.FOVFillMiddleColor or State.SilentAimState.FOVColor
        SilentFOVFillCircle.Radius = State.SilentAimState.FOV
        SilentFOVFillCircle.Transparency = math.clamp(State.SilentAimState.FOVTransparency or 0.7, 0, 1)
        SilentFOVFillCircle.NumSides = 64
        SilentFOVFillCircle.ZIndex = 0
        SilentFOVFillCircle.Visible = false
    end

    local ok2, silentCircle = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Circle") end)
    if ok2 and silentCircle then
        SilentFOVCircle = silentCircle
        SilentFOVCircle.Thickness = 2
        SilentFOVCircle.Transparency = 0.7
        SilentFOVCircle.Color = State.SilentAimState.FOVColor
        SilentFOVCircle.Radius = State.SilentAimState.FOV
        SilentFOVCircle.Filled = false
        SilentFOVCircle.Transparency = State.SilentAimState.FOVTransparency
        SilentFOVCircle.Thickness = State.SilentAimState.FOVThickness
        SilentFOVCircle.NumSides = 64
        SilentFOVCircle.ZIndex = 1
        SilentFOVCircle.Visible = false
    end

    local ok3, flickCircle = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Circle") end)
    if ok3 and flickCircle then
        FlickbotFOVCircle = flickCircle
        FlickbotFOVCircle.Thickness = State.flickbotFOVThickness
        FlickbotFOVCircle.Transparency = State.flickbotFOVTransparency
        FlickbotFOVCircle.Color = State.flickbotFOVColor
        FlickbotFOVCircle.Radius = State.flickbotFOV
        FlickbotFOVCircle.Filled = false
        FlickbotFOVCircle.NumSides = 64
        FlickbotFOVCircle.ZIndex = 1
        FlickbotFOVCircle.Visible = false
    end

    local function newLine()
        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
        line.Thickness = State.customCrosshairThickness or 2
        line.Transparency = 1
        line.Color = State.customCrosshairColor
        line.Visible = false
        return line
    end

    local function newOutlineLine()
        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
        line.Thickness = (State.customCrosshairThickness or 2) + 2
        line.Transparency = 0.6
        line.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        line.Visible = false
        return line
    end

    local function newGradientLine()
        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
        line.Thickness = 2
        line.Transparency = 1
        line.Color = State.fovCircleColor
        line.ZIndex = 2
        line.Visible = false
        return line
    end

    CustomCrosshair = {
        Top = newLine(),
        Bottom = newLine(),
        Left = newLine(),
        Right = newLine(),
    }

    CustomCrosshairOutline = {
        Top = newOutlineLine(),
        Bottom = newOutlineLine(),
        Left = newOutlineLine(),
        Right = newOutlineLine(),
    }

    for i = 1, State.rageOrbitVisualSegments do
        local okLine, line = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Line") end)
        if okLine and line then
            line.Thickness = 2
            line.Transparency = 1
            line.Color = State.rageOrbitVirtualizeColor
            line.ZIndex = 8
            line.Visible = false
            table.insert(RageOrbitVisualLines, line)
        end
    end

    local okDot, dot = pcall(function() return Drawing.new("Circle") end)
    if okDot and dot then
        RageOrbitTargetDot = dot
        RageOrbitTargetDot.Filled = true
        RageOrbitTargetDot.Radius = State.rageOrbitTargetDotSize
        RageOrbitTargetDot.Transparency = 1
        RageOrbitTargetDot.Color = State.rageOrbitTargetDotColor
        RageOrbitTargetDot.NumSides = 24
        RageOrbitTargetDot.ZIndex = 9
        RageOrbitTargetDot.Visible = false
    end
end

function State.normalizeFOVPositionMode(mode)
    if mode == "Position on barrel" or mode == "FOV on Gun Muzzle" or mode == "FOV on Muzzle" then
        return "FOV on Gun Muzzle"
    end

    if mode == "Position on target" or mode == "FOV on Target" then
        return "FOV on Target"
    end

    return "Center"
end

function State.getLocalEquippedItem()
    if fighterReq and fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem then
        return fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem
    end

    pcall(function()
        local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
        local controllers = lp and lp.PlayerScripts and lp.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
        local fighter = controllers and controllers:FindFirstChild("FighterController")
        if fighter then
            local controller = require(fighter)
            if controller and controller.LocalFighter then
                fighterReq = controller
            end
        end
    end)

    return fighterReq and fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem or nil
end

function State.getMuzzleWorldPosition(camera)
    local equippedItem = State.getLocalEquippedItem()
    if equippedItem then
        local viewModel = nil
        pcall(function()
            viewModel = equippedItem.ViewModel
        end)

        local ok, muzzle = pcall(function()
            if viewModel and typeof(viewModel.GetMuzzlePosition) == "function" then
                return viewModel:GetMuzzlePosition()
            end

            if typeof(equippedItem.GetMuzzlePosition) == "function" then
                return equippedItem:GetMuzzlePosition()
            end
        end)
        if ok and typeof(muzzle) == "Vector3" then
            return muzzle
        end

        for _, container in ipairs({viewModel, equippedItem}) do
            if typeof(container) == "Instance" then
                local marker = container:FindFirstChild("Muzzle", true)
                    or container:FindFirstChild("Barrel", true)
                    or container:FindFirstChild("Handle", true)
                if marker then
                    if marker:IsA("Attachment") then
                        return marker.WorldPosition
                    elseif marker:IsA("BasePart") then
                        return marker.Position + marker.CFrame.LookVector * 5
                    end
                end

                local part = container:IsA("BasePart") and container or container:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                if part then
                    return part.Position + part.CFrame.LookVector * 5
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return camera and (camera.CFrame.Position + camera.CFrame.LookVector * 5) or nil
end

function State.getFOVTargetPart(kind, target, camera)
    if not target and kind == "Aimbot" then
        target = State.currentTarget or State.cachedAimbotTarget
    elseif not target and kind == "Silent" then
        target = State.cachedSilentTarget
    elseif not target and kind == "Flickbot" then
        target = State.flickbotCurrentTarget
    end

    if kind == "Flickbot" and State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart and State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart.Parent then
        return State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart
    end

    if target and target.Character then
        if kind == "Aimbot" and State.getCachedAimbotTargetPart then
            return State.getCachedAimbotTargetPart(target, camera)
        elseif kind == "Silent" and State.getCachedSilentTargetPart then
            return State.getCachedSilentTargetPart(target, camera)
        end

        return target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
            or target.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    end

    return nil
end

function State.getFOVCenter(camera, mode, target, targetPart, kind)
    if not camera then
        return Vector2.zero
    end

    mode = State.normalizeFOVPositionMode(mode)
    if mode == "FOV on Gun Muzzle" then
        local muzzlePos = State.getMuzzleWorldPosition(camera)
        if muzzlePos then
            local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(muzzlePos)
            if onScreen then
                return Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
            end
        end
    elseif mode == "FOV on Target" then
        local part = targetPart
        if not part or not part.Parent then
            part = State.getFOVTargetPart(kind, target, camera)
        end
        if part then
            local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
            if onScreen then
                return Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
            end
        end
    end

    return camera.ViewportSize / 2
end

function State.getSmoothedFOVCenter(key, targetCenter, delta)
    if not targetCenter then
        State[key] = nil
        return nil
    end

    local current = State[key]
    if not current then
        State[key] = targetCenter
        return targetCenter
    end

    local alpha = math.clamp((tonumber(delta) or 1 / 60) * (State.fovSmoothSpeed or 18), 0, 1)
    State[key] = current:Lerp(targetCenter, alpha)
    return State[key]
end

function State.updateFOVFillDrawing(circle, center, radius, shouldShow, color, transparency)
    if not circle then
        return
    end

    circle.Visible = shouldShow
    if not shouldShow then
        return
    end

    circle.Position = center
    circle.Radius = radius
    circle.Filled = true
    circle.Color = color
    circle.Transparency = math.clamp(tonumber(transparency) or 0.7, 0, 1)
    circle.NumSides = 64
    circle.ZIndex = 0
end

function Core.isSameTeam(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or targetPlayer == Players.LocalPlayer then return false end
    local playerTeamID = targetPlayer:GetAttribute("TeamID")
    local localTeamID = Players.LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("TeamID")
    return (playerTeamID and localTeamID) and (playerTeamID == localTeamID) or false
end

function Core.getPlayerEnvironmentID(player, character)
    if not player then return nil end

    local env = player:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID")
    if env ~= nil then
        return env
    end

    character = character or player.Character
    if character then
        env = character:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID")
        if env ~= nil then
            return env
        end
    end

    local workspaceModel = workspace:FindFirstChild(player.Name)
    if workspaceModel then
        env = workspaceModel:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID")
        if env ~= nil then
            return env
        end
    end

    return nil
end

function Core.isLobbyVisible()
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    local mainGui = playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
    local mainFrame = mainGui and mainGui:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
    local lobby = mainFrame and mainFrame:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
    if not (lobby and lobby:IsA("GuiObject")) then
        return false
    end

    local current = lobby
    while current and current ~= playerGui do
        if current:IsA("GuiObject") and not current.Visible then
            return false
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end

    return true
end

function Core.hasLocalViewModel(localPlayer)
    localPlayer = localPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
    if not localPlayer then
        return false
    end

    local equippedItem = State.getLocalEquippedItem and State.getLocalEquippedItem()
    if equippedItem then
        local viewModel = nil
        pcall(function()
            viewModel = equippedItem.ViewModel
        end)
        if viewModel and (typeof(viewModel) ~= "Instance" or viewModel.Parent) then
            return true
        end
    end

    local viewModels = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    if not viewModels then
        return false
    end

    local firstPerson = viewModels:FindFirstChild("FirstPerson")
    if firstPerson then
        for _, descendant in ipairs(firstPerson:GetDescendants()) do
            if descendant:IsA("BasePart") then
                return true
            end
        end

        if #firstPerson:GetChildren() > 0 then
            return true
        end
    end

    local localPrefix = localPlayer.Name .. " - "
    for _, viewModel in ipairs(viewModels:GetChildren()) do
        if viewModel ~= firstPerson and type(viewModel.Name) == "string" then
            if viewModel.Name == localPlayer.Name or viewModel.Name:sub(1, #localPrefix) == localPrefix then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

function Core.isLocalPlayerInMatch(localPlayer, localCharacter)
    return Core.hasLocalViewModel(localPlayer)
end

function Core.isSameMatch(targetPlayer, targetCharacter, localPlayer, localCharacter)
    localPlayer = localPlayer or Players.LocalPlayer
    if not targetPlayer or not localPlayer then return false end

    local targetEnv = Core.getPlayerEnvironmentID(targetPlayer, targetCharacter)
    local localEnv = Core.getPlayerEnvironmentID(localPlayer, localCharacter)
    if targetEnv == nil or localEnv == nil then
        return true
    end

    return targetEnv == localEnv
end

local function rawWorkspaceRaycast(origin, direction, params)
    local raycast = State.originalWorkspaceRaycast or workspace.Raycast
    return raycast(workspace, origin, direction, params)
end

local AntiWeapon = {}

function AntiWeapon:GetWeaponName(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer then return "" end
    local now = tick()
    local cache = State.weaponNameCache
    if not State.lastWeaponNameCacheUpdate or now - State.lastWeaponNameCacheUpdate >= 0.25 then
        for key in pairs(cache) do
            cache[key] = nil
        end
        State.lastWeaponNameCacheUpdate = now
        local viewModels = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
        if viewModels then
            for _, model in ipairs(viewModels:GetChildren()) do
                if model:IsA("Model") then
                    local modelName = model.Name
                    local separator = modelName:find(" - ", 1, true)
                    if separator then
                        cache[modelName:sub(1, separator - 1)] = modelName:sub(separator + 3)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return (cache[targetPlayer.Name] or ""):lower()
end

function AntiWeapon:IsKatana(targetPlayer)
