    local weaponName = self:GetWeaponName(targetPlayer)
    return weaponName:find("katana", 1, true) ~= nil
end

function AntiWeapon:IsShield(targetPlayer)
    local weaponName = self:GetWeaponName(targetPlayer)
    return weaponName:find("riot", 1, true) ~= nil or weaponName:find("shield", 1, true) ~= nil
end

function AntiWeapon:IsAntiWeapon(targetPlayer)
    return self:IsKatana(targetPlayer) or self:IsShield(targetPlayer)
end

function AntiWeapon:ShouldAttack(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Character then return false end
    local hum = targetPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
    if not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    if self:IsAntiWeapon(targetPlayer) then
        return false
    end
    return true
end

function Core.isTargetVisible(targetPart)
    if not targetPart or not targetPart:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return false end

    local origin = camera.CFrame.Position
    local direction = targetPart.Position - origin
    if direction.Magnitude <= 0 then return true end

    local params = State.visibilityRaycastParams or RaycastParams.new()
    State.visibilityRaycastParams = params
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {Players.LocalPlayer.Character}
    params.IgnoreWater = true

    local result = rawWorkspaceRaycast(origin, direction, params)
    if not result then
        return true
    end

    local hitInstance = result.Instance
    if not hitInstance then
        return true
    end

    return hitInstance == targetPart or hitInstance:IsDescendantOf(targetPart.Parent)
end

State.rapidFireEnabled = false
State.rapidFirePercent = 100
State.meleeCooldownEnabled = false
State.meleeCooldownPercent = 100
State.dashCooldownEnabled = false
State.dashCooldownPercent = 100
State.noSpreadEnabled = false
State.noRecoilEnabled = false
State.instantADSEnabled = false
State.weaponModsHooked = false
State.clientItemOriginalInput = nil
State.gunStartShootingOriginal = nil
State.gunStartAimingOriginal = nil
State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal = nil
State.gunEquipOriginal = nil
State.alwaysBackstabEnabled = false
State.alwaysBackstabDistance = 12
State.alwaysBackstabOffset = 2.75
State.meleeStartAimingOriginal = nil
State.meleeStartAimingHook = nil

State.weaponBackups = {}
State.lastBackupSelf = nil
local startWeaponModRetry

function State.isKnifeItem(item)
    if not item then return false end
    local name = item.Name
    if type(name) ~= "string" and type(item.Get) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            name = item:Get("Name")
        end)
    end
    name = tostring(name or "")
    return name == "Knife" or name == "Glast Shard"
end

function State.findBackstabTarget(maxDistance)
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local localCharacter = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not localRoot then return nil end

    local bestPlayer = nil
    local bestRoot = nil
    local bestDistance = maxDistance or State.alwaysBackstabDistance
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer
            and player.Character
            and Core.isSameMatch(player, player.Character, localPlayer, localCharacter)
            and not Core.isSameTeam(player)
        then
            local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and root and not root:FindFirstChild("TeammateLabel") then
                local distance = (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if distance <= bestDistance then
                    bestPlayer = player
                    bestRoot = root
                    bestDistance = distance
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return bestPlayer, bestRoot
end

function State.positionForBackstab(targetRoot)
    local character = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root or not targetRoot then return false end

    local behind = targetRoot.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.15, State.alwaysBackstabOffset)
    root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(behind.Position, targetRoot.Position)
    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    return true
end

local function applyAlwaysBackstabHook()
    if not State.alwaysBackstabEnabled then return false end

    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local ok, meleeModule = pcall(function()
        return require(localPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Melee)
    end)
    if not ok or not meleeModule or type(meleeModule.StartAiming) ~= "function" then
        return false
    end

    if State.meleeStartAimingHook and meleeModule.StartAiming == State.meleeStartAimingHook then
        return true
    end

    State.meleeStartAimingOriginal = meleeModule.StartAiming
    local original = State.meleeStartAimingOriginal
    local hook
    hook = function(controller, ...)
        if State.alwaysBackstabEnabled then
            local equipped = State.getLocalEquippedItem()
            if State.isKnifeItem(equipped) then
                local _, targetRoot = State.findBackstabTarget(State.alwaysBackstabDistance)
                if targetRoot then
                    pcall(State.positionForBackstab, targetRoot)
                end
            end
        end
        return original(controller, ...)
    end

    State.meleeStartAimingHook = hook
    meleeModule.StartAiming = hook
    return true
end

function State.enableAlwaysBackstab()
    State.alwaysBackstabEnabled = true
    applyAlwaysBackstabHook()
    startWeaponModRetry()
end

function State.disableAlwaysBackstab()
    State.alwaysBackstabEnabled = false

    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    pcall(function()
        local meleeModule = require(localPlayer.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Melee)
        if State.meleeStartAimingOriginal and meleeModule.StartAiming == State.meleeStartAimingHook then
            meleeModule.StartAiming = State.meleeStartAimingOriginal
        end
    end)

    State.meleeStartAimingOriginal = nil
    State.meleeStartAimingHook = nil
end

local meleeCooldownCache, dashCooldownCache, meleeCooldownBackups, dashCooldownBackups = {}, {}, {}, {}
local lastMeleeScanTime, lastDashScanTime, SCAN_COOLDOWN = 0, 0, 1

local function findAndCacheCooldownObjects(objectType, targetProperty)
    local now = tick()
    local cache = objectType == "melee" and meleeCooldownCache or dashCooldownCache
    local lastScanTime = objectType == "melee" and lastMeleeScanTime or lastDashScanTime
    
    if #cache > 0 or (now - lastScanTime) < SCAN_COOLDOWN then
        return cache
    end
    
    local foundObjects = {}
    for _, gcVal in pairs(getgc(true)) do
        if type(gcVal) == "table" and rawget(gcVal, targetProperty) then
            table.insert(foundObjects, gcVal)
        end
    end
    
    if objectType == "melee" then
        meleeCooldownCache = foundObjects
        lastMeleeScanTime = now
    else
        dashCooldownCache = foundObjects
        lastDashScanTime = now
    end
    
    return foundObjects
end

local function applyCooldownReductions()

    if State.meleeCooldownEnabled and State.meleeCooldownPercent < 100 then
        local meleObjects = findAndCacheCooldownObjects("melee", "AttackCooldown")
        for _, gcVal in pairs(meleObjects) do
            pcall(function()
                local key = tostring(gcVal)
                if not meleeCooldownBackups[key] then
                    meleeCooldownBackups[key] = gcVal.AttackCooldown
                end
                if meleeCooldownBackups[key] then
                    gcVal.AttackCooldown = meleeCooldownBackups[key] * (State.meleeCooldownPercent / 100)
                end
            end)
        end
    else

        for _, gcVal in pairs(meleeCooldownCache) do
            pcall(function()
                local key = tostring(gcVal)
                if meleeCooldownBackups[key] then
                    gcVal.AttackCooldown = meleeCooldownBackups[key]
                end
            end)
        end
        meleeCooldownBackups = {}
        meleeCooldownCache = {}
    end
    
    if State.dashCooldownEnabled and State.dashCooldownPercent < 100 then
        local dashObjects = findAndCacheCooldownObjects("dash", "DashCooldown")
        for _, gcVal in pairs(dashObjects) do
            pcall(function()
                local key = tostring(gcVal)
                if not dashCooldownBackups[key] then
                    dashCooldownBackups[key] = gcVal.DashCooldown
                end
                if dashCooldownBackups[key] then
                    gcVal.DashCooldown = dashCooldownBackups[key] * (State.dashCooldownPercent / 100)
                end
            end)
        end
    else

        for _, gcVal in pairs(dashCooldownCache) do
            pcall(function()
                local key = tostring(gcVal)
                if dashCooldownBackups[key] then
                    gcVal.DashCooldown = dashCooldownBackups[key]
                end
            end)
        end
        dashCooldownBackups = {}
        dashCooldownCache = {}
    end
end

local function applyInstantADSHooks(gunModule)
    if not gunModule then return end

    if not State.gunStartAimingOriginal and type(gunModule.StartAiming) == "function" then
        State.gunStartAimingOriginal = gunModule.StartAiming
    end
    if not State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal and type(gunModule.GetAimSpeed) == "function" then
        State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal = gunModule.GetAimSpeed
    end

    if State.instantADSEnabled then
        if type(gunModule.StartAiming) == "function" then
            gunModule.StartAiming = function(self, ...)
                pcall(function()
                    if self.SetReplicate then
                        self:SetReplicate("IsAiming", true)
                        if self.Info then
                            self:SetReplicate("FOVOffset", self.Info.AimFOVOffset)
                        end
                    end
                    if self.StopSprinting and type(self.StopSprinting.Fire) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() self.StopSprinting:Fire() end)
                    end
                    if self.ViewModel and type(self.ViewModel.SetAiming) == "function" then
                        pcall(function() self.ViewModel:SetAiming(true) end)
                    end
                    if self.ViewModel and self.ViewModel.CurrentAimValue ~= nil then
                        self.ViewModel.CurrentAimValue = 1
                    end
                end)
                return true, "StartAiming"
            end
        end

        if type(gunModule.GetAimSpeed) == "function" then
            gunModule.GetAimSpeed = function()
                return 999
            end
        end
    else
        if State.gunStartAimingOriginal and type(State.gunStartAimingOriginal) == "function" then
            gunModule.StartAiming = State.gunStartAimingOriginal
        end
        if State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal and type(State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal) == "function" then
            gunModule.GetAimSpeed = State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal
        end
    end
end

local function getFlickbotDuration()
    local speed = math.max(5, tonumber(State.flickbotSpeed) or 30)
    return math.clamp(1 / speed, 0.05, 0.25)
end

function State.getFlickbotOveraimPercent()
    return math.clamp(tonumber(State.flickbotOveraim) or 0, 0, 100)
end

local function getFlickbotEase(alpha)
    alpha = math.clamp(alpha, 0, 1)
    local accel = math.clamp((tonumber(State.flickbotAcceleration) or 0) / 100, 0, 1)
    if accel <= 0 then
        return alpha
    end
    return alpha ^ (1 + accel * 3)
end

local function animateFlickbotCamera(targetCFrame, duration, overaimPercent, settleMode, targetWorldPosition)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or not targetCFrame then
        return false
    end

    if type(mousemoverel) ~= "function" then
        duration = tonumber(duration) or 0
        if duration <= 0.004 then
            camera.CFrame = targetCFrame
            return true
        end

        local startCFrame = camera.CFrame
        local startTime = tick()
        repeat
            local alpha = math.clamp((tick() - startTime) / duration, 0, 1)
            camera.CFrame = startCFrame:Lerp(targetCFrame, getFlickbotEase(alpha))
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        until alpha >= 1 or not State.flickbotBusy

        if State.flickbotBusy then
            camera.CFrame = targetCFrame
        end
        return true
    end

    duration = math.max(tonumber(duration) or 0, 0.05)
    overaimPercent = math.clamp(tonumber(overaimPercent) or 0, 0, 100)
    local overaimScale = overaimPercent / 100
    local stepCount = math.clamp(math.ceil(duration * 240), 8, 30)
    local lastEase = 0
    local desiredPoint = targetWorldPosition or (camera.CFrame.Position + targetCFrame.LookVector * 1000)
    local screenPoint = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(desiredPoint)
    local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local baseDelta = Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y) - mousePos
    local travelDelta = baseDelta * (1 + overaimScale)

    if travelDelta.Magnitude > 0.5 then
        for step = 1, stepCount do
            if not State.flickbotBusy then
                break
            end

            local alpha = step / stepCount
            local eased = getFlickbotEase(alpha)
            local stepEase = math.clamp(eased - lastEase, 0, 1)

            if step == stepCount then
                stepEase = 1 - lastEase
            end

            if stepEase > 0 then
                mousemoverel(travelDelta.X * stepEase, travelDelta.Y * stepEase)
            end

            lastEase = math.max(lastEase, eased)

            if step < stepCount then
                task.wait(duration / stepCount)
            end
        end
    end

    if State.flickbotBusy and settleMode == "Damped" then
        local dampDelta = baseDelta * math.max(overaimScale, 0.04)
        for _, scale in ipairs({0.55, 0.32, 0.18, 0.09}) do
            if dampDelta.Magnitude <= 0.5 then
                break
            end

            mousemoverel(dampDelta.X * scale, dampDelta.Y * scale)
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    elseif State.flickbotBusy and settleMode == "Exact" then
        for _ = 1, 2 do
            camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not camera then
                break
            end

            local desiredPoint = targetWorldPosition or (camera.CFrame.Position + targetCFrame.LookVector * 1000)
            local screenPoint = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(desiredPoint)
            local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
            local delta = Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y) - mousePos
            if delta.Magnitude <= 0.75 then
                break
            end

            mousemoverel(delta.X, delta.Y)
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end
    return true
end

function State.syncFlickbotCameraRotation(cameraCFrame)
    if not cameraCFrame then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local modules = rs:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        local utilityModule = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("Utility")
        local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local replication = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Replication")
        local fighter = replication and replication:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
        local remote = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("UpdateCameraRotation")
        local utility = utilityModule and require(utilityModule)
        if utility and remote and type(utility.EncodeCameraRotation) == "function" then
            remote:FireServer(utility:EncodeCameraRotation(cameraCFrame.Rotation), nil)
        end
    end)
end

function State.getFlickbotAimDelta(part)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera or not part or not part.Parent then
        return nil, false
    end

    local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
    if not onScreen or screenPoint.Z <= 0 then
        return nil, false
    end

    local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    return Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y) - mousePos, true
end

function State.isFlickbotAimAligned(part, tolerance)
    local delta, onScreen = State.getFlickbotAimDelta(part)
    if not onScreen or not delta then
        return false
    end

    return delta.Magnitude <= (tonumber(tolerance) or State.flickbotAimTolerance or 10)
end

function State.waitForFlickbotAimAlignment(part, timeout)
    local started = tick()
    timeout = math.clamp(tonumber(timeout) or 0.12, 0, 0.25)

    repeat
        if State.isFlickbotAimAligned(part, State.flickbotAimTolerance) then
            return true
        end

        State.syncFlickbotCameraRotation(workspace.CurrentCamera and workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame or nil)
        RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
    until tick() - started >= timeout or not State.flickbotBusy

    return State.isFlickbotAimAligned(part, State.flickbotAimTolerance)
end

function State.getFlickbotOveraimCFrame(camera, targetPart, overaimPercent)
    if not camera or not targetPart then
        return nil
    end

    local cameraPosition = camera.CFrame.Position
    local fallback = CFrame.new(cameraPosition, targetPart.Position)
    local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPart.Position)
    if not onScreen or screenPoint.Z <= 0 then
        return fallback
    end

    local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local overaimScale = math.clamp(tonumber(overaimPercent) or 0, 0, 100) / 100
    local target2D = Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y)
    local overaim2D = mousePos + (target2D - mousePos) * (1 + overaimScale)
    local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(overaim2D.X, overaim2D.Y)
    local aimPoint = ray.Origin + ray.Direction * math.max(screenPoint.Z, 1)

    return CFrame.new(cameraPosition, aimPoint)
end

function State.setFlickbotFireTarget(part, duration, allowShootCorrection)
    State.flickbotActiveTargetPart = part
    State.flickbotActiveUntil = tick() + (tonumber(duration) or 0.2)
    State.flickbotFireConsumed = false
    State.flickbotShootCorrectionActive = not not allowShootCorrection
end

function State.clearFlickbotFireTarget()
    State.flickbotActiveTargetPart = nil
    State.flickbotActiveUntil = 0
    State.flickbotFireConsumed = false
    State.flickbotShootCorrectionActive = false
end

function State.getFlickbotFireTargetPart()
    if not State.flickbotActiveUntil or tick() > State.flickbotActiveUntil then
        State.clearFlickbotFireTarget()
        return nil
    end

    local part = State.flickbotActiveTargetPart
    if part and part.Parent then
        if not State.flickbotShootCorrectionActive and not State.isFlickbotAimAligned(part, State.flickbotAimTolerance) then
            State.clearFlickbotFireTarget()
            return nil
        end

        State.flickbotFireConsumed = true
        return part
    end

    State.clearFlickbotFireTarget()
    return nil
end

function State.getClosestFlickbotTarget()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local localCharacter = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not camera or not localPlayer or not localRoot or type(State.resolveTargetPart) ~= "function" then
        State.flickbotCurrentTarget = nil
        State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart = nil
        return nil, nil
    end

    local screenCenter = State.getFOVCenter(camera, State.flickbotFOVPositionMode, State.flickbotCurrentTarget, State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart, "Flickbot")
    local closestTarget = nil
    local closestPart = nil
    local closestDistance = math.huge
    local maxDistance = State.aimbotMaxDistance or 400

    local players = Players:GetPlayers()
    for i = 1, #players do
        local player = players[i]
        if player ~= localPlayer then
            local character = player.Character
            local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local targetRoot = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if character and humanoid and targetRoot and humanoid.Health > 0.09 and not character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
                if not State.flickbotTeamCheck or not Core.isSameTeam(player) then
                    local part = State.resolveTargetPart(character, State.flickbotPart, camera, screenCenter)
                    if part then
                        local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                        if onScreen and (targetRoot.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude <= maxDistance then
                            local screenDistance = (Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y) - screenCenter).Magnitude
                            if (not State.flickbotFOVEnabled or screenDistance <= State.flickbotFOV)
                                and (not State.flickbotWallCheck or Core.isTargetVisible(part)) then
                                if screenDistance < closestDistance then
                                    closestDistance = screenDistance
                                    closestTarget = player
                                    closestPart = part
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    State.flickbotCurrentTarget = closestTarget
    State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart = closestPart
    return closestTarget, closestPart
end

function State.tryFlickbotShot(controller, originalStartShooting, ...)
    if not State.flickbotEnabled or State.flickbotBusy or type(originalStartShooting) ~= "function" then
        return nil
    end

    local clientFighter = controller and controller.ClientFighter
    if not clientFighter or not clientFighter.IsLocalPlayer then
        return nil
    end

    local target, targetPart = State.getClosestFlickbotTarget()
    if not target or not targetPart then
        return nil
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then
        return nil
    end

    State.flickbotBusy = true
    local originalCFrame = camera.CFrame
    local targetCFrame = CFrame.new(originalCFrame.Position, targetPart.Position)
    local duration = getFlickbotDuration()
    local overaim = State.getFlickbotOveraimPercent()
    local visualCFrame = State.getFlickbotOveraimCFrame(camera, targetPart, overaim) or targetCFrame

    animateFlickbotCamera(visualCFrame, duration, overaim, nil, targetPart.Position)
    State.setFlickbotFireTarget(targetPart, State.flickbotFireHoldTime, true)
    State.syncFlickbotCameraRotation(targetCFrame)

    local args = { ... }
    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        State.syncFlickbotCameraRotation(targetCFrame)
        return { originalStartShooting(controller, table.unpack(args)) }
    end)

    if State.flickbotFireConsumed then
        task.wait(math.clamp(tonumber(State.flickbotReturnDelay) or 0.015, 0, 0.08))
    end

    if not State.flickbotDisableReturn and originalCFrame then
        animateFlickbotCamera(originalCFrame, duration, overaim, "Damped")
        if workspace.CurrentCamera then
            State.syncFlickbotCameraRotation(workspace.CurrentCamera.CFrame)
        end
    end

    State.clearFlickbotFireTarget()

    State.flickbotBusy = false
    if not ok then
        error(result)
    end
    return result
end

local function restoreWeaponInfo(weapon)
    if not weapon or not weapon.Info then return end
    local backup = State.weaponBackups[weapon]
    if not backup then return end

    pcall(function()
        if State.rapidFireEnabled then
            weapon.Info.ProjectileSpeed = 99999999
            if backup.ShootCooldown then
                weapon.Info.ShootCooldown = backup.ShootCooldown * (State.rapidFirePercent / 100)
            end
            if backup.QuickShotCooldown then
                weapon.Info.QuickShotCooldown = backup.QuickShotCooldown * (State.rapidFirePercent / 100)
            end
        else
            if backup.ShootCooldown ~= nil then weapon.Info.ShootCooldown = backup.ShootCooldown end
            if backup.QuickShotCooldown ~= nil then weapon.Info.QuickShotCooldown = backup.QuickShotCooldown end
            if backup.ProjectileSpeed ~= nil then weapon.Info.ProjectileSpeed = backup.ProjectileSpeed end
        end

        if State.noRecoilEnabled then
            weapon.Info.ShootRecoil = 0
        else
            if backup.ShootRecoil ~= nil then weapon.Info.ShootRecoil = backup.ShootRecoil end
        end

        if State.noSpreadEnabled then
            weapon.Info.ShootSpread = 0
        else
            if backup.ShootSpread ~= nil then weapon.Info.ShootSpread = backup.ShootSpread end
        end

        if State.infiniteJumpEnabled then
            weapon.Info.MaxDoubleJumps = 1000000
        else
            if backup then weapon.Info.MaxDoubleJumps = backup.MaxDoubleJumps end
        end
    end)
end

local function refreshWeaponModValues()
    for weapon, _ in pairs(State.weaponBackups) do
        restoreWeaponInfo(weapon)
    end
end

local function applyWeaponModHooks()
    if State.weaponModsHooked then
        if State.instantADSEnabled then
            local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
            if lp and lp.PlayerScripts then
                local ok, gunModule = pcall(function()
                    return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
                end)
                if ok and gunModule then
                    applyInstantADSHooks(gunModule)
                end
            end
        end
        return true
    end
    
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not lp or not lp.PlayerScripts then return false end
    
    local clientItemOk, clientItemModule = pcall(function()
        return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem)
    end)
    
    if not clientItemOk or not clientItemModule then return false end
    
    if not State.clientItemOriginalInput then
        State.clientItemOriginalInput = clientItemModule.Input
    end
    
    clientItemModule.Input = function(self, ...)
        if self and self.Info then

            if not State.weaponBackups[self] then
                pcall(function()
                    State.weaponBackups[self] = {
                        ShootCooldown = self.Info.ShootCooldown,
                        QuickShotCooldown = self.Info.QuickShotCooldown,
                        ProjectileSpeed = self.Info.ProjectileSpeed,
                        ShootRecoil = self.Info.ShootRecoil,
                        ShootSpread = self.Info.ShootSpread,
                        MaxDoubleJumps = self.Info.MaxDoubleJumps,
                    }
                end)
            end

            local backup = State.weaponBackups[self]
            
            if State.rapidFireEnabled then
                pcall(function()
                    self.Info.ProjectileSpeed = 99999999
                    if backup and backup.ShootCooldown then
                        self.Info.ShootCooldown = backup.ShootCooldown * (State.rapidFirePercent / 100)
                    end
                    if backup and backup.QuickShotCooldown then
                        self.Info.QuickShotCooldown = backup.QuickShotCooldown * (State.rapidFirePercent / 100)
                    end
                end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    if backup and backup.ShootCooldown ~= nil then self.Info.ShootCooldown = backup.ShootCooldown end
                    if backup and backup.QuickShotCooldown ~= nil then self.Info.QuickShotCooldown = backup.QuickShotCooldown end
                    if backup and backup.ProjectileSpeed ~= nil then self.Info.ProjectileSpeed = backup.ProjectileSpeed end
                end)
            end

            if State.noRecoilEnabled then
                pcall(function() self.Info.ShootRecoil = 0 end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    if backup and backup.ShootRecoil ~= nil then self.Info.ShootRecoil = backup.ShootRecoil end
                end)
            end

            if State.noSpreadEnabled then
                pcall(function() self.Info.ShootSpread = 0 end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    if backup and backup.ShootSpread ~= nil then self.Info.ShootSpread = backup.ShootSpread end
                end)
            end

                    if State.infiniteJumpEnabled then
                pcall(function() self.Info.MaxDoubleJumps = 1000000 end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    if backup then self.Info.MaxDoubleJumps = backup.MaxDoubleJumps end
                end)
            end
        end
        return State.clientItemOriginalInput(self, ...)
    end
    
    local gunOk, gunModule = pcall(function()
        return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
    end)
    
    if gunOk and gunModule then

        if not State.gunStartShootingOriginal then
            State.gunStartShootingOriginal = gunModule.StartShooting
        end
        
        gunModule.StartShooting = function(controller, ...)
            local result = State.tryFlickbotShot(controller, State.gunStartShootingOriginal, ...)
            if not result then
                result = {State.gunStartShootingOriginal(controller, ...)}
            end
            
            local clientFighter = controller and controller.ClientFighter
            if clientFighter and clientFighter.IsLocalPlayer and State.noSpreadEnabled then
                local cameraData = result[3]
                if cameraData and typeof(cameraData) == "table" then
                    result[4] = true
                end
            end
            
            return unpack(result)
        end

        applyInstantADSHooks(gunModule)
    end
    
    State.weaponModsHooked = true
    return true
end

local function restoreWeaponModHooks()

    return true
end

local weaponModRetryConn = nil
local lastWeaponModUpdate = 0
local WEAPON_MOD_UPDATE_INTERVAL = 0.25

startWeaponModRetry = function()
    if weaponModRetryConn then return end

    weaponModRetryConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = tick()
        
        local anyWeaponModEnabled = State.rapidFireEnabled or State.noSpreadEnabled or State.noRecoilEnabled or State.meleeCooldownEnabled or State.dashCooldownEnabled or State.instantADSEnabled or State.infiniteJumpEnabled or State.flickbotEnabled or State.alwaysBackstabEnabled

        if not anyWeaponModEnabled then

            if weaponModRetryConn then
                weaponModRetryConn:Disconnect()
                weaponModRetryConn = nil
            end
            return
        end

        if now - lastWeaponModUpdate >= WEAPON_MOD_UPDATE_INTERVAL then
            lastWeaponModUpdate = now
            applyCooldownReductions()
            if State.alwaysBackstabEnabled then
                applyAlwaysBackstabHook()
            end
        end

        if not State.weaponModsHooked then
            if State.rapidFireEnabled or State.noSpreadEnabled or State.noRecoilEnabled or State.instantADSEnabled or State.infiniteJumpEnabled or State.flickbotEnabled then
                applyWeaponModHooks()
            end
        end
    end)
end

function State.enableBulletTracers()
    State.bulletTracerEnabled = true
    if State.bulletTracerHooked then return end

    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not lp or not lp.PlayerScripts then return end

    local ok, gunModule = pcall(function()
        return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
    end)
    if not ok or not gunModule or type(gunModule._Tracers) ~= "function" then return end

    State.bulletTracerOriginalTracers = gunModule._Tracers
    gunModule._Tracers = function(self, data, ...)
        local tracerArgs = {...}
        pcall(function()
            if State.bulletTracerOriginalTracers then
                State.bulletTracerOriginalTracers(self, data, table.unpack(tracerArgs))
            end
        end)

        if not State.bulletTracerEnabled or not data then return end

        local rays = data.RaycastResults or {}
        if #rays == 0 then return end

        local muzzle
        if self and self.ViewModel and typeof(self.ViewModel.GetMuzzlePosition) == "function" then
            muzzle = self.ViewModel:GetMuzzlePosition()
        end
        if not muzzle then
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            muzzle = cam and cam.CFrame.Position
        end

        local isEnemy = data.IsEnemy
        if isEnemy and not State.bulletTracerEnemy then return end
        if not isEnemy and not State.bulletTracerLocal then return end

        local tracerColor = isEnemy and State.bulletTracerEnemyColor or State.bulletTracerLocalColor
        for _, ray in ipairs(rays) do
            local startPos = ray.StartPosition or muzzle
            local endPos = ray.Position
			if startPos and endPos then
				local distance = (endPos - startPos).Magnitude
				if distance >= 0.5 then
					local part = Instance.new("Part")
					part.Anchored = true
					part.CanCollide = false
					part.CanQuery = false
					part.CanTouch = false
					part.Transparency = 0.2
					part.Color = tracerColor
					part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
					part.Size = Vector3.new(0.02, 0.02, distance)
					part.CFrame = CFrame.new(startPos, endPos) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -distance / 2)
					part.Parent = workspace

					task.delay(State.bulletTracerDuration, function()
						if part and part.Parent then
							pcall(function() part:Destroy() end)
						end
					end)
				end
			end
        end
    end
    State.bulletTracerHooked = true
end

function State.disableBulletTracers()
    State.bulletTracerEnabled = false
    if not State.bulletTracerHooked then return end

    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not lp or not lp.PlayerScripts then return end

    local ok, gunModule = pcall(function()
        return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
    end)
    if ok and gunModule and State.bulletTracerOriginalTracers then
        gunModule._Tracers = State.bulletTracerOriginalTracers
    end

    State.bulletTracerOriginalTracers = nil
    State.bulletTracerHooked = false
end

local function getHitNotifierCharacterFromInstance(inst)
    local current = inst
    while current and current ~= workspace do
        if current:IsA("Model") then
            local hum = current:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                return current
            end
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end
    return nil
end

local function getHitNotifierMessage(playerName, damage, part)
    local format = State.hitNotifierMessageFormat or ""
    if format ~= "" then
        return format
            :gsub("{player}", playerName)
            :gsub("{damage}", damage)
            :gsub("{part}", part)
    end

    return "Hit " .. playerName .. " for " .. damage .. " in the " .. part
end

local function getHitNotifierDamageLabelValue(label)
    if not label or not label:IsA("TextLabel") then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local plainText = tostring(label.Text or "")
        :gsub("<[^>]->", "")
        :gsub(",", "")
        :gsub("%s+", "")
    if plainText:find("%", 1, true) then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local numericText = plainText:match("[-+]?%d+%.?%d*")
    local damage = numericText and tonumber(numericText)
    if not damage then
        return nil, nil
    end

    damage = math.abs(damage)
    if damage <= 0 or damage > 10000 then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local damageText = damage % 1 == 0 and tostring(math.floor(damage)) or string.format("%.1f", damage)
    return damage, damageText
end

local function isHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
    local current = label
    for _ = 1, 14 do
        if not current then
            break
        end

        local name = tostring(current.Name or ""):lower():gsub("[%s_%-]", "")
        if name:find("damagenumber", 1, true)
            or name:find("damagetext", 1, true)
            or name:find("damageeffect", 1, true)
            or name:find("hitnumber", 1, true)
            or name == "damage"
        then
            return true
        end

        if current:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            return true
        end

        for attributeName, attributeValue in pairs(current:GetAttributes()) do
            local attribute = tostring(attributeName):lower():gsub("[%s_%-]", "")
            local value = tostring(attributeValue):lower():gsub("[%s_%-]", "")
            if attribute:find("damage", 1, true)
                or attribute:find("hit", 1, true)
                or value:find("damage", 1, true)
                or value:find("hit", 1, true)
            then
                return true
            end
        end

        current = current.Parent
    end

    return false
end

local function isHitNotifierGuiActive(label)
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    local belongsToLocalGui = playerGui and label:IsDescendantOf(playerGui) or false
    local hasEnabledWorldGui = false
    local current = label

    for _ = 1, 16 do
        if not current then
            break
        end

        if current:IsA("GuiObject") and not current.Visible then
            return false
        end

        if current:IsA("LayerCollector") then
            if not current.Enabled then
                return false
            end
            if current:IsA("BillboardGui") or current:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                hasEnabledWorldGui = true
            end
        end

        if current == playerGui then
            break
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end

    if label.TextTransparency >= 0.99 then
        return false
    end

    return belongsToLocalGui or hasEnabledWorldGui
end

local function getHitNotifierLabelColor(label)
    local text = tostring(label.Text or "")
    local red, green, blue = text:match(
        "[Cc][Oo][Ll][Oo][Rr]%s*=%s*[\"'][Rr][Gg][Bb]%((%d+),%s*(%d+),%s*(%d+)%)"
    )
    if red and green and blue then
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            math.clamp(tonumber(red) or 255, 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(green) or 255, 0, 255),
            math.clamp(tonumber(blue) or 255, 0, 255)
        )
    end

    local hex = text:match("[Cc][Oo][Ll][Oo][Rr]%s*=%s*[\"']#(%x%x%x%x%x%x)")
    if hex then
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            tonumber(hex:sub(1, 2), 16) or 255,
            tonumber(hex:sub(3, 4), 16) or 255,
            tonumber(hex:sub(5, 6), 16) or 255
        )
    end

    return label.TextColor3
end

local function getHitNotifierPartFromColor(label)
    local color = getHitNotifierLabelColor(label)
    local redDominance = color.R - math.max(color.G, color.B)
    if color.R >= 0.7 and redDominance >= 0.25 then
        return "Head"
    end
    return "Body"
end

local function isHitNotifierTargetAllowed(player)
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player or not localPlayer or player == localPlayer then
        return false
    end

    if Core.isSameTeam(player) then
        return false
    end

    local targetEnvironment = Core.getPlayerEnvironmentID(player, player.Character)
    local localEnvironment = Core.getPlayerEnvironmentID(localPlayer, localPlayer.Character)
    return targetEnvironment ~= nil
        and localEnvironment ~= nil
        and targetEnvironment == localEnvironment
end

local function getHitNotifierBillboardTarget(label)
    local current = label
    for _ = 1, 14 do
        if not current then
            break
        end

        if current:IsA("BillboardGui") then
            local source = current.Adornee or current.Parent
            if source and source:IsA("Attachment") then
                source = source.Parent
            end

            local character = source and getHitNotifierCharacterFromInstance(source)
            local player = character and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
            if character and player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
                return player, source:IsA("BasePart") and source or nil
            end
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end

    return nil, nil
end

local function getHitNotifierClosestScreenTarget(label)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local center = label.AbsolutePosition + label.AbsoluteSize / 2
    local bestPlayer
    local bestPart
    local bestDistance = math.huge

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local character = isHitNotifierTargetAllowed(player) and player.Character
        if character then
            for _, candidateName in ipairs({
                "HitboxHead",
                "HitboxHeadSmall",
                "Head",
                "HitboxBody",
                "HitboxBodySmall",
                "HumanoidRootPart",
            }) do
                local part = character:FindFirstChild(candidateName)
                if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local viewport, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                    if onScreen and viewport.Z > 0 then
                        local distance = (Vector2.new(viewport.X, viewport.Y) - center).Magnitude
                        if distance < bestDistance then
                            bestDistance = distance
                            bestPlayer = player
                            bestPart = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if bestDistance <= 220 then
        return bestPlayer, bestPart
    end
    return nil, nil
end

local function getHitNotifierTargetFromLabel(label)
    local player, part = getHitNotifierBillboardTarget(label)
    if player then
        return player, part
    end
    return getHitNotifierClosestScreenTarget(label)
end

local function disconnectHitNotifierLabel(label)
    local connections = State.hitNotifierLabelConnections[label]
    if connections then
        for _, connection in ipairs(connections) do
            pcall(function()
                connection:Disconnect()
            end)
        end
    end
    State.hitNotifierLabelConnections[label] = nil
end

local function processHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
    if not (State.hitNotifierEnabled or State.hitEffectEnabled)
        or not label.Parent
        or not isHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
        or not isHitNotifierGuiActive(label)
    then
        return
    end

    local damage, damageText = getHitNotifierDamageLabelValue(label)
    if not damage then
        return
    end

    local now = tick()
    local textKey = tostring(label.Text)
    local targetPlayer = getHitNotifierTargetFromLabel(label)
    if not isHitNotifierTargetAllowed(targetPlayer) then
        return
    end

    local labelState = State.hitNotifierLabelState[label]
    if labelState
        and labelState.Text == textKey
        and now - (labelState.Time or 0) < 0.5
    then
        return
    end
    State.hitNotifierLabelState[label] = {
        Text = textKey,
        Time = now,
    }

    local playerName = targetPlayer.Name
    local partName = getHitNotifierPartFromColor(label)
    local detectionKey = playerName .. "|" .. partName .. "|" .. damageText
    if State.hitDetectionLastKey == detectionKey and now - (State.hitDetectionLastTime or 0) < 0.08 then
        return
    end
    State.hitDetectionLastKey = detectionKey
    State.hitDetectionLastTime = now

    if State.hitEffectEnabled and type(State.playHitEffect) == "function" then
        pcall(State.playHitEffect, targetPlayer.Character, partName)
    end

    if not State.hitNotifierEnabled then
        return
    end

    if State.hitNotifierLastNotifyKey == detectionKey and now - (State.hitNotifierLastNotifyTime or 0) < 0.08 then
        return
    end

    State.hitNotifierLastNotifyKey = detectionKey
    State.hitNotifierLastNotifyTime = now
    notify(
        "Hit Notifier",
        getHitNotifierMessage(playerName, damageText, partName),
        State.hitNotifierNotifySeconds or 2
    )
end

local function queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
    task.defer(processHitNotifierDamageLabel, label)
    task.delay(0.05, function()
        if (State.hitNotifierEnabled or State.hitEffectEnabled) and label.Parent then
            processHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
        end
    end)
end

local function watchHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
    if not label:IsA("TextLabel") or State.hitNotifierWatchedLabels[label] then
        return
    end

    State.hitNotifierWatchedLabels[label] = true
    local connections = {
        label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
        end),
        label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
            queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
        end),
        label:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextTransparency"):Connect(function()
            queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
        end),
        label.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
            if parent then
                queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
            else
                disconnectHitNotifierLabel(label)
                State.hitNotifierLabelState[label] = nil
            end
        end),
    }

    local current = label.Parent
    for _ = 1, 16 do
        if not current then
            break
        end

        if current:IsA("GuiObject") then
            table.insert(connections, current:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
            end))
        elseif current:IsA("LayerCollector") then
            table.insert(connections, current:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Enabled"):Connect(function()
                queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
            end))
        end
        current = current.Parent
    end

    State.hitNotifierLabelConnections[label] = connections
    queueHitNotifierDamageLabel(label)
end

local function startHitDetection()
    if #State.hitNotifierConnections > 0 then
        return
    end

    table.insert(State.hitNotifierConnections, game.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
        if (State.hitNotifierEnabled or State.hitEffectEnabled) and instance:IsA("TextLabel") then
            local function tryWatchDamageLabel()
                if (State.hitNotifierEnabled or State.hitEffectEnabled)
                    and instance.Parent
                    and isHitNotifierDamageLabel(instance)
                then
                    watchHitNotifierDamageLabel(instance)
                end
            end
            task.defer(tryWatchDamageLabel)
            task.delay(0.05, tryWatchDamageLabel)
        end
    end))

    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    if playerGui then
        for _, instance in ipairs(playerGui:GetDescendants()) do
            if instance:IsA("TextLabel") and isHitNotifierDamageLabel(instance) then
                watchHitNotifierDamageLabel(instance)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function stopHitDetection()
    if State.hitNotifierEnabled or State.hitEffectEnabled then
        return
    end

    for _, connection in ipairs(State.hitNotifierConnections) do
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    State.hitNotifierConnections = {}

    local labels = {}
    for label in pairs(State.hitNotifierLabelConnections) do
        table.insert(labels, label)
    end
    for _, label in ipairs(labels) do
        disconnectHitNotifierLabel(label)
    end

    State.hitNotifierWatchedLabels = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    State.hitNotifierLabelState = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    State.hitNotifierLastNotifyKey = nil
    State.hitNotifierLastNotifyTime = 0
    State.hitDetectionLastKey = nil
    State.hitDetectionLastTime = 0
end

function State.enableHitNotifier()
    State.hitNotifierEnabled = true
    startHitDetection()
end

function State.disableHitNotifier()
    State.hitNotifierEnabled = false
    State.hitNotifierLastNotifyKey = nil
    State.hitNotifierLastNotifyTime = 0
    stopHitDetection()
end

function State.enableHitEffect()
    State.hitEffectEnabled = true
    startHitDetection()
end

function State.disableHitEffect()
    State.hitEffectEnabled = false
    if type(State.clearHitEffects) == "function" then
        State.clearHitEffects()
    end
    stopHitDetection()
end

local function destroyEffectInstance(instance)
    if instance then
        pcall(function()
            instance:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end

local function createLocalAuraPart(container, basePart, className, name, size, color, transparency, c0)
    local part = Instance.new(className or "Part")
    part.Name = name
    part.Anchored = false
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.CanQuery = false
    part.CanTouch = false
    part.CastShadow = false
    part.Massless = true
    part.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
    part.Size = size
    part.Color = color
    part.Transparency = transparency or 0
    part.Parent = container

    local weld = Instance.new("Weld")
    weld.Name = name .. "Weld"
    weld.Part0 = basePart
    weld.Part1 = part
    weld.C0 = c0 or CFrame.identity
    weld.Parent = part
    return part, weld
end

local function createLocalAuraCarrier(container, root, name, c0)
    return createLocalAuraPart(
        container,
        root,
        "Part",
        name,
        Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1),
        Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
        1,
        c0
    )
end

local function createLocalAuraRing(container, carrier, name, radius, count, color, thickness, vertical)
    local segmentLength = math.max(0.18, (math.pi * 2 * radius / count) * 0.72)
    for index = 1, count do
        local angle = (index - 1) / count * math.pi * 2
        local position
        local rotation
        if vertical then
            position = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, math.sin(angle) * radius, 0)
            rotation = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, angle)
        else
            position = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle) * radius)
            rotation = CFrame.Angles(0, -angle, 0)
        end

        createLocalAuraPart(
            container,
            carrier,
            "Part",
            name .. tostring(index),
            vertical
                and Vector3.new(segmentLength, thickness, thickness)
                or Vector3.new(thickness, thickness, segmentLength),
            color,
            0.12,
            CFrame.new(position) * rotation
        )
    end
end

local function createLocalAuraWings(container, torso, color, secondaryColor, sizeScale, pairOffset)
    local animationObjects = State.localAuraAnimationObjects
    for _, side in ipairs({-1, 1}) do
        local carrier, carrierWeld = createLocalAuraCarrier(
            container,
            torso,
            side < 0 and "LeftWingCarrier" or "RightWingCarrier",
            CFrame.new(side * (0.55 + pairOffset), 0.4 - pairOffset * 0.15, 0.45 + pairOffset * 0.2)
        )
        table.insert(animationObjects.Wings, {
            Weld = carrierWeld,
            Base = carrierWeld.C0,
            Side = side,
            Phase = pairOffset * 2.5,
        })

        for featherIndex = 1, 6 do
            local featherLength = (1.15 - featherIndex * 0.08) * sizeScale
            local featherColor = featherIndex % 2 == 0 and secondaryColor or color
            createLocalAuraPart(
                container,
                carrier,
                "WedgePart",
                string.format("%sWingFeather%d", side < 0 and "L" or "R", featherIndex),
                Vector3.new(0.16 * sizeScale, featherLength, 0.42 * sizeScale),
                featherColor,
                0.08,
                CFrame.new(
                    side * featherIndex * 0.26 * sizeScale,
                    (0.55 - featherIndex * 0.12) * sizeScale,
                    featherIndex * 0.035
                ) * CFrame.Angles(
                    0,
                    side * math.rad(12),
                    side * math.rad(20 + featherIndex * 7)
                )
            )
        end
    end
end

local function createLocalAuraParticles(parent, color, secondaryColor, intensity, sizeScale, mode)
    local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    emitter.Name = "PhantomLocalAuraParticles"
    emitter.Texture = "rbxassetid://243660364"
    emitter.LightEmission = 1
    emitter.LightInfluence = 0
    emitter.LockedToPart = false
    emitter.Rate = math.floor((mode == "Flame" and 30 or 18) * intensity)
    emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.55, 1.15)
    emitter.Speed = mode == "Flame"
        and NumberRange.new(1.5 * sizeScale, 3.8 * sizeScale)
        or NumberRange.new(0.25 * sizeScale, 1.4 * sizeScale)
    emitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(360, 360)
    emitter.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
    emitter.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-120, 120)
    emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, color),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, secondaryColor),
    })
    emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25 * sizeScale),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, 0.65 * sizeScale),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0),
    })
    emitter.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.08),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
    })
    emitter.Parent = parent
    return emitter
end

function State.clearLocalAura()
    if State.localAuraAnimationConnection then
        pcall(function()
            State.localAuraAnimationConnection:Disconnect()
        end)
        State.localAuraAnimationConnection = nil
    end

    destroyEffectInstance(State.localAuraContainer)
    State.localAuraContainer = nil
    State.localAuraCharacter = nil
    State.localAuraAnimationObjects = nil
end

function State.buildLocalAura()
    State.clearLocalAura()
    if not State.localAuraEnabled then
        return
    end

    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local character = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local torso = character and (character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or root)
    if not character or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not root or not torso then
        return
    end

    local existing = character:FindFirstChild("PhantomLocalAura")
    if existing then
        destroyEffectInstance(existing)
    end

    local container = Instance.new("Folder")
    container.Name = "PhantomLocalAura"
    container.Parent = character
    State.localAuraContainer = container
    State.localAuraCharacter = character
    State.localAuraAnimationObjects = {
        Rotators = {},
        Wings = {},
    }
    local emitterCarrier = createLocalAuraCarrier(container, root, "AuraEmitterCarrier", CFrame.identity)

    local preset = State.localAuraPreset or "Astral Crown"
    local color = State.localAuraColor
    local secondaryColor = State.localAuraSecondaryColor
    local sizeScale = math.clamp(State.localAuraSize or 1, 0.5, 3)
    local intensity = math.clamp(State.localAuraIntensity or 1, 0.1, 3)

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "PhantomLocalAuraHighlight"
    highlight.Adornee = character
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.FillColor = color
    highlight.OutlineColor = secondaryColor
    highlight.FillTransparency = math.clamp(0.92 - intensity * 0.08, 0.55, 0.9)
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = math.clamp(0.35 - intensity * 0.12, 0, 0.35)
    highlight.Parent = container

    local light = Instance.new("PointLight")
    light.Name = "PhantomLocalAuraLight"
    light.Color = color
    light.Brightness = 1.4 * intensity
    light.Range = 9 * sizeScale
    light.Shadows = false
    light.Parent = emitterCarrier

    local function addRotatingRing(name, offset, radius, count, ringColor, vertical, speed, direction, bob)
        local carrier, weld = createLocalAuraCarrier(container, root, name .. "Carrier", offset)
        createLocalAuraRing(
            container,
            carrier,
            name,
            radius * sizeScale,
            count,
            ringColor,
            0.07 * sizeScale,
            vertical
        )
        table.insert(State.localAuraAnimationObjects.Rotators, {
            Weld = weld,
            Base = weld.C0,
            Speed = speed,
            Direction = direction or 1,
            Bob = bob or 0,
            Vertical = vertical,
        })
        return carrier
    end

    if preset == "Astral Crown" then
        addRotatingRing("AstralGroundRing", CFrame.new(0, -2.65 * sizeScale, 0), 2.5, 20, color, false, 0.75, 1, 0.08)
        local crown = addRotatingRing("AstralCrown", CFrame.new(0, 2.65 * sizeScale, 0), 1.35, 14, secondaryColor, false, 1.15, -1, 0.12)
        for spikeIndex = 1, 10 do
            local angle = (spikeIndex - 1) / 10 * math.pi * 2
            createLocalAuraPart(
                container,
                crown,
                "WedgePart",
                "AstralCrownSpike" .. spikeIndex,
                Vector3.new(0.18, 0.75, 0.34) * sizeScale,
                spikeIndex % 2 == 0 and color or secondaryColor,
                0.05,
                CFrame.new(math.cos(angle) * 1.18 * sizeScale, 0.38 * sizeScale, math.sin(angle) * 1.18 * sizeScale)
                    * CFrame.Angles(0, -angle, math.rad(180))
            )
        end
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, color, secondaryColor, intensity * 1.35, sizeScale, "Astral")
    elseif preset == "Angel Wings" then
        createLocalAuraWings(container, torso, Color3.fromRGB(235, 240, 255), secondaryColor, sizeScale, 0)
        addRotatingRing("AngelHalo", CFrame.new(0, 2.65 * sizeScale, 0), 0.78, 14, secondaryColor, false, 0.55, 1, 0.06)
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255), secondaryColor, intensity, sizeScale, "Light")
    elseif preset == "Seraph" then
        createLocalAuraWings(container, torso, Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255), secondaryColor, sizeScale, 0)
        createLocalAuraWings(container, torso, secondaryColor, color, sizeScale * 0.85, 0.55)
        addRotatingRing("SeraphHalo", CFrame.new(0, 2.8 * sizeScale, 0), 0.95, 16, color, false, 0.7, 1, 0.08)
        addRotatingRing("SeraphGround", CFrame.new(0, -2.6 * sizeScale, 0), 2.1, 18, secondaryColor, false, 0.45, -1, 0.05)
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, color, secondaryColor, intensity * 1.25, sizeScale, "Light")
    elseif preset == "Void Monarch" then
        local crown = addRotatingRing("VoidCrown", CFrame.new(0, 2.55 * sizeScale, 0), 1.25, 12, color, false, 1.35, -1, 0.14)
        addRotatingRing("VoidOrbit", CFrame.new(0, 0.25, 0), 2.15, 18, secondaryColor, true, 0.85, 1, 0.1)
        for spikeIndex = 1, 8 do
            local angle = (spikeIndex - 1) / 8 * math.pi * 2
            createLocalAuraPart(
                container,
                crown,
                "WedgePart",
                "VoidSpike" .. spikeIndex,
                Vector3.new(0.22, 0.95, 0.45) * sizeScale,
                spikeIndex % 2 == 0 and secondaryColor or color,
                0.02,
                CFrame.new(math.cos(angle) * sizeScale, 0.45 * sizeScale, math.sin(angle) * sizeScale)
                    * CFrame.Angles(0, -angle, math.rad(180))
            )
        end
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, color, secondaryColor, intensity * 1.4, sizeScale, "Void")
    elseif preset == "Ice Halo" then
        addRotatingRing("IceHalo", CFrame.new(0, 2.6 * sizeScale, 0), 0.95, 16, secondaryColor, false, 0.7, -1, 0.1)
        local shardCarrier = addRotatingRing("IceOrbit", CFrame.new(0, 0.1, 0), 1.65, 12, color, false, 0.95, 1, 0.15)
        for shardIndex = 1, 8 do
            local angle = (shardIndex - 1) / 8 * math.pi * 2
            createLocalAuraPart(
                container,
                shardCarrier,
                "WedgePart",
                "IceShard" .. shardIndex,
                Vector3.new(0.2, 0.85, 0.35) * sizeScale,
                shardIndex % 2 == 0 and color or secondaryColor,
                0.08,
                CFrame.new(math.cos(angle) * 1.45 * sizeScale, 0.4 * sizeScale, math.sin(angle) * 1.45 * sizeScale)
                    * CFrame.Angles(0, -angle, 0)
            )
        end
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, color, secondaryColor, intensity, sizeScale, "Ice")
    elseif preset == "Flame Wings" then
        createLocalAuraWings(container, torso, color, secondaryColor, sizeScale, 0)
        addRotatingRing("FlameGround", CFrame.new(0, -2.6 * sizeScale, 0), 1.9, 16, color, false, 1.15, 1, 0.08)
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, color, secondaryColor, intensity * 1.6, sizeScale, "Flame")
    elseif preset == "Lightning Orbit" then
        addRotatingRing("LightningOrbitA", CFrame.new(0, 0.3, 0), 1.8, 14, color, true, 1.75, 1, 0.18)
        addRotatingRing("LightningOrbitB", CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(90), 0), 2.15, 18, secondaryColor, true, 1.25, -1, 0.12)
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, color, secondaryColor, intensity * 1.5, sizeScale, "Lightning")
    else
        addRotatingRing("CelestialGround", CFrame.new(0, -2.55 * sizeScale, 0), 2.25, 20, color, false, 0.65, 1, 0.06)
        addRotatingRing("CelestialMiddle", CFrame.new(0, 0.1, 0), 1.75, 16, secondaryColor, false, 1.05, -1, 0.14)
        addRotatingRing("CelestialHalo", CFrame.new(0, 2.65 * sizeScale, 0), 0.85, 14, color, false, 1.35, 1, 0.09)
        createLocalAuraParticles(emitterCarrier, color, secondaryColor, intensity * 1.2, sizeScale, "Astral")
    end

    local animationClock = 0
    State.localAuraAnimationConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(delta)
        local auraHumanoid = State.localAuraCharacter
            and State.localAuraCharacter:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not State.localAuraEnabled
            or not State.localAuraContainer
            or not State.localAuraContainer.Parent
            or Players.LocalPlayer.Character ~= State.localAuraCharacter
            or not auraHumanoid
            or auraHumanoid.Health <= 0
        then
            task.defer(State.buildLocalAura)
            return
        end

        animationClock = (animationClock + math.min(delta, 0.05) * math.clamp(State.localAuraSpeed or 1, 0, 5)) % 1000
        local objects = State.localAuraAnimationObjects
        if not objects then
            return
        end

        for _, rotator in ipairs(objects.Rotators) do
            if rotator.Weld and rotator.Weld.Parent then
                local angle = animationClock * rotator.Speed * rotator.Direction
                local bob = math.sin(animationClock * 2 + rotator.Speed) * rotator.Bob
                local rotation = rotator.Vertical
                    and CFrame.Angles(0, 0, angle)
                    or CFrame.Angles(0, angle, 0)
                rotator.Weld.C0 = rotator.Base * CFrame.new(0, bob, 0) * rotation
            end
        end

        for _, wing in ipairs(objects.Wings) do
            if wing.Weld and wing.Weld.Parent then
                local flap = math.sin(animationClock * 3.2 + wing.Phase) * math.rad(13) * wing.Side
                wing.Weld.C0 = wing.Base * CFrame.Angles(0, flap * 0.35, flap)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function State.refreshLocalAura()
    if State.localAuraEnabled then
        State.buildLocalAura()
    else
        State.clearLocalAura()
    end
end

function State.queueLocalAuraRefresh()
    State.localAuraRefreshVersion = (State.localAuraRefreshVersion or 0) + 1
    local refreshVersion = State.localAuraRefreshVersion
    task.delay(0.08, function()
        if State.localAuraEnabled and State.localAuraRefreshVersion == refreshVersion then
            State.buildLocalAura()
        end
    end)
end

function State.enableLocalAura()
    State.localAuraEnabled = true
    State.buildLocalAura()
    if not State.localAuraCharacterConnection and Players.LocalPlayer then
        State.localAuraCharacterConnection = Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.delay(0.25, function()
                if State.localAuraEnabled then
                    State.buildLocalAura()
                end
            end)
        end)
    end
end

function State.disableLocalAura()
    State.localAuraEnabled = false
    State.clearLocalAura()
    if State.localAuraCharacterConnection then
        pcall(function()
            State.localAuraCharacterConnection:Disconnect()
        end)
        State.localAuraCharacterConnection = nil
    end
end

local HitEffectTweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local function removeHitEffectRecord(record)
    if not record then
        return
    end
    destroyEffectInstance(record.Gui)
    destroyEffectInstance(record.Highlight)
end

function State.clearHitEffects()
    for _, record in ipairs(State.hitEffectActive or {}) do
        removeHitEffectRecord(record)
    end
    State.hitEffectActive = {}
    State.hitEffectLastByCharacter = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
end

local function createHitEffectShape(canvas, name, color, position, size, rotation, rounded, strokeColor)
    local shape = Instance.new("Frame")
    shape.Name = name
    shape.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    shape.BackgroundColor3 = color
    shape.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    shape.BorderSizePixel = 0
    shape.Position = position
    shape.Rotation = rotation or 0
    shape.Size = size
    shape.ZIndex = 2
    shape.Parent = canvas

    if rounded then
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        corner.Parent = shape
    end

    if strokeColor then
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Name = "Glow"
        stroke.Color = strokeColor
        stroke.Thickness = 2
        stroke.Transparency = 0.05
        stroke.Parent = shape
    end
    return shape
end

local function createHitEffectRing(canvas, name, color, size)
    local ring = createHitEffectShape(
        canvas,
        name,
        color,
        UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        size,
        0,
        true,
        color
    )
    ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    local stroke = ring:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if stroke then
        stroke.Thickness = 3
    end
    return ring
end

local function tweenHitEffectShape(shape, duration, goal)
    local tween = HitEffectTweenService:Create(
        shape,
        TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        goal
    )
    tween:Play()

    local stroke = shape:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    if stroke then
        HitEffectTweenService:Create(
            stroke,
            TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
            {Transparency = 1}
        ):Play()
    end
end

local function buildClawHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    for index = 1, 3 do
        local claw = createHitEffectShape(
            canvas,
            "Claw" .. index,
            index == 2 and secondary or primary,
            UDim2.new(0.5, (index - 2) * 24, 0.5, 0),
            UDim2.fromOffset(9, 112),
            -24,
            true,
            secondary
        )
        tweenHitEffectShape(claw, duration, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, (index - 2) * 35, 0.42, -15),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 145),
        })
    end
end

local function buildRadialHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration, count, mode)
    for index = 1, count do
        local angle = (index - 1) / count * math.pi * 2
        local distance = mode == "Ice" and 58 or 72
        local color = index % 2 == 0 and secondary or primary
        local shard = createHitEffectShape(
            canvas,
            mode .. index,
            color,
            UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
            mode == "Ice" and UDim2.fromOffset(13, 38) or UDim2.fromOffset(8, 30),
            math.deg(angle) + 45,
            mode ~= "Ice",
            secondary
        )
        tweenHitEffectShape(shard, duration, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, math.cos(angle) * distance, 0.5, math.sin(angle) * distance),
            Size = mode == "Ice" and UDim2.fromOffset(5, 66) or UDim2.fromOffset(3, 54),
        })
    end
end

local function buildBombHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    local core = createHitEffectShape(
        canvas,
        "BombCore",
        primary,
        UDim2.fromScale(0.5, 0.5),
        UDim2.fromOffset(44, 44),
        0,
        true,
        secondary
    )
    tweenHitEffectShape(core, duration * 0.8, {
        BackgroundTransparency = 1,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(105, 105),
    })

    for ringIndex = 1, 3 do
        local ring = createHitEffectRing(canvas, "BombRing" .. ringIndex, ringIndex == 2 and secondary or primary, UDim2.fromOffset(28, 28))
        tweenHitEffectShape(ring, duration * (0.75 + ringIndex * 0.08), {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(95 + ringIndex * 34, 95 + ringIndex * 34),
        })
    end
    buildRadialHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration, 12, "BombRay")
end

local function buildLightningHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    local points = {
        {-35, -65, -28},
        {-15, -35, 34},
        {5, -8, -32},
        {-7, 20, 30},
        {18, 46, -30},
        {4, 70, 25},
    }
    for index, point in ipairs(points) do
        local segment = createHitEffectShape(
            canvas,
            "Lightning" .. index,
            index % 2 == 0 and secondary or primary,
            UDim2.new(0.5, point[1], 0.5, point[2]),
            UDim2.fromOffset(7, 46),
            point[3],
            true,
            secondary
        )
        tweenHitEffectShape(segment, duration, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(2, 58),
        })
    end
end

local function buildSlashHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    for index = 1, 3 do
        local slash = createHitEffectShape(
            canvas,
            "Slash" .. index,
            index == 2 and secondary or primary,
            UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, (index - 2) * 22),
            UDim2.fromOffset(135 - math.abs(index - 2) * 20, 9),
            -32,
            true,
            secondary
        )
        tweenHitEffectShape(slash, duration, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 18, 0.5, (index - 2) * 32 - 12),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(168, 3),
        })
    end
end

local function buildVoidHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    for ringIndex = 1, 4 do
        local ring = createHitEffectRing(canvas, "VoidRing" .. ringIndex, ringIndex % 2 == 0 and secondary or primary, UDim2.fromOffset(18, 18))
        ring.Rotation = ringIndex * 18
        tweenHitEffectShape(ring, duration * (0.65 + ringIndex * 0.08), {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(55 + ringIndex * 35, 55 + ringIndex * 35),
            Rotation = ring.Rotation + (ringIndex % 2 == 0 and -120 or 120),
        })
    end
    buildRadialHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration, 10, "VoidRay")
end

local function buildStarfallHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    for index = 1, 11 do
        local x = ((index * 37) % 130) - 65
        local star = createHitEffectShape(
            canvas,
            "Star" .. index,
            index % 2 == 0 and secondary or primary,
            UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, -80 - (index % 3) * 15),
            UDim2.fromOffset(13, 13),
            45,
            false,
            secondary
        )
        tweenHitEffectShape(star, duration * (0.75 + (index % 4) * 0.08), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x * 0.65, 0.5, 72),
            Rotation = 225,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(4, 4),
        })
    end
end

local function buildSoulHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    for index = 1, 9 do
        local x = ((index * 29) % 100) - 50
        local soul = createHitEffectShape(
            canvas,
            "Soul" .. index,
            index % 2 == 0 and secondary or primary,
            UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, 40),
            UDim2.fromOffset(18 + index % 3 * 5, 18 + index % 3 * 5),
            0,
            true,
            secondary
        )
        tweenHitEffectShape(soul, duration * (0.8 + index * 0.025), {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x * 1.2, 0.5, -75),
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, 5),
        })
    end
end

local function buildShockwaveHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    for ringIndex = 1, 5 do
        local ring = createHitEffectRing(canvas, "Shockwave" .. ringIndex, ringIndex % 2 == 0 and secondary or primary, UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16))
        tweenHitEffectShape(ring, duration * (0.55 + ringIndex * 0.08), {
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(45 + ringIndex * 35, 45 + ringIndex * 35),
        })
    end
end

local function buildCrownBreakHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    for index = 1, 9 do
        local x = (index - 5) * 17
        local spike = createHitEffectShape(
            canvas,
            "CrownSpike" .. index,
            index % 2 == 0 and secondary or primary,
            UDim2.new(0.5, x, 0.5, -42 + math.abs(index - 5) * 7),
            UDim2.fromOffset(10, 54 - math.abs(index - 5) * 4),
            (index - 5) * 7,
            false,
            secondary
        )
        tweenHitEffectShape(spike, duration, {
            BackgroundTransparency = 1,
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, x * 1.25, 0.5, 50),
            Rotation = spike.Rotation + (index - 5) * 10,
            Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, 74),
        })
    end
end

function State.playHitEffect(character, partName)
    if not State.hitEffectEnabled or not character or not character.Parent then
        return
    end

    local now = tick()
    local lastTime = State.hitEffectLastByCharacter[character]
    if lastTime and now - lastTime < 0.035 then
        return
    end
    State.hitEffectLastByCharacter[character] = now

    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerGui = localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    local adornee = partName == "Head" and character:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not playerGui or not adornee then
        return
    end

    while #State.hitEffectActive >= (State.hitEffectMaxActive or 12) do
        removeHitEffectRecord(table.remove(State.hitEffectActive, 1))
    end

    local sizeScale = math.clamp(State.hitEffectSize or 1, 0.5, 3)
    local intensity = math.clamp(State.hitEffectIntensity or 1, 0.1, 3)
    local duration = math.clamp(State.hitEffectDuration or 0.8, 0.15, 3)
    local primary = State.hitEffectColor
    local secondary = State.hitEffectSecondaryColor

    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "PhantomHitEffect"
    billboard.Adornee = adornee
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = State.hitEffectThroughWalls
    billboard.LightInfluence = 0
    billboard.MaxDistance = 10000
    billboard.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(180 * sizeScale, 180 * sizeScale)
    billboard.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, partName == "Head" and 0.25 or 0.65, 0)
    billboard.Parent = playerGui

    local canvas = Instance.new("Frame")
    canvas.Name = "Canvas"
    canvas.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    canvas.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
    canvas.Parent = billboard

    local canvasScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
    canvasScale.Scale = math.clamp(0.85 + intensity * 0.15, 0.85, 1.3)
    canvasScale.Parent = canvas

    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.Name = "PhantomHitHighlight"
    highlight.Adornee = character
    highlight.DepthMode = State.hitEffectThroughWalls
        and Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        or Enum.HighlightDepthMode.Occluded
    highlight.FillColor = primary
    highlight.OutlineColor = secondary
    highlight.FillTransparency = math.clamp(0.88 - intensity * 0.12, 0.45, 0.88)
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = math.clamp(0.3 - intensity * 0.1, 0, 0.3)
    highlight.Parent = character

    local record = {
        Gui = billboard,
        Highlight = highlight,
    }
    table.insert(State.hitEffectActive, record)

    local preset = State.hitEffectPreset or "Claw"
    if preset == "Claw" then
        buildClawHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    elseif preset == "Ice" then
        buildRadialHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration, 10, "Ice")
    elseif preset == "Bomb" then
        buildBombHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    elseif preset == "Lightning" then
        buildLightningHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    elseif preset == "Slash" then
        buildSlashHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    elseif preset == "Void Burst" then
        buildVoidHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    elseif preset == "Starfall" then
        buildStarfallHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    elseif preset == "Soul Rise" then
        buildSoulHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    elseif preset == "Shockwave" then
        buildShockwaveHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    else
        buildCrownBreakHitEffect(canvas, primary, secondary, duration)
    end

    HitEffectTweenService:Create(
        highlight,
        TweenInfo.new(duration, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
        {FillTransparency = 1, OutlineTransparency = 1}
    ):Play()

    task.delay(duration + 0.15, function()
        local index = table.find(State.hitEffectActive, record)
        if index then
            table.remove(State.hitEffectActive, index)
        end
        removeHitEffectRecord(record)
    end)
end

local function applySoundHook()
    local Utility = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Utility)
    if not State.originalCreateSound then
        State.originalCreateSound = Utility.CreateSound
    end

    Utility.CreateSound = function(self, soundId, volume, pitch, parent, playNow, destroyDelay, rollOffMin, rollOffMax, soundGroup, customProps)
