        if State.hitsoundChangerEnabled and (soundId == "rbxassetid://16537449730" or soundId == "rbxassetid://16537337310" or soundId == "rbxassetid://13110130082") then
            soundId = State.hitsoundId
            pitch = State.hitsoundPitch
            volume = State.hitsoundVolume
        end

        if State.shootSoundChangerEnabled and parent and typeof(parent) == "Instance" and parent.Name == "ClientItem" then
            if soundId ~= "rbxassetid://16537449730" and soundId ~= "rbxassetid://16537337310" and soundId ~= "rbxassetid://13110130082" then
                local tb = debug.traceback("", 2)
                local isShootSound = false
                if tb then
                    local tbLower = string.lower(tb)
                    if string.find(tbLower, "_shoot") or string.find(tbLower, "startshooting") or string.find(tbLower, "finalshoot") then
                        isShootSound = true
                    end
                end
                if isShootSound then
                    soundId = State.shootSoundId
                    pitch = State.shootSoundPitch
                    volume = State.shootSoundVolume
                end
            end
        end

        return State.originalCreateSound(self, soundId, volume, pitch, parent, playNow, destroyDelay, rollOffMin, rollOffMax, soundGroup, customProps)
    end
end

local function removeSoundHook()
    local Utility = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Modules.Utility)
    if State.originalCreateSound then
        Utility.CreateSound = State.originalCreateSound
        if not State.hitsoundChangerEnabled and not State.shootSoundChangerEnabled then
            State.originalCreateSound = nil
        end
    end
end

function State.enableHitsoundChanger()
    State.hitsoundChangerEnabled = true
    applySoundHook()
end

function State.disableHitsoundChanger()
    State.hitsoundChangerEnabled = false
    if not State.shootSoundChangerEnabled then
        removeSoundHook()
    end
end

function State.enableShootSoundChanger()
    State.shootSoundChangerEnabled = true
    applySoundHook()
end

function State.disableShootSoundChanger()
    State.shootSoundChangerEnabled = false
    if not State.hitsoundChangerEnabled then
        removeSoundHook()
    end
end

State.ffaAutoGrabConnection = nil
State.ffaDropAddedConnection = nil
State.ffaDropRemovedConnection = nil
State.ffaDrops = {}

local function trackFFADrop(obj)
    if obj and obj.Name == "_drop" and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
        State.ffaDrops[obj] = true
    end
end

local function untrackFFADrop(obj)
    if obj then
        State.ffaDrops[obj] = nil
    end
end

function State.enableFFAAutoGrab()
    if State.ffaAutoGrabConnection then return end

    State.ffaDrops = {}
    for _, obj in workspace:GetChildren() do
        trackFFADrop(obj)
    end

    State.ffaDropAddedConnection = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(trackFFADrop)
    State.ffaDropRemovedConnection = workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(untrackFFADrop)

    local lastGrabTime = 0
    State.ffaAutoGrabConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastGrabTime < 0.15 then return end
        lastGrabTime = now

        local character = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end
        local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local needsHealth = humanoid and humanoid.Health < humanoid.MaxHealth
        for obj in pairs(State.ffaDrops) do
            if not obj.Parent then
                State.ffaDrops[obj] = nil
            elseif (State.ffaCollectHealth and obj:FindFirstChild("Health") and needsHealth) or (State.ffaCollectAmmo and obj:FindFirstChild("Ammo")) then
                firetouchinterest(hrp, obj, 0)
                firetouchinterest(hrp, obj, 1)
            end
        end
    end)
end

function State.disableFFAAutoGrab()
    if State.ffaAutoGrabConnection then
        State.ffaAutoGrabConnection:Disconnect()
        State.ffaAutoGrabConnection = nil
    end
    if State.ffaDropAddedConnection then
        State.ffaDropAddedConnection:Disconnect()
        State.ffaDropAddedConnection = nil
    end
    if State.ffaDropRemovedConnection then
        State.ffaDropRemovedConnection:Disconnect()
        State.ffaDropRemovedConnection = nil
    end
    for obj in pairs(State.ffaDrops) do
        State.ffaDrops[obj] = nil
    end
end

State.ffaAutoRespawnConnection = nil
State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection = nil

function State.enableFFAAutoRespawn()
    if State.ffaAutoRespawnConnection then return end
    
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local respawnRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Duels"):WaitForChild("RespawnNow")
    
    local function setupRespawn(character)
        local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        if State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection then
            State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection:Disconnect()
            State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection = nil
        end
        State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection = humanoid.Died:Connect(function()
            if not State.ffaAutoRespawnConnection then return end
            task.wait()
            pcall(function()
                respawnRemote:FireServer()
            end)
        end)
    end
    
    State.ffaAutoRespawnConnection = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(setupRespawn)
    if LocalPlayer.Character then
        setupRespawn(LocalPlayer.Character)
    end
end

function State.disableFFAAutoRespawn()
    if State.ffaAutoRespawnConnection then
        State.ffaAutoRespawnConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    if State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection then
        State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    State.ffaAutoRespawnConnection = nil
    State.ffaAutoRespawnDiedConnection = nil
end

local function unlockAll()
    local plrs, reps, http = game:GetService("Players"), game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"), game:GetService("HttpService")
local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
local lps, ctrls, rmods = lp.PlayerScripts, lps.Controllers, reps.Modules
local rebuildEnabled = false
local elib = require(rmods:WaitForChild("EnumLibrary", 10))
if elib then elib:WaitForEnumBuilder() end
local clib = require(rmods:WaitForChild("CosmeticLibrary", 10))
local ilib = require(rmods:WaitForChild("ItemLibrary", 10))
local dctrl = require(ctrls:WaitForChild("PlayerDataController", 10))
local coss = clib.Cosmetics

local cdata = dctrl.CurrentData
if not cdata then
	task.spawn(function()
		repeat task.wait() until dctrl.CurrentData
		cdata = dctrl.CurrentData
	end)
end

local equip, favs, fcache = {}, {}, {}
local cwep, vprof, lwep
local lpname = lp.Name

local function banned(n)
	if type(n) ~= "string" then return true end
	return n:find("MISSING_") or n:find("Bubblegum") or n:find("Ragdoll") or n:find("Fall Apart") or n:find("Every Finisher Ever")
end

local function toenum(n)
	if not elib then return nil end
	local ok, id = pcall(elib.ToEnum, elib, n)
	return ok and id or nil
end

local function clonecos(name, ctype, inv, favonly)
	if banned(name) then return nil end
	local base = coss[name]
	if not base then return nil end
	local d = table.clone(base)
	d.Name = name
	d.Type = d.Type or ctype
	d.Seed = d.Seed or math.random(1, 1000000)
	local eid = toenum(name)
	if eid then d.Enum = eid d.ObjectID = d.ObjectID or eid end
	if inv ~= nil then d.Inverted = inv end
	if favonly ~= nil then d.OnlyUseFavorites = favonly end
	return d
end

local savef = "PhantomRivals/unlockall.json"

local function savecfg()
	if not writefile then return end
	pcall(function()
		local cfg = { equipped = {}, favorites = favs }
		for wep, cos in equip do
			local slot = {}
			cfg.equipped[wep] = slot
			for ct, cd in cos do
				if cd and cd.Name and not banned(cd.Name) then
					slot[ct] = { name = cd.Name, seed = cd.Seed, inverted = cd.Inverted }
				end
			end
		end
		makefolder("PhantomRivals")
		writefile(savef, http:JSONEncode(cfg))
	end)
end

function loadcfg()
	if not readfile or not isfile or not isfile(savef) then return end
	pcall(function()
		local cfg = http:JSONDecode(readfile(savef))
		if cfg.equipped then
			for wep, cos in pairs(cfg.equipped) do
				equip[wep] = {}
				for ct, cd in pairs(cos) do
					if cd and not banned(cd.name) then
						local cl = clonecos(cd.name, ct, cd.inverted)
						if cl then
							cl.Seed = cd.seed
							equip[wep][ct] = cl
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		favs = cfg.favorites or {}
	end)
end

local finv = {}
local cachedCosmeticProxy = nil
local cachedWeaponData = {}
local cachedFavorited = nil

local function rebuildinv()
    if not rebuildEnabled then return end
	table.clear(finv)
	for name in coss do
		if not banned(name) then finv[name] = true end
	end
	for _, cos in equip do
		for _, cd in cos do
			if cd and cd.Name and not banned(cd.Name) then finv[cd.Name] = true end
		end
	end
	cachedCosmeticProxy = nil
	cachedWeaponData = {}
	cachedFavorited = nil
end

rebuildinv()

local oget = dctrl.Get
dctrl.Get = function(self, key)
	local data = oget(self, key)
	if key == "CosmeticInventory" then
		if cachedCosmeticProxy then return cachedCosmeticProxy end
		local proxy = {}
		if data then for k, v in data do if not banned(k) then proxy[k] = v end end end
		for name in finv do proxy[name] = true end
		cachedCosmeticProxy = proxy
		return proxy
	end
	if key == "FavoritedCosmetics" then
		if cachedFavorited then return cachedFavorited end
		local res = data and table.clone(data) or {}
		for wep, fv in favs do
			local slot = res[wep] or {}
			res[wep] = slot
			for name, isfav in fv do
				if not banned(name) then slot[name] = isfav end
			end
		end
		cachedFavorited = res
		return res
	end
	return data
end

local ogetwep = dctrl.GetWeaponData
dctrl.GetWeaponData = function(self, wname)
	if cachedWeaponData[wname] then return cachedWeaponData[wname] end
	local data = ogetwep(self, wname)
	if not data then return nil end
	local merged = table.clone(data)
	merged.Name = wname
	local weq = equip[wname]
	if weq then for ct, cd in weq do merged[ct] = cd end end
	cachedWeaponData[wname] = merged
	return merged
end

local function saferep(key)
	pcall(function()
		if not cdata and dctrl.CurrentData then cdata = dctrl.CurrentData end
		if cdata then cdata:Replicate(key) end
	end)
end

local fctrl
pcall(function() fctrl = require(ctrls:WaitForChild("FighterController", 10)) end)

local function getewep()
	if not fctrl then return nil end
	local fighter = fctrl:GetFighter(lp)
	if not fighter or not fighter.Items then return nil end
	for _, item in fighter.Items do
		if item.IsEquipped then return item.Name end
	end
	return nil
end

if hookmetamethod then
	local rems = reps:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local drems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Data")
	local eqrem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("EquipCosmetic")
	local favrem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("FavoriteCosmetic")
	local rrems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Replication")
	local frems = rrems and rrems:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
	local uirem = frems and frems:FindFirstChild("UseItem")

	if eqrem then
		local oncall
		oncall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
			if getnamecallmethod() ~= "FireServer" then return oncall(self, ...) end
			local args = { ... }

			if uirem and self == uirem and fctrl then
				pcall(function()
					local fighter = fctrl:GetFighter(lp)
					if fighter and fighter.Items then
						local oid = args[1]
						for _, item in fighter.Items do
							if item:Get("ObjectID") == oid then
								lwep = item.Name
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			if self == eqrem then
				local wname, ctype, cname, opts = args[1], args[2], args[3], args[4] or {}
				if not cname or cname == "None" or cname == "" then
					equip[wname] = equip[wname] or {}
					equip[wname][ctype] = nil
					if not next(equip[wname]) then equip[wname] = nil end
					rebuildinv()
					task.defer(function()
						saferep("WeaponInventory")
						task.wait(0.2)
						savecfg()
					end)
					return oncall(self, ...)
				end
				if banned(cname) then return oncall(self, ...) end
				local rdata = oget(dctrl, "CosmeticInventory")
				if rdata and type(rdata[cname]) ~= "boolean" and rdata[cname] ~= nil then
					return oncall(self, ...)
				end
				equip[wname] = equip[wname] or {}
				local cloned = clonecos(cname, ctype, opts.IsInverted, opts.OnlyUseFavorites)
				if cloned then equip[wname][ctype] = cloned end
				if ctype == "Finisher" then fcache[wname] = cname end
				rebuildinv()
				task.defer(function()
					saferep("WeaponInventory")
					task.wait(0.2)
					savecfg()
				end)
				return
			end

			if self == favrem then
				local fwep, fname, fstate = args[1], args[2], args[3]
				if not fname or fname == "None" or fname == "" then return oncall(self, ...) end
				if banned(fname) then return oncall(self, ...) end
				favs[fwep] = favs[fwep] or {}
				favs[fwep][fname] = fstate or nil
				cachedFavorited = nil
				savecfg()
				task.spawn(saferep, "FavoritedCosmetics")
				return
			end

			return oncall(self, ...)
		end)
	end
end

local citem
pcall(function() citem = require(lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem) end)

if citem and citem._CreateViewModel then
	local ocvm = citem._CreateViewModel
	citem._CreateViewModel = function(self, vmref)
		local wname = self.Name
		local wplr = self.ClientFighter and self.ClientFighter.Player
		cwep = (wplr == lp) and wname or nil
		if wplr == lp and equip[wname] and equip[wname].Skin and vmref then
			local skin = equip[wname].Skin
			local dk = self:ToEnum("Data")
			if vmref[dk] then
				vmref[dk][self:ToEnum("Skin")] = skin
				vmref[dk][self:ToEnum("Name")] = skin.Name
			elseif vmref.Data then
				vmref.Data.Skin = skin
				vmref.Data.Name = skin.Name
			end
		end
		local res = ocvm(self, vmref)
		cwep = nil
		return res
	end
end

local vmmod = lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")
if vmmod then
	local cvm = require(vmmod)
	if cvm.GetWrap then
		local ogw = cvm.GetWrap
		cvm.GetWrap = function(self)
			local ci = self.ClientItem
			local wname = ci and ci.Name
			local wplr = ci and ci.ClientFighter and ci.ClientFighter.Player
			local weq = wname and wplr == lp and equip[wname]
			return (weq and weq.Wrap) or ogw(self)
		end
	end
	local onew = cvm.new
	cvm.new = function(rdata, cliitm)
		local wplr = cliitm.ClientFighter and cliitm.ClientFighter.Player
		local wname = cwep or cliitm.Name
		if wplr == lp and equip[wname] then
			local rcls = require(reps.Modules.ReplicatedClass)
			local dk = rcls:ToEnum("Data")
			rdata[dk] = rdata[dk] or {}
			local cos = equip[wname]
			local slot = rdata[dk]
			if cos.Skin then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Skin")] = cos.Skin end
			if cos.Wrap then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Wrap")] = cos.Wrap end
			if cos.Charm then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Charm")] = cos.Charm end
		end
		local res = onew(rdata, cliitm)
		if wplr == lp and equip[wname] and equip[wname].Wrap and res._UpdateWrap then
			res:_UpdateWrap()
			task.delay(0.1, function() if not res._destroyed then res:_UpdateWrap() end end)
		end
		return res
	end
end

local ogvi = ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData
ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = function(self, wdata, hires)
	if not wdata then return ogvi(self, wdata, hires) end
	local wname = wdata.Name
	local weq = equip[wname]
	if weq and weq.Skin and (wdata.Skin == weq.Skin or vprof == lp) then
		local sinfo = self.ViewModels[weq.Skin.Name]
		if sinfo then return sinfo[hires and "ImageHighResolution" or "Image"] or sinfo.Image end
	end
	return ogvi(self, wdata, hires)
end

pcall(function()
	local vpmod = require(lps.Modules.Pages.ViewProfile)
	if vpmod and vpmod.Fetch then
		local ofetch = vpmod.Fetch
		vpmod.Fetch = function(self, tplr)
			vprof = tplr
			return ofetch(self, tplr)
		end
	end
end)

local cent
pcall(function() cent = require(lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientEntity) end)

if cent and cent._PlayFinisher then
	local ofin = cent._PlayFinisher
	cent._PlayFinisher = function(self, fname, ...)
		local ewep = getewep()
		local tfin = ewep and (fcache[ewep] or (equip[ewep] and equip[ewep].Finisher and equip[ewep].Finisher.Name))
		return ofin(self, tfin or fname, ...)
	end
end

loadcfg()
rebuildEnabled = true
rebuildinv()

for wname, wdata in equip do
	if wdata.Finisher and wdata.Finisher.Name then
		fcache[wname] = wdata.Finisher.Name
	end
end
end

local UnlockAllState = {
	enabled = false,
	hookReady = false,
	namecallHookReady = false,
	viewHooksReady = false,
	equip = {},
	favs = {},
	finv = {},
	cachedCosmeticProxy = nil,
	cachedWeaponData = {},
	cachedFavorited = nil,
	originalGet = nil,
	originalGetWeaponData = nil,
	originalGetUnlockedWeapons = nil,
	originalCreateViewModel = nil,
	originalGetWrap = nil,
	originalCVMNew = nil,
	originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = nil,
	originalViewProfileFetch = nil,
	cwep = nil,
	vprof = nil,
	configLoaded = false,
	cosmeticInventoryBuilt = false,
	serverSyncApplied = false
}

local UnlockAllWeaponsState = {
	enabled = false,
	cachedInventoryProxy = nil,
	cachedUnlockedWeapons = nil,
	weaponNames = nil,
	weaponLookup = nil
}

local UnlockUnrestrictedState = {
	enabled = false,
	originalGetReleasedOwnableWeapons = nil,
	originalOwnsCosmetic = nil,
	originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate = nil,
	originalCanUseWeapon = nil,
	canUseWeaponFighter = nil,
	fighterRetrySerial = 0,
	itemEntriesReady = false,
	activeWeaponVisuals = {},
	injectedItems = {},
	injectedViewModels = {},
	injectedAlphabetized = {},
	cachedSkinItems = nil,
	cachedReleasedWeapons = nil,
	cachedReleasedWeaponsSource = nil
}

local AddSkinCaseState = {
	enabled = false,
	rewards = {},
	cachedUnclaimedRewards = nil
}

local unlockAllDataController, unlockAllCurrentData, unlockAllCosmetics, unlockAllCosmeticLibrary = nil, nil, nil, nil
local unlockAllEnumLibrary, unlockAllFighterController, unlockAllShopLibrary, unlockAllItemLibrary = nil, nil, nil, nil
local unlockAllFinisherCache = {}
local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
local unlockAllSaveFile = "PhantomRivals/unlockall.json"
local saferep
local UnlockRuntimeState = {
	modules = nil,
	controllers = nil
}

local function requireUnlockModule(parent, name)
	if not parent then
		error("Unlock runtime folder is missing", 0)
	end

	local module = parent:FindFirstChild(name) or parent:WaitForChild(name, 10)
	if not module then
		error("Unlock module not found: " .. tostring(name), 0)
	end

	local ok, result = pcall(require, module)
	if not ok then
		error(result, 0)
	end
	return result
end

local function ensureUnlockDependencies(options)
	options = options or {}

	if not UnlockRuntimeState.modules then
		local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		UnlockRuntimeState.modules = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules")
			or replicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules", 10)
	end

	if not UnlockRuntimeState.controllers then
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		local playerScripts = localPlayer and (localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts") or localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerScripts", 10))
		UnlockRuntimeState.controllers = playerScripts
			and (playerScripts:FindFirstChild("Controllers") or playerScripts:WaitForChild("Controllers", 10))
	end

	local modules = UnlockRuntimeState.modules
	local controllers = UnlockRuntimeState.controllers
	if not modules or not controllers then
		error("Unlock runtime is not ready", 0)
	end

	if options.enum and not unlockAllEnumLibrary then
		unlockAllEnumLibrary = requireUnlockModule(modules, "EnumLibrary")
		if unlockAllEnumLibrary and unlockAllEnumLibrary.WaitForEnumBuilder then
			unlockAllEnumLibrary:WaitForEnumBuilder()
		end
	end

	if options.cosmetics and not unlockAllCosmeticLibrary then
		unlockAllCosmeticLibrary = requireUnlockModule(modules, "CosmeticLibrary")
		unlockAllCosmetics = unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.Cosmetics
		UnlockAllState.cosmeticInventoryBuilt = false
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedSkinItems = nil
	end

	if options.shop and not unlockAllShopLibrary then
		unlockAllShopLibrary = requireUnlockModule(modules, "ShopLibrary")
	end

	if options.items and not unlockAllItemLibrary then
		unlockAllItemLibrary = requireUnlockModule(modules, "ItemLibrary")
	end

	if options.data and not unlockAllDataController then
		unlockAllDataController = requireUnlockModule(controllers, "PlayerDataController")
	end

	if options.data then
		unlockAllCurrentData = unlockAllCurrentData
			or (unlockAllDataController and unlockAllDataController.CurrentData)
		local deadline = os.clock() + 10
		while not unlockAllCurrentData and os.clock() < deadline do
			task.wait(0.05)
			unlockAllCurrentData = unlockAllDataController and unlockAllDataController.CurrentData
		end
		if not unlockAllCurrentData then
			error("Player data is not ready", 0)
		end
	end

	if options.fighter and not unlockAllFighterController then
		pcall(function()
			unlockAllFighterController = requireUnlockModule(controllers, "FighterController")
		end)
	end

	return modules, controllers
end

local function bannedCosmetic(name)
	if type(name) ~= "string" then
		return true
	end
	return name:find("MISSING_") or name:find("Bubblegum") or name:find("Ragdoll") or name:find("Fall Apart") or name:find("Every Finisher Ever")
end

local function toenum(name)
	if not unlockAllEnumLibrary then
		return nil
	end
	local ok, id = pcall(unlockAllEnumLibrary.ToEnum, unlockAllEnumLibrary, name)
	return ok and id or nil
end

local function clonecos(name, ctype, inv, favonly)
	if bannedCosmetic(name) then
		return nil
	end
	if not unlockAllCosmetics then
		return nil
	end
	local base = unlockAllCosmetics[name]
	if not base then
		return nil
	end
	local d = table.clone(base)
	d.Name = name
	d.Type = d.Type or ctype
	d.Seed = d.Seed or math.random(1, 1000000)
	local eid = toenum(name)
	if eid then
		d.Enum = eid
		d.ObjectID = d.ObjectID or eid
	end
	if inv ~= nil then
		d.Inverted = inv
	end
	if favonly ~= nil then
		d.OnlyUseFavorites = favonly
	end
	return d
end

local function resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedInventoryProxy = nil
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedUnlockedWeapons = nil
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponNames = nil
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponLookup = nil
	table.clear(UnlockAllState.cachedWeaponData)
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedReleasedWeapons = nil
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedReleasedWeaponsSource = nil
end

local function isWeaponUnlockActive()
	return UnlockAllWeaponsState.enabled or UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled
end

local function isCosmeticUnlockActive()
	return UnlockAllState.enabled or UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled
end

local UnrestrictedWeaponBaseByVisual = {
	Scepter = "Energy Pistols",
	["Glass Cannon"] = "Revolver",
	["Glast Shard"] = "Knife",
	["RNG Dice"] = "Subspace Tripmine",
	Elixir = "Molotov"
}

local UnrestrictedWeaponAliases = {
	Scepter = "Scepter",
	["Glass Cannon"] = "Glass Cannon",
	["Glass Canon"] = "Glass Cannon",
	["Glast Shard"] = "Glast Shard",
	["RNG Dice"] = "RNG Dice",
	Elixir = "Elixir",
	Elxir = "Elixir"
}

local function getUnrestrictedWeaponVisualName(name)
	if type(name) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	return UnrestrictedWeaponAliases[name] or name
end

local function getUnrestrictedWeaponBaseName(name)
	local visualName = getUnrestrictedWeaponVisualName(name)
	if not visualName then
		return nil
	end
	return UnrestrictedWeaponBaseByVisual[visualName], visualName
end

local function getActiveUnrestrictedWeaponVisual(baseName)
	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled or type(baseName) ~= "string" then
		return nil
	end
	return UnlockUnrestrictedState.activeWeaponVisuals[baseName]
end

local function getUnrestrictedViewModelNameForWeapon(name)
	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		return nil
	end
	local baseName, visualName = getUnrestrictedWeaponBaseName(name)
	if baseName then
		return visualName
	end
	return getActiveUnrestrictedWeaponVisual(name)
end

local function getUnlockAllEquippedCosmetics(name, fallbackName)
	if not isCosmeticUnlockActive() then
		return nil
	end
	if name and UnlockAllState.equip[name] then
		return UnlockAllState.equip[name]
	end
	if fallbackName and UnlockAllState.equip[fallbackName] then
		return UnlockAllState.equip[fallbackName]
	end
	return nil
end

local function hasNameInList(list, name)
	if type(list) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end
	for _, value in pairs(list) do
		if value == name then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function ensureUnlockUnrestrictedItemLibraryEntries()
	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled or not unlockAllItemLibrary then
		return
	end
	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.itemEntriesReady then
		return
	end

	local items = unlockAllItemLibrary.Items
	local viewModels = unlockAllItemLibrary.ViewModels
	local alphabetized = unlockAllItemLibrary.ItemsAlphabetized
	local alphabetizedChanged = false

	for visualName, baseName in pairs(UnrestrictedWeaponBaseByVisual) do
		if type(items) == "table" and not items[visualName] and type(items[baseName]) == "table" then
			local cloned = table.clone(items[baseName])
			cloned.Name = visualName
			items[visualName] = cloned
			UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedItems[visualName] = true
		end

		if type(viewModels) == "table" and not viewModels[visualName] and type(viewModels[baseName]) == "table" then
			local cloned = table.clone(viewModels[baseName])
			cloned.Name = visualName
			viewModels[visualName] = cloned
			UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedViewModels[visualName] = true
		end

		if type(alphabetized) == "table" and type(items) == "table" and items[visualName] and not hasNameInList(alphabetized, visualName) then
			table.insert(alphabetized, visualName)
			UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedAlphabetized[visualName] = true
			alphabetizedChanged = true
		end
	end

	if alphabetizedChanged and type(alphabetized) == "table" then
		table.sort(alphabetized)
	end
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.itemEntriesReady = true
end

local function restoreUnlockUnrestrictedItemLibraryEntries()
	if unlockAllItemLibrary then
		local alphabetized = unlockAllItemLibrary.ItemsAlphabetized
		if type(alphabetized) == "table" then
			for i = #alphabetized, 1, -1 do
				if UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedAlphabetized[alphabetized[i]] then
					table.remove(alphabetized, i)
				end
			end
		end

		local items = unlockAllItemLibrary.Items
		if type(items) == "table" then
			for name in pairs(UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedItems) do
				items[name] = nil
			end
		end

		local viewModels = unlockAllItemLibrary.ViewModels
		if type(viewModels) == "table" then
			for name in pairs(UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedViewModels) do
				viewModels[name] = nil
			end
		end
	end

	table.clear(UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedItems)
	table.clear(UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedViewModels)
	table.clear(UnlockUnrestrictedState.injectedAlphabetized)
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.itemEntriesReady = false
end

local function normalizeUnrestrictedPickedWeapons(weapons)
	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled or type(weapons) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	local normalized = table.clone(weapons)
	local nextVisuals = {}
	local changed = false

	for slot, weaponName in pairs(weapons) do
		local baseName, visualName = getUnrestrictedWeaponBaseName(weaponName)
		if baseName then
			normalized[slot] = baseName
			nextVisuals[baseName] = visualName
			changed = true
		end
	end

	if changed then
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.activeWeaponVisuals = nextVisuals
		resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
		task.defer(saferep, "WeaponInventory")
		return normalized
	end

	if next(UnlockUnrestrictedState.activeWeaponVisuals) then
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.activeWeaponVisuals = {}
		resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
		task.defer(saferep, "WeaponInventory")
	end
	return nil
end

local function resetAddSkinCaseCache()
	AddSkinCaseState.cachedUnclaimedRewards = nil
end

local function hasAddedSkinCaseRewards()
	return AddSkinCaseState.enabled and #AddSkinCaseState.rewards > 0
end

local function cloneRewardData(rewardData)
	local cloned = table.clone(rewardData)
	cloned.Quantity = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(cloned.Quantity) or 1))
	return cloned
end

local function rewardsMatch(a, b)
	return a and b and a.Name == b.Name and a.Weapon == b.Weapon
end

local function getAddedUnclaimedRewards(data)
	if AddSkinCaseState.cachedUnclaimedRewards then
		return AddSkinCaseState.cachedUnclaimedRewards
	end

	local proxy = {}
	if type(data) == "table" then
		for _, rewardData in pairs(data) do
			if type(rewardData) == "table" then
				table.insert(proxy, rewardData)
			end
		end
	end

	for _, rewardData in ipairs(AddSkinCaseState.rewards) do
		table.insert(proxy, cloneRewardData(rewardData))
	end

	AddSkinCaseState.cachedUnclaimedRewards = proxy
	return proxy
end

local function getRealUnclaimedRewardsCount()
	if not unlockAllDataController or not UnlockAllState.originalGet then
		return 0
	end
	local data = UnlockAllState.originalGet(unlockAllDataController, "UnclaimedRewards")
	return type(data) == "table" and #data or 0
end

local function consumeAddedSkinCaseRewardByIndex(index, amount)
	if not hasAddedSkinCaseRewards() or type(index) ~= "number" then
		return false
	end

	local fakeIndex = index - getRealUnclaimedRewardsCount()
	local rewardData = AddSkinCaseState.rewards[fakeIndex]
	if not rewardData then
		return false
	end

	amount = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(amount) or 1))
	local quantity = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(rewardData.Quantity) or 1))
	if quantity > amount then
		rewardData.Quantity = quantity - amount
	else
		table.remove(AddSkinCaseState.rewards, fakeIndex)
	end

	if #AddSkinCaseState.rewards == 0 then
		AddSkinCaseState.enabled = false
	end
	resetAddSkinCaseCache()
	task.defer(saferep, "UnclaimedRewards")
	return true
end

local function getUnlockAllWeaponNames()
	if UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponNames then
		return UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponNames
	end
	ensureUnlockUnrestrictedItemLibraryEntries()

	local names = {}
	local seen = {}
	local function add(name)
		if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" or seen[name] then
			return
		end
		if unlockAllItemLibrary and unlockAllItemLibrary.Items and not unlockAllItemLibrary.Items[name] then
			return
		end
		seen[name] = true
		table.insert(names, name)
	end

	if unlockAllShopLibrary then
		local sources = {
			unlockAllShopLibrary.OwnableWeaponsAlphabetized,
			unlockAllShopLibrary.OwnableWeapons
		}
		for _, source in pairs(sources) do
			if type(source) == "table" then
				for _, name in pairs(source) do
					add(name)
				end
			end
		end
		if type(unlockAllShopLibrary.Weapons) == "table" then
			for name in pairs(unlockAllShopLibrary.Weapons) do
				add(name)
			end
		end
	end

	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		for visualName in pairs(UnrestrictedWeaponBaseByVisual) do
			add(visualName)
		end
	end

	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled and unlockAllItemLibrary then
		if type(unlockAllItemLibrary.Items) == "table" then
			for name, info in pairs(unlockAllItemLibrary.Items) do
				if type(info) == "table" and info.Class and (not unlockAllItemLibrary.Classes or unlockAllItemLibrary.Classes[info.Class]) then
					add(name)
				end
			end
		end
		if type(unlockAllItemLibrary.ViewModels) == "table" then
			for name in pairs(unlockAllItemLibrary.ViewModels) do
				if not unlockAllItemLibrary.Items or unlockAllItemLibrary.Items[name] then
					add(name)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled and unlockAllCosmeticLibrary and type(unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.Cosmetics) == "table" then
		for _, info in pairs(unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.Cosmetics) do
			if type(info) == "table" and info.ItemName then
				add(info.ItemName)
			end
		end
	end

	table.sort(names)
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponNames = names
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponLookup = seen
	return names
end

local function isUnlockAllWeapon(name)
	getUnlockAllWeaponNames()
	return UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponLookup and UnlockAllWeaponsState.weaponLookup[name] == true
end

local function normalizeUnlockAllWeaponData(data, name)
	local cloned = type(data) == "table" and table.clone(data) or {}
	cloned.Name = cloned.Name or name
	cloned.Level = cloned.Level or 1
	cloned.XP = cloned.XP or 0
	if cloned.IsFavorited == nil then
		cloned.IsFavorited = false
	end
	return cloned
end

local function createUnlockAllWeaponData(name)
	if not isWeaponUnlockActive() or not isUnlockAllWeapon(name) then
		return nil
	end

	local baseName, visualName
	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		baseName, visualName = getUnrestrictedWeaponBaseName(name)
	end
	local data
	if baseName and UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData and unlockAllDataController then
		pcall(function()
			data = UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData(unlockAllDataController, baseName)
		end)
	end

	local normalized = normalizeUnlockAllWeaponData(data, visualName or name)
	if baseName then
		normalized.Name = visualName
		normalized._AspectBaseWeapon = baseName
		normalized._AspectVisualWeapon = visualName
	end
	return normalized
end

local function getUnlockAllWeaponInventory(data)
	if UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedInventoryProxy then
		return UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedInventoryProxy
	end

	local proxy = {}
	local owned = {}
	if type(data) == "table" then
		for _, weaponData in pairs(data) do
			if type(weaponData) == "table" and weaponData.Name then
				local cloned = normalizeUnlockAllWeaponData(weaponData, weaponData.Name)
				table.insert(proxy, cloned)
				owned[cloned.Name] = true
			end
		end
	end

	for _, name in pairs(getUnlockAllWeaponNames()) do
		if not owned[name] then
			table.insert(proxy, createUnlockAllWeaponData(name))
			owned[name] = true
		end
	end

	UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedInventoryProxy = proxy
	return proxy
end

local function getUnlockAllUnlockedWeapons(data)
	if UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedUnlockedWeapons then
		return UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedUnlockedWeapons
	end

	local proxy = type(data) == "table" and table.clone(data) or {}
	for _, name in pairs(getUnlockAllWeaponNames()) do
		proxy[name] = true
	end

	UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedUnlockedWeapons = proxy
	return proxy
end

local function savecfg()
	if not writefile then
		return
	end
	pcall(function()
		local cfg = { equipped = {}, favorites = UnlockAllState.favs }
		for wep, cos in pairs(UnlockAllState.equip) do
			local slot = {}
			cfg.equipped[wep] = slot
			for ct, cd in pairs(cos) do
				if cd and cd.Name and not bannedCosmetic(cd.Name) then
					slot[ct] = { name = cd.Name, seed = cd.Seed, inverted = cd.Inverted }
				end
			end
		end
		makefolder("PhantomRivals")
		writefile(unlockAllSaveFile, http:JSONEncode(cfg))
	end)
end

local function loadcfg()
	if UnlockAllState.configLoaded then
		return
	end
	UnlockAllState.configLoaded = true

	if not readfile or not isfile or not isfile(unlockAllSaveFile) then
		return
	end
	pcall(function()
		local cfg = http:JSONDecode(readfile(unlockAllSaveFile))
		if cfg.equipped then
			for wep, cos in pairs(cfg.equipped) do
				UnlockAllState.equip[wep] = {}
				for ct, cd in pairs(cos) do
					if cd and not bannedCosmetic(cd.name) then
						local cl = clonecos(cd.name, ct, cd.inverted)
						if cl then
							cl.Seed = cd.seed
							UnlockAllState.equip[wep][ct] = cl
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		UnlockAllState.favs = cfg.favorites or {}
	end)
end

local function rebuildinv()
	if not unlockAllCosmetics then
		return
	end

	if not UnlockAllState.cosmeticInventoryBuilt then
		table.clear(UnlockAllState.finv)
		for name in pairs(unlockAllCosmetics) do
			if not bannedCosmetic(name) then
				UnlockAllState.finv[name] = true
			end
		end
		UnlockAllState.cosmeticInventoryBuilt = true
	end

	for _, cos in pairs(UnlockAllState.equip) do
		for _, cd in pairs(cos) do
			if cd and cd.Name and not bannedCosmetic(cd.Name) then
				UnlockAllState.finv[cd.Name] = true
			end
		end
	end
	UnlockAllState.cachedCosmeticProxy = nil
	table.clear(UnlockAllState.cachedWeaponData)
	UnlockAllState.cachedFavorited = nil
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedInventoryProxy = nil
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.cachedUnlockedWeapons = nil
	resetAddSkinCaseCache()
end

saferep = function(key)
	pcall(function()
		if not unlockAllCurrentData and unlockAllDataController and unlockAllDataController.CurrentData then
			unlockAllCurrentData = unlockAllDataController.CurrentData
		end
		if unlockAllCurrentData then
			unlockAllCurrentData:Replicate(key)
		end
	end)
end

local function refreshEquippedCosmeticVisuals(weaponName, cosmeticType)
	if type(weaponName) ~= "string" or weaponName == "" then
		return
	end
	local unrestrictedBaseName
	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		unrestrictedBaseName = getUnrestrictedWeaponBaseName(weaponName)
	end

	task.defer(function()
		saferep("WeaponInventory")
		task.wait()
		saferep("WeaponInventory")

		if not unlockAllFighterController then
			return
		end

		local fighter
		pcall(function()
			fighter = unlockAllFighterController:GetFighter(game.Players.LocalPlayer)
		end)
		if not fighter then
			return
		end

		local refreshed = {}
		local function refreshItem(item)
			if type(item) ~= "table" or refreshed[item] or (item.Name ~= weaponName and item.Name ~= unrestrictedBaseName) then
				return
			end
			refreshed[item] = true

			local vm = item.ViewModel
			local wasEquipped = false
			if vm and vm.IsEquipped then
				pcall(function()
					wasEquipped = vm:IsEquipped()
				end)
			end

			if vm and vm._UpdateWrap then
				pcall(vm._UpdateWrap, vm)
				task.delay(0.1, function()
					if not vm._destroyed then
						pcall(vm._UpdateWrap, vm)
					end
				end)
			end

			if cosmeticType == "Skin" and vm and item._CreateViewModel then
				local serial = type(vm) == "table" and rawget(vm, "_serial")
				if serial ~= nil then
					local ok, newViewModel = pcall(item._CreateViewModel, item, serial)
					if ok and newViewModel then
						if vm.Destroy then
							pcall(vm.Destroy, vm)
						end
						item.ViewModel = newViewModel
						if newViewModel._UpdateWrap then
							pcall(newViewModel._UpdateWrap, newViewModel)
						end
						if wasEquipped and newViewModel.Equip then
							pcall(newViewModel.Equip, newViewModel, true)
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if fighter.EquippedItem then
			refreshItem(fighter.EquippedItem)
		end
		for _, item in pairs(fighter.Items or {}) do
			refreshItem(item)
		end
	end)
end

local function refreshAllEquippedCosmeticVisuals()
	for weaponName, cosmetics in pairs(UnlockAllState.equip) do
		local cosmeticType = cosmetics.Skin and "Skin" or next(cosmetics)
		if cosmeticType then
			refreshEquippedCosmeticVisuals(weaponName, cosmeticType)
		end
	end
end

local function applyUnlockAllHooks()
	if UnlockAllState.hookReady then
		return
	end
	if not unlockAllDataController then
		return
	end

	if not UnlockAllState.originalGet then
		UnlockAllState.originalGet = unlockAllDataController.Get
	end
	if not UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData then
		UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData = unlockAllDataController.GetWeaponData
	end
	if not UnlockAllState.originalGetUnlockedWeapons then
		UnlockAllState.originalGetUnlockedWeapons = unlockAllDataController.GetUnlockedWeapons
	end

	if unlockAllDataController.Get == UnlockAllState.originalGet then
		unlockAllDataController.Get = function(self, key)
			local data = UnlockAllState.originalGet(self, key)
			if not isCosmeticUnlockActive() and not isWeaponUnlockActive() and not hasAddedSkinCaseRewards() then
				return data
			end

			if key == "UnclaimedRewards" and hasAddedSkinCaseRewards() then
				return getAddedUnclaimedRewards(data)
			end

			if key == "WeaponInventory" and isWeaponUnlockActive() then
				return getUnlockAllWeaponInventory(data)
			end

			if key == "CosmeticInventory" then
				if not isCosmeticUnlockActive() then
					return data
				end
				if UnlockAllState.cachedCosmeticProxy then
					return UnlockAllState.cachedCosmeticProxy
				end
				local proxy = {}
				if data then
					for k, v in pairs(data) do
						if not bannedCosmetic(k) then
							proxy[k] = v
						end
					end
				end
				for name in pairs(UnlockAllState.finv) do
					proxy[name] = true
				end
				UnlockAllState.cachedCosmeticProxy = proxy
				return proxy
			end

			if key == "FavoritedCosmetics" then
				if not isCosmeticUnlockActive() then
					return data
				end
				if UnlockAllState.cachedFavorited then
					return UnlockAllState.cachedFavorited
				end
				local res = data and table.clone(data) or {}
				for wep, fv in pairs(UnlockAllState.favs) do
					local slot = res[wep] or {}
					res[wep] = slot
					for name, isfav in pairs(fv) do
						if not bannedCosmetic(name) then
							slot[name] = isfav
						end
					end
				end
				UnlockAllState.cachedFavorited = res
				return res
			end

			return data
		end
	end

	if unlockAllDataController.GetWeaponData == UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData then
		unlockAllDataController.GetWeaponData = function(self, wname)
			local baseName, visualName
			if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
				baseName, visualName = getUnrestrictedWeaponBaseName(wname)
				if not baseName then
					visualName = getActiveUnrestrictedWeaponVisual(wname)
				end
			end
			local lookupName = baseName or wname
			local cacheKey = visualName and (tostring(wname) .. "\0" .. visualName) or wname

			if UnlockAllState.cachedWeaponData[cacheKey] then
				return UnlockAllState.cachedWeaponData[cacheKey]
			end
			local data = UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData(self, lookupName)
			if not data and isWeaponUnlockActive() then
				data = createUnlockAllWeaponData(wname)
			end
			if not data then
				return nil
			end
			local merged = table.clone(data)
			merged.Name = visualName or wname
			if visualName then
				merged._AspectBaseWeapon = lookupName
				merged._AspectVisualWeapon = visualName
			end
			local weq = getUnlockAllEquippedCosmetics(visualName or wname, lookupName)
			if weq then
				for ct, cd in pairs(weq) do
					merged[ct] = cd
				end
			end
			UnlockAllState.cachedWeaponData[cacheKey] = merged
			return merged
		end
	end

	if unlockAllDataController.GetUnlockedWeapons == UnlockAllState.originalGetUnlockedWeapons then
		unlockAllDataController.GetUnlockedWeapons = function(self, ...)
			local data = UnlockAllState.originalGetUnlockedWeapons(self, ...)
			if not isWeaponUnlockActive() then
				return data
			end
			return getUnlockAllUnlockedWeapons(data)
		end
	end

	local rems = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Remotes")
	local drems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Data")
	local eqrem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("EquipCosmetic")
	local favrem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("FavoriteCosmetic")
	local useRewardRem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("UseUnclaimedReward")
	local rrems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Replication")
	local frems = rrems and rrems:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
	local uirem = frems and frems:FindFirstChild("UseItem")
	local pickWeaponsRem = frems and frems:FindFirstChild("PickWeapons")
	local duelRems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Duels")
	local pickWeaponsAheadRem = duelRems and duelRems:FindFirstChild("PickWeaponsAheadOfTime")

	if not UnlockAllState.namecallHookReady
		and hookmetamethod
		and (eqrem or favrem or useRewardRem or uirem or pickWeaponsRem or pickWeaponsAheadRem)
	then
		local oncall
		oncall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
			local method = getnamecallmethod()
			local args = { ... }

			if method == "InvokeServer" then
				if useRewardRem and self == useRewardRem and consumeAddedSkinCaseRewardByIndex(args[1], args[2]) then
					return "Success"
				end
				return oncall(self, ...)
			end
			if method ~= "FireServer" then
				return oncall(self, ...)
			end

			if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled and (self == pickWeaponsRem or self == pickWeaponsAheadRem) then
				local normalized = normalizeUnrestrictedPickedWeapons(args[1])
				if normalized then
					args[1] = normalized
					return oncall(self, table.unpack(args))
				end
			end

			if not isCosmeticUnlockActive() then
				return oncall(self, ...)
			end

			if uirem and self == uirem and unlockAllFighterController then
				pcall(function()
					local fighter = unlockAllFighterController:GetFighter(game.Players.LocalPlayer)
					if fighter and fighter.Items then
						local oid = args[1]
						for _, item in pairs(fighter.Items) do
							if item:Get("ObjectID") == oid then
								lwep = item.Name
								break
							end
						end
					end
				end)
			end

			if self == eqrem then
				local wname, ctype, cname, opts = args[1], args[2], args[3], args[4] or {}
				if not cname or cname == "None" or cname == "" then
					UnlockAllState.equip[wname] = UnlockAllState.equip[wname] or {}
					UnlockAllState.equip[wname][ctype] = nil
					if not next(UnlockAllState.equip[wname]) then
						UnlockAllState.equip[wname] = nil
					end
					rebuildinv()
					refreshEquippedCosmeticVisuals(wname, ctype)
					task.defer(function()
						saferep("WeaponInventory")
						task.wait(0.2)
						savecfg()
					end)
					return oncall(self, ...)
				end
				if bannedCosmetic(cname) then
					return oncall(self, ...)
				end
				local rdata = UnlockAllState.originalGet(unlockAllDataController, "CosmeticInventory")
				if rdata and type(rdata[cname]) ~= "boolean" and rdata[cname] ~= nil then
					return oncall(self, ...)
				end
				UnlockAllState.equip[wname] = UnlockAllState.equip[wname] or {}
				local cloned = clonecos(cname, ctype, opts.IsInverted, opts.OnlyUseFavorites)
				if cloned then
					UnlockAllState.equip[wname][ctype] = cloned
				end
				if ctype == "Finisher" then
					unlockAllFinisherCache[wname] = cname
				end
				rebuildinv()
				refreshEquippedCosmeticVisuals(wname, ctype)
				task.defer(function()
					saferep("WeaponInventory")
					task.wait(0.2)
					savecfg()
				end)
				return
			end

			if self == favrem then
				local fwep, fname, fstate = args[1], args[2], args[3]
				if not fname or fname == "None" or fname == "" then
					return oncall(self, ...)
				end
				if bannedCosmetic(fname) then
					return oncall(self, ...)
				end
				UnlockAllState.favs[fwep] = UnlockAllState.favs[fwep] or {}
				UnlockAllState.favs[fwep][fname] = fstate or nil
				UnlockAllState.cachedFavorited = nil
				savecfg()
				task.spawn(saferep, "FavoritedCosmetics")
				return
			end

			return oncall(self, ...)
		end)
		UnlockAllState.namecallHookReady = true
	end

	UnlockAllState.hookReady = true
end

local function applyUnlockAllViewHooks()
	if UnlockAllState.viewHooksReady then
		return
	end

	local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
	local reps = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
	local lps = lp and lp.PlayerScripts
	local rmods = reps:FindFirstChild("Modules")
	if not lps or not rmods then
		return
	end

	local citem
	pcall(function()
		citem = require(lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem)
	end)

	local ilib
	pcall(function()
		ilib = require(rmods:WaitForChild("ItemLibrary", 10))
	end)
	if ilib and type(ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData) ~= "function" then
		ilib = nil
	end

	if citem and citem._CreateViewModel then
		if not UnlockAllState.originalCreateViewModel then
			UnlockAllState.originalCreateViewModel = citem._CreateViewModel
		end
		citem._CreateViewModel = function(self, vmref)
			local wname = self.Name
			local wplr = self.ClientFighter and self.ClientFighter.Player
			UnlockAllState.cwep = (wplr == lp) and wname or nil
			local visualName = (wplr == lp) and getUnrestrictedViewModelNameForWeapon(wname)
			local weq = getUnlockAllEquippedCosmetics(visualName or wname, wname)
			if isCosmeticUnlockActive() and wplr == lp and weq and vmref then
				local skin = weq.Skin
				if skin then
					local dk = self:ToEnum("Data")
					if vmref[dk] then
						vmref[dk][self:ToEnum("Skin")] = skin
						vmref[dk][self:ToEnum("Name")] = skin.Name
					elseif vmref.Data then
						vmref.Data.Skin = skin
						vmref.Data.Name = skin.Name
					end
				end
			end
			if visualName and vmref then
				local dk = self:ToEnum("Data")
				if vmref[dk] then
					vmref[dk][self:ToEnum("Name")] = visualName
				elseif vmref.Data then
					vmref.Data.Name = visualName
				end
			end
			local res = UnlockAllState.originalCreateViewModel(self, vmref)
			UnlockAllState.cwep = nil
			return res
		end
	end

	local vmmod
	pcall(function()
		local itemModule = lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem
		vmmod = itemModule and itemModule:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")
	end)

	if vmmod then
		local cvm = require(vmmod)
		if cvm.GetWrap then
			if not UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap then
				UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap = cvm.GetWrap
			end
			cvm.GetWrap = function(self)
				if not isCosmeticUnlockActive() then
					return UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap(self)
				end
				local ci = self.ClientItem
				local wname = ci and ci.Name
				local wplr = ci and ci.ClientFighter and ci.ClientFighter.Player
				local visualName = wname and wplr == lp and getUnrestrictedViewModelNameForWeapon(wname)
				local weq = wname and wplr == lp and getUnlockAllEquippedCosmetics(visualName or wname, wname)
				return (weq and weq.Wrap) or UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap(self)
			end
		end

		if not UnlockAllState.originalCVMNew then
			UnlockAllState.originalCVMNew = cvm.new
		end
		cvm.new = function(rdata, cliitm)
			local wplr = cliitm.ClientFighter and cliitm.ClientFighter.Player
			local wname = UnlockAllState.cwep or cliitm.Name
			local visualName = (wplr == lp) and getUnrestrictedViewModelNameForWeapon(wname)
			local weq = getUnlockAllEquippedCosmetics(visualName or wname, wname)
			if isCosmeticUnlockActive() and wplr == lp and weq then
				local rcls = require(reps.Modules.ReplicatedClass)
				local dk = rcls:ToEnum("Data")
				rdata[dk] = rdata[dk] or {}
				local slot = rdata[dk]
				if weq.Skin then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Skin")] = weq.Skin end
				if weq.Wrap then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Wrap")] = weq.Wrap end
				if weq.Charm then slot[rcls:ToEnum("Charm")] = weq.Charm end
			end
			if visualName then
				local rcls = require(reps.Modules.ReplicatedClass)
				local dk = rcls:ToEnum("Data")
				rdata[dk] = rdata[dk] or {}
				rdata[dk][rcls:ToEnum("Name")] = visualName
			end
			local res = UnlockAllState.originalCVMNew(rdata, cliitm)
			if isCosmeticUnlockActive() and wplr == lp and weq and res._UpdateWrap then
				res:_UpdateWrap()
				task.delay(0.1, function()
					if not res._destroyed then
						res:_UpdateWrap()
					end
				end)
			end
			return res
		end
	end

	if ilib and ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData then
		if not UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData then
			UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData
		end
		ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = function(self, wdata, hires)
			if not isCosmeticUnlockActive() then
				return UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData(self, wdata, hires)
			end
			if not wdata then
				return UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData(self, wdata, hires)
			end
			local wname = wdata.Name
			local visualName = wdata._AspectVisualWeapon or getUnrestrictedViewModelNameForWeapon(wname)
			if visualName and self.ViewModels then
				local vinfo = self.ViewModels[visualName]
				if vinfo then
					return vinfo[hires and "ImageHighResolution" or "Image"] or vinfo.Image
				end
			end
			local weq = getUnlockAllEquippedCosmetics(wname, wdata._AspectBaseWeapon)
			if weq and weq.Skin then
				local shouldOverride = UnlockAllState.vprof == lp
				if not shouldOverride and wdata.Skin and type(wdata.Skin) == "table" then
					shouldOverride = wdata.Skin.Name == weq.Skin.Name
				end
				if shouldOverride then
					local sinfo = self.ViewModels[weq.Skin.Name]
					if sinfo then
						return sinfo[hires and "ImageHighResolution" or "Image"] or sinfo.Image
					end
				end
			end
			return UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData(self, wdata, hires)
		end
	end

	pcall(function()
		local vpmod = require(lps.Modules.Pages.ViewProfile)
		if vpmod and vpmod.Fetch then
			if not UnlockAllState.originalViewProfileFetch then
				UnlockAllState.originalViewProfileFetch = vpmod.Fetch
			end
			vpmod.Fetch = function(self, tplr)
				UnlockAllState.vprof = tplr
				return UnlockAllState.originalViewProfileFetch(self, tplr)
			end
		end
	end)
	UnlockAllState.viewHooksReady = true
end

local function getUnrestrictedReleasedWeapons(baseList)
	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedReleasedWeapons
		and UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedReleasedWeaponsSource == baseList
	then
		return UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedReleasedWeapons
	end

	local result = {}
	local seen = {}
	if type(baseList) == "table" then
		for _, name in pairs(baseList) do
			if type(name) == "string" and not seen[name] then
				seen[name] = true
				table.insert(result, name)
			end
		end
	end

	local extras = {}
	for _, name in pairs(getUnlockAllWeaponNames()) do
		if type(name) == "string" and not seen[name] then
			table.insert(extras, name)
		end
	end
	table.sort(extras)
	for _, name in ipairs(extras) do
		seen[name] = true
		table.insert(result, name)
	end

	UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedReleasedWeapons = result
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedReleasedWeaponsSource = baseList
	return result
end

local function withUnrestrictedSkinItems(selectedWeapon, callback)
	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled or not selectedWeapon or not unlockAllCosmeticLibrary then
		return callback()
	end

	local skinItems = UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedSkinItems
	if not skinItems then
		skinItems = {}
		for _, info in pairs(unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.Cosmetics or {}) do
			if type(info) == "table" and info.Type == "Skin" then
				table.insert(skinItems, info)
			end
		end
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.cachedSkinItems = skinItems
	end

	local changed = {}
	for _, info in ipairs(skinItems) do
		if info.ItemName ~= selectedWeapon then
			changed[info] = info.ItemName
			info.ItemName = selectedWeapon
		end
	end

	local results = { pcall(callback) }
	for info, itemName in pairs(changed) do
		info.ItemName = itemName
	end

	if not results[1] then
		error(results[2])
	end
	return table.unpack(results, 2)
end

local function applyUnlockUnrestrictedFighterHooks()
	local fighter = unlockAllFighterController and unlockAllFighterController.LocalFighter
	if not fighter or type(fighter.CanUseWeapon) ~= "function" then
		return false
	end

	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter and UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter ~= fighter and UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon then
		local oldFighter = UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter
		if oldFighter.CanUseWeapon ~= UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon then
			oldFighter.CanUseWeapon = UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon
		end
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon = nil
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter = nil
	end

	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon then
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon = fighter.CanUseWeapon
		UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter = fighter
	end

	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter == fighter and fighter.CanUseWeapon == UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon then
		local originalCanUseWeapon = UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon
		fighter.CanUseWeapon = function(self, weaponName, ...)
			if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled and type(weaponName) == "string" then
				local baseName = getUnrestrictedWeaponBaseName(weaponName)
				if baseName or isUnlockAllWeapon(weaponName) then
					return true
				end
			end
			return originalCanUseWeapon(self, weaponName, ...)
		end
	end

	return true
end

local function applyUnlockUnrestrictedHooks()
	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		return
	end

	ensureUnlockUnrestrictedItemLibraryEntries()
	applyUnlockUnrestrictedFighterHooks()
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.fighterRetrySerial = UnlockUnrestrictedState.fighterRetrySerial + 1
	local retrySerial = UnlockUnrestrictedState.fighterRetrySerial
	task.spawn(function()
		for _ = 1, 20 do
			if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled
				or retrySerial ~= UnlockUnrestrictedState.fighterRetrySerial
			then
				return
			end
			if applyUnlockUnrestrictedFighterHooks() then
				return
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	if unlockAllShopLibrary and unlockAllShopLibrary.GetReleasedOwnableWeapons then
		if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalGetReleasedOwnableWeapons then
			UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalGetReleasedOwnableWeapons = unlockAllShopLibrary.GetReleasedOwnableWeapons
		end
		if unlockAllShopLibrary.GetReleasedOwnableWeapons == UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalGetReleasedOwnableWeapons then
			unlockAllShopLibrary.GetReleasedOwnableWeapons = function(self, ...)
				local data = UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalGetReleasedOwnableWeapons(self, ...)
				if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
					return data
				end
				return getUnrestrictedReleasedWeapons(data)
			end
		end
	end

	if unlockAllCosmeticLibrary and unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.OwnsCosmetic then
		if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalOwnsCosmetic then
			UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalOwnsCosmetic = unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.OwnsCosmetic
		end
		if unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.OwnsCosmetic == UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalOwnsCosmetic then
			unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.OwnsCosmetic = function(self, inv, name, weapon)
				if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled and name and self.Cosmetics and self.Cosmetics[name] then
					return true
				end
				return UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalOwnsCosmetic(self, inv, name, weapon)
			end
		end
	end

	pcall(function()
		local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
		local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
		local lps = lp and lp.PlayerScripts
		local modules = lps and lps:FindFirstChild("Modules")
		local ui = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("UserInterface")
		local equipment = ui and ui:FindFirstChild("Equipment")
		local interface = equipment and equipment:FindFirstChild("Interface")
		local customize = interface and interface:FindFirstChild("Customize")
		local cosmeticsModule = customize and customize:FindFirstChild("Cosmetics")
		if not cosmeticsModule then
			return
		end
		local cosmeticsClass = require(cosmeticsModule)
		if not cosmeticsClass or not cosmeticsClass._Generate then
			return
		end
		if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate then
			UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate = cosmeticsClass._Generate
		end
		if cosmeticsClass._Generate == UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate then
			cosmeticsClass._Generate = function(self, ...)
				if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
					return UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate(self, ...)
				end
				local args = { ... }
				local selectedWeapon
				pcall(function()
					selectedWeapon = self.Customize.Interface.Equipment:GetSelectedWeapon()
				end)
				return withUnrestrictedSkinItems(selectedWeapon, function()
					return UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate(self, table.unpack(args))
				end)
			end
		end
	end)
end

local function restoreUnlockUnrestrictedHooks()
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.fighterRetrySerial = UnlockUnrestrictedState.fighterRetrySerial + 1
	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon then
		local fighter = UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter or (unlockAllFighterController and unlockAllFighterController.LocalFighter)
		if fighter and fighter.CanUseWeapon ~= UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon then
			fighter.CanUseWeapon = UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon
		end
	end
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCanUseWeapon = nil
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.canUseWeaponFighter = nil

	if unlockAllShopLibrary and UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalGetReleasedOwnableWeapons then
		unlockAllShopLibrary.GetReleasedOwnableWeapons = UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalGetReleasedOwnableWeapons
	end
	if unlockAllCosmeticLibrary and UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalOwnsCosmetic then
		unlockAllCosmeticLibrary.OwnsCosmetic = UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalOwnsCosmetic
	end
	pcall(function()
		local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
		local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
		local lps = lp and lp.PlayerScripts
		local modules = lps and lps:FindFirstChild("Modules")
		local ui = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("UserInterface")
		local equipment = ui and ui:FindFirstChild("Equipment")
		local interface = equipment and equipment:FindFirstChild("Interface")
		local customize = interface and interface:FindFirstChild("Customize")
		local cosmeticsModule = customize and customize:FindFirstChild("Cosmetics")
		if cosmeticsModule and UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate then
			require(cosmeticsModule)._Generate = UnlockUnrestrictedState.originalCustomizeCosmeticsGenerate
		end
	end)
	restoreUnlockUnrestrictedItemLibraryEntries()
	table.clear(UnlockUnrestrictedState.activeWeaponVisuals)
	resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
end

local function restoreUnlockAllDataHooksIfUnused()
	if UnlockAllState.enabled or UnlockAllWeaponsState.enabled or UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled or hasAddedSkinCaseRewards() then
		return
	end

	if unlockAllDataController then
		if UnlockAllState.originalGet then
			unlockAllDataController.Get = UnlockAllState.originalGet
		end
		if UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData then
			unlockAllDataController.GetWeaponData = UnlockAllState.originalGetWeaponData
		end
		if UnlockAllState.originalGetUnlockedWeapons then
			unlockAllDataController.GetUnlockedWeapons = UnlockAllState.originalGetUnlockedWeapons
		end
		if unlockAllDataController._RequestPlayerData then
			pcall(function()
				unlockAllDataController:_RequestPlayerData()
			end)
		end
	end

	UnlockAllState.hookReady = false
end

local function enableUnlockAll()
	if UnlockAllState.enabled then
		return
	end

	local reps = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	ensureUnlockDependencies({
		enum = true,
		cosmetics = true,
		items = true,
		data = true,
		fighter = true
	})

	loadcfg()
	applyUnlockAllHooks()
	applyUnlockAllViewHooks()
	UnlockAllState.enabled = true
	rebuildinv()
	saferep("CosmeticInventory")
	saferep("WeaponInventory")
	saferep("FavoritedCosmetics")
	refreshAllEquippedCosmeticVisuals()

	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(1)
		if UnlockAllState.serverSyncApplied then
			return
		end
		UnlockAllState.serverSyncApplied = true
		local rems = reps:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
		local drems = rems and rems:FindFirstChild("Data")
		local eqrem = drems and drems:FindFirstChild("EquipCosmetic")
		if not eqrem then
			UnlockAllState.serverSyncApplied = false
			return
		end
		if readfile and isfile and isfile(unlockAllSaveFile) then
			pcall(function()
				local cfg = http:JSONDecode(readfile(unlockAllSaveFile))
				if cfg.equipped then
					for wep, cos in pairs(cfg.equipped) do
						for ct, cd in pairs(cos) do
							if cd and cd.name then
								eqrem:FireServer(wep, ct, cd.name, {})
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

local function enableUnlockAllWeapons()
	if UnlockAllWeaponsState.enabled then
		return
	end

	ensureUnlockDependencies({
		shop = true,
		items = true,
		data = true
	})

	resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
	UnlockAllWeaponsState.enabled = true
	applyUnlockAllHooks()
	saferep("WeaponInventory")
end

local function disableUnlockAllWeapons()
	if not UnlockAllWeaponsState.enabled then
		return
	end

	UnlockAllWeaponsState.enabled = false
	resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
	restoreUnlockAllDataHooksIfUnused()
	saferep("WeaponInventory")
end

local function enableUnlockUnrestricted()
	if UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		return
	end

	ensureUnlockDependencies({
		enum = true,
		cosmetics = true,
		shop = true,
		items = true,
		data = true,
		fighter = true
	})

	loadcfg()
	UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled = true
	resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
	applyUnlockAllHooks()
	applyUnlockAllViewHooks()
	applyUnlockUnrestrictedHooks()
	rebuildinv()
	saferep("CosmeticInventory")
	saferep("WeaponInventory")
	saferep("FavoritedCosmetics")
	refreshAllEquippedCosmeticVisuals()
end

local function disableUnlockUnrestricted()
	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		return
	end

	UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled = false
	table.clear(UnlockUnrestrictedState.activeWeaponVisuals)
	resetUnlockAllWeaponCaches()
	UnlockAllState.cachedCosmeticProxy = nil
	table.clear(UnlockAllState.cachedWeaponData)
	UnlockAllState.cachedFavorited = nil
	restoreUnlockUnrestrictedHooks()

	if not UnlockAllState.enabled then
		local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
		local reps = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
		local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
		local lps = lp and lp.PlayerScripts
		local rmods = reps:FindFirstChild("Modules")
		local citem, vmmod, ilib
		pcall(function()
			if lps then
				citem = require(lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem)
			end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			if rmods then
				ilib = require(rmods:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary"))
			end
		end)
		if lps and lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem then
			vmmod = lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")
		end
		if citem and UnlockAllState.originalCreateViewModel then
			citem._CreateViewModel = UnlockAllState.originalCreateViewModel
		end
		if vmmod then
			pcall(function()
				local cvm = require(vmmod)
				if cvm then
					if UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap then
						cvm.GetWrap = UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap
					end
					if UnlockAllState.originalCVMNew then
						cvm.new = UnlockAllState.originalCVMNew
					end
				end
			end)
		end
		if ilib and UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData then
			ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData
		end
		UnlockAllState.viewHooksReady = false
	end

	restoreUnlockAllDataHooksIfUnused()
	saferep("CosmeticInventory")
	saferep("WeaponInventory")
	saferep("FavoritedCosmetics")
end

local function ensureAddSkinCaseHooks()
	ensureUnlockDependencies({
		data = true
	})

	applyUnlockAllHooks()
end

local function addSkinCaseRewardToBackpack(rewardData)
	if type(rewardData) ~= "table" or type(rewardData.Name) ~= "string" or rewardData.Name == "" then
		return false, "Invalid reward"
	end

	ensureAddSkinCaseHooks()

	local normalized = cloneRewardData(rewardData)
	for _, existing in ipairs(AddSkinCaseState.rewards) do
		if rewardsMatch(existing, normalized) then
			existing.Quantity = math.max(1, math.floor(tonumber(existing.Quantity) or 1)) + normalized.Quantity
			AddSkinCaseState.enabled = true
			resetAddSkinCaseCache()
			saferep("UnclaimedRewards")
			return true, existing.Quantity
		end
	end

	table.insert(AddSkinCaseState.rewards, normalized)
	AddSkinCaseState.enabled = true
	resetAddSkinCaseCache()
	saferep("UnclaimedRewards")
	return true, normalized.Quantity
end

local function clearAddedSkinCaseRewards()
	table.clear(AddSkinCaseState.rewards)
	AddSkinCaseState.enabled = false
	resetAddSkinCaseCache()
	restoreUnlockAllDataHooksIfUnused()
	saferep("UnclaimedRewards")
end

local function disableUnlockAll()
	if not UnlockAllState.enabled then
		return
	end

	UnlockAllState.enabled = false
	UnlockAllState.cachedCosmeticProxy = nil
	table.clear(UnlockAllState.cachedWeaponData)
	UnlockAllState.cachedFavorited = nil

	local plrs = game:GetService("Players")
	local reps = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
	local lp = plrs.LocalPlayer
	local lps = lp and lp.PlayerScripts
	local rmods = reps:FindFirstChild("Modules")

	local citem, vmmod, ilib
	pcall(function()
		if lps then
			citem = require(lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem)
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		if rmods then
			ilib = require(rmods:FindFirstChild("ItemLibrary"))
		end
	end)
	if lps and lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem then
		vmmod = lps.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.ClientItem:FindFirstChild("ClientViewModel")
	end

	if not UnlockUnrestrictedState.enabled then
		if citem and UnlockAllState.originalCreateViewModel then
			citem._CreateViewModel = UnlockAllState.originalCreateViewModel
		end

		if vmmod then
			pcall(function()
				local cvm = require(vmmod)
				if cvm then
					if UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap then
						cvm.GetWrap = UnlockAllState.originalGetWrap
					end
					if UnlockAllState.originalCVMNew then
						cvm.new = UnlockAllState.originalCVMNew
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		if ilib and UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData then
			ilib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData = UnlockAllState.originalGetViewModelImageFromWeaponData
		end
		UnlockAllState.viewHooksReady = false
	end

	restoreUnlockAllDataHooksIfUnused()
	saferep("CosmeticInventory")
	saferep("WeaponInventory")
	saferep("FavoritedCosmetics")
end

unlockAll = function()
	enableUnlockAll()
end

unlockAllWeapons = function()
	enableUnlockAllWeapons()
end

unlockUnrestricted = function()
	enableUnlockUnrestricted()
end

function State.enableRapidFire()
    State.rapidFireEnabled = true
    applyWeaponModHooks()
    startWeaponModRetry()
end

function State.disableRapidFire()
    State.rapidFireEnabled = false
    refreshWeaponModValues()

    for weapon, backup in pairs(State.weaponBackups) do
        pcall(function()
            if weapon and weapon.Info and backup then
                if backup.ShootCooldown ~= nil then weapon.Info.ShootCooldown = backup.ShootCooldown end
                if backup.QuickShotCooldown ~= nil then weapon.Info.QuickShotCooldown = backup.QuickShotCooldown end
                if backup.ProjectileSpeed ~= nil then weapon.Info.ProjectileSpeed = backup.ProjectileSpeed end
                if backup.ShootRecoil ~= nil then weapon.Info.ShootRecoil = backup.ShootRecoil end
                if backup.ShootSpread ~= nil then weapon.Info.ShootSpread = backup.ShootSpread end
            end
        end)
    end

    if weaponModRetryConn then
        local anyWeaponModEnabled = State.rapidFireEnabled or State.noSpreadEnabled or State.noRecoilEnabled or State.meleeCooldownEnabled or State.dashCooldownEnabled or State.instantADSEnabled or State.infiniteJumpEnabled or State.flickbotEnabled or State.alwaysBackstabEnabled
        if not anyWeaponModEnabled then
            weaponModRetryConn:Disconnect()
            weaponModRetryConn = nil
        end
    end
end

function State.enableFlickbot()
    State.flickbotEnabled = true
    applyWeaponModHooks()
    startWeaponModRetry()
end

function State.disableFlickbot()
    State.flickbotEnabled = false
    State.flickbotBusy = false
    State.flickbotCurrentTarget = nil
    State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart = nil
    State.flickbotActiveTargetPart = nil
    State.flickbotActiveUntil = 0
    State.flickbotFireConsumed = false
    State.flickbotShootCorrectionActive = false
end

function State.enableNoSpread()
    State.noSpreadEnabled = true
    applyWeaponModHooks()
    startWeaponModRetry()
end

function State.disableNoSpread()
    State.noSpreadEnabled = false
    refreshWeaponModValues()
end

function State.enableInstantADS()
    State.instantADSEnabled = true
    applyWeaponModHooks()
    startWeaponModRetry()
end

function State.enableNoRecoil()
    State.noRecoilEnabled = true
    applyWeaponModHooks()
    startWeaponModRetry()
end

function State.disableNoRecoil()
    State.noRecoilEnabled = false
    refreshWeaponModValues()
end

function State.disableInstantADS()
    State.instantADSEnabled = false
    refreshWeaponModValues()

    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if lp and lp.PlayerScripts then
        local ok, gunModule = pcall(function()
            return require(lp.PlayerScripts.Modules.ItemTypes.Gun)
        end)
        if ok and gunModule then
            if State.gunStartAimingOriginal and type(State.gunStartAimingOriginal) == "function" then
                gunModule.StartAiming = State.gunStartAimingOriginal
            end
            if State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal and type(State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal) == "function" then
                gunModule.GetAimSpeed = State.gunGetAimSpeedOriginal
            end
        end
    end
end

local function buildUI()
    local AimbotTab = Pages.Main.Aim:AddLeftTabbox("Aim Assist")
    local MainGroupBox = instrumentVisibilityRows(AimbotTab:AddTab("Aimbot"))
    local SilentAimGroupBox = instrumentVisibilityRows(AimbotTab:AddTab("Silent Aim"))
    local TriggerbotGroupBox = instrumentVisibilityRows(AimbotTab:AddTab("Triggerbot"))
    local FlickbotGroupBox = instrumentVisibilityRows(Pages.Main.Aim:AddRightGroupbox("Flickbot"))

local aimbotMasterToggle = MainGroupBox:AddToggle("AimbotEnabled", {
    Text = "Aimbot",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        State.aimbotEnabled = Value
        if Value then
            notify("Aimbot", "Aimbot enabled")
        else
            State.currentTarget = nil
            State.targetLocked = false
            if FOVCircle then
                FOVCircle.Visible = false
            end
            if SilentFOVCircle then
                SilentFOVCircle.Visible = false
            end
            notify("Aimbot", "Aimbot disabled")
        end
    end,
})

MainGroupBox:AddDropdown("AimPartDropdown", {
    Values = State.targetPartDropdownValues,
    Default = 7,
    Text = "Aimbot Aim Part",
    Callback = function(Value)
        State.lockPart = Value
        State.lastAimbotTargetScan = nil
        State.cachedAimbotTargetPart = nil
    end,
})

MainGroupBox:AddLabel("Aimbot bind"):AddKeyPicker("AimbotKeybind", {
    Default = State.aimbotKeybind,
    Text = "Aimbot keybind",
    Modes = {"Always", "Toggle", "Hold", "Press"},
    ChangedCallback = function(Value)
        State.aimbotKeybind = Value
    end,
})

    Text = "Smoothness",

    Min = 0.05,
    Max = 1.0,
    Rounding = 2,
    Callback = function(Value)
        State.smoothnessValue = Value
    end,
})

	Text = "FOV Circle",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		if not State.aimbotEnabled then
			warn("FOV Circle", "Enable Aimbot first!")
		end
		State.fovCircleEnabled = Value
		if FOVCircle then
			FOVCircle.Visible = State.fovCircleEnabled and State.showFOVCircle and State.aimbotEnabled
		end
	end,
})

	Text = "Show FOV Circle",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.showFOVCircle = Value
		if not State.fovCircleEnabled then
			warn("FOV Circle", "Enable FOV Circle first!")
		end
		if FOVCircle then
			FOVCircle.Visible = State.fovCircleEnabled and State.showFOVCircle and State.aimbotEnabled
		end
	end,
})

	Callback = function(Value)
		if Value then
			State.enablePerfectBlock()
			State.startRageBot()
			notify("Rage Bot", "Rage Bot enabled!")
		else
			State.stopRageBot()
			State.disablePerfectBlock()
			notify("Rage Bot", "Rage Bot disabled")
		end
		if refreshRageBotUi then
			refreshRageBotUi(Value)
		end
	end,
})

RageBotGroupBox:AddToggle("VoidSpamToggle", {
	Text = "Void Spam",
	Default = true,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.voidSpamEnabled = Value
		if Value then
			notify("Void Spam", "Void Spam enabled")
		else
			notify("Void Spam", "Void Spam disabled - Attack only")
		end
		if refreshRageBotUi then
			refreshRageBotUi()
		end
	end,
})

voidSpamMethodDropdown = RageBotGroupBox:AddDropdown("VoidSpamMethodDropdown", {
    Values = {"Random","Stable","Drift","Chaotic","Circle","Spiral","Lissajous","Perlin Noise","Flicker Void","Gravity Well","Extreme Desync","Quantum Oscillation","Frame Skip"},
    Default = 1,
    Text = "Void Spam Method",
    Callback = function(Value)
        State.rageVoidMethod = Value
    end,
})

	Max = 1.0,
	Rounding = 2,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.rageVoidTime = Value
	end,
})

    end,
})

    Callback = function(Value)

    end,
})

ragePreferredWeaponDropdown = RageBotGroupBox:AddDropdown("RagePreferredWeaponDropdown", {
    Values = {"Primary", "Secondary"},
    Default = 1,
    Text = "Preferred Weapon",
    Callback = function(Value)
        State.ragePreferredWeapon = Value
    end,
})

RageBotGroupBox:AddDropdown("RageWeaponTypeDropdown", {
    Values = {"Gun", "Melee", "Knife"},
    Default = 1,
    Text = "Weapon Type",
    Callback = function(Value)
        State.rageWeaponType = Value
        if refreshRageBotUi then
            refreshRageBotUi()
        end
    end,
})

refreshRageBotUi = function(masterEnabled)
    local enabled = masterEnabled
    if enabled == nil then
        enabled = rageBotMasterToggle.Value == true
    end

    local voidEnabled = enabled and State.voidSpamEnabled
    safeSetVisible(voidSpamMethodDropdown, voidEnabled)
    safeSetVisible(voidSpamHideTimeSlider, voidEnabled)
    safeSetVisible(voidSpamAttackTimeSlider, voidEnabled)
    safeSetVisible(ragePreferredWeaponDropdown, enabled and not isRageMeleeMode())
    setGroupboxContentVisible(RageMiscSettingsGroupBox, enabled)
end

bindGroupboxMasterVisibility(RageBotGroupBox, rageBotMasterToggle, refreshRageBotUi)

local WorldCloudProperties = {"Cover", "Density", "Color", "Enabled"}
local WorldTimeProperties = {"ClockTime", "GeographicLatitude"}
local WorldExposureProperties = {"ExposureCompensation"}
local WorldTechnologyProperties = {"Technology"}
