local function setInstanceProperty(instance, property, value)
    if not instance then
        return false
    end

    local ok = pcall(function()
        instance[property] = value
    end)
    return ok
end

local function getInstanceProperty(instance, property)
    if not instance then
        return false, nil
    end

    return pcall(function()
        return instance[property]
    end)
end

local function safeIsA(instance, className)
    if not instance then
        return false
    end

    local ok, result = pcall(function()
        return instance:IsA(className)
    end)
    return ok and result
end

local function captureInstanceProperties(instance, properties)
    local cache = {}
    if not instance then
        return cache
    end

    for _, property in ipairs(properties) do
        local ok, value = getInstanceProperty(instance, property)
        if ok then
            cache[property] = value
        end
    end

    return cache
end

local function restoreInstanceProperties(instance, cache)
    if not instance or type(cache) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    for property, value in pairs(cache) do
        setInstanceProperty(instance, property, value)
    end
end

local function findChildOfClass(container, className, preferredName)
    if not container then
        return nil
    end

    if preferredName then
        local preferred = container:FindFirstChild(preferredName)
        if safeIsA(preferred, className) then
            return preferred
        end
    end

    for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
        if safeIsA(child, className) then
            return child
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function createManagedInstance(className, name)
    local ok, instance = pcall(function()
        return Instance.new(className)
    end)

    if not ok or not instance then
        return nil
    end

    instance.Name = name
    return instance
end

local function findEnumItemByName(enumType, name)
    local ok, items = pcall(function()
        return enumType:GetEnumItems()
    end)

    if not ok or type(items) ~= "table" then
        return nil
    end

    for _, item in ipairs(items) do
        if item.Name == name then
            return item
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function applyWorldFogPreset(name)
    local preset = WorldFogPresets[name]
    if not preset then
        return
    end

    State.worldFogStart = preset.Start
    State.worldFogEnd = preset.End
    State.worldFogColor = preset.Color

    if State.worldFogEnabled then
        State.applyWorldFog()
    end
end

local function applyWorldAtmospherePreset(name)
    local preset = WorldAtmospherePresets[name]
    if not preset then
        return
    end

    State.worldAtmosphereDensity = preset.Density
    State.worldAtmosphereOffset = preset.Offset
    State.worldAtmosphereColor = preset.Color
    State.worldAtmosphereDecay = preset.Decay
    State.worldAtmosphereGlare = preset.Glare
    State.worldAtmosphereHaze = preset.Haze

    if State.worldAtmosphereEnabled then
        State.applyWorldAtmosphere()
    end
end

function State.applyWorldFog()
    if not State.worldFogEnabled then
        return
    end

    setInstanceProperty(Lighting, "FogStart", State.worldFogStart)
    setInstanceProperty(Lighting, "FogEnd", State.worldFogEnd)
    setInstanceProperty(Lighting, "FogColor", State.worldFogColor)
end

function State.enableWorldFog()
    if State.worldFogEnabled then
        State.applyWorldFog()
        return
    end

    State.worldFogOriginal = captureInstanceProperties(Lighting, WorldFogProperties)
    State.worldFogEnabled = true
    State.applyWorldFog()
end

function State.disableWorldFog()
    State.worldFogEnabled = false
    restoreInstanceProperties(Lighting, State.worldFogOriginal)
    State.worldFogOriginal = nil
end

function State.applyWorldAtmosphere()
    if not State.worldAtmosphereEnabled or not State.worldAtmosphereInstance then
        return
    end

    setInstanceProperty(State.worldAtmosphereInstance, "Density", State.worldAtmosphereDensity)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldAtmosphereInstance, "Offset", State.worldAtmosphereOffset)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldAtmosphereInstance, "Color", State.worldAtmosphereColor)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldAtmosphereInstance, "Decay", State.worldAtmosphereDecay)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldAtmosphereInstance, "Glare", State.worldAtmosphereGlare)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldAtmosphereInstance, "Haze", State.worldAtmosphereHaze)
end

function State.enableWorldAtmosphere()
    if State.worldAtmosphereEnabled then
        State.applyWorldAtmosphere()
        return
    end

    local atmosphere = findChildOfClass(Lighting, "Atmosphere", State.worldAtmosphereName)
    local created = false

    if not atmosphere then
        atmosphere = findChildOfClass(Lighting, "Atmosphere")
    end

    if not atmosphere then
        atmosphere = createManagedInstance("Atmosphere", State.worldAtmosphereName)
        if atmosphere then
            created = true
        end
    end

    if not atmosphere then
        notify("Atmosphere", "Not supported in this executor")
        return
    end

    State.worldAtmosphereInstance = atmosphere
    State.worldAtmosphereCreated = created
    if created then
        State.worldAtmosphereOriginal = nil
    else
        State.worldAtmosphereOriginal = captureInstanceProperties(atmosphere, WorldAtmosphereProperties)
    end

    State.worldAtmosphereEnabled = true
    atmosphere.Parent = Lighting
    State.applyWorldAtmosphere()
end

function State.disableWorldAtmosphere()
    State.worldAtmosphereEnabled = false

    local atmosphere = State.worldAtmosphereInstance
    if atmosphere then
        if State.worldAtmosphereCreated then
            pcall(function()
                atmosphere:Destroy()
            end)
        else
            restoreInstanceProperties(atmosphere, State.worldAtmosphereOriginal)
        end
    end

    State.worldAtmosphereInstance = nil
    State.worldAtmosphereOriginal = nil
    State.worldAtmosphereCreated = false
end

function State.applyWorldSunRays()
    if not State.worldSunRaysEnabled or not VisualSunRaysEffect then
        return
    end

    setInstanceProperty(VisualSunRaysEffect, "Intensity", State.worldSunRaysIntensity)
    setInstanceProperty(VisualSunRaysEffect, "Spread", State.worldSunRaysSpread)
    setInstanceProperty(VisualSunRaysEffect, "Enabled", true)
end

function State.enableWorldSunRays()
    if not VisualSunRaysEffect then
        notify("Sun Rays", "Not supported in this executor")
        return
    end

    State.worldSunRaysEnabled = true
    VisualSunRaysEffect.Parent = Lighting
    State.applyWorldSunRays()
end

function State.disableWorldSunRays()
    State.worldSunRaysEnabled = false
    if VisualSunRaysEffect then
        setInstanceProperty(VisualSunRaysEffect, "Enabled", false)
        VisualSunRaysEffect.Parent = nil
    end
end

    setInstanceProperty(VisualDepthOfFieldEffect, "NearIntensity", State.worldDepthNearIntensity)

function State.applyWorldClouds()
    if not State.worldCloudsEnabled or not State.worldCloudsInstance then
        return
    end

    setInstanceProperty(State.worldCloudsInstance, "Cover", State.worldCloudsCover)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldCloudsInstance, "Density", State.worldCloudsDensity)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldCloudsInstance, "Color", State.worldCloudsColor)
    setInstanceProperty(State.worldCloudsInstance, "Enabled", true)
end

function State.enableWorldClouds()
    if State.worldCloudsEnabled then
        State.applyWorldClouds()
        return
    end

    local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if not terrain then
        terrain = workspace.Terrain
    end

    if not terrain then
        notify("Clouds", "Terrain not found")
        return
    end

    local clouds = findChildOfClass(terrain, "Clouds", State.worldCloudsName)
    local created = false

    if not clouds then
        clouds = findChildOfClass(terrain, "Clouds")
    end

    if not clouds then
        clouds = createManagedInstance("Clouds", State.worldCloudsName)
        if clouds then
            created = true
        end
    end

    if not clouds then
        notify("Clouds", "Not supported in this executor")
        return
    end

    State.worldCloudsInstance = clouds
    State.worldCloudsCreated = created
    if created then
        State.worldCloudsOriginal = nil
    else
        State.worldCloudsOriginal = captureInstanceProperties(clouds, WorldCloudProperties)
    end

    State.worldCloudsEnabled = true
    clouds.Parent = terrain
    State.applyWorldClouds()
end

function State.disableWorldClouds()
    State.worldCloudsEnabled = false

    local clouds = State.worldCloudsInstance
    if clouds then
        if State.worldCloudsCreated then
            pcall(function()
                clouds:Destroy()
            end)
        else
            restoreInstanceProperties(clouds, State.worldCloudsOriginal)
        end
    end

    State.worldCloudsInstance = nil
    State.worldCloudsOriginal = nil
    State.worldCloudsCreated = false
end

local PerformanceDisabledClasses = {
    ParticleEmitter = true,
    Trail = true,
    Beam = true,
    Smoke = true,
    Fire = true,
    Sparkles = true,
    BloomEffect = true,
    BlurEffect = true,
    ColorCorrectionEffect = true,
    ColorGradingEffect = true,
    DepthOfFieldEffect = true,
    SunRaysEffect = true,
    Clouds = true,
}
local PerformanceRenderFidelity = findEnumItemByName(Enum.RenderFidelity, "Performance")
local PerformanceTechnology = findEnumItemByName(Enum.Technology, "Compatibility")
    or findEnumItemByName(Enum.Technology, "Voxel")
local PerformanceQualityLevel = findEnumItemByName(Enum.QualityLevel, "Level01")

local function setPerformanceProperty(instance, property, value)
    if not State.performanceModeEnabled or not instance then
        return false
    end

    local instanceCache = State.performanceModeCache[instance]
    if not instanceCache then
        instanceCache = {}
        State.performanceModeCache[instance] = instanceCache
    end

    if instanceCache[property] == nil then
        local ok, original = getInstanceProperty(instance, property)
        if not ok then
            return false
        end
        instanceCache[property] = {Value = original}
    end

    return setInstanceProperty(instance, property, value)
end

function State.applyPerformanceModeToInstance(instance)
    if not State.performanceModeEnabled or not instance then
        return
    end

    local className = instance.ClassName
    if safeIsA(instance, "BasePart") then
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Material", Enum.Material.Plastic)
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "MaterialVariant", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Reflectance", 0)
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "CastShadow", false)

        if className == "MeshPart" then
            setPerformanceProperty(instance, "TextureID", "")
            if PerformanceRenderFidelity then
                setPerformanceProperty(instance, "RenderFidelity", PerformanceRenderFidelity)
            end
        end
    elseif className == "SpecialMesh" then
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "TextureId", "")
    elseif className == "Decal" or className == "Texture" then
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Texture", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Transparency", 1)
    elseif className == "SurfaceAppearance" then
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "ColorMap", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "MetalnessMap", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "NormalMap", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "RoughnessMap", "")
    elseif PerformanceDisabledClasses[className] then
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Enabled", false)
    elseif className == "Atmosphere" then
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Density", 0)
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Glare", 0)
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "Haze", 0)
    elseif className == "Sky" then
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "SkyboxBk", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "SkyboxDn", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "SkyboxFt", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "SkyboxLf", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "SkyboxRt", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "SkyboxUp", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "SunTextureId", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "MoonTextureId", "")
        setPerformanceProperty(instance, "StarCount", 0)
    end
end

function State.enforcePerformanceMode()
    if not State.performanceModeEnabled then
        return
    end

    setPerformanceProperty(Lighting, "GlobalShadows", false)
    setPerformanceProperty(Lighting, "EnvironmentDiffuseScale", 0)
    setPerformanceProperty(Lighting, "EnvironmentSpecularScale", 0)
    setPerformanceProperty(Lighting, "ShadowSoftness", 0)

    if PerformanceTechnology then
        setPerformanceProperty(Lighting, "Technology", PerformanceTechnology)
    end

    local terrain = workspace:FindFirstChildOfClass("Terrain")
    if terrain then
        setPerformanceProperty(terrain, "Decoration", false)
        setPerformanceProperty(terrain, "WaterWaveSize", 0)
        setPerformanceProperty(terrain, "WaterWaveSpeed", 0)
        setPerformanceProperty(terrain, "WaterReflectance", 0)
        setPerformanceProperty(terrain, "WaterTransparency", 1)
    end

    for _, instance in ipairs(Lighting:GetDescendants()) do
        State.applyPerformanceModeToInstance(instance)
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if camera then
        for _, instance in ipairs(camera:GetDescendants()) do
            State.applyPerformanceModeToInstance(instance)
        end
    end
end

function State.enablePerformanceMode()
    if State.performanceModeEnabled then
        State.enforcePerformanceMode()
        return
    end

    State.performanceModeEnabled = true
    State.performanceModeGeneration = State.performanceModeGeneration + 1
    State.performanceModeCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    local generation = State.performanceModeGeneration

    local ok, quality = pcall(function()
        return settings().Rendering.QualityLevel
    end)
    if ok then
        State.performanceModeQualityCaptured = true
        State.performanceModeQualityOriginal = quality
        if PerformanceQualityLevel then
            pcall(function()
                settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = PerformanceQualityLevel
            end)
        end
    end

    State.enforcePerformanceMode()

    for _, connection in ipairs(State.performanceModeConnections) do
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    State.performanceModeConnections = {}

    table.insert(State.performanceModeConnections, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
        if State.performanceModeEnabled and State.performanceModeGeneration == generation then
            State.applyPerformanceModeToInstance(instance)
        end
    end))
    table.insert(State.performanceModeConnections, Lighting.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(instance)
        if State.performanceModeEnabled and State.performanceModeGeneration == generation then
            State.applyPerformanceModeToInstance(instance)
        end
    end))

    task.spawn(function()
        local descendants = workspace:GetDescendants()
        for index, instance in ipairs(descendants) do
            if not State.performanceModeEnabled or State.performanceModeGeneration ~= generation then
                return
            end

            State.applyPerformanceModeToInstance(instance)
            if index % 400 == 0 then
                task.wait()
            end
        end

        if State.performanceModeEnabled and State.performanceModeGeneration == generation then
            State.enforcePerformanceMode()
        end
    end)
end

function State.disablePerformanceMode()
    if not State.performanceModeEnabled and not next(State.performanceModeCache) then
        return
    end

    State.performanceModeEnabled = false
    State.performanceModeGeneration = State.performanceModeGeneration + 1

    for _, connection in ipairs(State.performanceModeConnections) do
        pcall(function()
            connection:Disconnect()
        end)
    end
    State.performanceModeConnections = {}

    for instance, properties in pairs(State.performanceModeCache) do
        for property, record in pairs(properties) do
            pcall(function()
                instance[property] = record.Value
            end)
        end
    end
    State.performanceModeCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

    if State.performanceModeQualityCaptured then
        pcall(function()
            settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = State.performanceModeQualityOriginal
        end)
    end
    State.performanceModeQualityCaptured = false
    State.performanceModeQualityOriginal = nil
end

local function getNormalizedCameraCFrame(camera)
    if not camera then
        return nil
    end

    local current = camera.CFrame
    local position = current.Position
    local look = current.LookVector
    local up = current.UpVector

    if look.Magnitude < 0.0001 then
        return current
    end
    look = look.Unit

    up = up - look * up:Dot(look)
    if up.Magnitude < 0.0001 then
        local fallback = math.abs(look.Y) < 0.99 and Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)
        up = fallback - look * fallback:Dot(look)
    end
    up = up.Unit

    return CFrame.lookAt(position, position + look, up)
end

function State.applyAspectRatio()
    if not State.aspectRatioEnabled then
        return
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local baseCFrame = getNormalizedCameraCFrame(camera)
    if not camera or not baseCFrame then
        return
    end

    local scaleX = math.clamp(tonumber(State.aspectRatioX) or 1, 0.01, 1)
    local scaleY = math.clamp(tonumber(State.aspectRatioY) or 1, 0.01, 1)
    local distortion = CFrame.new(
        0, 0, 0,
        scaleX, 0, 0,
        0, scaleY, 0,
        0, 0, 1
    )

    camera.CFrame = baseCFrame * distortion
end

function State.enableAspectRatio()
    State.aspectRatioEnabled = true
    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(State.aspectRatioRenderStepName)
    end)
    RunService:BindToRenderStep(
        State.aspectRatioRenderStepName,
        Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 1,
        State.applyAspectRatio
    )
    State.applyAspectRatio()
end

function State.disableAspectRatio()
    local wasEnabled = State.aspectRatioEnabled
    State.aspectRatioEnabled = false
    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(State.aspectRatioRenderStepName)
    end)
    if not wasEnabled then
        return
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local normalized = getNormalizedCameraCFrame(camera)
    if camera and normalized then
        camera.CFrame = normalized
    end
end

function State.updateWorldVisualEffects(delta)
    State.worldEffectUpdateAccumulator = (State.worldEffectUpdateAccumulator or 0) + (delta or 0)
    if State.worldEffectUpdateAccumulator < 0.25 then
        return
    end

    State.worldEffectUpdateAccumulator = 0
    State.applyWorldFog()
    State.applyWorldAtmosphere()
    State.applyWorldSunRays()
    State.applyWorldDepthOfField()
    State.applyWorldClouds()
    State.applyWorldTime()
    State.applyWorldExposure()
    State.applyWorldTechnology()
    State.applyWorldMotionBlur()
    State.enforcePerformanceMode()
end

	NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.3),
	NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.4)
})

ShaderSnowParticle:Clone().Parent = ShaderSnowEffect

local ShaderSnowConnection = nil

function State.enableShader()
	if State.shaderEnabled then return end
	State.shaderEnabled = true

	ShaderCacheLighting = {
		Lighting.Ambient,
		Lighting.Brightness,
		Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom,
		Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
		Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
		Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
		Lighting.GlobalShadows,
		Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
		Lighting.ShadowSoftness,
		Lighting.TimeOfDay,
	}

	Lighting.Ambient = State.shaderAmbientColor
	Lighting.Brightness = State.shaderBrightness
	Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = State.shaderColorShiftBottom
	Lighting.ColorShift_Top = State.shaderColorShiftTop
	Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = State.shaderEnvironmentDiffuseScale
	Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = State.shaderEnvironmentSpecularScale
	Lighting.GlobalShadows = State.shaderGlobalShadows
	Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = State.shaderOutdoorAmbient
	Lighting.ShadowSoftness = State.shaderShadowSoftness
	Lighting.TimeOfDay = State.shaderTime..':00:00'

	ShaderBlurEffect.Size = State.shaderBlurSize
	ShaderColorCorrectionEffect.Saturation = State.shaderSaturation

	ShaderSky.Parent = Lighting
	ShaderBlurEffect.Parent = Lighting
	ShaderColorCorrectionEffect.Parent = Lighting

	if State.shaderSnowEnabled then
		ShaderSnowEffect.Parent = workspace
		ShaderSnowConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
			local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
			if lp and lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				ShaderSnowEffect.Position = lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 90, 0)
			end
		end)
	end
end

function State.disableShader()
	if not State.shaderEnabled then return end
	State.shaderEnabled = false

	Lighting.Ambient = ShaderCacheLighting[1]
	Lighting.Brightness = ShaderCacheLighting[2]
	Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = ShaderCacheLighting[3]
	Lighting.ColorShift_Top = ShaderCacheLighting[4]
	Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = ShaderCacheLighting[5]
	Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = ShaderCacheLighting[6]
	Lighting.GlobalShadows = ShaderCacheLighting[7]
	Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = ShaderCacheLighting[8]
	Lighting.ShadowSoftness = ShaderCacheLighting[9]
	Lighting.TimeOfDay = ShaderCacheLighting[10]

	ShaderSky.Parent = nil
	ShaderBlurEffect.Parent = nil
	ShaderColorCorrectionEffect.Parent = nil

	ShaderSnowEffect.Parent = nil
	if ShaderSnowConnection then
		ShaderSnowConnection:Disconnect()
		ShaderSnowConnection = nil
	end
end

EffectsGroupBox:AddToggle("ShaderToggle", {
	Text = "Shader",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		if Value then
			State.enableShader()
			notify("Shader", "Shader enabled")
		else
			State.disableShader()
			notify("Shader", "Shader disabled")
		end
	end,
})

EffectsGroupBox:AddToggle("ShaderSnowToggle", {
	Text = "Shader Snow",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.shaderSnowEnabled = Value
		if State.shaderEnabled then
			if Value then
				ShaderSnowEffect.Parent = workspace
				if not ShaderSnowConnection then
					ShaderSnowConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
						local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
						if lp and lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							ShaderSnowEffect.Position = lp.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 90, 0)
						end
					end)
				end
			else
				ShaderSnowEffect.Parent = nil
				if ShaderSnowConnection then
					ShaderSnowConnection:Disconnect()
					ShaderSnowConnection = nil
				end
			end
		end
	end,
})

local SoundsTabbox = Pages.Visual.Sounds:AddLeftTabbox("Sounds")
local HitsoundOptionsGroup = SoundsTabbox:AddTab("Hitsound")
local HitsoundIdInputBox = nil

local function normalizeSoundId(value)
	value = tostring(value or ""):gsub("%s+", "")
	if value == "" then
		return State.hitsoundId
	end
	if value:find("^rbxassetid://") then
		return value
	end
	local id = value:match("(%d+)")
	if id then
		return "rbxassetid://" .. id
	end
	return value
end

local function applyHitsoundSelection(selection)
	State.hitsoundMode = selection
	if selection == "Custom" then
		local customValue = HitsoundIdInputBox and HitsoundIdInputBox.Value or State.hitsoundId
		State.hitsoundId = normalizeSoundId(customValue)
	else
		State.hitsoundId = Sounds[selection] or State.hitsoundId
	end
end

HitsoundOptionsGroup:AddToggle("HitsoundChangerToggle", {
	Text = "Hit Sound",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		if Value then
			State.enableHitsoundChanger()
			notify("Hit Sound", "Enabled")
		else
			State.disableHitsoundChanger()
			notify("Hit Sound", "Disabled")
		end
	end,
})

HitsoundIdInputBox = HitsoundOptionsGroup:AddInput("HitsoundIdInput", {
	Text = "Sound ID (rbxassetid://...)",
	Default = State.hitsoundId,
	Numeric = false,
	Finished = true,
	Callback = function(Value)
		if State.hitsoundMode == "Custom" then
			State.hitsoundId = normalizeSoundId(Value)
		end
	end,
})

HitsoundOptionsGroup:AddDropdown("SoundDropdown", {
	Values = (function()
		local t = {"Custom"}
		for k in pairs(Sounds) do
			table.insert(t, k)
		end
		table.sort(t)
		return t
	end)(),
	Default = "Neverlose",
	Text = "Sound Preset",
	Callback = function(Value)
		applyHitsoundSelection(Value)
		safeSetVisible(HitsoundIdInputBox, Value == "Custom")
		if State.hitsoundChangerEnabled then
			notify("Hitsound", "Changed to " .. Value)
		end
	end,
})

safeSetVisible(HitsoundIdInputBox, false)

HitsoundOptionsGroup:AddSlider("HitsoundPitchSlider", {
	Text = "Hitsound Pitch",
	Default = 1,
	Min = 0.5,
	Max = 2,
	Rounding = 1,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.hitsoundPitch = Value
	end,
})

HitsoundOptionsGroup:AddSlider("HitsoundVolumeSlider", {
	Text = "Hitsound Volume",
	Default = 1,
	Min = 0,
	Max = 2,
	Rounding = 1,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.hitsoundVolume = Value
	end,
})

local function anim2track(asset_id)
    local objs = game:GetObjects(asset_id)
    for i = 1, #objs do
        if objs[i]:IsA("Animation") then
            return objs[i].AnimationId
        end
    end
    return asset_id
end

function State.startAnimatePlayer()
    if State.animatePlayerEnabled then return end
    State.animatePlayerEnabled = true
    
    local Player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local animid = State.animationId
    
    if not animid:find("rbxassetid://") then
        animid = "rbxassetid://" .. animid
    end
    
    animid = anim2track(animid)
    
    local animation = Instance.new("Animation")
    animation.AnimationId = animid
    
    local function playAnim(character)
        if not State.animatePlayerEnabled then return end
        local Hum = character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Humanoid")
        if not Hum then return end
        
        for _, track in next, Hum:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() do
            track:Stop()
        end
        
        local anim = Hum:LoadAnimation(animation)
        anim.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action4
        anim:Play()
        anim:AdjustSpeed(State.animationSpeed)
        
        table.insert(State.animationTracks, anim)
        
        anim.Stopped:Connect(function()
            if State.animatePlayerEnabled then
                playAnim(character)
            end
        end)
    end
    
    local charAddedConn = Player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
        character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
        playAnim(character)
    end)
    
    table.insert(State.animationConnections, charAddedConn)
    
    if Player.Character then
        playAnim(Player.Character)
    end
end

function State.stopAnimatePlayer()
    State.animatePlayerEnabled = false
    
    for _, conn in ipairs(State.animationConnections) do
        pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
    end
    State.animationConnections = {}
    
    for _, track in ipairs(State.animationTracks) do
        pcall(function() track:Stop() end)
    end
    State.animationTracks = {}
end

function State.enableAntiOutOfBounds()
    if State.antiOutOfBoundsHooked then
        State.antiOutOfBoundsEnabled = true
        return true
    end

    if type(hookfunction) ~= "function" then
        return false, "hookfunction is not available"
    end

    local ok, Utility = pcall(function()
        return require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Utility"))
    end)

    if not ok or type(Utility) ~= "table" then
        return false, "Utility module not found"
    end

    if type(Utility.IsWithinPart) ~= "function" or type(Utility.IsWithinTaggedParts) ~= "function" then
        return false, "Out-of-bounds checks not found"
    end

    local wrap = type(newcclosure) == "function" and newcclosure or function(fn) return fn end

    local oldPart
    local oldTagged

    local hookOk, hookErr = pcall(function()
        oldPart = hookfunction(Utility.IsWithinPart, wrap(function(...)
            if not State.antiOutOfBoundsEnabled then
                return oldPart(...)
            end

            return false
        end))

        oldTagged = hookfunction(Utility.IsWithinTaggedParts, wrap(function(self, tag, pos, size, returnAll)
            if not State.antiOutOfBoundsEnabled then
                return oldTagged(self, tag, pos, size, returnAll)
            end

            if returnAll then
                return {}
            end

            return nil
        end))
    end)

    if not hookOk then
        if oldPart then
            pcall(function()
                hookfunction(Utility.IsWithinPart, oldPart)
            end)
        end
        return false, hookErr
    end

    State.antiOutOfBoundsEnabled = true
    State.antiOutOfBoundsHooked = true
    State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalPart = oldPart
    State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalTagged = oldTagged

    return true
end

function State.disableAntiOutOfBounds()
    State.antiOutOfBoundsEnabled = false

    if not State.antiOutOfBoundsHooked then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        local Utility = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Utility"))

        if State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalPart then
            hookfunction(Utility.IsWithinPart, State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalPart)
        end

        if State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalTagged then
            hookfunction(Utility.IsWithinTaggedParts, State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalTagged)
        end
    end)

    State.antiOutOfBoundsHooked = false
    State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalPart = nil
    State.antiOutOfBoundsOriginalTagged = nil
end

local SpooferTabbox = Pages.Visual.Spoofers:AddLeftTabbox("Spoofer")
local FunctionsGroupBox = SpooferTabbox:AddTab("Device")

FunctionsGroupBox:AddToggle("DeviceSpoofToggle", {
	Text = "Device Spoofer",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		if Value then
			State.enableDeviceSpoof()
			notify("Device Spoofer", "Spoofed as " .. State.selectedDevice)
			
			if State.deviceSpoofSyncConnection then
				State.deviceSpoofSyncConnection:Disconnect()
			end
			local lastDeviceSpoofSync = 0
			State.deviceSpoofSyncConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				local now = tick()
				if State.deviceSpoofEnabled and now - lastDeviceSpoofSync >= 1 then
					lastDeviceSpoofSync = now
					local targetDevice = DeviceSpoofControls[State.selectedDevice]
					if targetDevice then
						State.fireDeviceSpoofControls(targetDevice)
					end
				end
			end)
		else

			if State.deviceSpoofSyncConnection then
				State.deviceSpoofSyncConnection:Disconnect()
				State.deviceSpoofSyncConnection = nil
			end
			State.disableDeviceSpoof()
			notify("Device Spoofer", "Disabled")
		end
	end,
})

FunctionsGroupBox:AddDropdown("DeviceTypeDropdown", {
	Values = {"Computer", "Mobile", "Console", "VR", "Car"},
	Default = 1,
	Text = "Device Type",
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.changeDevice(Value)
		if State.deviceSpoofEnabled then
			notify("Device Spoofer", "Changed to " .. Value)
		end
	end,
})

					local now = tick()

						lastNameSpoofSync = now

				State.nameSpoofSyncConnection:Disconnect()

		end

	local function getCurrentPlayerData()
		local controller = State.getPlayerDataController and State.getPlayerDataController()
		return controller and controller.CurrentData
	end

	local function safeSetAttribute(target, name, value)
		if target and typeof(target.SetAttribute) == "function" then
			pcall(function()
				target:SetAttribute(name, value)
			end)
		end
	end

	local function safeSetIntValue(parent, name, value)
		if not parent then
			return
		end
		local stat = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
		if stat and stat:IsA("IntValue") then
			stat.Value = value
		end
	end

	local function applyLeaderstats(player)
		if not player then
			return
		end
		local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("CustomLeaderstats")
		if leaderstats then
			if State.levelSpoofEnabled then
				safeSetIntValue(leaderstats, "Level", tonumber(State.levelSpoofValue) or 1)
			end
			if State.streakSpoofEnabled then
				safeSetIntValue(leaderstats, "Win Streak", tonumber(State.streakSpoofValue) or 0)
			end
		end
	end

	function State.spoofPlayerDataValue(key, value)
		local data = getCurrentPlayerData()
		if data then
			pcall(function()
				if typeof(data.Set) == "function" then
					data:Set(key, value)
				end
				if typeof(data.SetReplicate) == "function" then
					data:SetReplicate(key, value)
				end
				if typeof(data.Replicate) == "function" then
					data:Replicate(key)
				end
			end)
			return true
		end

		local player = Players.LocalPlayer
		safeSetAttribute(player, key, value)
		return true
	end

	local function getCurrencyContainer()
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		return localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame.Lobby:FindFirstChild("Currency")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame.Lobby.Currency:FindFirstChild("Container")
	end

	local function applyKeySpoof()
		if not State.keySpoofEnabled then
			return
		end

		local container = getCurrencyContainer()
		if not container then
			return
		end

		local textValue = tostring(State.keySpoofValue or "")
		for _, icon in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
			if icon.Name == "Icon" and type(icon.Image) == "string" and icon.Image == "rbxassetid://17860673529" then
				local title = icon.Parent and icon.Parent.Parent and icon.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("Title")
				if title and title:IsA("TextLabel") then
					title.Text = textValue
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function getViewProfilePlayerFrame()
		local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
		if not localPlayer then
			return nil
		end

		local pages = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
			and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame:FindFirstChild("Pages")
		if not pages then
			return nil
		end

		local viewProfile = pages:FindFirstChild("ViewProfile")
		if not viewProfile then
			return nil
		end

		local active = viewProfile:FindFirstChild("Active")
		if not active then
			return nil
		end

		return active:FindFirstChild("Player")
	end

	local function applyViewProfileLevelSpoof()
		if not State.levelSpoofEnabled then
			return
		end

		local profilePlayer = getViewProfilePlayerFrame()
		if not profilePlayer then
			return
		end

		local displayLabel = profilePlayer:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")
		if not displayLabel or not displayLabel:IsA("TextLabel") then
			return
		end

		local displayedText = tostring(displayLabel.Text or "")
		local localDisplayName = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.DisplayName or ""
		local spoofDisplayName = State.nameSpoofDisplayName ~= "" and State.nameSpoofDisplayName or State.nameSpoofOriginalDisplayName

		if displayedText == localDisplayName or displayedText == spoofDisplayName then
			local bragging = profilePlayer:FindFirstChild("Bragging")
			local levelFrame = bragging and bragging:FindFirstChild("Level")
			local valueLabel = levelFrame and levelFrame:FindFirstChild("Value")
			if valueLabel and valueLabel:IsA("TextLabel") then
				valueLabel.Text = tostring(State.levelSpoofValue or 0)
			end
		end
	end

	function State.applyPlayerDataSpoof()
		if not (State.levelSpoofEnabled or State.streakSpoofEnabled or State.eloSpoofEnabled or State.keySpoofEnabled) then
			return
		end

		local player = Players.LocalPlayer
		if State.levelSpoofEnabled then
			State.spoofPlayerDataValue("Level", tonumber(State.levelSpoofValue) or 1)
			safeSetAttribute(player, "Level", tonumber(State.levelSpoofValue) or 1)
		end

		if State.streakSpoofEnabled then
			State.spoofPlayerDataValue("StatisticDuelsWinStreak", tonumber(State.streakSpoofValue) or 0)
			safeSetAttribute(player, "StatisticDuelsWinStreak", tonumber(State.streakSpoofValue) or 0)
		end

		if State.eloSpoofEnabled then
			State.spoofPlayerDataValue("DisplayELO", tonumber(State.eloSpoofValue) or 1000)
			safeSetAttribute(player, "DisplayELO", tonumber(State.eloSpoofValue) or 1000)
		end

		if State.keySpoofEnabled then
			applyKeySpoof()
		end

		if State.levelSpoofEnabled then
			applyViewProfileLevelSpoof()
		end

		applyLeaderstats(player)
	end

	function State.refreshPlayerDataSpoof()
		local isEnabled = State.levelSpoofEnabled or State.streakSpoofEnabled or State.eloSpoofEnabled or State.keySpoofEnabled
		State.playerDataSpoofEnabled = isEnabled

		if isEnabled then
			State.applyPlayerDataSpoof()
			if not State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection then
				State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if State.playerDataSpoofEnabled then
						State.applyPlayerDataSpoof()
					end
				end)
			end
			return
		end

		if State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection then
			State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection:Disconnect()
			State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection = nil
		end
	end

function State.startMaulSlamMultiplier()
    if State.maulSlamConnection then return end
    
    State.maulSlamConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not State.maulSlamMultiplierEnabled then return end
        State.applyMaulSlamMultiplier()
    end)
end

function State.stopMaulSlamMultiplier()
    if State.maulSlamConnection then
        State.maulSlamConnection:Disconnect()
        State.maulSlamConnection = nil
    end
    State.restoreMaulSlamProperties()
end

function State.applyMaulSlamMultiplier()
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not LocalPlayer or not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
    
    pcall(function()
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        
        if not humanoid then return end
        
        local maulWeapon = nil
        local weaponName = "Maul"
        
        for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") or child:IsA("Model") then
                if string.lower(child.Name):find("maul") then
                    maulWeapon = child
                    break
                end
            end
        end
        
        if not maulWeapon then
            local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
            if backpack then
                for _, item in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
                    if item:IsA("Tool") and string.lower(item.Name):find("maul") then
                        maulWeapon = item
                        break
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        
        if not maulWeapon then return end
        
        local weaponId = tostring(maulWeapon)
        
        if not State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId] then
            State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId] = {
                appliedMultiplier = 0,
                properties = {}
            }
        end
        
        if State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].appliedMultiplier == State.maulSlamRangeMultiplier then
            return
        end
        
        State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].appliedMultiplier = State.maulSlamRangeMultiplier
        
        for _, instance in ipairs(maulWeapon:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()

                if instance:IsA("NumberValue") or instance:IsA("IntValue") or instance:IsA("DoubleValue") then
                    local lowerName = string.lower(instance.Name)
                    
                    if lowerName:find("range") or lowerName:find("radius") or lowerName:find("distance") 
                        or lowerName:find("slam") or lowerName:find("affect") or lowerName:find("detection") then
                        
                        if not State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance)] then
                            State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance)] = instance.Value
                        end
                        
                        local originalValue = State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance)]
                        instance.Value = originalValue * State.maulSlamRangeMultiplier
                    end
                end
                
                if instance:IsA("Instance") and instance.GetAttribute and instance.SetAttribute then
                    for _, attribute in ipairs(instance:GetAttributes()) do
                        local lowerAttr = string.lower(tostring(attribute))
                        if lowerAttr:find("range") or lowerAttr:find("radius") or lowerAttr:find("slam") then
                            pcall(function()
                                local value = instance:GetAttribute(attribute)
                                if typeof(value) == "number" then
                                    if not State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance) .. attribute] then
                                        State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance) .. attribute] = value
                                    end
                                    local originalValue = State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance) .. attribute]
                                    instance:SetAttribute(attribute, originalValue * State.maulSlamRangeMultiplier)
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local lowerName = string.lower(instance.Name)
                    if lowerName:find("slam") or lowerName:find("detection") or lowerName:find("hit") or lowerName:find("radius") then
                        if not State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance) .. "_size"] then
                            State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance) .. "_size"] = instance.Size
                        end
                        local originalSize = State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[tostring(instance) .. "_size"]
                        instance.Size = originalSize * State.maulSlamRangeMultiplier
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        
        pcall(function()
            maulWeapon:SetAttribute("MaulSlamMultiplierApplied", State.maulSlamRangeMultiplier)
        end)
    end)
end

function State.restoreMaulSlamProperties()
    pcall(function()
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
        if not LocalPlayer then return end
        
        local character = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not character then return end
        
        local maulWeapons = {}
        
        for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if child:IsA("Tool") or child:IsA("Model") then
                if string.lower(child.Name):find("maul") then
                    table.insert(maulWeapons, child)
                end
            end
        end
        
        local backpack = LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if backpack then
            for _, item in ipairs(backpack:GetChildren()) do
                if item:IsA("Tool") and string.lower(item.Name):find("maul") then
                    table.insert(maulWeapons, item)
                end
            end
        end
        
        for _, maulWeapon in ipairs(maulWeapons) do
            local weaponId = tostring(maulWeapon)
            if State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId] and State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties then
                
                for _, instance in ipairs(maulWeapon:GetDescendants()) do
                    pcall(function()
                        local backupKey = tostring(instance)
                        
                        if (instance:IsA("NumberValue") or instance:IsA("IntValue") or instance:IsA("DoubleValue")) 
                            and State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[backupKey] then
                            instance.Value = State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[backupKey]
                        end
                        
                        if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local sizeBackupKey = backupKey .. "_size"
                            if State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[sizeBackupKey] then
                                instance.Size = State.maulSlamBackups[weaponId].properties[sizeBackupKey]
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
        
        State.maulSlamBackups = {}
    end)
end

function State.startAutoBanWeapons()
    if State.autoBanWeaponsEnabled then return end
    State.autoBanWeaponsEnabled = true

    State.autoBanConnection = task.spawn(function()
        local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local index = 1

        while State.autoBanWeaponsEnabled do
            local votes = { State.autoBanWeapon1 or "", State.autoBanWeapon2 or "" }
            if votes[1] == "" and votes[2] == "" then
                State.autoBanWeaponsEnabled = false
                break
            end

            index = math.clamp(index, 1, #votes)
            local weapon = votes[index]
            if weapon and weapon ~= "" then
                pcall(function()
                    local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes")
                    local duels = remotes:WaitForChild("Duels")
                    local vote = duels:WaitForChild("Vote")
                    vote:FireServer(weapon)
                end)
            end
            index = index % #votes + 1
            task.wait(1)
        end

        State.autoBanConnection = nil
    end)
end

function State.stopAutoBanWeapons()
    State.autoBanWeaponsEnabled = false
    State.autoBanConnection = nil
end

        end

function State.startAutoMatchmaking()
    if State.autoMatchmakingThread then return end
    
    State.autoMatchmakingEnabled = true
    State.autoMatchmakingThread = task.spawn(function()
        while State.autoMatchmakingEnabled do
            task.wait(State.autoMatchmakingInterval)
            
            if State.autoMatchmakingEnabled then
                local RepStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                local Remotes = RepStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                
                if Remotes then
                    local Matchmaking = Remotes:FindFirstChild("Matchmaking")
                    if Matchmaking then
                        local JoinQueueRF = Matchmaking:FindFirstChild("JoinQueue")
                        if JoinQueueRF and JoinQueueRF:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
                            pcall(function()
                                JoinQueueRF:InvokeServer(State.selectedQueueMode)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function State.stopAutoMatchmaking()
    State.autoMatchmakingEnabled = false
    if State.autoMatchmakingThread then
        task.cancel(State.autoMatchmakingThread)
        State.autoMatchmakingThread = nil
    end
end

function State.startAutoRandomMapVote()
    if State.autoRandomMapVoteThread then return end

    State.autoRandomMapVoteEnabled = true
    State.autoRandomMapVoteThread = task.spawn(function()
        local RepStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
        local maps = State.randomDuelMaps

        while State.autoRandomMapVoteEnabled do
            local success, err = pcall(function()
                local Remotes = RepStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                local Duels = Remotes and Remotes:FindFirstChild("Duels")
                local Vote = Duels and Duels:FindFirstChild("Vote")
                if Vote and Vote.FireServer then
                    local mapName = State.selectedDuelMap
                    if not mapName or not table.find(maps, mapName) then
                        mapName = maps[1]
                    end
                    if mapName then
                        Vote:FireServer(mapName)
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(State.autoRandomMapVoteInterval)
        end
    end)
end

function State.stopAutoRandomMapVote()
    State.autoRandomMapVoteEnabled = false
    if State.autoRandomMapVoteThread then
        task.cancel(State.autoRandomMapVoteThread)
        State.autoRandomMapVoteThread = nil
    end
end

		else
			State.disableFFAAutoRespawn()
			notify("FFA", "Auto Respawn disabled")
		end
	end,
})

local ModDetectorGroupBox = Pages.Functions.Safety:AddLeftGroupbox("Mod Detector")

ModDetectorGroupBox:AddToggle("ModDetectorToggle", {
	Text = "Enable Mod Detector",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.modDetectorEnabled = Value
		if Value then
			notify("Mod Detector", "Enabled")
			if State.modDetectorConnection then
				State.modDetectorConnection:Disconnect()
			end
			State.modDetectorConnection = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
				task.wait(0.5)
				if runModDetection() then
					return
				end
			end)
			runModDetection()
		else
			notify("Mod Detector", "Disabled")
			if State.modDetectorConnection then
				State.modDetectorConnection:Disconnect()
				State.modDetectorConnection = nil
			end
		end
	end,
})

ModDetectorGroupBox:AddDropdown("ModDetectorModeDropdown", {
	Text = "Detection Mode",
	Values = {"Notify", "Kick"},
	Default = 1,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.modDetectorMode = Value
		notify("Mod Detector", "Mode set to: " .. Value)
	end,
})

ModDetectorGroupBox:AddToggle("ModDetectorAutoLoadToggle", {
	Text = "Auto Load on Teleport",
	Default = false,
	Callback = function(Value)
		State.modDetectorAutoLoad = Value
		if Value then
			notify("Mod Detector", "AutoLoad enabled")
			queue_on_teleport([[
				pcall(function()
					if getgenv().ModDetectorEnabled == nil then
						getgenv().ModDetectorEnabled = true
						getgenv().ModDetectorMode = "Notify"
					end
					local ok, err = xpcall(function()
						task.wait(1)
						repeat task.wait() until game:IsLoaded()
						local Players = game:GetService("Players")
						local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
						
						local function isStaffRoleName(role)
							if role and typeof(role) == "string" then
								local r = string.lower(role)
								return string.find(r, "mod") or string.find(r, "staff") or string.find(r, "admin")
							end
							return false
						end
						
						local function getStaffInServer()
							local staffNames = {}
							for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
									local role = pcall(function()
										return plr:GetRoleInGroup(game.CreatorId)
									end)
									if role and isStaffRoleName(role) then
										table.insert(staffNames, plr.Name)
									end
								end
							end
							return staffNames
						end
						
						local function playNotificationSound()
							pcall(function()
								local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
								sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12221967"
								sound.Volume = 2
								sound.Parent = workspace
								sound:Play()
								game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound, 2)
							end)
						end
						
						if getgenv().ModDetectorEnabled then
							local staffList = getStaffInServer()
							if #staffList > 0 then
								if getgenv().ModDetectorMode == "Kick" then
									LocalPlayer:Kick("Moderator detected: " .. table.concat(staffList, ", "))
								else
									playNotificationSound()
								end
							end
						end
					end, function(err) warn("ModDetector Error:", err) end)
				end)
			]])
		else
			notify("Mod Detector", "AutoLoad disabled")
			getgenv().ModDetectorEnabled = false
		end
	end,
})

local MaulSlamGroupBox = Pages.Functions.Safety:AddRightGroupbox("Maul Slam Multiapplier")

})

			State.restoreMaulSlamProperties()

})

	ThemeFolder = "Phantom/specific-game/Rivals/themes",
	AutoloadFile = "Phantom/specific-game/Rivals/settings/autoload.txt",
	DefaultThemeFile = "Phantom/specific-game/Rivals/themes/default.txt",
}

local function directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	Name = tostring(Name or ""):gsub("[^%w%s_%-]", "")
	Name = Name:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
	return Name
end

function DirectSettings:EnsureFolders()
	if type(isfolder) ~= "function" or type(makefolder) ~= "function" then
		return false, "filesystem APIs unavailable"
	end

	for _, Folder in ipairs({
		"Phantom",
		"Phantom/specific-game",
		"Phantom/specific-game/Rivals",
		self.ConfigFolder,
		self.ThemeFolder,
	}) do
		if not isfolder(Folder) then
			local ok = pcall(makefolder, Folder)
			if not ok and not isfolder(Folder) then
				return false, "failed to create " .. Folder
			end
		end
	end
	return true
end

function DirectSettings:ListJsonFiles(Folder)
	local Result = {}
	if type(listfiles) ~= "function" then
		return Result
	end

	self:EnsureFolders()
	local ok, Files = pcall(listfiles, Folder)
	if not ok or type(Files) ~= "table" then
		return Result
	end

	for _, File in ipairs(Files) do
		local Name = tostring(File):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
		if Name then
			table.insert(Result, Name)
		end
	end
	table.sort(Result)
	return Result
end

function DirectSettings:ShouldIgnoreFlag(Idx)
	Idx = tostring(Idx or "")
	return Idx:sub(1, 12) == "DirectConfig"
		or Idx:sub(1, 11) == "DirectTheme"
		or Idx == "MenuKeybind"
end

function DirectSettings:SerializeObject(Idx, Object)
	if not Object or not Object.Type or self:ShouldIgnoreFlag(Idx) then
		return nil
	end

	if Object.Type == "Toggle" then
		return {type = "Toggle", idx = Idx, value = Object.Value}
	elseif Object.Type == "Slider" then
		return {type = "Slider", idx = Idx, value = Object.Value}
	elseif Object.Type == "Dropdown" then
		return {type = "Dropdown", idx = Idx, value = Object.Value, multi = Object.Multi}
	elseif Object.Type == "Input" then
		return {type = "Input", idx = Idx, value = Object.Value}
	elseif Object.Type == "ColorPicker" and typeof(Object.Value) == "Color3" then
		return {
			type = "ColorPicker",
			idx = Idx,
			value = Object.Value:ToHex(),
			transparency = Object.Transparency,
		}
	elseif Object.Type == "KeyPicker" then
		return {
			type = "KeyPicker",
			idx = Idx,
			key = Object.Value,
			mode = Object.Mode,
			modifiers = Object.Modifiers,
		}
	end
	return nil
end

function DirectSettings:SaveConfig(Name)
	Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	if Name == "" then
		return false, "invalid config name"
	end

	local foldersOk, folderError = self:EnsureFolders()
	if not foldersOk then
		return false, folderError
	end
	if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
		return false, "writefile unavailable"
	end

	local Data = {version = 1, objects = {}}
	for Idx, Toggle in pairs(Library.Toggles) do
		local Serialized = self:SerializeObject(Idx, Toggle)
		if Serialized then
			table.insert(Data.objects, Serialized)
		end
	end
	for Idx, Option in pairs(Library.Options) do
		local Serialized = self:SerializeObject(Idx, Option)
		if Serialized then
			table.insert(Data.objects, Serialized)
		end
	end

	local encodedOk, Encoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, Data)
	if not encodedOk then
		return false, "failed to encode config"
	end
	local writeOk, writeError = pcall(writefile, self.ConfigFolder .. "/" .. Name .. ".json", Encoded)
	return writeOk, writeOk and nil or tostring(writeError)
end

function DirectSettings:ApplyConfigObject(Data)
	local Object = Data.type == "Toggle" and Library.Toggles[Data.idx] or Library.Options[Data.idx]
	if not Object or self:ShouldIgnoreFlag(Data.idx) then
		return
	end

	if Data.type == "ColorPicker" and Data.value then
		Object:SetValueRGB(Color3.fromHex(Data.value), Data.transparency)
	elseif Data.type == "KeyPicker" then
		Object:SetValue({Data.key, Data.mode, Data.modifiers})
	elseif Data.type == "Input" then
		Object:SetValue(tostring(Data.value or ""))
	elseif Data.type == "Toggle" or Data.type == "Slider" or Data.type == "Dropdown" then
		Object:SetValue(Data.value)
	end
end

function DirectSettings:LoadConfig(Name)
	Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	local Path = self.ConfigFolder .. "/" .. Name .. ".json"
	if Name == "" or type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(Path) then
		return false, "config not found"
	end

	local readOk, Contents = pcall(readfile, Path)
	if not readOk then
		return false, "failed to read config"
	end
	local decodeOk, Data = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, Contents)
	if not decodeOk or type(Data) ~= "table" or type(Data.objects) ~= "table" then
		return false, "invalid config data"
	end

	table.sort(Data.objects, function(A, B)
		local Order = {Toggle = 1, Dropdown = 2, Slider = 3, Input = 4, ColorPicker = 5, KeyPicker = 6}
		return (Order[A.type] or 99) < (Order[B.type] or 99)
	end)
	for _, ObjectData in ipairs(Data.objects) do
		pcall(self.ApplyConfigObject, self, ObjectData)
	end
	return true
end

function DirectSettings:DeleteConfig(Name)
	Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	local Path = self.ConfigFolder .. "/" .. Name .. ".json"
	if Name == "" or type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(Path) then
		return false, "config not found"
	end
	if type(delfile) ~= "function" then
		return false, "delfile unavailable"
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(delfile, Path)
	return ok, ok and nil or tostring(err)
end

function DirectSettings:SetAutoload(Name)
	Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	if Name == "" or type(writefile) ~= "function" then
		return false, "invalid config"
	end
	self:EnsureFolders()
	local ok, err = pcall(writefile, self.AutoloadFile, Name)
	return ok, ok and nil or tostring(err)
end

function DirectSettings:ApplyTheme(Data, SyncControls)
	if type(Data) ~= "table" then
		return false
	end

	for _, Key in ipairs({"FontColor", "MainColor", "BackgroundColor", "AccentColor", "OutlineColor", "RiskColor"}) do
		local Value = Data[Key]
		if type(Value) == "string" then
			local ok, Color = pcall(Color3.fromHex, Value)
			if ok then
				Value = Color
			end
		end
		if typeof(Value) == "Color3" then
			Library[Key] = Value
		end
	end

	Library.AccentColorDark = Library:GetDarkerColor(Library.AccentColor)
	Library:UpdateColorsUsingRegistry()

	if Data.UIEffectsEnabled ~= nil then
		Library:SetUIEffectsEnabled(Data.UIEffectsEnabled)
	end
	if Data.UIEffectIntensity ~= nil then
		Library:SetUIEffectIntensity(Data.UIEffectIntensity)
	end
	if Data.MenuBlurEnabled ~= nil then
		Library:SetMenuBlurEnabled(Data.MenuBlurEnabled)
	end
	if Data.MenuBlurSize ~= nil then
		Library:SetMenuBlurSize(Data.MenuBlurSize)
	end
	if Data.AnimationTime ~= nil then
		Library.AnimationTime = math.clamp(tonumber(Data.AnimationTime) or 0.16, 0, 0.6)
	end
	if Data.UIImageScale ~= nil then
		Library:SetUIImageScale(Data.UIImageScale)
	end
	if Data.CornerRadius ~= nil then
		Library:SetCornerRadiusPixels(Data.CornerRadius)
	end
	if Data.NotificationTransparency ~= nil then
		Library.NotificationStyle.Transparency = math.clamp(tonumber(Data.NotificationTransparency) or 0.08, 0, 0.8)
	end
	if Data.NotificationWidth ~= nil then
		Library.NotificationStyle.Width = math.clamp(tonumber(Data.NotificationWidth) or 280, 220, 420)
	end
	if Data.NotificationPosition then
		Library:SetNotificationPosition(Data.NotificationPosition)
	end

	if SyncControls then
		local ColorFlags = {
			FontColor = "DirectThemeFontColor",
			MainColor = "DirectThemeMainColor",
			BackgroundColor = "DirectThemeBackgroundColor",
			AccentColor = "DirectThemeAccentColor",
			OutlineColor = "DirectThemeOutlineColor",
			RiskColor = "DirectThemeRiskColor",
		}
		for Key, Flag in pairs(ColorFlags) do
			if Library.Options[Flag] then
				Library.Options[Flag]:SetValueRGB(Library[Key])
			end
		end
		local ToggleFlags = {
			DirectThemeUIEffects = Library.UIEffectsEnabled,
			DirectThemeMenuBlur = Library.MenuBlurEnabled,
		}
		for Flag, Value in pairs(ToggleFlags) do
			if Library.Toggles[Flag] then
				Library.Toggles[Flag]:SetValue(Value)
			end
		end
		local OptionFlags = {
			DirectThemeEffectIntensity = Library.UIEffectIntensity,
			DirectThemeBlurSize = Library.MenuBlurSize,
			DirectThemeAnimationTime = Library.AnimationTime,
			DirectThemeUIImageScale = Library.UIImageScale,
			DirectThemeCornerRadius = Library.CornerRadius.Offset,
			DirectThemeNotifyTransparency = Library.NotificationStyle.Transparency,
			DirectThemeNotifyWidth = Library.NotificationStyle.Width,
			DirectThemeNotifyPosition = Library.NotificationPosition or "Top Left",
		}
		for Flag, Value in pairs(OptionFlags) do
			if Library.Options[Flag] then
				Library.Options[Flag]:SetValue(Value)
			end
		end
	end
	return true
end

function DirectSettings:GetCurrentThemeData()
	return {
		FontColor = Library.FontColor:ToHex(),
		MainColor = Library.MainColor:ToHex(),
		BackgroundColor = Library.BackgroundColor:ToHex(),
		AccentColor = Library.AccentColor:ToHex(),
		OutlineColor = Library.OutlineColor:ToHex(),
		RiskColor = Library.RiskColor:ToHex(),
		UIEffectsEnabled = Library.UIEffectsEnabled,
		UIEffectIntensity = Library.UIEffectIntensity,
		MenuBlurEnabled = Library.MenuBlurEnabled,
		MenuBlurSize = Library.MenuBlurSize,
		AnimationTime = Library.AnimationTime,
		UIImageScale = Library.UIImageScale,
		CornerRadius = Library.CornerRadius.Offset,
		NotificationTransparency = Library.NotificationStyle.Transparency,
		NotificationWidth = Library.NotificationStyle.Width,
		NotificationPosition = Library.NotificationPosition or "Top Left",
	}
end

function DirectSettings:SaveTheme(Name)
	Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	if Name == "" then
		return false, "invalid theme name"
	end
	local foldersOk, folderError = self:EnsureFolders()
	if not foldersOk then
		return false, folderError
	end
	if type(writefile) ~= "function" then
		return false, "writefile unavailable"
	end

	local encodeOk, Encoded = pcall(HttpService.JSONEncode, HttpService, self:GetCurrentThemeData())
	if not encodeOk then
		return false, "failed to encode theme"
	end
	local writeOk, writeError = pcall(writefile, self.ThemeFolder .. "/" .. Name .. ".json", Encoded)
	return writeOk, writeOk and nil or tostring(writeError)
end

function DirectSettings:LoadTheme(Name)
	Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	local Path = self.ThemeFolder .. "/" .. Name .. ".json"
	if Name == "" or type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(Path) then
		return false, "theme not found"
	end
	local readOk, Contents = pcall(readfile, Path)
	if not readOk then
		return false, "failed to read theme"
	end
	local decodeOk, Data = pcall(HttpService.JSONDecode, HttpService, Contents)
	if not decodeOk then
		return false, "invalid theme data"
	end
	return self:ApplyTheme(Data, true)
end

function DirectSettings:DeleteTheme(Name)
	Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(Name)
	local Path = self.ThemeFolder .. "/" .. Name .. ".json"
	if Name == "" or type(isfile) ~= "function" or not isfile(Path) then
		return false, "theme not found"
	end
	if type(delfile) ~= "function" then
		return false, "delfile unavailable"
	end
	local ok, err = pcall(delfile, Path)
	return ok, ok and nil or tostring(err)
end

function DirectSettings:LoadDefaults()
	if type(isfile) == "function" and type(readfile) == "function" then
		if isfile(self.DefaultThemeFile) then
			local ok, ThemeName = pcall(readfile, self.DefaultThemeFile)
			if ok then
				self:LoadTheme(ThemeName)
			end
		end
		if isfile(self.AutoloadFile) then
			local ok, ConfigName = pcall(readfile, self.AutoloadFile)
			if ok then
				self:LoadConfig(ConfigName)
			end
		end
	end
end

	table.insert(ThemePresetNames, Name)

	Callback = function(Value)
		local Preset = DirectThemePresets[Value]

			DirectSettings:ApplyTheme(Preset, true)

local function applyDirectThemeColor(Key, Value)
	if typeof(Value) == "Color3" then
		Library[Key] = Value
		if Key == "AccentColor" then
			Library.AccentColorDark = Library:GetDarkerColor(Value)
		end
		Library:UpdateColorsUsingRegistry()
	end
end

local ConfigGroup = Pages.Configs.Settings:AddRightGroupbox("Configs")

	Text = "Config Name",
	Default = "default",
	Placeholder = "Config name",
})

	Text = "Saved Configs",
	Values = DirectSettings:ListJsonFiles(DirectSettings.ConfigFolder),
	AllowNull = true,
	Default = 1,
	Search = true,
})

local function refreshDirectConfigList(SelectName)
	local Values = DirectSettings:ListJsonFiles(DirectSettings.ConfigFolder)
	ConfigListDropdown:SetValues(Values)
	if SelectName and table.find(Values, SelectName) then
		ConfigListDropdown:SetValue(SelectName)
	elseif #Values > 0 then
		ConfigListDropdown:SetValue(Values[1])
	else
		ConfigListDropdown:SetValue(nil)
	end
end

ConfigGroup:AddButton("Save Config", function()
	local Name = directSettingsSanitizeName(ConfigNameInput.Value)
	local ok, err = DirectSettings:SaveConfig(Name)
	notify("Config", ok and ("Saved " .. Name) or ("Failed: " .. tostring(err)))
	if ok then refreshDirectConfigList(Name) end
end)
ConfigGroup:AddButton("Load Config", function()
	local Name = ConfigListDropdown.Value
	local ok, err = DirectSettings:LoadConfig(Name)
	notify("Config", ok and ("Loaded " .. tostring(Name)) or ("Failed: " .. tostring(err)))
end)
ConfigGroup:AddButton("Delete Config", function()
	local Name = ConfigListDropdown.Value
	local ok, err = DirectSettings:DeleteConfig(Name)
	notify("Config", ok and ("Deleted " .. tostring(Name)) or ("Failed: " .. tostring(err)))
	if ok then refreshDirectConfigList() end
end)
ConfigGroup:AddButton("Refresh Configs", function()
	refreshDirectConfigList(ConfigListDropdown.Value)
end)
ConfigGroup:AddButton("Set Config Autoload", function()
	local Name = ConfigListDropdown.Value
	local ok, err = DirectSettings:SetAutoload(Name)
	notify("Config", ok and ("Autoload: " .. tostring(Name)) or ("Failed: " .. tostring(err)))
end)
ConfigGroup:AddButton("Clear Config Autoload", function()
	local ok = type(isfile) == "function"
		and type(delfile) == "function"
		and isfile(DirectSettings.AutoloadFile)
		and pcall(delfile, DirectSettings.AutoloadFile)
	notify("Config", ok and "Autoload cleared" or "No autoload config")
end)

function State.resolveTargetPart(character, mode, camera, screenCenter)
    if not character then
        return nil
    end

    mode = mode or "Head"

    if mode == "Closest" then
        camera = camera or workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            return nil
        end

        screenCenter = screenCenter or (camera.ViewportSize / 2)
        local closestPart = nil
        local closestDistance = math.huge

        for i = 1, #State.targetPartNames do
            local part = character:FindFirstChild(State.targetPartNames[i])
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                if onScreen then
                    local screenPosition = Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y)
                    local distance = (screenPosition - screenCenter).Magnitude
                    if distance < closestDistance then
                        closestDistance = distance
                        closestPart = part
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return closestPart
    end

    if mode == "Random" then
        local parts = {}
        for i = 1, #State.targetPartNames do
            local part = character:FindFirstChild(State.targetPartNames[i])
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                parts[#parts + 1] = part
            end
        end

        if #parts == 0 then
            return nil
        end

        return parts[math.random(1, #parts)]
    end

    local part = character:FindFirstChild(mode)
    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
        return part
    end

    return nil
end

function State.getCachedAimbotTargetPart(target, camera)
    local character = target and target.Character
    local cachedPart = State.cachedAimbotTargetPart

    if State.cachedAimbotTarget == target
        and State.cachedAimbotPartMode == State.lockPart
        and character
        and cachedPart
        and cachedPart:IsDescendantOf(character) then
        return cachedPart
    end

    return State.resolveTargetPart(character, State.lockPart, camera)
end

function State.getCachedSilentTargetPart(target, camera)
    local character = target and target.Character
    local cachedPart = State.cachedSilentTargetPart

    if State.cachedSilentTarget == target
        and State.cachedSilentPartMode == State.SilentAimState.LockPart
        and character
        and cachedPart
        and cachedPart:IsDescendantOf(character) then
        return cachedPart
    end

    return State.resolveTargetPart(character, State.SilentAimState.LockPart, camera)
end

function State.getClosest(force)
    local now = tick()
    local interval = State.getAdaptiveFrameInterval and State.getAdaptiveFrameInterval(State.aimbotTargetInterval, 1) or (1 / 60)
    if not force and State.cachedAimbotPartMode == State.lockPart and State.lastAimbotTargetScan and now - State.lastAimbotTargetScan < interval then
        return State.cachedAimbotTarget
    end
    State.lastAimbotTargetScan = now
    State.cachedAimbotPartMode = State.lockPart

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then
        State.cachedAimbotTarget = nil
        State.cachedAimbotTargetPart = nil
        return nil
    end

    local closestTarget = nil
    local closestTargetPart = nil
    local closestDistance = math.huge
    local screenCenter = State.getFOVCenter(camera, State.fovPositionMode, State.currentTarget or State.cachedAimbotTarget, State.cachedAimbotTargetPart, "Aimbot")
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local localCharacter = localPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not localRoot then
        State.cachedAimbotTarget = nil
        State.cachedAimbotTargetPart = nil
        return nil
    end

    local maxDistance = State.aimbotMaxDistance or math.huge

    local players = Players:GetPlayers()
    for i = 1, #players do
        local player = players[i]
        if player ~= localPlayer then
            local character = player.Character
            if character and not character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0.09 then
                    if not State.teamCheckEnabled or not Core.isSameTeam(player) then
                        local part = State.resolveTargetPart(character, State.lockPart, camera, screenCenter)
                        local targetRoot = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if part and targetRoot then
                            local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                            if onScreen then
                                local screenPos = Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y)
                                local distFromCenter = (screenPos - screenCenter).Magnitude
                                if not State.fovCircleEnabled or distFromCenter <= State.fov then
                                    if distFromCenter < closestDistance then
                                        local distance = (targetRoot.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude
                                        if distance <= maxDistance then
                                            if not State.wallCheckEnabled or Core.isTargetVisible(part) then
                                                closestDistance = distFromCenter
                                                closestTarget = player
                                                closestTargetPart = part
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    State.cachedAimbotTarget = closestTarget
    State.cachedAimbotTargetPart = closestTargetPart
    return closestTarget
end

function State.getAimbotPredictedPosition(target, aimPart)
    local aimPos = aimPart and aimPart.Position
    if not aimPos or not State.aimbotPredictionEnabled then
        return aimPos
    end

    local predictionTime = math.clamp(tonumber(State.aimbotPredictionTime) or 0, 0, 0.5)
    if predictionTime <= 0 then
        return aimPos
    end

    local velocity = nil
    local character = target and target.Character
    local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local velocityParts = {}
    if root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
        velocityParts[#velocityParts + 1] = root
    end
    if aimPart and aimPart:IsA("BasePart") and aimPart ~= root then
        velocityParts[#velocityParts + 1] = aimPart
    end

    for _, part in ipairs(velocityParts) do
        local ok, partVelocity = pcall(function()
            return part.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        end)
        if ok and typeof(partVelocity) == "Vector3" then
            velocity = partVelocity
            break
        end

        ok, partVelocity = pcall(function()
            return part.Velocity
        end)
        if ok and typeof(partVelocity) == "Vector3" then
            velocity = partVelocity
            break
        end
    end

    if not velocity then
        return aimPos
    end

    return aimPos + velocity * predictionTime
end

function State.lockOntoTarget(target)
    if not target or not target.Character then
        State.targetLocked = false
        return
    end

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local aimPart = State.getCachedAimbotTargetPart(target, camera)
    if not aimPart then
        State.targetLocked = false
        return
    end

    local aimPos = State.getAimbotPredictedPosition(target, aimPart)
    local currentScreenPos = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPos)

    if not State.targetLocked or State.currentTarget ~= target then
        State.smoothedTargetPos = Vector2.new(currentScreenPos.X, currentScreenPos.Y)
        State.targetLocked = true
    else
        State.smoothedTargetPos = State.smoothedTargetPos:Lerp(
            Vector2.new(currentScreenPos.X, currentScreenPos.Y), 0.15
        )
    end

    local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    local delta = State.smoothedTargetPos - mousePos
    local moveFraction = math.clamp(State.smoothnessValue, 0.05, 1.0)

    if delta.Magnitude > 1 then
        mousemoverel(delta.X * moveFraction, delta.Y * moveFraction)
    end
end

function State.getClosestSilent(force)
    local now = tick()
    local interval = State.silentTargetInterval or 0.05
    if not force and State.cachedSilentPartMode == State.SilentAimState.LockPart and State.lastSilentTargetScan and now - State.lastSilentTargetScan < interval then
        return State.cachedSilentTarget
    end
    State.lastSilentTargetScan = now
    State.cachedSilentPartMode = State.SilentAimState.LockPart

    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then
        State.cachedSilentTarget = nil
        State.cachedSilentTargetPart = nil
        return nil
    end
    local screenCenter = State.getFOVCenter(camera, State.SilentAimState.FOVPositionMode, State.cachedSilentTarget, State.cachedSilentTargetPart, "Silent")
    local closestTarget = nil
    local closestTargetPart = nil
    local closestDistance = State.SilentAimState.FOVEnabled and State.SilentAimState.FOV or math.huge
    local maxDistance = 400
    local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("Head") then
        State.cachedSilentTarget = nil
        State.cachedSilentTargetPart = nil
        return nil
    end
    local myHead = char.Head.Position
    local params = State.silentRaycastParams or RaycastParams.new()
    State.silentRaycastParams = params
    params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char}
    params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
    params.IgnoreWater = true

    local players = Players:GetPlayers()
    for i = 1, #players do
        local player = players[i]
        if player ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
            local character = player.Character
            if character and not character:FindFirstChild("ForceField") then
                local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if humanoid and humanoid.Health > 0 and humanoid:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
                    if not State.SilentAimState.TeamCheck or not Core.isSameTeam(player) then
                        local targetPart = State.resolveTargetPart(character, State.SilentAimState.LockPart, camera, screenCenter)
                        local hrp = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp and targetPart then
                            local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPart.Position)
                            if onScreen then
                                local screenVector = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
                                local distFromCenter = (screenVector - screenCenter).Magnitude
                                if not State.SilentAimState.FOVEnabled or distFromCenter <= State.SilentAimState.FOV then
                                    if distFromCenter <= maxDistance then
                                        local direction = (targetPart.Position - myHead).Unit
                                        local distance = (targetPart.Position - myHead).Magnitude
                                        local result = rawWorkspaceRaycast(myHead, direction * distance, params)
                                        local isVisible = (result == nil) or (result.Instance and result.Instance:IsDescendantOf(character))
                                        if isVisible and distFromCenter < closestDistance then
                                            closestDistance = distFromCenter
                                            closestTarget = player
                                            closestTargetPart = targetPart
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    State.cachedSilentTarget = closestTarget
    State.cachedSilentTargetPart = closestTargetPart
    return closestTarget
end

function State.initSilentAimHook()
    local retryConnection = nil
    local function getKeyCode(keyName)
        if typeof(keyName) ~= "string" then return nil end
        return Enum.KeyCode[keyName] or Enum.KeyCode[keyName:gsub("%s+", "")]
    end

    local function isSilentAimActive()
        if not State.SilentAimState.Enabled then
            return false
        end
