        if Options.SilentAimKeybind and typeof(Options.SilentAimKeybind.GetState) == "function" then
            return Options.SilentAimKeybind:GetState()
        end

        local keyName = State.SilentAimState.Keybind or State.silentAimKeybind
        local keyCode = getKeyCode(keyName)
        if keyCode then
            return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(keyCode)
        end
        return true
    end

    local function passHitChance()
        local chance = State.SilentAimState.HitChance or 100
        if chance <= 0 then return false end
        if chance >= 100 then return true end
        return math.random(0, 100) <= chance
    end

    local function getSilentTarget()
        if not isSilentAimActive() then
            return nil
        end
        local target = State.getClosestSilent()
        if target and target.Character then
            if passHitChance() then
                return target
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function tryHookUtility()
        local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

        local modules = rs:FindFirstChild("Modules")
        if not modules then return false end
        local utilModule = modules:FindFirstChild("Utility")
        if not utilModule then return false end
        local ok, util = pcall(require, utilModule)
        if not ok or type(util) ~= "table" or typeof(util.Raycast) ~= "function" then
            return false
        end

        local function closest()
            local target = State.getClosestSilent()
            return target, target and target.Character or nil
        end
        local old_ray = util.Raycast
        util.Raycast = function(s, o, d, len, f, ft, viz)
            local flickPart = State.getFlickbotFireTargetPart and State.getFlickbotFireTargetPart()
            if flickPart and len == 999 and len > 100 and f then
                local hitpos = flickPart.Position
                return {
                    Position = hitpos,
                    Distance = o and (hitpos - o).Magnitude or 0,
                    Instance = flickPart,
                    Material = flickPart.Material,
                    Normal = Vector3.yAxis
                }
            end

            if State.flickbotBusy then
                return old_ray(s, o, d, len, f, ft, viz)
            end

            if State.SilentAimState.Enabled and len == 999 and len > 100 and f then
                local target, tgt = closest()
                local hitPart = State.getCachedSilentTargetPart(target, workspace.CurrentCamera)
                if tgt and hitPart then
                    local hitpos = hitPart.Position
                    return {
                        Position = hitpos,
                        Distance = (hitpos - o).Magnitude,
                        Instance = hitPart,
                        Material = hitPart.Material,
                        Normal = Vector3.yAxis
                    }
                end
            end
            return old_ray(s, o, d, len, f, ft, viz)
        end

        return true
    end

    local function tryHookWorkspaceRaycast()
        if State.workspaceRaycastHooked then
            return true
        end

        local oldRaycast = State.originalWorkspaceRaycast or workspace.Raycast
        if typeof(oldRaycast) ~= "function" then
            return false
        end

        State.originalWorkspaceRaycast = oldRaycast
        workspace.Raycast = function(self, origin, direction, params)
            local flickPart = State.getFlickbotFireTargetPart and State.getFlickbotFireTargetPart()
            if flickPart then
                return {
                    Instance = flickPart,
                    Position = flickPart.Position,
                    Distance = origin and (flickPart.Position - origin).Magnitude or 0,
                    Material = flickPart.Material,
                    Normal = Vector3.yAxis,
                }
            end

            if State.flickbotBusy then
                return oldRaycast(self, origin, direction, params)
            end

            local target = getSilentTarget()
            if target and target.Character then
                local hitPart = State.getCachedSilentTargetPart(target, workspace.CurrentCamera)
                if hitPart then
                    return {
                        Instance = hitPart,
                        Position = hitPart.Position,
                        Distance = (hitPart.Position - origin).Magnitude,
                        Material = hitPart.Material,
                        Normal = Vector3.yAxis,
                    }
                end
            end
            return oldRaycast(self, origin, direction, params)
        end

        State.workspaceRaycastHooked = true
        return true
    end

    local lastRetry = 0
    retryConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastRetry < 0.25 then return end
        lastRetry = now

        if tryHookUtility() or tryHookWorkspaceRaycast() then
            if retryConnection then
                retryConnection:Disconnect()
                retryConnection = nil
            end
        end
    end)
end

State.initSilentAimHook()

local ESPUtil = {}

function ESPUtil.createDrawing(typeName)
    local success, result = pcall(function() return Drawing.new(typeName) end)
    if success then
        if result and typeName == "Text" and Library and Library.RegisterFontChangerDrawingText then
            Library:RegisterFontChangerDrawingText(result)
        end
        return result
    end
    return nil
end

function ESPUtil.sanitizePlayerKey(player)
    return player and player.UserId and tostring(player.UserId) or tostring(player)
end

function ESPUtil.selectionHas(selection, value)
    if type(selection) == "table" then
        return selection[value] == true or table.find(selection, value) ~= nil
    end

    return selection == value
end

function ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(obj)
    if obj and type(obj.Remove) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            obj:Remove()
        end)
    end
end

function ESPUtil.destroyInstance(obj)
    if obj and typeof(obj) == "Instance" then
        pcall(function()
            obj:Destroy()
        end)
    end
end

function ESPUtil.normalizeImageSource(value)
    local source = tostring(value or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")
    if source == "" then
        return ""
    end

    if source:find("^rbxassetid://") or source:find("^rbxasset://") or source:find("^rbxthumb://") or source:find("^https?://") then
        return source
    end

    local assetId = source:match("^%d+$") or source:match("id=(%d+)")
    if assetId then
        return "rbxassetid://" .. assetId
    end

    if type(getcustomasset) == "function" then
        local ok, customAsset = pcall(getcustomasset, source)
        if ok and type(customAsset) == "string" and customAsset ~= "" then
            return customAsset
        end
    end

    return source
end

function ESPUtil.getBoxImageSource()
    local rawSource = tostring(State.espBoxImageSource or "")
    if State.espBoxImageSourceCache == rawSource then
        return State.espBoxImageResolved or ""
    end

    local resolved = ESPUtil.normalizeImageSource(rawSource)
    State.espBoxImageSourceCache = rawSource
    State.espBoxImageResolved = resolved
    return resolved
end

function ESPUtil.getImageGui()
    if State.espImageGui and State.espImageGui.Parent then
        return State.espImageGui
    end

    local parent = nil
    pcall(function()
        parent = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end)

    if not parent then
        return nil
    end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "PhantomRivalsESPImageGui"
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
    gui.DisplayOrder = 999
    gui.Parent = parent

    State.espImageGui = gui
    return gui
end

function ESPUtil.ensureImageLabel(entry)
    if entry.boxImageLabel and entry.boxImageLabel.Parent then
        return entry.boxImageLabel
    end

    local gui = ESPUtil.getImageGui()
    if not gui then
        return nil
    end

    local label = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    label.Name = "BoxImage"
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    label.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    label.Visible = false
    label.ZIndex = 8
    label.Parent = gui

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Name = "Outline"
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
    stroke.Enabled = State.espBoxOutlineEnabled
    stroke.Parent = label

    entry.boxImageLabel = label
    entry.boxImageStroke = stroke
    return label
end

function ESPUtil.removeESPEntry(entry)
    if not entry then return end
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.box)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.boxOutline)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.boxFill)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.highlight)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.name)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.distance)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.charm)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.weapon)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.health)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.healthBackground)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.snapline)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.snaplineOutline)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.headDot)
    ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(entry.headDotOutline)
    ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.boxImageLabel)
    ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.glowCharm)
    if entry.lines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.lines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
    if entry.lineOutlines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.lineOutlines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
    if entry.skeletonLines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.skeletonLines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
    if entry.skeletonOutlineLines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.skeletonOutlineLines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
    if entry.cornerLines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.cornerLines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
    if entry.cornerOutlineLines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.cornerOutlineLines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
    if entry.arrowLines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.arrowLines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
    if entry.arrowOutlineLines then
        for _, line in ipairs(entry.arrowOutlineLines) do
            ESPUtil.removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.removeESPForPlayer(playerKey)
    local entry = State.espData[playerKey]
    if not entry then return end
    ESPUtil.removeESPEntry(entry)
    State.espData[playerKey] = nil
end

function ESPUtil.clearESPEntries(data)
    for key, entry in pairs(data) do
        ESPUtil.removeESPEntry(entry)
        data[key] = nil
    end
end

function State.clearESP()
    for playerKey, _ in pairs(State.espData) do
        ESPUtil.removeESPForPlayer(playerKey)
    end
    State.espData = {}
    State.characterBoundsCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    if State.espImageGui then
        ESPUtil.destroyInstance(State.espImageGui)
        State.espImageGui = nil
    end
end

function State.clearWorldESP()
    if State.worldESPData then
        ESPUtil.clearESPEntries(State.worldESPData)
    end
    State.worldESPPartCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
    State.worldESPActiveKeys = nil
end

function State.destroyESPPreview()
    ESPUtil.destroyInstance(State.espPreviewGui)
    State.espPreviewGui = nil
    State.espPreviewObjects = nil
end

function ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(parent, name, zIndex)
    local line = Instance.new("Frame")
    line.Name = name
    line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    line.BorderSizePixel = 0
    line.Visible = false
    line.ZIndex = zIndex or 55
    line.Parent = parent
    return line
end

function ESPUtil.setPreviewLine(line, x1, y1, x2, y2, color, visible, thickness)
    if not line then return end
    line.Visible = visible == true
    if not line.Visible then return end

    local dx = x2 - x1
    local dy = y2 - y1
    local length = math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)
    local angle = 0

    if math.abs(dx) < 0.001 then
        angle = dy >= 0 and math.pi / 2 or -math.pi / 2
    else
        angle = math.atan(dy / dx)
        if dx < 0 then
            angle = angle + math.pi
        end
    end

    line.BackgroundColor3 = color or Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((x1 + x2) / 2, (y1 + y2) / 2)
    line.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(length, 1), math.max(thickness or 1, 1))
    line.Rotation = math.deg(angle)
end

function ESPUtil.createPreviewBox(parent, name, zIndex)
    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Name = name
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Visible = false
    frame.ZIndex = zIndex or 52
    frame.Parent = parent

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    stroke.Parent = frame

    return frame, stroke
end

function ESPUtil.createPreviewLabel(parent, name, text, textSize, zIndex)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = name
    label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.BorderSizePixel = 0
    label.Font = Enum.Font.Code
    label.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(130, 18)
    label.Text = text or ""
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    label.TextSize = textSize or 13
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
    label.Visible = false
    label.ZIndex = zIndex or 60
    label.Parent = parent
    return label
end

function ESPUtil.ensureESPPreview()
    if State.espPreviewGui and State.espPreviewGui.Parent and State.espPreviewObjects then
        return State.espPreviewObjects
    end

    State.destroyESPPreview()

    local parent = nil
    pcall(function()
        parent = gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    end)

    if not parent and Players.LocalPlayer then
        parent = Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
    end

    if not parent then
        return nil
    end

    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "PhantomRivalsESPPreviewGui"
    gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Global
    gui.DisplayOrder = 1000
    gui.Parent = parent

    local root = Instance.new("Frame")
    root.Name = "Preview"
    root.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    root.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 12, 18)
    root.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
    root.BorderSizePixel = 0
    root.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, 0)
    root.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(190, 270)
    root.ZIndex = 45
    root.Parent = gui

    local rootCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    rootCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
    rootCorner.Parent = root

    local rootStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    rootStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(58, 65, 82)
    rootStroke.Thickness = 1
    rootStroke.Transparency = 0.15
    rootStroke.Parent = root

    local title = ESPUtil.createPreviewLabel(root, "Title", "ESP Preview", 13, 60)
    title.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(95, 8)
    title.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 18)
    title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 225, 235)
    title.Visible = true

    local canvas = Instance.new("Frame")
    canvas.Name = "Canvas"
    canvas.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    canvas.BorderSizePixel = 0
    canvas.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 34)
    canvas.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 226)
    canvas.ZIndex = 46
    canvas.Parent = root

    local baseBody = Instance.new("Frame")
    baseBody.Name = "BaseBody"
    baseBody.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 45, 58)
    baseBody.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    baseBody.BorderSizePixel = 0
    baseBody.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(73, 82)
    baseBody.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(24, 70)
    baseBody.ZIndex = 47
    baseBody.Parent = canvas

    local baseBodyCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    baseBodyCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    baseBodyCorner.Parent = baseBody

    local baseHead = Instance.new("Frame")
    baseHead.Name = "BaseHead"
    baseHead.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(48, 54, 69)
    baseHead.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
    baseHead.BorderSizePixel = 0
    baseHead.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(74, 52)
    baseHead.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 22)
    baseHead.ZIndex = 47
    baseHead.Parent = canvas

    local baseHeadCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    baseHeadCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    baseHeadCorner.Parent = baseHead

    local boxFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    boxFill.Name = "BoxFill"
    boxFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    boxFill.Visible = false
    boxFill.ZIndex = 48
    boxFill.Parent = canvas

    local boxOutline, boxOutlineStroke = ESPUtil.createPreviewBox(canvas, "BoxOutline", 49)
    local box, boxStroke = ESPUtil.createPreviewBox(canvas, "Box", 50)

    local boxImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    boxImage.Name = "BoxImage"
    boxImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    boxImage.BorderSizePixel = 0
    boxImage.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    boxImage.Visible = false
    boxImage.ZIndex = 50
    boxImage.Parent = canvas

    local boxImageStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    boxImageStroke.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
    boxImageStroke.Thickness = 1
    boxImageStroke.Parent = boxImage

    local glowOutline, glowOutlineStroke = ESPUtil.createPreviewBox(canvas, "GlowOutline", 51)
    local glow, glowStroke = ESPUtil.createPreviewBox(canvas, "Glow", 52)
    glow.BackgroundColor3 = State.espGlowCharmColor
    glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.9

    local healthBackground = Instance.new("Frame")
    healthBackground.Name = "HealthBackground"
    healthBackground.BorderSizePixel = 0
    healthBackground.Visible = false
    healthBackground.ZIndex = 53
    healthBackground.Parent = canvas

    local healthBar = Instance.new("Frame")
    healthBar.Name = "HealthBar"
    healthBar.BorderSizePixel = 0
    healthBar.Visible = false
    healthBar.ZIndex = 54
    healthBar.Parent = canvas

    local nameLabel = ESPUtil.createPreviewLabel(canvas, "Name", "Enemy", 13, 60)
    local distanceLabel = ESPUtil.createPreviewLabel(canvas, "Distance", "42m", 12, 60)
    local weaponLabel = ESPUtil.createPreviewLabel(canvas, "Weapon", "Assault Rifle", 12, 60)
    local charmLabel = ESPUtil.createPreviewLabel(canvas, "Charm", "*", 16, 61)

    local headDotOutline = Instance.new("Frame")
    headDotOutline.Name = "HeadDotOutline"
    headDotOutline.BorderSizePixel = 0
    headDotOutline.Visible = false
    headDotOutline.ZIndex = 58
    headDotOutline.Parent = canvas

    local headDotOutlineCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    headDotOutlineCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    headDotOutlineCorner.Parent = headDotOutline

    local headDot = Instance.new("Frame")
    headDot.Name = "HeadDot"
    headDot.BorderSizePixel = 0
    headDot.Visible = false
    headDot.ZIndex = 59
    headDot.Parent = canvas

    local headDotCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
    headDotCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    headDotCorner.Parent = headDot

    local objects = {
        root = root,
        canvas = canvas,
        boxFill = boxFill,
        boxOutline = boxOutline,
        boxOutlineStroke = boxOutlineStroke,
        box = box,
        boxStroke = boxStroke,
        boxImage = boxImage,
        boxImageStroke = boxImageStroke,
        glowOutline = glowOutline,
        glowOutlineStroke = glowOutlineStroke,
        glow = glow,
        glowStroke = glowStroke,
        healthBackground = healthBackground,
        healthBar = healthBar,
        nameLabel = nameLabel,
        distanceLabel = distanceLabel,
        weaponLabel = weaponLabel,
        charmLabel = charmLabel,
        headDotOutline = headDotOutline,
        headDot = headDot,
        snapline = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "Snapline", 55),
        snaplineOutline = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "SnaplineOutline", 54),
        cornerLines = {},
        cornerOutlineLines = {},
        box3DLines = {},
        box3DOutlineLines = {},
        skeletonLines = {},
        skeletonOutlineLines = {},
        arrowLines = {},
        arrowOutlineLines = {},
    }

    for index = 1, 8 do
        objects.cornerOutlineLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "CornerOutline" .. index, 52)
        objects.cornerLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "Corner" .. index, 53)
    end

    for index = 1, 12 do
        objects.box3DOutlineLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "Box3DOutline" .. index, 52)
        objects.box3DLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "Box3D" .. index, 53)
    end

    for index = 1, 6 do
        objects.skeletonOutlineLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "SkeletonOutline" .. index, 56)
        objects.skeletonLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "Skeleton" .. index, 57)
    end

    for index = 1, 3 do
        objects.arrowOutlineLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "ArrowOutline" .. index, 56)
        objects.arrowLines[index] = ESPUtil.createPreviewLine(canvas, "Arrow" .. index, 57)
    end

    State.espPreviewGui = gui
    State.espPreviewObjects = objects
    return objects
end

function ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(lines, segments, color, visible, thickness)
    for index, line in ipairs(lines or {}) do
        local segment = segments[index]
        if segment then
            ESPUtil.setPreviewLine(line, segment[1], segment[2], segment[3], segment[4], color, visible, thickness)
        else
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

function State.updateESPPreview()
    if not State.espPreviewEnabled then
        State.destroyESPPreview()
        return
    end

    local objects = ESPUtil.ensureESPPreview()
    if not objects then return end

    local boxX = 57
    local boxY = 46
    local boxW = 56
    local boxH = 132
    local headX = boxX + boxW / 2
    local headY = boxY + 16
    local mode = State.espBoxMode or "2D"
    local boxVisible = State.espBoxEnabled == true

    objects.box.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX, boxY)
    objects.box.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(boxW, boxH)
    objects.box.Visible = boxVisible and mode == "2D"
    objects.boxStroke.Color = State.espBoxColor
    objects.boxStroke.Thickness = 1

    objects.boxOutline.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - 1, boxY - 1)
    objects.boxOutline.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(boxW + 2, boxH + 2)
    objects.boxOutline.Visible = boxVisible and mode == "2D" and State.espBoxOutlineEnabled
    objects.boxOutlineStroke.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
    objects.boxOutlineStroke.Thickness = 3

    objects.boxFill.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX, boxY)
    objects.boxFill.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(boxW, boxH)
    objects.boxFill.BackgroundColor3 = State.espBoxFillColor
    objects.boxFill.BackgroundTransparency = math.clamp(1 - (State.espBoxFillTransparency or 0.5), 0, 1)
    objects.boxFill.Visible = boxVisible and mode == "2D" and State.espBoxFillEnabled

    local imageSource = ESPUtil.getBoxImageSource()
    objects.boxImage.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX, boxY)
    objects.boxImage.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(boxW, boxH)
    objects.boxImage.Image = imageSource
    objects.boxImage.ImageTransparency = math.clamp(State.espBoxImageTransparency or 0, 0, 1)
    objects.boxImage.Visible = boxVisible and mode == "Image" and imageSource ~= ""
    objects.boxImageStroke.Enabled = objects.boxImage.Visible and State.espBoxOutlineEnabled
    objects.boxImageStroke.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor

    local cornerLength = 15
    local cornerSegments = {
        {boxX, boxY, boxX + cornerLength, boxY},
        {boxX, boxY, boxX, boxY + cornerLength},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY, boxX + boxW - cornerLength, boxY},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY, boxX + boxW, boxY + cornerLength},
        {boxX, boxY + boxH, boxX + cornerLength, boxY + boxH},
        {boxX, boxY + boxH, boxX, boxY + boxH - cornerLength},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY + boxH, boxX + boxW - cornerLength, boxY + boxH},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY + boxH, boxX + boxW, boxY + boxH - cornerLength},
    }
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.cornerOutlineLines, cornerSegments, State.espBoxOutlineColor, boxVisible and mode == "Corner" and State.espBoxOutlineEnabled, 4)
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.cornerLines, cornerSegments, State.espBoxColor, boxVisible and mode == "Corner", 2)

    local offset = 10
    local backX = boxX + offset
    local backY = boxY - offset
    local box3DSegments = {
        {boxX, boxY, boxX + boxW, boxY},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY, boxX + boxW, boxY + boxH},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY + boxH, boxX, boxY + boxH},
        {boxX, boxY + boxH, boxX, boxY},
        {backX, backY, backX + boxW, backY},
        {backX + boxW, backY, backX + boxW, backY + boxH},
        {backX + boxW, backY + boxH, backX, backY + boxH},
        {backX, backY + boxH, backX, backY},
        {boxX, boxY, backX, backY},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY, backX + boxW, backY},
        {boxX + boxW, boxY + boxH, backX + boxW, backY + boxH},
        {boxX, boxY + boxH, backX, backY + boxH},
    }
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.box3DOutlineLines, box3DSegments, State.espBoxOutlineColor, boxVisible and mode == "3D" and State.espBoxOutlineEnabled, 4)
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.box3DLines, box3DSegments, State.esp3DColor, boxVisible and mode == "3D", 2)

    objects.nameLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(headX, boxY - 20)
    objects.nameLabel.Text = "Enemy"
    objects.nameLabel.TextColor3 = State.espNameColor
    objects.nameLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = State.espNameOutlineColor
    objects.nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = State.espNameOutlineEnabled and 0 or 1
    objects.nameLabel.Visible = State.espNameEnabled

    objects.weaponLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(headX, boxY - 36)
    objects.weaponLabel.Text = "Assault Rifle"
    objects.weaponLabel.TextColor3 = State.espWeaponColor
    objects.weaponLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = State.espWeaponOutlineColor
    objects.weaponLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = State.espWeaponOutlineEnabled and 0 or 1
    objects.weaponLabel.Visible = State.espWeaponEnabled

    objects.distanceLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(headX, boxY + boxH + 3)
    objects.distanceLabel.Text = "42m"
    objects.distanceLabel.TextColor3 = State.espDistanceColor
    objects.distanceLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = State.espDistanceOutlineColor
    objects.distanceLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = State.espDistanceOutlineEnabled and 0 or 1
    objects.distanceLabel.Visible = State.espDistanceEnabled

    objects.charmLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(headX, headY - 22)
    objects.charmLabel.TextColor3 = State.espCharmColor
    objects.charmLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = State.espCharmOutlineColor
    objects.charmLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = State.espCharmOutlineEnabled and 0 or 1
    objects.charmLabel.Visible = State.espCharmEnabled

    local healthRatio = 0.72
    local healthHeight = math.max(1, boxH * healthRatio)
    local healthColor = State.espHealthDynamicColor and ESPUtil.getHealthColor(72, 100) or State.espHealthColor
    objects.healthBackground.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - 10, boxY)
    objects.healthBackground.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(5, boxH)
    objects.healthBackground.BackgroundColor3 = State.espHealthOutlineColor
    objects.healthBackground.Visible = State.espHealthEnabled and State.espHealthOutlineEnabled
    objects.healthBar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - 9, boxY + boxH - healthHeight)
    objects.healthBar.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(3, healthHeight)
    objects.healthBar.BackgroundColor3 = healthColor
    objects.healthBar.Visible = State.espHealthEnabled

    ESPUtil.setPreviewLine(objects.snaplineOutline, 85, 226, headX, boxY + boxH, State.espLineOutlineColor, State.espLineEnabled and State.espLineOutlineEnabled, 4)
    ESPUtil.setPreviewLine(objects.snapline, 85, 226, headX, boxY + boxH, State.espLineColor, State.espLineEnabled, 2)

    local radius = math.clamp(State.espHeadDotRadius or 4, 2, 12)
    objects.headDotOutline.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(headX - radius - 2, headY - radius - 2)
    objects.headDotOutline.Size = UDim2.fromOffset((radius + 2) * 2, (radius + 2) * 2)
    objects.headDotOutline.BackgroundColor3 = State.espHeadDotOutlineColor
    objects.headDotOutline.Visible = State.espHeadDotEnabled and State.espHeadDotOutlineEnabled
    objects.headDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(headX - radius, headY - radius)
    objects.headDot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(radius * 2, radius * 2)
    objects.headDot.BackgroundColor3 = State.espHeadDotColor
    objects.headDot.Visible = State.espHeadDotEnabled

    local skeletonSegments = {
        {headX, headY + 10, headX, boxY + 58},
        {headX, boxY + 58, headX, boxY + 108},
        {headX, boxY + 66, boxX + 8, boxY + 92},
        {headX, boxY + 66, boxX + boxW - 8, boxY + 92},
        {headX, boxY + 108, boxX + 12, boxY + boxH - 4},
        {headX, boxY + 108, boxX + boxW - 12, boxY + boxH - 4},
    }
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.skeletonOutlineLines, skeletonSegments, State.espSkeletonOutlineColor, State.espSkeletonEnabled and State.espSkeletonOutlineEnabled, 4)
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.skeletonLines, skeletonSegments, State.espSkeletonColor, State.espSkeletonEnabled, 2)

    local arrowSize = math.clamp(State.espArrowSize or 16, 8, 40)
    local arrowX = 145
    local arrowY = 40
    local arrowSegments = {
        {arrowX, arrowY - arrowSize, arrowX - arrowSize * 0.75, arrowY + arrowSize * 0.6},
        {arrowX - arrowSize * 0.75, arrowY + arrowSize * 0.6, arrowX + arrowSize * 0.75, arrowY + arrowSize * 0.6},
        {arrowX + arrowSize * 0.75, arrowY + arrowSize * 0.6, arrowX, arrowY - arrowSize},
    }
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.arrowOutlineLines, arrowSegments, State.espArrowOutlineColor, State.espArrowEnabled and State.espArrowOutlineEnabled, 5)
    ESPUtil.updatePreviewSegments(objects.arrowLines, arrowSegments, State.espArrowColor, State.espArrowEnabled, 2)

    objects.glowOutline.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - 6, boxY - 6)
    objects.glowOutline.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(boxW + 12, boxH + 12)
    objects.glowOutline.Visible = State.espGlowCharmEnabled and State.espGlowCharmOutlineEnabled
    objects.glowOutlineStroke.Color = State.espGlowCharmOutlineColor
    objects.glowOutlineStroke.Thickness = 4
    objects.glow.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxX - 4, boxY - 4)
    objects.glow.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(boxW + 8, boxH + 8)
    objects.glow.BackgroundColor3 = State.espGlowCharmColor
    objects.glow.Visible = State.espGlowCharmEnabled
    objects.glowStroke.Color = State.espGlowCharmColor
    objects.glowStroke.Thickness = 2
end

function State.removeAuraEntry(entry)
    if not entry then return end
    ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.highlight)
    ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.selectionBox)
    ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.pointLight)
    ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.pulse)
end

function State.clearAuraESP()
    for key, entry in pairs(State.auraObjects or {}) do
        State.removeAuraEntry(entry)
        State.auraObjects[key] = nil
    end
end

function State.shouldAuraTargetPlayer(player)
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not player or not localPlayer then
        return false
    end

    if player == localPlayer then
        return ESPUtil.selectionHas(State.auraTargets, "Self")
    end

    if not ESPUtil.selectionHas(State.auraTargets, "Others") then
        return false
    end

    if not Core.isSameMatch(player, player.Character, localPlayer, localPlayer.Character) then
        return false
    end

    if State.auraTeamCheckEnabled and Core.isSameTeam(player) then
        return false
    end

    return true
end

function State.updateAuraForPlayer(player)
    local char = player and player.Character
    local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local key = ESPUtil.sanitizePlayerKey(player)

    if not key or not char or not root or not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 or not State.shouldAuraTargetPlayer(player) then
        local oldEntry = State.auraObjects[key]
        if oldEntry then
            State.removeAuraEntry(oldEntry)
            State.auraObjects[key] = nil
        end
        ESPUtil.removePlayerAura(char)
        return false
    end

    local entry = State.auraObjects[key] or {}
    State.auraObjects[key] = entry

    local wantsHighlight = ESPUtil.selectionHas(State.auraTypes, "Highlight")
    local wantsSelectionBox = ESPUtil.selectionHas(State.auraTypes, "Selection Box")
    local wantsPointLight = ESPUtil.selectionHas(State.auraTypes, "Point Light")
    local wantsPulse = ESPUtil.selectionHas(State.auraTypes, "Pulse")

    if wantsHighlight then
        if not entry.highlight or not entry.highlight.Parent then
            local ok, highlight = pcall(function()
                return Instance.new("Highlight")
            end)
            if ok and highlight then
                highlight.Name = "PhantomRivalsAuraHighlight"
                highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
                highlight.Parent = char
                entry.highlight = highlight
            end
        end

        if entry.highlight then
            entry.highlight.Adornee = char
            
            local auraColor = ESPUtil.getActiveAuraColor()
            entry.highlight.FillColor = auraColor or State.auraColor
            entry.highlight.OutlineColor = auraColor or State.auraOutlineColor
            entry.highlight.FillTransparency = 0.65
            entry.highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
            entry.highlight.Enabled = true
        end
    else
        ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.highlight)
        entry.highlight = nil
    end

    if wantsSelectionBox then
        if not entry.selectionBox or not entry.selectionBox.Parent then
            local selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
            selectionBox.Name = "PhantomRivalsAuraSelectionBox"
            selectionBox.LineThickness = 0.04
            selectionBox.SurfaceTransparency = 1
            selectionBox.Parent = root
            entry.selectionBox = selectionBox
        end

        entry.selectionBox.Adornee = root
        entry.selectionBox.Color3 = State.auraColor
        entry.selectionBox.Visible = true
    else
        ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.selectionBox)
        entry.selectionBox = nil
    end

    if wantsPointLight then
        if not entry.pointLight or not entry.pointLight.Parent then
            local pointLight = Instance.new("PointLight")
            pointLight.Name = "PhantomRivalsAuraPointLight"
            pointLight.Shadows = false
            pointLight.Range = 9
            pointLight.Brightness = 2
            pointLight.Parent = root
            entry.pointLight = pointLight
        end

        entry.pointLight.Color = State.auraColor
        entry.pointLight.Enabled = true
    else
        ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.pointLight)
        entry.pointLight = nil
    end

    if wantsPulse then
        if not entry.pulse or not entry.pulse.Parent then
            local pulse = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
            pulse.Name = "PhantomRivalsAuraPulse"
            pulse.Texture = "rbxassetid://243660364"
            pulse.Rate = 18
            pulse.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 0.9)
            pulse.Speed = NumberRange.new(0, 0)
            pulse.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(360, 360)
            pulse.Size = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1.6),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 3.8),
            })
            pulse.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25),
                NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
            })
            pulse.Parent = root
            entry.pulse = pulse
        end

        entry.pulse.Color = ColorSequence.new(State.auraColor)
        entry.pulse.Enabled = true
    else
        ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.pulse)
        entry.pulse = nil
    end

    return true
end

function State.updateAuraESP(playerList)
    if not State.auraESPEnabled or not Core.isLocalPlayerInMatch() then
        State.clearAuraESP()
        return
    end

    local active = {}
    for _, player in ipairs(playerList or Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if State.updateAuraForPlayer(player) then
            active[ESPUtil.sanitizePlayerKey(player)] = true
        end
    end

    for key, entry in pairs(State.auraObjects or {}) do
        if not active[key] then
            State.removeAuraEntry(entry)
            State.auraObjects[key] = nil
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.isPlayerDescendant(instance)
    local parent = instance
    while parent and parent ~= workspace do
        if parent:IsA("Model") and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(parent) then
            return true
        end
        parent = parent.Parent
    end
    return false
end

function ESPUtil.isProjectileName(name)
    return type(name) == "string" and name:find("^Throwable%s*[-–—]%s*") ~= nil
end

function ESPUtil.getProjectileRoot(instance)
    if not instance then
        return nil
    end

    if instance:IsA("Model") and ESPUtil.isProjectileName(instance.Name) then
        return instance
    end

    if instance:IsA("BasePart") then
        if ESPUtil.isProjectileName(instance.Name) then
            return instance
        end

        local model = instance:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("Model")
        if model and ESPUtil.isProjectileName(model.Name) then
            return model
        end
    end

    return nil
end

function ESPUtil.getProjectileParts(root)
    if not root then
        return {}
    end

    if root:IsA("BasePart") then
        return {root}
    end

    local parts = {}
    for _, desc in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if desc:IsA("BasePart") and desc.Transparency < 1 and not desc:IsDescendantOf(workspace.CurrentCamera) then
            table.insert(parts, desc)
        end
    end
    return parts
end

function ESPUtil.getCachedProjectileParts(root)
    if not root then
        return {}
    end

    local now = tick()
    local cached = State.worldESPPartCache[root]
    if cached and cached.parts and now - cached.time < (State.worldESPPartCacheInterval or 0.25) then
        return cached.parts
    end

    local parts = ESPUtil.getProjectileParts(root)
    State.worldESPPartCache[root] = {
        parts = parts,
        time = now,
    }
    return parts
end

function State.trackWorldESPProjectileRoot(obj)
    local root = ESPUtil.getProjectileRoot(obj)
    if root and root:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
        State.worldESPProjectileRoots[root] = true
    end
end

function State.initWorldESPProjectileTracking()
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
        State.trackWorldESPProjectileRoot(obj)
    end

    workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
        State.trackWorldESPProjectileRoot(obj)
    end)
end

State.initWorldESPProjectileTracking()

function ESPUtil.computePartBounds(part)
    local size = part.Size
    local cf = part.CFrame
    local half = Vector3.new(size.X / 2, size.Y / 2, size.Z / 2)
    local right = cf.RightVector * half.X
    local up = cf.UpVector * half.Y
    local back = cf.LookVector * half.Z

    return {
        cf.Position + right + up + back,
        cf.Position - right + up + back,
        cf.Position + right + up - back,
        cf.Position - right + up - back,
        cf.Position + right - up + back,
        cf.Position - right - up + back,
        cf.Position + right - up - back,
        cf.Position - right - up - back,
    }
end

function ESPUtil.getCharacterExtentsSize(char)
    if not char then
        return Vector3.new(4, 6, 2)
    end

    local now = tick()
    local cached = State.characterBoundsCache[char]

    if cached and cached.size and now - cached.time < 0.5 then
        return cached.size
    end

    local ok, size = pcall(function()
        return char:GetExtentsSize()
    end)

    if not ok or typeof(size) ~= "Vector3" then
        size = Vector3.new(4, 6, 2)
    end

    State.characterBoundsCache[char] = {
        size = size,
        time = now,
    }
    return size
end

function State.updateWorldESP(camera, delta)
    local enabled = State.worldESPProjectileEnabled
    if not enabled then
        State.clearWorldESP()
        return
    end

    if not Core.isLocalPlayerInMatch() then
        State.clearWorldESP()
        return
    end

    if not State.shouldRunAdaptiveTask("worldESP", delta, State.worldESPUpdateInterval, 1) then
        return
    end

    local activeKeys = State.worldESPActiveKeys or {}
    State.worldESPActiveKeys = activeKeys
    for key in pairs(activeKeys) do
        activeKeys[key] = nil
    end

    for root in pairs(State.worldESPProjectileRoots) do
        if root and root.Parent then
            local displayName = root.Name
            local parts = ESPUtil.getCachedProjectileParts(root)
            if #parts == 0 then
                State.worldESPProjectileRoots[root] = nil
                State.worldESPPartCache[root] = nil
            else
                local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
                local validCount = 0
                for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
                    if not ESPUtil.isPlayerDescendant(part) then
                        local points = ESPUtil.computePartBounds(part)
                        for index = 1, 8 do
                            local point2D, onScreen, depth = ESPUtil.projectPoint(points[index], camera)
                            if onScreen and depth > 0 then
                                validCount = validCount + 1
                                minX = math.min(minX, point2D.X)
                                minY = math.min(minY, point2D.Y)
                                maxX = math.max(maxX, point2D.X)
                                maxY = math.max(maxY, point2D.Y)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if validCount > 0 and minX <= maxX and minY <= maxY then
                    local key = root
                    local entry = State.worldESPData[key] or ESPUtil.createESPEntry(key, State.worldESPData)
                    activeKeys[key] = true

                    local boxPos = Vector2.new(minX, minY)
                    local boxSize = Vector2.new(maxX - minX, maxY - minY)
                    ESPUtil.updateSceneEntry(
                        entry,
                        camera,
                        boxPos,
                        boxSize,
                        displayName,
                        State.worldESPProjectileColor,
                        State.worldESPProjectileBoxEnabled,
                        State.worldESPProjectileBoxMode,
                        State.worldESPProjectileHighlightEnabled,
                        State.worldESPProjectileHighlightColor,
                        State.worldESPProjectileNameEnabled,
                        State.worldESPProjectileNameColor
                    )
                else
                    local key = root
                    local entry = State.worldESPData[key]
                    if entry then
                        ESPUtil.hideESPEntry(entry)
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            State.worldESPProjectileRoots[root] = nil
            State.worldESPPartCache[root] = nil
        end
    end

    for key, entry in pairs(State.worldESPData) do
        if not activeKeys[key] then
            ESPUtil.removeESPEntry(entry)
            State.worldESPData[key] = nil
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.createESPEntry(entryKey, dataTable)
    local entry = {
        box = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Square"),
        boxOutline = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Square"),
        boxFill = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Square"),
        name = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Text"),
        distance = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Text"),
        charm = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Text"),
        health = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line"),
        healthBackground = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line"),
        snapline = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line"),
        snaplineOutline = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line"),
        headDot = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Circle"),
        headDotOutline = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Circle"),
        arrowLines = {},
        arrowOutlineLines = {},
        lines = {},
        lineOutlines = {},
        skeletonLines = {},
        skeletonOutlineLines = {},
        corners = {},
        projected = {},
    }

    if entry.box then
        entry.box.Visible = false
        entry.box.Filled = false
        entry.box.Thickness = 2
        entry.box.Color = State.espBoxColor
        entry.box.Transparency = 1
        entry.box.ZIndex = 2
    end

    if entry.boxOutline then
        entry.boxOutline.Visible = false
        entry.boxOutline.Filled = false
        entry.boxOutline.Thickness = 4
        entry.boxOutline.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
        entry.boxOutline.Transparency = 1
        entry.boxOutline.ZIndex = 1
    end

    if entry.boxFill then
        entry.boxFill.Visible = false
        entry.boxFill.Filled = true
        entry.boxFill.Thickness = 0
        entry.boxFill.Color = State.espBoxFillColor
        entry.boxFill.Transparency = State.espBoxFillTransparency
        entry.boxFill.ZIndex = 1
    end

    entry.highlight = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Square")
    if entry.highlight then
        entry.highlight.Visible = false
        entry.highlight.Filled = false
        entry.highlight.Thickness = 4
        entry.highlight.Color = State.espHighlightColor
        entry.highlight.Transparency = 1
        entry.highlight.ZIndex = 3
    end

    if entry.name then
        entry.name.Visible = false
        entry.name.Color = State.espNameColor
        entry.name.Size = 16
        entry.name.Center = true
        entry.name.Outline = State.espNameOutlineEnabled
        entry.name.OutlineColor = State.espNameOutlineColor
        entry.name.Transparency = 1
    end

    if entry.distance then
        entry.distance.Visible = false
        entry.distance.Color = State.espDistanceColor
        entry.distance.Size = 14
        entry.distance.Center = true
        entry.distance.Outline = State.espDistanceOutlineEnabled
        entry.distance.OutlineColor = State.espDistanceOutlineColor
        entry.distance.Transparency = 1
    end

    entry.weapon = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Text")
    if entry.weapon then
        entry.weapon.Visible = false
        entry.weapon.Color = State.espWeaponColor
        entry.weapon.Size = 14
        entry.weapon.Center = true
        entry.weapon.Outline = State.espWeaponOutlineEnabled
        entry.weapon.OutlineColor = State.espWeaponOutlineColor
        entry.weapon.Transparency = 1
    end

    if entry.charm then
        entry.charm.Visible = false
        entry.charm.Color = State.espCharmColor
        entry.charm.Size = 18
        entry.charm.Center = true
        entry.charm.Outline = State.espCharmOutlineEnabled
        entry.charm.OutlineColor = State.espCharmOutlineColor
        entry.charm.Text = "✦"
        entry.charm.Transparency = 1
    end

    entry.auras = {}
    local auraNames = {"Fire", "Ice", "Lightning", "Wind", "Water", "Earth", "Light", "Dark"}
    for _, auraName in ipairs(auraNames) do
        entry.auras[auraName] = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Text")
        if entry.auras[auraName] then
            entry.auras[auraName].Visible = false
            entry.auras[auraName].Size = 14
            entry.auras[auraName].Center = true
            entry.auras[auraName].Outline = true
            entry.auras[auraName].OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            entry.auras[auraName].Transparency = 1
        end
    end

    if entry.health then
        entry.health.Visible = false
        entry.health.Color = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
        entry.health.Thickness = 4
        entry.health.Transparency = 1
    end

    if entry.healthBackground then
        entry.healthBackground.Visible = false
        entry.healthBackground.Color = State.espHealthOutlineColor
        entry.healthBackground.Thickness = 6
        entry.healthBackground.Transparency = 0.5
    end

    if entry.snapline then
        entry.snapline.Visible = false
        entry.snapline.Color = State.espLineColor
        entry.snapline.Thickness = 1
        entry.snapline.Transparency = 1
        entry.snapline.ZIndex = 3
    end

    if entry.snaplineOutline then
        entry.snaplineOutline.Visible = false
        entry.snaplineOutline.Color = State.espLineOutlineColor
        entry.snaplineOutline.Thickness = 3
        entry.snaplineOutline.Transparency = 1
        entry.snaplineOutline.ZIndex = 2
    end

    if entry.headDot then
        entry.headDot.Visible = false
        entry.headDot.Filled = true
        entry.headDot.Radius = State.espHeadDotRadius
        entry.headDot.Color = State.espHeadDotColor
        entry.headDot.Transparency = 1
        entry.headDot.ZIndex = 4
    end

    if entry.headDotOutline then
        entry.headDotOutline.Visible = false
        entry.headDotOutline.Filled = true
        entry.headDotOutline.Radius = State.espHeadDotRadius + 2
        entry.headDotOutline.Color = State.espHeadDotOutlineColor
        entry.headDotOutline.Transparency = 1
        entry.headDotOutline.ZIndex = 3
    end

    for i = 1, 12 do
        local outlineLine = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if outlineLine then
            outlineLine.Visible = false
            outlineLine.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
            outlineLine.Thickness = 4
            outlineLine.Transparency = 1
            outlineLine.ZIndex = 1
        end
        table.insert(entry.lineOutlines, outlineLine)

        local line = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if line then
            line.Visible = false
            line.Color = State.esp3DColor
            line.Thickness = 2
            line.Transparency = 1
            line.ZIndex = 2
        end
        table.insert(entry.lines, line)
    end

    entry.cornerLines = {}
    entry.cornerOutlineLines = {}

    for i = 1, 8 do
        local outlineLine = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if outlineLine then
            outlineLine.Visible = false
            outlineLine.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
            outlineLine.Thickness = 4
            outlineLine.Transparency = 1
            outlineLine.ZIndex = 2
        end
        table.insert(entry.cornerOutlineLines, outlineLine)

        local line = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if line then
            line.Visible = false
            line.Color = State.espBoxColor
            line.Thickness = 2
            line.Transparency = 1
            line.ZIndex = 3
        end
        table.insert(entry.cornerLines, line)
    end

    for i = 1, 14 do
        local outlineLine = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if outlineLine then
            outlineLine.Visible = false
            outlineLine.Color = State.espSkeletonOutlineColor
            outlineLine.Thickness = 4
            outlineLine.Transparency = 1
            outlineLine.ZIndex = 1
        end
        table.insert(entry.skeletonOutlineLines, outlineLine)

        local line = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if line then
            line.Visible = false
            line.Color = State.espSkeletonColor
            line.Thickness = 2
            line.Transparency = 1
            line.ZIndex = 2
        end
        table.insert(entry.skeletonLines, line)
    end

    for i = 1, 3 do
        local outlineLine = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if outlineLine then
            outlineLine.Visible = false
            outlineLine.Color = State.espArrowOutlineColor
            outlineLine.Thickness = 4
            outlineLine.Transparency = 1
            outlineLine.ZIndex = 4
        end
        table.insert(entry.arrowOutlineLines, outlineLine)

        local line = ESPUtil.createDrawing("Line")
        if line then
            line.Visible = false
            line.Color = State.espArrowColor
            line.Thickness = 2
            line.Transparency = 1
            line.ZIndex = 5
        end
        table.insert(entry.arrowLines, line)
    end

    if dataTable then
        dataTable[entryKey] = entry
    else
        State.espData[entryKey] = entry
    end
    return entry
end

function ESPUtil.projectPoint(point, camera)
    local screenPoint, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(point)
    return Vector2.new(screenPoint.X, screenPoint.Y), onScreen, screenPoint.Z
end

function ESPUtil.getWeaponNameForPlayer(plr)
    if not plr then return "" end
    local now = tick()
    local cache = State.weaponNameCache
    if State.lastWeaponNameCacheUpdate and now - State.lastWeaponNameCacheUpdate < 0.25 then
        return cache[plr.Name] or ""
    end

    for key in pairs(cache) do
        cache[key] = nil
    end
    State.lastWeaponNameCacheUpdate = now

    local vmFolder = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
    if not vmFolder then return cache[plr.Name] or "" end
    for _, vm in ipairs(vmFolder:GetChildren()) do
        if type(vm.Name) == "string" then
            local parts = string.split(vm.Name, " - ")
            if parts[1] then
                cache[parts[1]] = parts[2] or ""
            end
        end
    end
    return cache[plr.Name] or ""
end

function ESPUtil.getHealthColor(health, maxHealth)
    local ratio = math.clamp((tonumber(health) or 0) / math.max(tonumber(maxHealth) or 1, 1), 0, 1)
    if ratio > 0.5 then
        return Color3.new((1 - ratio) * 2, 1, 0)
    end

    return Color3.new(1, ratio * 2, 0)
end

function ESPUtil.setLine(line, from, to, color, visible, thickness)
    if not line then return end
    if not visible then
        line.Visible = false
        return
    end

    line.From = from
    line.To = to
    line.Color = color
    line.Thickness = thickness or line.Thickness
    line.Visible = true
end

function ESPUtil.hideLines(lines)
    for _, line in ipairs(lines or {}) do
        if line and line.Visible then
            line.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.updateTriangle(lines, points, color, visible, thickness)
    local pairs = {
        {1, 2},
        {2, 3},
        {3, 1},
    }

    for index, line in ipairs(lines or {}) do
        local pair = pairs[index]
        if line and pair then
            ESPUtil.setLine(line, points[pair[1]], points[pair[2]], color, visible, thickness)
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.updateArrow(entry, camera, worldPosition, visible)
    if not State.espArrowEnabled or not visible or not camera or not worldPosition then
        ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.arrowLines)
        ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.arrowOutlineLines)
        return false
    end

    local viewport = camera.ViewportSize
    local center = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y / 2)
    local projected, _, depth = ESPUtil.projectPoint(worldPosition, camera)
    local direction = projected - center

    if depth < 0 then
        direction = center - projected
    end

    if direction.Magnitude < 1 then
        direction = Vector2.new(0, -1)
    end

    local unit = direction.Unit
    local margin = State.espArrowMargin or 38
    local maxX = math.max(1, viewport.X / 2 - margin)
    local maxY = math.max(1, viewport.Y / 2 - margin)
    local scaleX = unit.X ~= 0 and (maxX / math.abs(unit.X)) or math.huge
    local scaleY = unit.Y ~= 0 and (maxY / math.abs(unit.Y)) or math.huge
    local tip = center + unit * math.min(scaleX, scaleY)
    local size = State.espArrowSize or 16
    local base = tip - unit * size
    local side = Vector2.new(-unit.Y, unit.X) * (size * 0.55)
    local points = {
        tip,
        base + side,
        base - side,
    }

    if State.espArrowOutlineEnabled then
        ESPUtil.updateTriangle(entry.arrowOutlineLines, points, State.espArrowOutlineColor, true, 4)
    else
        ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.arrowOutlineLines)
    end

    ESPUtil.updateTriangle(entry.arrowLines, points, State.espArrowColor, true, 2)
    return true
end

function ESPUtil.updateGlowCharm(entry, char, visible)
    if not visible or not char then
        ESPUtil.destroyInstance(entry.glowCharm)
        entry.glowCharm = nil
        return
    end

    if not entry.glowCharm or not entry.glowCharm.Parent then
        local ok, highlight = pcall(function()
            return Instance.new("Highlight")
        end)
        if ok and highlight then
            highlight.Name = "PhantomRivalsGlowCharmESP"
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Parent = char
            entry.glowCharm = highlight
        end
    end

    if entry.glowCharm then
        entry.glowCharm.Adornee = char
        entry.glowCharm.FillColor = State.espGlowCharmColor
        entry.glowCharm.OutlineColor = State.espGlowCharmOutlineColor
        entry.glowCharm.FillTransparency = 0.72
        entry.glowCharm.OutlineTransparency = State.espGlowCharmOutlineEnabled and 0 or 1
        entry.glowCharm.Enabled = true
    end
end

function ESPUtil.getActiveAuraColor()
    local activeCount = 0
    local mixR, mixG, mixB = 0, 0, 0

    if State.auraFireEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraFireColor.R, mixG + State.auraFireColor.G, mixB + State.auraFireColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end
    if State.auraIceEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraIceColor.R, mixG + State.auraIceColor.G, mixB + State.auraIceColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end
    if State.auraLightningEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraLightningColor.R, mixG + State.auraLightningColor.G, mixB + State.auraLightningColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end
    if State.auraWindEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraWindColor.R, mixG + State.auraWindColor.G, mixB + State.auraWindColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end
    if State.auraWaterEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraWaterColor.R, mixG + State.auraWaterColor.G, mixB + State.auraWaterColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end
    if State.auraEarthEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraEarthColor.R, mixG + State.auraEarthColor.G, mixB + State.auraEarthColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end
    if State.auraLightEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraLightColor.R, mixG + State.auraLightColor.G, mixB + State.auraLightColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end
    if State.auraDarkEnabled then
        mixR, mixG, mixB, activeCount = mixR + State.auraDarkColor.R, mixG + State.auraDarkColor.G, mixB + State.auraDarkColor.B, activeCount + 1
    end

    if activeCount > 0 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(
            math.floor(mixR / activeCount * 255),
            math.floor(mixG / activeCount * 255),
            math.floor(mixB / activeCount * 255)
        )
    end

    return nil
end

function ESPUtil.updatePlayerAura(char, auraColor)
    if not char then return end

    auraColor = auraColor or ESPUtil.getActiveAuraColor()

    if not auraColor then
        local existingAura = char:FindFirstChild("AuraHighlight")
        if existingAura then
            existingAura:Destroy()
        end
        return
    end

    local auraHighlight = char:FindFirstChild("AuraHighlight")
    if not auraHighlight then
        local ok, highlight = pcall(function()
            return Instance.new("Highlight")
        end)
        if ok and highlight then
            highlight.Name = "AuraHighlight"
            highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            highlight.Parent = char
            auraHighlight = highlight
        end
    end

    if auraHighlight then
        auraHighlight.Adornee = char
        auraHighlight.FillColor = auraColor
        auraHighlight.OutlineColor = auraColor
        auraHighlight.FillTransparency = 0.4
        auraHighlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
        auraHighlight.Enabled = true
    end
end

function ESPUtil.removePlayerAura(char)
    if not char then return end
    local auraHighlight = char:FindFirstChild("AuraHighlight")
    if auraHighlight then
        auraHighlight:Destroy()
    end
end

function ESPUtil.update3DBox(entry, points, camera)
    local lineIndices = {
        {1,2},{2,4},{4,3},{3,1},
        {5,6},{6,8},{8,7},{7,5},
        {1,5},{2,6},{3,7},{4,8},
    }

    for index = 1, #entry.lines do
        local line = entry.lines[index]
        local outlineLine = entry.lineOutlines and entry.lineOutlines[index]
        local pair = lineIndices[index]
        if line and pair then
            local p1, onScreen1 = ESPUtil.projectPoint(points[pair[1]], camera)
            local p2, onScreen2 = ESPUtil.projectPoint(points[pair[2]], camera)
            if onScreen1 and onScreen2 and points[pair[1]] and points[pair[2]] then
                if outlineLine then
                    ESPUtil.setLine(outlineLine, p1, p2, State.espBoxOutlineColor, State.espBoxOutlineEnabled, 4)
                end
                ESPUtil.setLine(line, p1, p2, State.esp3DColor, true, 2)
            else
                line.Visible = false
                if outlineLine then outlineLine.Visible = false end
            end
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.updateCornerBox(entry, boxPos, boxSize, color, visible, outlineEnabled, outlineColor)
    if not entry.cornerLines or #entry.cornerLines == 0 then return end
    if not visible then
        ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.cornerLines)
        ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.cornerOutlineLines)
        return
    end

    local length = math.clamp(math.min(boxSize.X, boxSize.Y) * 0.2, 8, 24)
    local x1, y1 = boxPos.X, boxPos.Y
    local x2, y2 = boxPos.X + boxSize.X, boxPos.Y + boxSize.Y
    local segments = {
        {Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x1 + length, y1)},
        {Vector2.new(x1, y1), Vector2.new(x1, y1 + length)},
        {Vector2.new(x2, y1), Vector2.new(x2 - length, y1)},
        {Vector2.new(x2, y1), Vector2.new(x2, y1 + length)},
        {Vector2.new(x1, y2), Vector2.new(x1 + length, y2)},
        {Vector2.new(x1, y2), Vector2.new(x1, y2 - length)},
        {Vector2.new(x2, y2), Vector2.new(x2 - length, y2)},
        {Vector2.new(x2, y2), Vector2.new(x2, y2 - length)},
    }

    for index, line in ipairs(entry.cornerLines) do
        local outlineLine = entry.cornerOutlineLines and entry.cornerOutlineLines[index]
        if line then
            local segment = segments[index]
            if outlineLine then
                outlineLine.Visible = visible and outlineEnabled and segment ~= nil
                if segment then
                    outlineLine.From = segment[1]
                    outlineLine.To = segment[2]
                    outlineLine.Color = outlineColor or State.espBoxOutlineColor
                    outlineLine.Thickness = 4
                    outlineLine.Transparency = visible and 1 or 0
                end
            end
            line.Visible = visible and segment ~= nil
            if segment then
                line.From = segment[1]
                line.To = segment[2]
                line.Color = color
                line.Thickness = 2
                line.Transparency = visible and 1 or 0
            end
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.updateSkeleton(entry, char, camera)
    local parts = {
        Head = char:FindFirstChild("Head"),
        UpperTorso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"),
        LowerTorso = char:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso"),
        LeftUpperArm = char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperArm"),
        LeftLowerArm = char:FindFirstChild("LeftLowerArm"),
        LeftHand = char:FindFirstChild("LeftHand"),
        RightUpperArm = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperArm"),
        RightLowerArm = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerArm"),
        RightHand = char:FindFirstChild("RightHand"),
        LeftUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("LeftUpperLeg"),
        LeftLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("LeftLowerLeg"),
        LeftFoot = char:FindFirstChild("LeftFoot"),
        RightUpperLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightUpperLeg"),
        RightLowerLeg = char:FindFirstChild("RightLowerLeg"),
        RightFoot = char:FindFirstChild("RightFoot"),
        LeftArm = char:FindFirstChild("Left Arm"),
        RightArm = char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm"),
        LeftLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Left Leg"),
        RightLeg = char:FindFirstChild("Right Leg"),
    }

    local bonePairs = {}
    if parts.UpperTorso and parts.LowerTorso then
        bonePairs = {
            {"Head", "UpperTorso"},
            {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
            {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"},
            {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"},
            {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
            {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"},
            {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"},
            {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
            {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"},
            {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"},
            {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
            {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"},
            {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"},
            {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
        }
    else
        bonePairs = {
            {"Head", "Torso"},
            {"Torso", "Left Arm"},
            {"Left Arm", "Left Leg"},
            {"Torso", "Right Arm"},
            {"Right Arm", "Right Leg"},
        }
    end

    for index = 1, #entry.skeletonLines do
        local line = entry.skeletonLines[index]
        local outlineLine = entry.skeletonOutlineLines and entry.skeletonOutlineLines[index]
        local pair = bonePairs[index]
        if line and pair then
            local p1 = parts[pair[1]]
            local p2 = parts[pair[2]]
            if p1 and p2 then
                local p1v, on1, d1 = ESPUtil.projectPoint(p1.Position, camera)
                local p2v, on2, d2 = ESPUtil.projectPoint(p2.Position, camera)
                if on1 and on2 and d1 > 0 and d2 > 0 then
                    if outlineLine then
                        ESPUtil.setLine(outlineLine, p1v, p2v, State.espSkeletonOutlineColor, State.espSkeletonOutlineEnabled, 4)
                    end
                    ESPUtil.setLine(line, p1v, p2v, State.espSkeletonColor, true, 2)
                else
                    line.Visible = false
                    if outlineLine then outlineLine.Visible = false end
                end
            else
                line.Visible = false
                if outlineLine then outlineLine.Visible = false end
            end
        end
    end
end

function ESPUtil.updateESPEntry(entry, camera, boxPos, boxSize, playerName, health, maxHealth, distance, headPos, plr)
    local box2DVisible = State.espBoxEnabled and State.espBoxMode == "2D"
    local imageVisible = State.espBoxEnabled and State.espBoxMode == "Image"

    if entry.boxOutline then
        entry.boxOutline.Position = boxPos - Vector2.new(1, 1)
        entry.boxOutline.Size = boxSize + Vector2.new(2, 2)
        entry.boxOutline.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
        entry.boxOutline.Visible = box2DVisible and State.espBoxOutlineEnabled
    end

    if entry.box then
        entry.box.Position = boxPos
        entry.box.Size = boxSize
        entry.box.Color = State.espBoxColor
        entry.box.Visible = box2DVisible
    end

    local imageLabel = entry.boxImageLabel
    if imageVisible then
        imageLabel = ESPUtil.ensureImageLabel(entry)
    end

    if imageLabel then
        local imageSource = ESPUtil.getBoxImageSource()
        imageLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(boxPos.X, boxPos.Y)
        imageLabel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, boxSize.X), math.max(1, boxSize.Y))
        imageLabel.Image = imageSource
        imageLabel.ImageTransparency = math.clamp(State.espBoxImageTransparency or 0, 0, 1)
        imageLabel.Visible = imageVisible and imageSource ~= ""

        if entry.boxImageStroke then
            entry.boxImageStroke.Enabled = imageLabel.Visible and State.espBoxOutlineEnabled
            entry.boxImageStroke.Color = State.espBoxOutlineColor
        end
    end

    if entry.boxFill then
        entry.boxFill.Position = boxPos
        entry.boxFill.Size = boxSize
        entry.boxFill.Color = State.espBoxFillColor
        entry.boxFill.Transparency = State.espBoxFillTransparency
        entry.boxFill.Visible = State.espBoxEnabled and State.espBoxMode == "2D" and State.espBoxFillEnabled
    end

    if entry.highlight then
        entry.highlight.Position = boxPos
        entry.highlight.Size = boxSize
        entry.highlight.Color = State.espHighlightColor
        entry.highlight.Visible = State.espHighlightEnabled
    end

    local cornerVisible = State.espBoxEnabled and State.espBoxMode == "Corner"
    ESPUtil.updateCornerBox(entry, boxPos, boxSize, State.espBoxColor, cornerVisible, State.espBoxOutlineEnabled, State.espBoxOutlineColor)

    if entry.name then
        entry.name.Position = boxPos - Vector2.new(0, 18)
        entry.name.Text = tostring(playerName or "")
        entry.name.Color = State.espNameColor
        entry.name.Outline = State.espNameOutlineEnabled
        entry.name.OutlineColor = State.espNameOutlineColor
        entry.name.Visible = State.espNameEnabled
    end

    if entry.distance then
        entry.distance.Position = Vector2.new(boxPos.X + boxSize.X / 2, boxPos.Y + boxSize.Y + 2)
        entry.distance.Text = string.format("%.0fm", distance or 0)
        entry.distance.Color = State.espDistanceColor
        entry.distance.Outline = State.espDistanceOutlineEnabled
        entry.distance.OutlineColor = State.espDistanceOutlineColor
        entry.distance.Visible = State.espDistanceEnabled
    end

    if entry.weapon then
        if State.espWeaponEnabled then
            local weaponName = plr and ESPUtil.getWeaponNameForPlayer(plr) or ""
            entry.weapon.Position = boxPos - Vector2.new(0, 36)
            entry.weapon.Text = weaponName
            entry.weapon.Color = State.espWeaponColor
            entry.weapon.Outline = State.espWeaponOutlineEnabled
            entry.weapon.OutlineColor = State.espWeaponOutlineColor
            entry.weapon.Visible = weaponName ~= ""
        else
            entry.weapon.Visible = false
        end
    end

    if entry.charm then
        if headPos and State.espCharmEnabled then
            entry.charm.Position = headPos - Vector2.new(0, 18)
            entry.charm.Color = State.espCharmColor
            entry.charm.Outline = State.espCharmOutlineEnabled
            entry.charm.OutlineColor = State.espCharmOutlineColor
            entry.charm.Visible = true
        else
            entry.charm.Visible = false
        end
    end

    if entry.auras and headPos then
        local auraConfig = {
            {name = "Fire", enabled = State.auraFireEnabled, color = State.auraFireColor, icon = "🔥"},
            {name = "Ice", enabled = State.auraIceEnabled, color = State.auraIceColor, icon = "❄"},
            {name = "Lightning", enabled = State.auraLightningEnabled, color = State.auraLightningColor, icon = "⚡"},
            {name = "Wind", enabled = State.auraWindEnabled, color = State.auraWindColor, icon = "💨"},
            {name = "Water", enabled = State.auraWaterEnabled, color = State.auraWaterColor, icon = "💧"},
            {name = "Earth", enabled = State.auraEarthEnabled, color = State.auraEarthColor, icon = "🌍"},
            {name = "Light", enabled = State.auraLightEnabled, color = State.auraLightColor, icon = "✨"},
            {name = "Dark", enabled = State.auraDarkEnabled, color = State.auraDarkColor, icon = "🌑"},
        }

        local auraOffset = -35
        for _, auraInfo in ipairs(auraConfig) do
            local auraText = entry.auras[auraInfo.name]
            if auraText then
                if auraInfo.enabled then
                    auraText.Position = headPos - Vector2.new(0, auraOffset)
                    auraText.Color = auraInfo.color
                    auraText.Text = auraInfo.icon .. " " .. auraInfo.name
                    auraText.Visible = true
                    auraOffset = auraOffset + 16
                else
                    auraText.Visible = false
                end
            end
        end
    else
        if entry.auras then
            for _, auraText in pairs(entry.auras) do
                if auraText then auraText.Visible = false end
            end
        end
    end

    if entry.snapline and entry.snaplineOutline then
        local viewport = camera and camera.ViewportSize or Vector2.zero
        local from = Vector2.new(viewport.X / 2, viewport.Y)
        local to = Vector2.new(boxPos.X + boxSize.X / 2, boxPos.Y + boxSize.Y)
        if State.espLineEnabled then
            if State.espLineOutlineEnabled then
                ESPUtil.setLine(entry.snaplineOutline, from, to, State.espLineOutlineColor, true, 3)
            else
                entry.snaplineOutline.Visible = false
            end
            ESPUtil.setLine(entry.snapline, from, to, State.espLineColor, true, 1)
        else
            entry.snapline.Visible = false
            entry.snaplineOutline.Visible = false
        end
    end

    if entry.headDot and entry.headDotOutline then
        if headPos and State.espHeadDotEnabled then
            entry.headDot.Position = headPos
            entry.headDot.Radius = State.espHeadDotRadius
            entry.headDot.Color = State.espHeadDotColor
            entry.headDot.Visible = true

            entry.headDotOutline.Position = headPos
            entry.headDotOutline.Radius = State.espHeadDotRadius + 2
            entry.headDotOutline.Color = State.espHeadDotOutlineColor
            entry.headDotOutline.Visible = State.espHeadDotOutlineEnabled
        else
            entry.headDot.Visible = false
            entry.headDotOutline.Visible = false
        end
    end

    ESPUtil.updateGlowCharm(entry, plr and plr.Character, State.espGlowCharmEnabled)

    if entry.health and entry.healthBackground then
        local healthHeight = math.max(1, boxSize.Y * math.clamp(health / math.max(maxHealth, 1), 0, 1))
        local healthTop = boxPos.Y + boxSize.Y - healthHeight
        local healthX = boxPos.X - 10

        entry.healthBackground.From = Vector2.new(healthX, boxPos.Y)
        entry.healthBackground.To = Vector2.new(healthX, boxPos.Y + boxSize.Y)
        entry.healthBackground.Color = State.espHealthOutlineColor
        entry.healthBackground.Thickness = 6
        entry.healthBackground.Visible = State.espHealthEnabled and State.espHealthOutlineEnabled

        entry.health.From = Vector2.new(healthX, healthTop)
        entry.health.To = Vector2.new(healthX, boxPos.Y + boxSize.Y)
        entry.health.Color = State.espHealthDynamicColor and ESPUtil.getHealthColor(health, maxHealth) or State.espHealthColor
        entry.health.Thickness = 4
        entry.health.Visible = State.espHealthEnabled
    end
end

function ESPUtil.hideESPEntry(entry, keepGlow)
    if not entry then return end
    if entry.box then entry.box.Visible = false end
    if entry.boxOutline then entry.boxOutline.Visible = false end
    if entry.boxFill then entry.boxFill.Visible = false end
    if entry.boxImageLabel then entry.boxImageLabel.Visible = false end
    if entry.highlight then entry.highlight.Visible = false end
    if entry.name then entry.name.Visible = false end
    if entry.distance then entry.distance.Visible = false end
    if entry.weapon then entry.weapon.Visible = false end
    if entry.charm then entry.charm.Visible = false end
    if entry.health then entry.health.Visible = false end
    if entry.healthBackground then entry.healthBackground.Visible = false end
    if entry.snapline then entry.snapline.Visible = false end
    if entry.snaplineOutline then entry.snaplineOutline.Visible = false end
    if entry.headDot then entry.headDot.Visible = false end
    if entry.headDotOutline then entry.headDotOutline.Visible = false end
    for _, line in ipairs(entry.lines or {}) do
        if line then line.Visible = false end
    end
    for _, line in ipairs(entry.lineOutlines or {}) do
        if line then line.Visible = false end
    end
    for _, skeletonLine in ipairs(entry.skeletonLines or {}) do
        if skeletonLine then skeletonLine.Visible = false end
    end
    for _, skeletonLine in ipairs(entry.skeletonOutlineLines or {}) do
        if skeletonLine then skeletonLine.Visible = false end
    end
    for _, cornerLine in ipairs(entry.cornerLines or {}) do
        if cornerLine then cornerLine.Visible = false end
    end
    for _, cornerLine in ipairs(entry.cornerOutlineLines or {}) do
        if cornerLine then cornerLine.Visible = false end
    end
    ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.arrowLines)
    ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.arrowOutlineLines)
    if entry.auras then
        for _, auraText in pairs(entry.auras) do
            if auraText then auraText.Visible = false end
        end
    end
    if not keepGlow then
        ESPUtil.updateGlowCharm(entry, nil, false)
    end
end

function ESPUtil.updateSceneEntry(entry, camera, boxPos, boxSize, displayName, color, boxEnabled, boxMode, highlightEnabled, highlightColor, nameEnabled, nameColor)
    if entry.boxOutline then
        entry.boxOutline.Visible = false
    end
    if entry.boxImageLabel then
        entry.boxImageLabel.Visible = false
    end

    if entry.box then
        entry.box.Position = boxPos
        entry.box.Size = boxSize
        entry.box.Color = color
        entry.box.Visible = boxEnabled and boxMode == "2D"
    end

    if entry.boxFill then
        entry.boxFill.Position = boxPos
        entry.boxFill.Size = boxSize
        entry.boxFill.Color = color
        entry.boxFill.Transparency = 0.4
        entry.boxFill.Visible = boxEnabled and boxMode == "2D"
    end

    if entry.highlight then
        entry.highlight.Position = boxPos
        entry.highlight.Size = boxSize
        entry.highlight.Color = highlightColor
        entry.highlight.Visible = highlightEnabled
    end

    local cornerVisible = boxEnabled and boxMode == "Corner"
    ESPUtil.updateCornerBox(entry, boxPos, boxSize, color, cornerVisible)

    if entry.name then
        entry.name.Position = boxPos - Vector2.new(0, 18)
        entry.name.Text = displayName
        entry.name.Color = nameColor
        entry.name.Visible = nameEnabled
    end

    if entry.health then entry.health.Visible = false end
    if entry.healthBackground then entry.healthBackground.Visible = false end
    if entry.distance then entry.distance.Visible = false end
    if entry.weapon then entry.weapon.Visible = false end
    if entry.charm then entry.charm.Visible = false end
    if entry.snapline then entry.snapline.Visible = false end
    if entry.snaplineOutline then entry.snaplineOutline.Visible = false end
    if entry.headDot then entry.headDot.Visible = false end
    if entry.headDotOutline then entry.headDotOutline.Visible = false end
    ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.arrowLines)
    ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.arrowOutlineLines)
    ESPUtil.updateGlowCharm(entry, nil, false)
end
