function State.updateESP(camera, playerList, delta)
    local enabled = State.espBoxEnabled
        or State.espHealthEnabled
        or State.espNameEnabled
        or State.espDistanceEnabled
        or State.espCharmEnabled
        or State.espSkeletonEnabled
        or State.espWeaponEnabled
        or State.espLineEnabled
        or State.espArrowEnabled
        or State.espHeadDotEnabled
        or State.espGlowCharmEnabled
    if not enabled then
        State.clearESP()
        return
    end

    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    local localCharacter = localPlayer and localPlayer.Character
    if not Core.isLocalPlayerInMatch(localPlayer, localCharacter) then
        State.clearESP()
        return
    end

    if not State.shouldRunAdaptiveTask("playerESP", delta, State.espUpdateInterval, 1) then
        return
    end

    local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not localRoot then
        State.clearESP()
        return
    end

    local players = playerList or Players:GetPlayers()
    local boxMode = State.espBoxMode or "2D"
    local wants3DBox = State.espBoxEnabled and boxMode == "3D"
    local wantsSkeleton = State.espSkeletonEnabled
    local wantsGlow = State.espGlowCharmEnabled

    local activeKeys = State.espActiveKeys
    for key in pairs(activeKeys) do
        activeKeys[key] = nil
    end

    for i = 1, #players do
        local plr = players[i]
        if plr ~= localPlayer then
            local char = plr.Character
            local humanoid = char and (char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid"))
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local playerKey = ESPUtil.sanitizePlayerKey(plr)
            local isValidESPPlayer = char
                and humanoid
                and root
                and humanoid.Health > 0
                and Core.isSameMatch(plr, char, localPlayer, localCharacter)
                and not (State.espTeamCheckEnabled and Core.isSameTeam(plr))

            if isValidESPPlayer then
                local distance = (root.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude

                if type(State.espMaxDistance) == "number" and State.espMaxDistance > 0 and distance > State.espMaxDistance then
                    if State.espData[playerKey] then
                        ESPUtil.removeESPForPlayer(playerKey)
                    end
                else
                    local entry = State.espData[playerKey] or ESPUtil.createESPEntry(playerKey, State.espData)
                    local wantsBox = State.espBoxEnabled
                    local wantsName = State.espNameEnabled
                    local wantsDistance = State.espDistanceEnabled
                    local wantsWeapon = State.espWeaponEnabled
                    local wantsCharm = State.espCharmEnabled
                    local wantsHeadDot = State.espHeadDotEnabled
                    local wantsLine = State.espLineEnabled
                    local wantsGlow = State.espGlowCharmEnabled
                    local rootOnScreen, rootDepth = false, 0

                    if State.espArrowEnabled then
                        local _, onScreen, depth = ESPUtil.projectPoint(root.Position, camera)
                        rootOnScreen = onScreen
                        rootDepth = depth
                    end

                    if wantsGlow then
                        ESPUtil.updateGlowCharm(entry, char, true)
                        activeKeys[playerKey] = true
                    end

                    local shouldComputeBounds = wants3DBox
                        or wantsSkeleton
                        or wantsBox
                        or wantsName
                        or wantsDistance
                        or wantsWeapon
                        or wantsCharm
                        or wantsHeadDot
                        or wantsLine

                    if shouldComputeBounds then
                        local corners = entry.corners
                        local size = ESPUtil.getCharacterExtentsSize(char)
                        local half = Vector3.new(size.X / 2, size.Y / 2, size.Z / 2)
                        local cf = root.CFrame
                        local right = cf.RightVector * half.X
                        local forward = cf.LookVector * half.Z
                        local up = Vector3.new(0, half.Y, 0)

                        corners[1] = root.Position + up + right + forward
                        corners[2] = root.Position + up - right + forward
                        corners[3] = root.Position + up + right - forward
                        corners[4] = root.Position + up - right - forward
                        corners[5] = root.Position - up + right + forward
                        corners[6] = root.Position - up - right + forward
                        corners[7] = root.Position - up + right - forward
                        corners[8] = root.Position - up - right - forward

                        local projected = entry.projected
                        local validCount = 0
                        local minX, minY, maxX, maxY = math.huge, math.huge, -math.huge, -math.huge
                        for index = 1, 8 do
                            local point2D, onScreen, depth = ESPUtil.projectPoint(corners[index], camera)
                            local pointData = projected[index] or {}
                            projected[index] = pointData
                            pointData.point2D = point2D
                            pointData.onScreen = onScreen
                            pointData.depth = depth

                            if onScreen and depth > 0 then
                                validCount = validCount + 1
                                minX = math.min(minX, point2D.X)
                                minY = math.min(minY, point2D.Y)
                                maxX = math.max(maxX, point2D.X)
                                maxY = math.max(maxY, point2D.Y)
                            end
                        end

                        if validCount > 0 then
                            if ESPUtil.updateArrow(entry, camera, root.Position, State.espArrowEnabled and not (rootOnScreen and rootDepth > 0)) then
                                activeKeys[playerKey] = true
                            end

                            if wants3DBox then
                                ESPUtil.update3DBox(entry, corners, camera)
                            else
                                ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.lines)
                                ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.lineOutlines)
                            end

                            if minX <= maxX and minY <= maxY then
                                local boxPos = Vector2.new(minX, minY)
                                local boxSize = Vector2.new(maxX - minX, maxY - minY)
                                local adjustedWidth = math.max(2, boxSize.X * 0.8)
                                if adjustedWidth < boxSize.X then
                                    local widthDelta = boxSize.X - adjustedWidth
                                    boxPos = boxPos + Vector2.new(widthDelta * 0.5, 0)
                                    boxSize = Vector2.new(adjustedWidth, boxSize.Y)
                                end

                                local headScreenPos = nil
                                if wantsCharm or wantsHeadDot then
                                    local headPart = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
                                    if headPart then
                                        local head2D, headOnScreen, headDepth = ESPUtil.projectPoint(headPart.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), camera)
                                        if headOnScreen and headDepth > 0 then
                                            headScreenPos = head2D
                                        end
                                    end
                                end

                                if wantsSkeleton then
                                    ESPUtil.updateSkeleton(entry, char, camera)
                                else
                                    ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.skeletonLines)
                                    ESPUtil.hideLines(entry.skeletonOutlineLines)
                                end

                                ESPUtil.updateESPEntry(entry, camera, boxPos, boxSize, plr.Name, humanoid.Health, humanoid.MaxHealth, distance, headScreenPos, plr)
                                activeKeys[playerKey] = true
                            else
                                ESPUtil.hideESPEntry(entry, true)
                                if wantsGlow then
                                    ESPUtil.updateGlowCharm(entry, char, true)
                                    activeKeys[playerKey] = true
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            ESPUtil.hideESPEntry(entry, true)
                            if ESPUtil.updateArrow(entry, camera, root.Position, State.espArrowEnabled and not (rootOnScreen and rootDepth > 0)) then
                                activeKeys[playerKey] = true
                            end
                            if wantsGlow then
                                ESPUtil.updateGlowCharm(entry, char, true)
                                activeKeys[playerKey] = true
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        ESPUtil.hideESPEntry(entry, true)
                        if ESPUtil.updateArrow(entry, camera, root.Position, State.espArrowEnabled and not (rootOnScreen and rootDepth > 0)) then
                            activeKeys[playerKey] = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            elseif State.espData[playerKey] then
                ESPUtil.removeESPForPlayer(playerKey)
            end
        end
    end
end

local function anyMovementEnabled()
    return State.flyEnabled or State.speedEnabled or State.jumpEnabled or State.infiniteJumpEnabled or State.slideBoostEnabled or State.autoSlideJumpEnabled
end

function State.getMechanicsController()
    if State.mechanicsController then
        return State.mechanicsController
    end

    local ok, mech = pcall(function()
        return require(Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.MechanicsController)
    end)

    if ok and mech then
        State.mechanicsController = mech
        return mech
    end

    return nil
end

function State.updateAutoSlideJump(now)
    if not State.autoSlideJumpEnabled then
        State.autoSlideJumpSliding = false
        State.autoSlideJumpFired = false
        State.autoSlideJumpSlideStart = nil
        return
    end

    local mech = State.getMechanicsController()
    local isSliding = mech and mech.IsSliding

    if not isSliding then
        State.autoSlideJumpSliding = false
        State.autoSlideJumpFired = false
        State.autoSlideJumpSlideStart = nil
        return
    end

    if not State.autoSlideJumpSliding then
        State.autoSlideJumpSliding = true
        State.autoSlideJumpFired = false
        State.autoSlideJumpSlideStart = now
    end

    if State.autoSlideJumpFired then
        return
    end

    if now - (State.autoSlideJumpSlideStart or now) < (State.autoSlideJumpDelay or 0) then
        return
    end

    State.autoSlideJumpFired = true
    pcall(function()
        if type(mech.HighJump) == "function" then
            mech:HighJump()
        elseif type(mech.Jump) == "function" then
            mech:Jump(nil, nil, true)
        end
    end)
end

function State.getAntiAimPitchRadians(defaultPitch)
    local mode = State.antiAimPitchMode or "Random"
    defaultPitch = defaultPitch or 0

    if mode == "Custom" then
        return math.rad(State.antiAimPitch or 0)
    end

    if mode == "Offset" then
        return defaultPitch + math.rad(State.antiAimPitchOffset or 0)
    end

    if mode == "Look UP" then
        return math.rad(89)
    end

    if mode == "Look Down" then
        return math.rad(-89)
    end

    if mode == "Extended Random" then
        return math.rad(math.random(-720, 720))
    end

    if mode == "Extended Custom" then
        return math.rad(State.antiAimPitchExtended or 0)
    end

    return math.rad(math.random(-180, 180))
end

function State.getAntiAimYawRadians(defaultYaw)
    local mode = State.antiAimYawMode or "Random"
    defaultYaw = defaultYaw or 0

    if mode == "Custom" then
        return math.rad(State.antiAimYaw or 0)
    end

    if mode == "Backward" then
        return defaultYaw + math.rad(180)
    end

    if mode == "Extended Random" then
        return math.rad(math.random(-720, 720))
    end

    if mode == "Extended Custom" then
        return math.rad(State.antiAimYawExtended or 0)
    end

    if mode == "Offsets" then
        return defaultYaw + math.rad(State.antiAimYawOffset or 0)
    end

    if mode == "Disabled" then
        return defaultYaw
    end

    if mode == "Random" then
        return math.rad(math.random(-180, 180))
    end

    return defaultYaw
end

function State.applyAntiAimToCFrame(cframe)
    if not State.antiAimEnabled or not cframe then
        return cframe
    end

    local basePitch, baseYaw = cframe:ToOrientation()
    local pitch = State.getAntiAimPitchRadians(basePitch)
    local yaw = State.getAntiAimYawRadians(baseYaw)
    return CFrame.new(cframe.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0) * CFrame.Angles(pitch, 0, 0)
end

function State.startAntiAim()
    if State.antiAimConnection then return end

    local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local updateCameraRotation = nil
    local utility = nil
    local cameraController = nil

    pcall(function()
        local remote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Replication"):WaitForChild("Fighter"):WaitForChild("UpdateCameraRotation")
        if remote and remote.FireServer then
            updateCameraRotation = remote
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        utility = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("Utility"))
    end)

    pcall(function()
        cameraController = require(Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.CameraController)
    end)

    State.antiAimConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not State.antiAimEnabled then return end

        local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end

        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid and State.antiAimHumanoid ~= humanoid then
            if State.antiAimHumanoid and State.antiAimOriginalAutoRotate ~= nil then
                pcall(function()
                    State.antiAimHumanoid.AutoRotate = State.antiAimOriginalAutoRotate
                end)
            end

            State.antiAimHumanoid = humanoid
            State.antiAimOriginalAutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
        end

        if humanoid then
            humanoid.AutoRotate = false
        end

        local basePitch = 0
        local baseYaw = 0
        if cameraController and cameraController.Rotation then
            basePitch = cameraController.Rotation.X or 0
            baseYaw = cameraController.Rotation.Y or 0
        else
            local currentPitch, currentYaw = root.CFrame:ToOrientation()
            basePitch = currentPitch
            baseYaw = currentYaw
        end

        local pitch = State.getAntiAimPitchRadians(basePitch)
        local yaw = State.getAntiAimYawRadians(baseYaw)

        if updateCameraRotation and utility and type(utility.EncodeCameraRotation) == "function" then
            local cameraRotation = Vector2.new(pitch, yaw)
            pcall(function()
                updateCameraRotation:FireServer(utility:EncodeCameraRotation(cameraRotation), nil)
            end)
        else
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position) * CFrame.Angles(0, yaw, 0) * CFrame.Angles(pitch, 0, 0)
        end
    end)
end

function State.stopAntiAim()
    State.antiAimEnabled = false

    if State.antiAimConnection then
        State.antiAimConnection:Disconnect()
        State.antiAimConnection = nil
    end

    if State.antiAimHumanoid and State.antiAimOriginalAutoRotate ~= nil then
        pcall(function()
            State.antiAimHumanoid.AutoRotate = State.antiAimOriginalAutoRotate
        end)
    end

    State.antiAimHumanoid = nil
    State.antiAimOriginalAutoRotate = nil
end

function State.startMovementHeartbeat()
    if State.movementHeartbeatConn then return end
    State.movementHeartbeatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
        local now = tick()
        local flyMode = State.flyMode or "Velocity"
        local movementUpdateFrequency = 0.03
        if State.flyEnabled and flyMode == "CFrame" then
            movementUpdateFrequency = State.flyCFrameFrequency or 0.005
        elseif State.flyEnabled and flyMode == "TP" then
            movementUpdateFrequency = State.flyTPFrequency or 0.005
        end
        if now - (State.lastMovementUpdate or 0) < movementUpdateFrequency then return end
        State.lastMovementUpdate = now

        if not anyMovementEnabled() then
            return
        end

        State.updateAutoSlideJump(now)

        local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end

		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if State.flyEnabled then
			local moveDir = Vector3.zero
			if State.flyKeys.W then moveDir = moveDir + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
			if State.flyKeys.S then moveDir = moveDir - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
			if State.flyKeys.A then moveDir = moveDir - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
			if State.flyKeys.D then moveDir = moveDir + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
			if State.flyKeys.LeftShift then moveDir = moveDir - Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			if State.flyKeys.Space then moveDir = moveDir + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) end
			local mode = flyMode
            local neutralizeGravity = mode == "CFrame" or mode == "TP"
            if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                if mode == "Velocity" then
                    root.Velocity = moveDir.Unit * (State.flySpeed or 16)
                elseif mode == "CFrame" then
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
                    root.CFrame = root.CFrame + moveDir.Unit * (State.flySpeed or 16) * deltaTime
                elseif mode == "TP" then
                    State.flyTPTime = State.flyTPTime or 0
                    root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
                    if now - State.flyTPTime >= (State.flyTPFrequency or 0.005) then
                        State.flyTPTime = now
                        root.CFrame = root.CFrame + moveDir.Unit * (State.flySpeed or 16) * deltaTime
                    end
                end
            elseif mode == "Velocity" then
                root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
            elseif neutralizeGravity then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.X, 0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Z)
            end
        end

        if not State.flyEnabled and State.speedEnabled then
            local moveDir = Vector3.zero
			if State.speedKeys.W then moveDir = moveDir + cam.CFrame.LookVector end
			if State.speedKeys.S then moveDir = moveDir - cam.CFrame.LookVector end
			if State.speedKeys.A then moveDir = moveDir - cam.CFrame.RightVector end
			if State.speedKeys.D then moveDir = moveDir + cam.CFrame.RightVector end
            moveDir = Vector3.new(moveDir.X, 0, moveDir.Z)

            if moveDir.Magnitude > 0 then
                local mode = State.speedMode or "Velocity"
                if mode == "Velocity" then
                    root.Velocity = Vector3.new(moveDir.Unit.X * (State.speedValue or 16), root.Velocity.Y, moveDir.Unit.Z * (State.speedValue or 16))
                elseif mode == "CFrame" then
                    root.CFrame = root.CFrame + moveDir.Unit * (State.speedValue or 16) * deltaTime
                elseif mode == "TP" then
                    State.speedTPTime = State.speedTPTime or 0
                    if now - State.speedTPTime >= (State.speedTPFrequency or 0.2) then
                        State.speedTPTime = now
                        root.CFrame = root.CFrame + moveDir.Unit * (State.speedValue or 16) * deltaTime
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        if not State.flyEnabled and not State.speedEnabled and State.jumpEnabled then
            local humanoid = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if humanoid and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then
                if humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air or State.infiniteJumpEnabled then
                    root.Velocity = Vector3.new(root.Velocity.X, (State.jumpPowerValue or 16), root.Velocity.Z)
                end
            end
        end

        if State.infiniteJumpEnabled then

        end

        if State.slideBoostEnabled then
            pcall(function()
                local mech = State.getMechanicsController()
                if mech then
                    local f = mech.LocalFighter
                    if f then
                        if not State.slideBaseSpeed then
                            State.slideBaseSpeed = f:Get("SlidingSpeedMax") or 3
                        end
                        f:Set("SlidingSpeedMax", State.slideBaseSpeed * (State.slideBoostMultiplier or 1.5))
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function State.stopMovementHeartbeat()
    if anyMovementEnabled() then
        return
    end

    if State.movementHeartbeatConn then
        State.movementHeartbeatConn:Disconnect()
        State.movementHeartbeatConn = nil
    end
end

function State.applyNameSpoof()
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not State.nameSpoofOriginalName then
        State.nameSpoofOriginalName = player.Name
        State.nameSpoofOriginalDisplayName = player.DisplayName
    end

    local function buildSpoofedValue(baseValue, originalValue)
        local finalValue = (baseValue ~= "" and baseValue or originalValue)
        if State.nameSpoofVerified then
            if not finalValue:find(" $") then
                finalValue = finalValue .. " "
            end
        else
            finalValue = finalValue:gsub(" $", "")
        end
        return finalValue
    end

    player.Name = buildSpoofedValue(State.nameSpoofName, State.nameSpoofOriginalName)
    player.DisplayName = buildSpoofedValue(State.nameSpoofDisplayName, State.nameSpoofOriginalDisplayName)
end

function State.resetNameSpoof()
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    if State.nameSpoofOriginalName then
        player.Name = State.nameSpoofOriginalName
        player.DisplayName = State.nameSpoofOriginalDisplayName
    end
end

local function extractStaffRoleIds(groupId)
    local url = ("https://groups.roblox.com/v1/groups/%d/roles"):format(groupId)
    local ok, response = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:GetAsync(url, true)
    end)
    if not ok or not response then
        return {}
    end
    local ok2, data = pcall(function()
        return HttpService:JSONDecode(response)
    end)
    if not ok2 or not data or not data.roles then
        return {}
    end

    local roleIds = {}
    for _, r in ipairs(data.roles) do
        local name = string.lower(r.name or "")
        if string.find(name, "mod") or string.find(name, "staff") or string.find(name, "admin") then
            table.insert(roleIds, r.id)
        end
    end
    return roleIds
end

local function fetchUsersInRole(groupId, roleId)
    local cursor = ""
    local collected = {}

    while true do
        local url = string.format("https://groups.roproxy.com/v1/groups/%d/roles/%d/users?limit=100&cursor=%s", groupId, roleId, cursor)
        local ok, response = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:GetAsync(url, true)
        end)
        if not ok or not response then
            break
        end
        local ok2, json = pcall(function()
            return HttpService:JSONDecode(response)
        end)
        if not ok2 or not json or not json.data then
            break
        end

        for _, user in ipairs(json.data) do
            if user.userId then
                collected[user.userId] = true
            end
        end

        if not json.nextPageCursor or json.nextPageCursor == "" then
            break
        end
        cursor = json.nextPageCursor
    end

    return collected
end

local function isStaffRoleName(role)
    if role and typeof(role) == "string" then
        local r = string.lower(role)
        return string.find(r, "mod") or string.find(r, "staff") or string.find(r, "admin")
    end
    return false
end

local function getStaffInServer()
    local staffNames = {}
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= Players.LocalPlayer then
            local role = pcall(function()
                return plr:GetRoleInGroup(game.CreatorId)
            end)
            if role and isStaffRoleName(role) then
                table.insert(staffNames, plr.Name)
            end
        end
    end
    return staffNames
end

local function playNotificationSound()
    pcall(function()
        local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
        sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://12221967"
        sound.Volume = 2
        sound.Parent = workspace
        sound:Play()
        game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound, 2)
    end)
end

local function showModDetectedUI()
    local staffList = getStaffInServer()
    local staffText = #staffList > 0 and table.concat(staffList, ", ") or "Unknown"

    if State.modDetectorMode == "Kick" then
        Players.LocalPlayer:Kick("Moderator detected: " .. staffText)
    elseif State.modDetectorMode == "Notify" then
        playNotificationSound()
        notify("Mod Detector", "⚠️ Moderator detected in server: " .. staffText)
    end
end

local function runModDetection()
    if not State.modDetectorEnabled then
        return false
    end

    local staffNames = getStaffInServer()
    if #staffNames > 0 then
        showModDetectedUI()
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function State.getPlayerDataController()
    if State.playerDataController then
        return State.playerDataController
    end
    local ok, controller = pcall(require, Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.PlayerDataController)
    if ok and controller then
        State.playerDataController = controller
        return controller
    end
    return nil
end

local function getCurrentPlayerData()
    local controller = State.getPlayerDataController()
    return controller and controller.CurrentData
end

local function safeSetAttribute(target, name, value)
    if target and typeof(target.SetAttribute) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            target:SetAttribute(name, value)
        end)
    end
end

local function safeSetIntValue(parent, name, value)
    if not parent then
        return
    end
    local stat = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
    if stat and stat:IsA("IntValue") then
        stat.Value = value
    end
end

local function applyLeaderstats(player)
    if not player then
        return
    end
    local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("CustomLeaderstats")
    if leaderstats then
        if State.levelSpoofEnabled then
            safeSetIntValue(leaderstats, "Level", tonumber(State.levelSpoofValue) or 1)
        end
        if State.streakSpoofEnabled then
            safeSetIntValue(leaderstats, "Win Streak", tonumber(State.streakSpoofValue) or 0)
        end
    end
end

function State.spoofPlayerDataValue(key, value)
    local data = getCurrentPlayerData()
    if data then
        pcall(function()
            if typeof(data.Set) == "function" then
                data:Set(key, value)
            end
            if typeof(data.SetReplicate) == "function" then
                data:SetReplicate(key, value)
            end
            if typeof(data.Replicate) == "function" then
                data:Replicate(key)
            end
        end)
        return true
    end

    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    safeSetAttribute(player, key, value)
    return true
end

local function getCurrencyContainer()
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    return localPlayer and localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame:FindFirstChild("Lobby")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame.Lobby:FindFirstChild("Currency")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame.Lobby.Currency:FindFirstChild("Container")
end

local function applyKeySpoof()
    if not State.keySpoofEnabled then
        return
    end

    local container = getCurrencyContainer()
    if not container then
        return
    end

    local textValue = tostring(State.keySpoofValue or "")
    for _, icon in ipairs(container:GetDescendants()) do
        if icon.Name == "Icon" and type(icon.Image) == "string" and icon.Image == "rbxassetid://17860673529" then
            local title = icon.Parent and icon.Parent.Parent and icon.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("Title")
            if title and title:IsA("TextLabel") then
                title.Text = textValue
            end
        end
    end
end

local function getViewProfilePlayerFrame()
    local localPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not localPlayer then
        return nil
    end

    local pages = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("MainGui")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui:FindFirstChild("MainFrame")
        and localPlayer.PlayerGui.MainGui.MainFrame:FindFirstChild("Pages")
    if not pages then
        return nil
    end

    local viewProfile = pages:FindFirstChild("ViewProfile")
    if not viewProfile then
        return nil
    end

    local active = viewProfile:FindFirstChild("Active")
    if not active then
        return nil
    end

    return active:FindFirstChild("Player")
end

local function applyViewProfileLevelSpoof()
    if not State.levelSpoofEnabled then
        return
    end

    local profilePlayer = getViewProfilePlayerFrame()
    if not profilePlayer then
        return
    end

    local displayLabel = profilePlayer:FindFirstChild("DisplayName")
    if not displayLabel or not displayLabel:IsA("TextLabel") then
        return
    end

    local displayedText = tostring(displayLabel.Text or "")
    local localDisplayName = Players.LocalPlayer and Players.LocalPlayer.DisplayName or ""
    local spoofDisplayName = State.nameSpoofDisplayName ~= "" and State.nameSpoofDisplayName or State.nameSpoofOriginalDisplayName

    if displayedText == localDisplayName or displayedText == spoofDisplayName then
        local bragging = profilePlayer:FindFirstChild("Bragging")
        local levelFrame = bragging and bragging:FindFirstChild("Level")
        local valueLabel = levelFrame and levelFrame:FindFirstChild("Value")
        if valueLabel and valueLabel:IsA("TextLabel") then
            valueLabel.Text = tostring(State.levelSpoofValue or 0)
        end
    end
end

local function getEloSpoofLeaderboardController()
    if type(State.eloSpoofLeaderboardController) == "table" then
        return State.eloSpoofLeaderboardController
    end

    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local playerScripts = player and player:FindFirstChild("PlayerScripts")
    local controllers = playerScripts and playerScripts:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
    local module = controllers and controllers:FindFirstChild("LeaderboardController")
    if not module then
        return nil
    end

    local ok, controller = pcall(require, module)
    if ok and type(controller) == "table" then
        State.eloSpoofLeaderboardController = controller
        return controller
    end
    return nil
end

local function sortHighestEloPlayers(players, localUserId)
    table.sort(players, function(a, b)
        local aValue = tonumber(a and a.value) or -math.huge
        local bValue = tonumber(b and b.value) or -math.huge
        if aValue == bValue then
            local aKey = tostring(a and a.key or "")
            local bKey = tostring(b and b.key or "")
            if aKey == localUserId then
                return true
            elseif bKey == localUserId then
                return false
            end
            return aKey < bKey
        end
        return aValue > bValue
    end)
end

local function highestEloPlayersContainKey(players, key)
    key = tostring(key or "")
    for _, entry in ipairs(players) do
        if tostring(entry and entry.key or "") == key then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

local function fireHighestEloRefreshed(controller)
    if controller and controller.Refreshed and type(controller.Refreshed.Fire) == "function" then
        pcall(controller.Refreshed.Fire, controller.Refreshed, "Highest ELO")
    end
end

function State.restoreEloSpoofLeaderboard()
    local controller = getEloSpoofLeaderboardController()
    local serial = controller and controller.LeaderboardSerials and controller.LeaderboardSerials["Highest ELO"]
    local players = serial and serial.Players
    if type(players) ~= "table" then
        State.eloSpoofLeaderboardRank = nil
        return
    end

    local localUserId = tostring(Players.LocalPlayer.UserId)
    local changed = false
    for index = #players, 1, -1 do
        local entry = players[index]
        if type(entry) == "table" and entry._phantomEloSpoof == true then
            table.remove(players, index)
            changed = true
        end
    end

    local original = State.eloSpoofOriginalLeaderboardEntry
    if original and not table.find(players, original) then
        if not highestEloPlayersContainKey(players, localUserId) then
            table.insert(players, {
                key = original.key,
                value = original.value,
            })
            changed = true
        end
    end

    local displaced = State.eloSpoofDisplacedLeaderboardEntry
    if displaced then
        if not highestEloPlayersContainKey(players, displaced.key) then
            table.insert(players, {
                key = displaced.key,
                value = displaced.value,
            })
            changed = true
        end
    end

    if changed then
        sortHighestEloPlayers(players, localUserId)
        fireHighestEloRefreshed(controller)
    end
    State.eloSpoofLeaderboardRank = nil
    State.eloSpoofDisplacedLeaderboardEntry = nil
end

local function applyEloSpoofLeaderboard(elo)
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - (State.eloSpoofLastLeaderboardSync or 0) < 0.35 then
        return
    end
    State.eloSpoofLastLeaderboardSync = now

    local controller = getEloSpoofLeaderboardController()
    local serial = controller and controller.LeaderboardSerials and controller.LeaderboardSerials["Highest ELO"]
    local players = serial and serial.Players
    if type(players) ~= "table" then
        return
    end

    local localUserId = tostring(Players.LocalPlayer.UserId)
    local originalCount = #players
    for index, entry in ipairs(players) do
        if tostring(entry and entry.key or "") == localUserId
            and entry._phantomEloSpoof == true
            and tonumber(entry.value) == elo then
            State.eloSpoofLeaderboardRank = index
            return
        end
    end

    local changed = false
    for index = #players, 1, -1 do
        local entry = players[index]
        if tostring(entry and entry.key or "") == localUserId then
            if entry._phantomEloSpoof ~= true then
                State.eloSpoofOriginalLeaderboardEntry = {
                    key = entry.key,
                    value = entry.value,
                }
            end
            table.remove(players, index)
            changed = true
        end
    end

    sortHighestEloPlayers(players, localUserId)
    local cutoff = 3600
    if #players >= 200 then
        cutoff = math.max(cutoff, tonumber(players[200].value) or cutoff)
    end
    State.eloSpoofArchnemesisCutoff = cutoff

    local qualifiesForArchnemesis = elo >= 3600 and (#players < 200 or elo >= cutoff)
    if qualifiesForArchnemesis then
        table.insert(players, {
            key = localUserId,
            value = elo,
            _phantomEloSpoof = true,
        })
        changed = true
        sortHighestEloPlayers(players, localUserId)
        while #players > math.max(originalCount, 1) do
            local displaced = table.remove(players)
            if displaced and displaced._phantomEloSpoof ~= true then
                State.eloSpoofDisplacedLeaderboardEntry = {
                    key = displaced.key,
                    value = displaced.value,
                }
            end
        end
        for index, entry in ipairs(players) do
            if tostring(entry and entry.key or "") == localUserId then
                State.eloSpoofLeaderboardRank = index
                break
            end
        end
    else
        local original = State.eloSpoofOriginalLeaderboardEntry
        if original and not highestEloPlayersContainKey(players, original.key) then
            table.insert(players, {
                key = original.key,
                value = original.value,
            })
            changed = true
        end
        local displaced = State.eloSpoofDisplacedLeaderboardEntry
        if displaced then
            if not highestEloPlayersContainKey(players, displaced.key) then
                table.insert(players, {
                    key = displaced.key,
                    value = displaced.value,
                })
                changed = true
            end
            State.eloSpoofDisplacedLeaderboardEntry = nil
        end
        sortHighestEloPlayers(players, localUserId)
        State.eloSpoofLeaderboardRank = nil
    end

    if changed then
        fireHighestEloRefreshed(controller)
    end
end

function State.applyPlayerDataSpoof()
    if not (State.levelSpoofEnabled or State.streakSpoofEnabled or State.eloSpoofEnabled or State.keySpoofEnabled) then
        return
    end

    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    if State.levelSpoofEnabled then
        State.spoofPlayerDataValue("Level", tonumber(State.levelSpoofValue) or 1)
        safeSetAttribute(player, "Level", tonumber(State.levelSpoofValue) or 1)
    end

    if State.streakSpoofEnabled then
        State.spoofPlayerDataValue("StatisticDuelsWinStreak", tonumber(State.streakSpoofValue) or 0)
        safeSetAttribute(player, "StatisticDuelsWinStreak", tonumber(State.streakSpoofValue) or 0)
    end

    if State.eloSpoofEnabled then
        local elo = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(State.eloSpoofValue) or 1000))
        State.spoofPlayerDataValue("DisplayELO", elo)
        safeSetAttribute(player, "DisplayELO", elo)
        applyEloSpoofLeaderboard(elo)
    end

    if State.keySpoofEnabled then
        applyKeySpoof()
    end

    if State.levelSpoofEnabled then
        applyViewProfileLevelSpoof()
    end

    applyLeaderstats(player)
end

function State.refreshPlayerDataSpoof()
    local isEnabled = State.levelSpoofEnabled or State.streakSpoofEnabled or State.eloSpoofEnabled or State.keySpoofEnabled
    State.playerDataSpoofEnabled = isEnabled

    if isEnabled then
        State.applyPlayerDataSpoof()
        if not State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection then
            State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                if State.playerDataSpoofEnabled then
                    State.applyPlayerDataSpoof()
                end
            end)
        end
        return
    end

    if State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection then
        State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection:Disconnect()
        State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection = nil
    end
end

local RankCharmFallbackELO = {
    ["Bronze 1"] = 0,
    ["Bronze 2"] = 200,
    ["Bronze 3"] = 400,
    ["Silver 1"] = 600,
    ["Silver 2"] = 800,
    ["Silver 3"] = 1000,
    ["Gold 1"] = 1200,
    ["Gold 2"] = 1400,
    ["Gold 3"] = 1600,
    ["Platinum 1"] = 1800,
    ["Platinum 2"] = 2000,
    ["Platinum 3"] = 2200,
    ["Diamond 1"] = 2400,
    ["Diamond 2"] = 2600,
    ["Diamond 3"] = 2800,
    ["Onyx 1"] = 3000,
    ["Onyx 2"] = 3200,
    ["Onyx 3"] = 3400,
    Nemesis = 3600,
    Archnemesis = 3600,
}

local function getRankCharmSpoofValues(seasonLibrary, seasonName)
    local rankName = State.rankCharmRank or "Unranked"
    if rankName == "Unranked" then
        return nil, nil
    end

    local season = seasonLibrary.Seasons and seasonLibrary.Seasons[seasonName]
    local profileName = season and season.RankProfile and season.RankProfile.Name
    local profile = profileName and seasonLibrary.RankProfiles and seasonLibrary.RankProfiles[profileName]
    local rankData = profile and profile.Ranks and profile.Ranks[rankName]
    local elo = rankData and rankData.RequiredELO or RankCharmFallbackELO[rankName]
    local leaderboardRank

    if rankData and rankData.RequiredELOLeaderboardRanking then
        leaderboardRank = math.clamp(
            math.floor(tonumber(State.rankCharmLeaderboardRank) or 1),
            1,
            rankData.RequiredELOLeaderboardRanking
        )
    elseif rankName == "Archnemesis" then
        leaderboardRank = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(State.rankCharmLeaderboardRank) or 1), 1, 200)
    end

    return elo, leaderboardRank
end

local function rebuildRankCharmItemViewModel(item)
    if type(item) ~= "table" or type(item._CreateViewModel) ~= "function" then
        return true
    end

    local oldViewModel = item.ViewModel
    local serial = type(oldViewModel) == "table" and rawget(oldViewModel, "_serial")
    if serial == nil then
        return true
    end

    local wasEquipped = false
    if type(oldViewModel.IsEquipped) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            wasEquipped = oldViewModel:IsEquipped()
        end)
    end

    local ok, newViewModel = pcall(item._CreateViewModel, item, serial)
    if not ok or not newViewModel then
        return false, ok and "Failed to rebuild viewmodel" or newViewModel
    end

    if type(oldViewModel.Destroy) == "function" then
        pcall(oldViewModel.Destroy, oldViewModel)
    end
    item.ViewModel = newViewModel

    if wasEquipped and type(newViewModel.Equip) == "function" then
        pcall(newViewModel.Equip, newViewModel, true)
    end

    return true
end

local function refreshRankCharmViewModels()
    local equippedItem = State.getLocalEquippedItem and State.getLocalEquippedItem()
    local localFighter = fighterReq and fighterReq.LocalFighter
    local queued = {}

    local function queueItem(item)
        if type(item) == "table" and not queued[item] then
            queued[item] = true
        end
    end

    queueItem(equippedItem)
    for _, item in pairs(localFighter and localFighter.Items or {}) do
        queueItem(item)
    end

    for item in pairs(queued) do
        local success, err = rebuildRankCharmItemViewModel(item)
        if not success and item == equippedItem then
            return false, err
        end
    end

    return true
end

function State.installRankCharmSpoofer()
    if State.rankCharmFormatHook then
        return true
    end

    local modules = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Modules")
    local seasonLibraryModule = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("SeasonLibrary")
    if not seasonLibraryModule then
        return false, "SeasonLibrary was not found"
    end

    local ok, seasonLibrary = pcall(require, seasonLibraryModule)
    if not ok or type(seasonLibrary) ~= "table" or type(seasonLibrary.FormatSeasonRankCharm) ~= "function" then
        return false, ok and "FormatSeasonRankCharm was not found" or seasonLibrary
    end

    local originalFormat = seasonLibrary.FormatSeasonRankCharm
    local formatHook
    formatHook = function(self, model, seasonName, elo, leaderboardRank)
        if State.rankCharmSpoofEnabled then
            elo, leaderboardRank = getRankCharmSpoofValues(self, seasonName)
        end
        return originalFormat(self, model, seasonName, elo, leaderboardRank)
    end

    State.rankCharmSeasonLibrary = seasonLibrary
    State.rankCharmOriginalFormat = originalFormat
    State.rankCharmFormatHook = formatHook
    seasonLibrary.FormatSeasonRankCharm = formatHook
    return true
end

function State.refreshRankCharmSpoof()
    local success, err = State.installRankCharmSpoofer()
    if not success then
        return false, err
    end
    return refreshRankCharmViewModels()
end

function State.disableRankCharmSpoofer()
    local hadHook = State.rankCharmFormatHook ~= nil
    State.rankCharmSpoofEnabled = false

    local seasonLibrary = State.rankCharmSeasonLibrary
    if seasonLibrary
        and State.rankCharmOriginalFormat
        and seasonLibrary.FormatSeasonRankCharm == State.rankCharmFormatHook
    then
        seasonLibrary.FormatSeasonRankCharm = State.rankCharmOriginalFormat
    end

    State.rankCharmSeasonLibrary = nil
    State.rankCharmOriginalFormat = nil
    State.rankCharmFormatHook = nil
    if not hadHook then
        return true
    end
    return refreshRankCharmViewModels()
end

function State.enableSnowEffect()
    if State.snowEffectGui then return end
    State.snowEffectGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    State.snowEffectGui.Name = "SnowEffect"
    State.snowEffectGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    State.snowEffectGui.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui")

    State.snowEffectConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not State.snowEffectEnabled then return end
        if (State.snowActiveCount or 0) >= (State.snowMaxActive or 120) then return end
        if math.random(1, State.snowFrequency) == 1 then
            local size = State.snowSize or 2
            local snow = Instance.new("Frame")
            snow.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
            snow.Position = UDim2.new(math.random(), 0, 0, -10)
            snow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
            snow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
            snow.Parent = State.snowEffectGui
            State.snowActiveCount = (State.snowActiveCount or 0) + 1
            local tween = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(snow, TweenInfo.new(5), {Position = UDim2.new(snow.Position.X.Scale, 0, 1, 10)})
            tween:Play()
            tween.Completed:Connect(function()
                State.snowActiveCount = math.max((State.snowActiveCount or 1) - 1, 0)
                snow:Destroy()
            end)
        end
    end)
end

function State.disableSnowEffect()
    if State.snowEffectConnection then
        State.snowEffectConnection:Disconnect()
        State.snowEffectConnection = nil
    end
    if State.snowEffectGui then
        State.snowEffectGui:Destroy()
        State.snowEffectGui = nil
    end
    State.snowActiveCount = 0
end

local function scaleModelProper(model, force)
    if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then return end
    local lp = Players.LocalPlayer
    if not lp then return end
    if not tostring(model.Name):find(lp.Name) then return end
    if not force and model:GetAttribute("BigGunScaled") and model:GetAttribute("BigGunMultiplier") == State.bigGunMultiplier then
        return
    end
    if not model:GetAttribute("BigGunScaled") then
        model:SetAttribute("BigGunScaled", true)
    end
    model:SetAttribute("BigGunMultiplier", State.bigGunMultiplier)

    local pivot = model:GetPivot()
    for _, part in ipairs(model:GetDescendants()) do
        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function()
                local origSize = part:GetAttribute("OrigSize") or part.Size
                local origCF = part:GetAttribute("OrigCF") or part.CFrame
                part:SetAttribute("OrigSize", origSize)
                part:SetAttribute("OrigCF", origCF)

                local relative = pivot:ToObjectSpace(origCF)
                part.Size = origSize * State.bigGunMultiplier
                part.CFrame = pivot * CFrame.new(relative.Position * State.bigGunMultiplier) * (relative - relative.Position)
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function hookViewModels()
    pcall(function()
        local vm = workspace:FindFirstChild("ViewModels")
        if vm then
            for _, v in ipairs(vm:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("Model") then
                    scaleModelProper(v)
                end
            end
            if not State.bigGunVMDescConn then
                State.bigGunVMDescConn = vm.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
                    local m = inst
                    while m and not m:IsA("Model") do
                        m = m.Parent
                    end
                    if m and m:IsA("Model") then
                        scaleModelProper(m, true)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
    if not State.bigGunVMChildConn then
        State.bigGunVMChildConn = workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c)
            if c and c.Name == "ViewModels" then
                hookViewModels()
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function getWeaponStateItemLibrary()
    if State.weaponStateItemLibrary then
        return State.weaponStateItemLibrary
    end

    local ok, itemLibrary = pcall(function()
        local modules = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules", 10)
        if not modules then return nil end

        local itemLibraryModule = modules:WaitForChild("ItemLibrary", 10)
        if not itemLibraryModule then return nil end

        return require(itemLibraryModule)
    end)

    if ok and itemLibrary then
        State.weaponStateItemLibrary = itemLibrary
        return itemLibrary
    end

    return nil
end

local function refreshWeaponStateUI()
    pcall(function()
        local playerDataController = require(Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerScripts.Controllers.PlayerDataController)
        local currentData = playerDataController.CurrentData
        if currentData then
            currentData:Replicate("WeaponInventory")
        end
    end)
end

function State.applyWeaponStateSpoof()
    local itemLibrary = getWeaponStateItemLibrary()
    if not itemLibrary or type(itemLibrary.Items) ~= "table" then
        return false, "ItemLibrary unavailable"
    end

    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        for weaponName, weaponData in pairs(itemLibrary.Items) do
            if type(weaponData) == "table" and weaponData.Status ~= nil then
                if State.weaponStateOriginalStatuses[weaponName] == nil then
                    State.weaponStateOriginalStatuses[weaponName] = weaponData.Status
                end
                weaponData.Status = State.selectedWeaponState
            end
        end
    end)

    if not ok then
        return false, err
    end

    refreshWeaponStateUI()
    return true
end

function State.enableWeaponStateSpoof()
    State.weaponStateSpoofEnabled = true
    local success, err = State.applyWeaponStateSpoof()
    if not success then
        State.weaponStateSpoofEnabled = false
    end
    return success, err
end

function State.disableWeaponStateSpoof()
    State.weaponStateSpoofEnabled = false

    local itemLibrary = getWeaponStateItemLibrary()
    if itemLibrary and type(itemLibrary.Items) == "table" then
        for weaponName, originalStatus in pairs(State.weaponStateOriginalStatuses) do
            local weaponData = itemLibrary.Items[weaponName]
            if type(weaponData) == "table" then
                weaponData.Status = originalStatus
            end
        end
    end

    State.weaponStateOriginalStatuses = {}
    refreshWeaponStateUI()
end

local function getSetControlsRemote()
    local cached = State.deviceSpoofSetControlsRemote
    if cached and cached.Parent then
        return cached
    end

    local replicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local remotes = replicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
    local replication = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Replication")
    local fighter = replication and replication:FindFirstChild("Fighter")
    local remote = fighter and fighter:FindFirstChild("SetControls")
    State.deviceSpoofSetControlsRemote = remote
    return remote
end

function State.fireDeviceSpoofControls(deviceName)
    local remote = getSetControlsRemote()
    if remote and remote.FireServer then
        pcall(function()
            remote:FireServer(deviceName)
        end)
    end
end

function State.enableDeviceSpoof()
    State.deviceSpoofEnabled = true
    local inputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
    local originalControls = "MouseKeyboard"
    if inputService.TouchEnabled then
        originalControls = "Touch"
    elseif inputService.GamepadEnabled then
        originalControls = "Gamepad"
    end
    State.deviceSpoofOriginalControls = originalControls

    local targetDevice = DeviceSpoofControls[State.selectedDevice]

    if targetDevice then
        State.fireDeviceSpoofControls(targetDevice)
    end

    pcall(function()
        local constants = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            :WaitForChild("Modules")
            :WaitForChild("CONSTANTS"))

        if constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES then
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VR == nil then
                State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VR = constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.VR
            end
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.Car == nil then
                State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.Car = constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.Car
            end
        end
        if constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED then
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VRCentered == nil then
                State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VRCentered = constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.VR
            end
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.CarCentered == nil then
                State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.CarCentered = constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.Car
            end
        end

        local carImage = State.deviceSpoofImage
        if constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES then
            if State.selectedDevice == "Car" then
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.VR = carImage
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.Car = carImage
            else
                if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VR then
                    constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.VR = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VR
                end
                if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.Car then
                    constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.Car = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.Car
                end
            end
        end

        if constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED then
            if State.selectedDevice == "Car" then
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.VR = carImage
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.Car = carImage
            else
                if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VRCentered then
                    constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.VR = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VRCentered
                end
                if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.CarCentered then
                    constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.Car = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.CarCentered
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

function State.disableDeviceSpoof()
    State.deviceSpoofEnabled = false
    pcall(function()
        local constants = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            :WaitForChild("Modules")
            :WaitForChild("CONSTANTS"))

        if constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES then
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VR then
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.VR = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VR
            end
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.Car then
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES.Car = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.Car
            end
        end
        if constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED then
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VRCentered then
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.VR = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.VRCentered
            end
            if State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.CarCentered then
                constants.CONTROLS_IMAGES_CENTERED.Car = State.deviceSpoofOriginalImages.CarCentered
            end
        end
    end)

    if State.deviceSpoofOriginalControls then
        State.fireDeviceSpoofControls(State.deviceSpoofOriginalControls)
    end
end

function State.changeDevice(device)
    State.selectedDevice = device
    if State.deviceSpoofEnabled then
        State.disableDeviceSpoof()
        State.enableDeviceSpoof()
    end
end

function State.setupAntiFlashbang()
    pcall(function()
        local player = Players.LocalPlayer
        local Flashed = require(player.PlayerScripts.Modules.ClientReplicatedClasses.ClientFighter.FighterInterface.Flashed)
        Flashed.Flash = function() end
    end)
end

function State.enableThirdPerson()
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local cameraController = nil
    local successCamera = pcall(function()
        cameraController = require(player.PlayerScripts.Controllers.CameraController)
    end)
    if not successCamera then return end
    if cameraController and cameraController.CameraState then
        if true then
            cameraController.CameraState:_SetPOVState(cameraController.CameraState.States.ThirdPerson)
        else
            cameraController.CameraState:_SetPOVState(cameraController.CameraState.States.FirstPerson)
        end
    end
    if Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
        task.wait(0.1)
        if cameraController and cameraController.CameraState then
            cameraController.CameraState:_SetPOVState(cameraController.CameraState.States.ThirdPerson)
        end
    end
    State.thirdPersonConnection = Players.LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.1)
        if cameraController and cameraController.CameraState then
            cameraController.CameraState:_SetPOVState(cameraController.CameraState.States.ThirdPerson)
        end
    end)
end

function State.disableThirdPerson()
    local player = Players.LocalPlayer
    local cameraController = nil
    local successCamera = pcall(function()
        cameraController = require(player.PlayerScripts.Controllers.CameraController)
    end)
    if not successCamera then return end
    if cameraController and cameraController.CameraState then
        cameraController.CameraState:_SetPOVState(cameraController.CameraState.States.FirstPerson)
    end
    if State.thirdPersonConnection then
        State.thirdPersonConnection:Disconnect()
        State.thirdPersonConnection = nil
    end
end

function State.setupColorCorrection()
    if not State.ccEffect then
        State.ccEffect = Instance.new("ColorCorrectionEffect")
        State.ccEffect.Parent = Lighting
    end
    State.ccEffect.TintColor = State.ccTintColor
    State.ccEffect.Saturation = State.ccSaturation
    State.ccEffect.Contrast = State.ccContrast
    State.ccEffect.Brightness = State.ccBrightness
end

function State.removeColorCorrection()
    if State.ccEffect then
        State.ccEffect:Destroy()
        State.ccEffect = nil
    end
end

function State.setupNoclip()
    local lastNoclipUpdate = 0
    RunService:BindToRenderStep("Noclip", 0, function()
        if not State.noclipEnabled then return end
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastNoclipUpdate < 0.15 then return end
        lastNoclipUpdate = now
        local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        for _, part in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
    end)
end

function State.removeNoclip()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Noclip")
    if Players.LocalPlayer.Character then
        for _, part in pairs(Players.LocalPlayer.Character:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then part.CanCollide = true end
        end
    end
end

function State.isTriggerbotKeyActive()
    if Options and Options.TriggerbotKeybind then
        return getKeyPickerActive(Options.TriggerbotKeybind, true)
    end

    if not State.triggerbotKeybind or State.triggerbotKeybind == "None" then
        return true
    end

    if typeof(State.triggerbotKeybind) == "EnumItem" then
        if State.triggerbotKeybind.EnumType == Enum.KeyCode then
            return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(State.triggerbotKeybind) and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
        end

        if State.triggerbotKeybind.EnumType == Enum.UserInputType then
            return UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(State.triggerbotKeybind) and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
        end
    end

    if State.triggerbotKeybind == "MB1" then
        return UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1) and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
    elseif State.triggerbotKeybind == "MB2" then
        return UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2) and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
    elseif State.triggerbotKeybind == "MB3" then
        return UserInputService:IsMouseButtonPressed(Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3) and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
    end

    local success, keyCode = pcall(function()
        return Enum.KeyCode[State.triggerbotKeybind] or Enum.KeyCode[tostring(State.triggerbotKeybind):gsub("%s+", "")]
    end)

    return success and keyCode and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(keyCode) and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox()
end

function State.isTriggerbotBlacklistedPart(part)
    if not part or type(State.triggerbotBlacklistParts) ~= "table" then
        return false
    end

    while part and part ~= workspace do
        if State.triggerbotBlacklistParts[part.Name] or table.find(State.triggerbotBlacklistParts, part.Name) then
            return true
        end

        part = part.Parent
    end

    return false
end

function State.getTriggerbotTarget(hit)
    if not hit then
        return nil, nil, nil
    end

    local model = hit:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    local hum = model and (model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or model:FindFirstChild("Humanoid"))

    if not hum and hit.Parent then
        hum = hit.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or hit.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        model = hum and hit.Parent or model
    end

    if not hum and hit.Parent and hit.Parent.Parent then
        hum = hit.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") or hit.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        model = hum and hit.Parent.Parent or model
    end

    return model, hum, model and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
end

function State.clearTriggerbotTarget()
    State.triggerbotLastTarget = nil
    State.triggerbotLastTargetPart = nil
end

local lastTriggerTime = 0
function State.setupTriggerbot()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Triggerbot")
    RunService:BindToRenderStep("Triggerbot", 0, function()
        if not State.triggerbotEnabled or not State.isTriggerbotKeyActive() then
            State.clearTriggerbotTarget()
            return
        end

        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            State.clearTriggerbotTarget()
            return
        end

        local mousePos = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
        local ray = camera:ViewportPointToRay(mousePos.X, mousePos.Y)
        local params = State.triggerbotRaycastParams or RaycastParams.new()
        State.triggerbotRaycastParams = params
        params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Blacklist
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances = Players.LocalPlayer.Character and {Players.LocalPlayer.Character} or {}

        local result = rawWorkspaceRaycast(ray.Origin, ray.Direction * 1000, params)
        if not result or not result.Instance or State.isTriggerbotBlacklistedPart(result.Instance) then
            State.clearTriggerbotTarget()
            return
        end

        local hit = result.Instance
        local targetModel, hum, targetPlayer = State.getTriggerbotTarget(hit)
        if not hum or targetModel == Players.LocalPlayer.Character or hum.Health <= 0.1
           or (State.teamCheckEnabled and targetPlayer and Core.isSameTeam(targetPlayer)) then
            State.clearTriggerbotTarget()
            return
        end

        local targetKey = targetPlayer or targetModel
        local sameTarget = State.triggerbotLastTarget == targetKey
        local now = tick()
        local reactionDelay = (State.triggerbotReactionTime or 50) / 1000

        if not sameTarget and now - lastTriggerTime < reactionDelay then
            return
        end

        lastTriggerTime = now
        State.triggerbotLastTarget = targetKey
        State.triggerbotLastTargetPart = hit

        pcall(function()
            mouse1press()
            task.delay(0.01, mouse1release)
        end)
    end)
end

function State.removeTriggerbot()
    RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Triggerbot")
    State.clearTriggerbotTarget()
end

function getRageRoot(char)
    return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

function ragePlayerIsDead(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    return not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or not getRageRoot(char)
end

function rageIsInvincible(plr)
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    if not char then return true end

    local root = getRageRoot(char)
    if not root then return true end

    for _, obj in root:GetChildren() do
        if obj:IsA("Attachment") and obj.Name == "Attachment" then
            return true
        end
    end

    return char:FindFirstChild("InvincibilityParticles", true) ~= nil
end

function rageIsValidMatch(plr, lplr)
    return plr:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID") == lplr:GetAttribute("EnvironmentID")
end

function shouldSkipRageTarget(plr, lplr)
    if plr == lplr or ragePlayerIsDead(plr) then return true end
    if not rageIsValidMatch(plr, lplr) then return true end
    if rageIsInvincible(plr) then return true end

    local root = getRageRoot(plr.Character)
    return root and root:FindFirstChild("TeammateLabel") ~= nil
end

function isRageHackerTarget(plr, lplr, targetRoot, localRoot)
    if plr == lplr or ragePlayerIsDead(plr) then return false end
    if not rageIsValidMatch(plr, lplr) then return false end
    if not targetRoot or not localRoot then return false end

    local distance = (targetRoot.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude
    local speed = targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Magnitude

    if (distance > 1200 and speed > 140) or (distance > 800 and speed > 260) or speed > 420 then
        return true
    end

    return false
end

function shouldBlockRageShot(target)
    local blockers = {
        "ShouldPerfectBlock",
        "ShouldBlockShotForPerfectBlock",
        "ShouldBlockShotForKatana",
    }

    for _, name in ipairs(blockers) do
        local blocker = rawget(_G, name)
        if type(blocker) == "function" then
            local ok, blocked = pcall(blocker, target)
            if ok and blocked == true then
                return true
            end
        end
    end

    local states = {
        rawget(_G, "PerfectBlockState"),
        rawget(_G, "AntiKatanaState"),
    }
    local targetPlayer = typeof(target) == "Instance" and (target:IsA("Player") and target or Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(target)) or nil
    local targetUserId = targetPlayer and targetPlayer.UserId
    local now = tick()

    for _, state in ipairs(states) do
        if type(state) == "table" and state.Enabled and type(state.DeflectingEnemies) == "table" then
            for key, expireTime in pairs(state.DeflectingEnemies) do
                if now >= expireTime then
                    state.DeflectingEnemies[key] = nil
                elseif not targetUserId or key == targetUserId then
                    return true
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return false
end

function getBestRageTarget(players, lplr)
    local root = getRageRoot(lplr.Character)
    if not root then return nil end

    local targetMode = State.rageTargetMode or "Closest"
    local useHealth = targetMode == "Lowest Health"
    local preferHackers = targetMode == "Hackers"
    local best, bestValue = nil, math.huge
    local bestHacker, bestHackerValue = nil, math.huge
    local playerList = players:GetPlayers()

    for i = 1, #playerList do
        local plr = playerList[i]
        if not shouldSkipRageTarget(plr, lplr) then
            local char = plr.Character
            local targetRoot = getRageRoot(char)
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if targetRoot and hum then
                local value = useHealth and hum.Health or (targetRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                if preferHackers and isRageHackerTarget(plr, lplr, targetRoot, root) then
                    if value < bestHackerValue then
                        bestHackerValue = value
                        bestHacker = plr
                    end
                elseif value < bestValue then
                    bestValue = value
                    best = plr
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if preferHackers and bestHacker then
        return bestHacker
    end

    return best
end

function getRageTargetHitbox(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer or not targetPlayer.Character then
        return nil
    end

    local char = targetPlayer.Character
    if not char.Parent then
        return nil
    end

    local preferredHitbox = char:FindFirstChild("HitboxHeadSmall")
        or char:FindFirstChild("HitboxHead")
        or char:FindFirstChild("PhysicalHitboxHead")
        or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    
    if preferredHitbox and preferredHitbox:IsA("BasePart") and preferredHitbox.Parent then
        return preferredHitbox
    end
    
    return nil
end

function initRageBot()
    local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
    local lplr = Players.LocalPlayer

    local utilModule = rs:FindFirstChild("Modules")
    if not utilModule then return false, "Modules not found" end

    local util = utilModule:FindFirstChild("Utility")
    if not util then return false, "Utility not found" end

    local enumsModule = rs.Modules:FindFirstChild("EnumLibrary")
    if not enumsModule then return false, "EnumLibrary not found" end

    local fighterController = lplr.PlayerScripts:FindFirstChild("Controllers")
    if not fighterController then return false, "Controllers not found" end

    local fighter = fighterController:FindFirstChild("FighterController")
    if not fighter then return false, "FighterController not found" end

    local utilReq = require(util)
    local enums = require(enumsModule)
    fighterReq = require(fighter)

    local currentTarget = nil
    local lastLogTime = 0
    local lastTargetCheck = 0
    local fpsUpdateElapsed = 0
    local fpsUpdateFrameCount = 0
    local cachedFps = 60
    local fpsUpdateInterval = 10
    local lastHeartbeat = nil
    local lastAttackFire = 0
    local lastPreferredEquip = 0
    local lastAutoSwitchInput = 0
    local rageBotDesyncExpire = 0
    local rageHeartbeatSequence = 0
    local rageAttackPendingHeartbeat = nil
    local rageAttackPendingSince = nil
    local rageAttackPendingTarget = nil
    local rageAttackPendingMode = nil
    local rageAttackTurnEndHeartbeat = nil
    local rageAttackFiredThisTurn = false
    local rageBotPhase = State.voidSpamEnabled and "Hide" or "Attack"
    local rageBotPhaseExpire = 0
    local rageVoidMethodBaseX = math.random(-1e8, 1e8)
    local rageVoidMethodBaseZ = math.random(-1e8, 1e8)
    local rageVoidMethodYBase = 1e9 + math.random(-5e8, 5e8)
    local rageVoidMethodOffset = 0
    local rageVoidFlickerToggle = false

    local function resetRageAttackWait()
        rageAttackPendingHeartbeat = nil
        rageAttackPendingSince = nil
        rageAttackPendingTarget = nil
        rageAttackPendingMode = nil
    end

    local function resetRageAttackTurnState()
        rageAttackTurnEndHeartbeat = nil
        rageAttackFiredThisTurn = false
        resetRageAttackWait()
    end

    local function markRageAttackFired()
        if State.voidSpamEnabled and rageBotPhase == "Attack" then
            rageAttackFiredThisTurn = true
            rageBotPhaseExpire = tick() + math.clamp(State.rageAttackTime or 0.01, 0.01, 1.0)
        end
        resetRageAttackWait()
    end

    local function resetClientData()
        State.rageBotClientC = nil
        State.rageBotClientV = nil
        State.rageBotClientVA = nil
        rageBotDesyncExpire = 0
        resetRageAttackTurnState()
    end

    local rageBotDesyncLocalCFrame = nil
    local rageBotDesyncLocalV = Vector3.zero
    local rageBotDesyncLocalAV = Vector3.zero

    local function scheduleRageBotDesync()
        rageBotDesyncExpire = tick() + 0.05
    end

    local function startRageBotDesyncSync()
        if State.rageBotDesyncConnection then
            return
        end

        State.rageBotDesyncConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not State.rageBotEnabled then
                return
            end

            local char = lplr.Character
            if not char then
                return
            end

            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then
                return
            end

            rageBotDesyncLocalCFrame = hrp.CFrame
            rageBotDesyncLocalV = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            rageBotDesyncLocalAV = hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity

            if State.rageBotClientC then
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.CFrame = State.rageBotClientC
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = State.rageBotClientV or Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = State.rageBotClientVA or Vector3.zero
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    State.rageBotTrackingConnections = State.rageBotTrackingConnections or {}

    local function track(p)
        local charConn = p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            if p == lplr then
                resetClientData()
            end
        end)
        table.insert(State.rageBotTrackingConnections, charConn)

        if p == lplr and p.Character then
            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                local diedConn = hum.Died:Connect(function()
                    resetClientData()
                end)
                table.insert(State.rageBotTrackingConnections, diedConn)
            end
        end
    end

    table.insert(State.rageBotTrackingConnections, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(track))
    local playerList = Players:GetPlayers()
    for i = 1, #playerList do
        track(playerList[i])
    end

    local function randLarge()
        return (math.random() * 2 - 1) * 2147483646
    end

    local function betterRandom(mi, ma, dmi, dma)
        local val = math.random(mi, ma)
        repeat
            val = math.random(mi, ma)
        until val < dmi or val > dma
        return val
    end

    local function updateTarget(now)

        if currentTarget then
            if ragePlayerIsDead(currentTarget) then
                currentTarget = nil
                resetClientData()
            elseif not currentTarget.Character then
                currentTarget = nil
                resetClientData()
            end
        end

        if currentTarget and rageIsInvincible(currentTarget) then
            resetClientData()
            return false
        end

        local targetCheckInterval = 0.05
        if now - lastTargetCheck >= targetCheckInterval
            and (State.rageAutoSwitch or not currentTarget)
        then
            lastTargetCheck = now
            if State.rageAutoSwitch then
                local target = getBestRageTarget(Players, lplr)
                if target then
                    currentTarget = target
                end
            elseif not currentTarget then
                currentTarget = getBestRageTarget(Players, lplr)
            end
        end

        State.rageBotCurrentTarget = currentTarget
        return true
    end

    local function setRageTpMotion(hrp)
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(randLarge(), randLarge(), randLarge())
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(randLarge(), randLarge(), randLarge())
    end

    local function TpToOffsetAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp)
        if not hrp or not targetHrp then return false end
        
        local offset = Vector3.new(
            State.rageOffsetX or 0,
            State.rageOffsetY or -2,
            State.rageOffsetZ or -1
        )
        local targetPosition = targetHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset)
        
        if isPositionInOOB(targetPosition.Position) then
            return false
        end
        
        State.rageBotClientC = hrp.CFrame
        State.rageBotClientV = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        State.rageBotClientVA = hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        scheduleRageBotDesync()

		local targetCFrame = targetHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(offset)
		hrp.CFrame = State.applyAntiAimToCFrame(CFrame.new(targetCFrame.Position, targetHrp.Position))
        setRageTpMotion(hrp)
        return true
    end

    local function TpToOrbitAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp, deltaTime)
        if not hrp or not targetHrp then return false end
        
        local baseRadius = State.rageOrbitDistance or 3
        local radius = baseRadius
        if isRageMeleeMode() then
            radius = math.max(0.5, baseRadius - 0.5)
        end

        local speed = math.max(1, State.rageOrbitSpeed or 80)
        State.rageOrbitAngle = ((State.rageOrbitAngle or 0) + (deltaTime or 0.016) * speed) % (math.pi * 2)

        local angle = State.rageOrbitAngle
        local offset = Vector3.new(math.cos(angle) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle) * radius)
        local orbitPosition = targetHrp.Position + offset
        
        State.rageBotClientC = hrp.CFrame
        State.rageBotClientV = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        State.rageBotClientVA = hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        scheduleRageBotDesync()

		hrp.CFrame = State.applyAntiAimToCFrame(CFrame.new(orbitPosition, targetHrp.Position))
        setRageTpMotion(hrp)
        return true
    end

    local function isPositionInOOB(position)
        if not position then return false end

		local ok, inside = pcall(function()
			if utilReq and type(utilReq.IsWithinPart) == "function" then
				return utilReq:IsWithinPart(position)
			end
			return false
		end)
		if ok and inside then
			return true
		end

		local cs = game:GetService("CollectionService")
		local ok2, oobParts = pcall(function()
			return cs:GetTagged("OutOfBoundsPart")
		end)
		if ok2 and oobParts then
			for _, part in ipairs(oobParts) do
