				if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
					local size = part.Size
					local cf = part.CFrame
					local rel = cf:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(position))
					if math.abs(rel.X) <= size.X/2 and math.abs(rel.Y) <= size.Y/2 and math.abs(rel.Z) <= size.Z/2 then
						return true
					end
				end
			end
		end

		return false
    end

    local function computeRageVoidTarget(hrp)
        local method = State.rageVoidMethod or "Random"
        local t = tick()
        local baseX = rageVoidMethodBaseX
        local baseZ = rageVoidMethodBaseZ
        local baseY = rageVoidMethodYBase
        local radius = 1e9
        local x, y, z = baseX, baseY, baseZ
        local vel = Vector3.zero
        local ang = Vector3.zero

        if method == "Random" then
            x = betterRandom(-2147483646, 2147483646, -1147483646, 1147483646)
            y = betterRandom(-2147483646, 2147483646, -1147483646, 1147483646)
            z = betterRandom(-2147483646, 2147483646, -1147483646, 1147483646)
        elseif method == "Stable" then
            x = baseX
            y = baseY
            z = baseZ
        elseif method == "Drift" then
            local dx = math.noise(t * 0.00004, 0) * radius * 0.3
            local dz = math.noise(0, t * 0.00004) * radius * 0.3
            x = baseX + dx
            z = baseZ + dz
            rageVoidMethodOffset = (rageVoidMethodOffset + 0.016) % (math.pi * 2)
        elseif method == "Chaotic" then
            x = baseX + (math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 0.6
            y = baseY + (math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 0.2
            z = baseZ + (math.random() - 0.5) * radius * 0.6
        elseif method == "Circle" then
            x = baseX + math.cos(t * 0.5) * radius
            z = baseZ + math.sin(t * 0.5) * radius
        elseif method == "Spiral" then
            local r = radius * (1 + math.sin(t * 0.1) * 0.2)
            x = baseX + math.cos(t * 0.5) * r
            z = baseZ + math.sin(t * 0.5) * r
            y = baseY + math.sin(t * 0.2) * 2e8
        elseif method == "Lissajous" then
            x = baseX + math.sin(t * 0.4) * radius
            z = baseZ + math.sin(t * 0.6) * radius
        elseif method == "Perlin Noise" then
            x = baseX + math.noise(t * 0.0001, 0) * radius
            z = baseZ + math.noise(0, t * 0.0001) * radius
        elseif method == "Flicker Void" then
            rageVoidFlickerToggle = not rageVoidFlickerToggle
            if rageVoidFlickerToggle then
                x = baseX + radius
                z = baseZ
            else
                x = baseX - radius
                z = baseZ
            end
            y = baseY + (rageVoidFlickerToggle and 1e8 or -1e8)
        elseif method == "Gravity Well" then
            local r = radius * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(t * 0.3))
            x = baseX + math.cos(t * 0.4) * r
            z = baseZ + math.sin(t * 0.4) * r
            y = baseY + math.sin(t * 0.8) * 1e8
        elseif method == "Extreme Desync" then
            x = baseX + math.sin(t * 5) * 1e11
            y = baseY + math.cos(t * 3) * 1e10
            z = baseZ + math.cos(t * 5) * 1e11
        elseif method == "Quantum Oscillation" then
            x = baseX + math.sin(t * 10) * 1e10
            y = baseY + math.cos(t * 6) * 1e10
            z = baseZ + math.cos(t * 10) * 1e10
        elseif method == "Frame Skip" then
            if math.floor(t * 10) % 2 == 0 then
                x = hrp.Position.X
                y = hrp.Position.Y
                z = hrp.Position.Z
            else
                x = baseX + radius
                y = baseY
                z = baseZ + radius
            end
        else
            x = betterRandom(-2147483646, 2147483646, -1147483646, 1147483646)
            y = betterRandom(-2147483646, 2147483646, -1147483646, 1147483646)
            z = betterRandom(-2147483646, 2147483646, -1147483646, 1147483646)
        end

        local targetPos = Vector3.new(x, y, z)
        if isPositionInOOB(targetPos) then
            return nil, nil, nil
        end

        return CFrame.new(x, y, z), vel, ang
    end

    local function TpToBackstabAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp)
        if not hrp or not targetHrp then return false end

        local behind = targetHrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.15, State.alwaysBackstabOffset or 2.75)
        if isPositionInOOB(behind.Position) then
            return false
        end

        State.rageBotClientC = hrp.CFrame
        State.rageBotClientV = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        State.rageBotClientVA = hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity
        scheduleRageBotDesync()

        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(behind.Position, targetHrp.Position)
        setRageTpMotion(hrp)
        return true
    end

    local function TpToAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp, deltaTime)
        local attackMode = isRageKnifeMode() and "Backstab" or (State.rageAttackTpMode or "Offset")
        local samePendingAttack = rageAttackPendingHeartbeat
            and rageAttackPendingTarget == targetHrp
            and rageAttackPendingMode == attackMode

        if not samePendingAttack then
            resetRageAttackWait()
        end

        local teleported
        if attackMode == "Backstab" then
            teleported = TpToBackstabAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp)
        elseif attackMode == "Orbit" then
            teleported = TpToOrbitAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp, deltaTime)
        else
            teleported = TpToOffsetAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp)
        end

        if not teleported then
            resetRageAttackWait()
            return false
        end

        if not samePendingAttack then
            rageAttackPendingHeartbeat = rageHeartbeatSequence
            rageAttackPendingSince = tick()
            rageAttackPendingTarget = targetHrp
            rageAttackPendingMode = attackMode
            return false
        end

        local settleTime = math.max(0, State.rageAttackSettleTime or 0.05)
        if rageHeartbeatSequence > rageAttackPendingHeartbeat
            and tick() - (rageAttackPendingSince or tick()) >= settleTime
        then
            return true
        end

        return false
    end

    local function hasAnyAmmo(item)
        if not item then return false end
        local okA, a = pcall(function() return (item.Get and item:Get("Ammo")) end)
        local okR, r = pcall(function() return (item.Get and item:Get("AmmoReserve")) end)
        if okA and type(a) == "number" and a > 0 then return true end
        if okR and type(r) == "number" and r > 0 then return true end

        return not (okA and type(a) == "number") and not (okR and type(r) == "number")
    end

    local function shouldVoidForReload(item)
        if not item then return false end
        local fighter = fighterReq.LocalFighter
        if fighter and fighter.Get and fighter:Get("Reloading") then
            return true
        end

        local ammo = item.Get and item:Get("Ammo")
        if type(ammo) == "number" and ammo <= 0 then

            local reserve = item.Get and item:Get("AmmoReserve")
            if type(reserve) == "number" and reserve <= 0 then

                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        return false
    end

    local function Shoot(target)

        if not target or not target.Character then return false end
        
        if target ~= currentTarget then return false end
        
        if ragePlayerIsDead(target) then
            currentTarget = nil
            return false
        end
        if shouldBlockRageShot(target) then return false end
        
        if not fighterReq or not fighterReq.LocalFighter then return false end
        local item = fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem
        if not item then return false end
        
        local fired = false
        local success = pcall(function()

            local targetHrp = getRageTargetHitbox(target)
            if not targetHrp or not targetHrp:IsA("BasePart") then return end
            if not targetHrp.Parent then return end
            
            local hitboxPos = targetHrp.Position
            if not hitboxPos or hitboxPos.X ~= hitboxPos.X then return end
            
            local hitboxCFrame = targetHrp.CFrame
            if not hitboxCFrame then return end
            
            local freshTargetHrp = getRageTargetHitbox(target)
            if not freshTargetHrp or freshTargetHrp ~= targetHrp or not freshTargetHrp.Parent then
                return
            end
            
            local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not camera then return end
            
            local cameraCFrame = camera.CFrame
            if isRageKnifeMode() then
                local localCharacter = lplr.Character
                local localRoot = localCharacter and localCharacter:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if localRoot then
                    cameraCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(localRoot.Position, hitboxPos)
                end
            end
            if not cameraCFrame or not cameraCFrame.X or cameraCFrame.X ~= cameraCFrame.X then

                local lplr = Players.LocalPlayer
                if lplr and lplr.Character and lplr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    cameraCFrame = lplr.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                else
                    return
                end
            end
            
			local camCF = CFrame.new(hitboxPos + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0), hitboxPos)
            if not camCF then return end
            
            local cameradata = {[utf8.char(1)] = {
                [utf8.char(0)] = utilReq:EncodeCFrame(cameraCFrame),
                [utf8.char(1)] = utilReq:EncodeCFrame(camCF),
                [utf8.char(2)] = freshTargetHrp,
                [utf8.char(3)] = utilReq:EncodeCFrame(hitboxCFrame:ToObjectSpace(CFrame.new(hitboxPos)))
            }}
            
            if not cameradata then return end
            
            local itemObjectID = item:Get("ObjectID")
            if not itemObjectID then return end
            
            local attackInput = isRageKnifeMode() and "StartAiming" or "StartShooting"
            local shootEnum = enums:ToEnum(attackInput)
            if not shootEnum then return end
            
            if not rs or not rs.Remotes or not rs.Remotes.Replication or not rs.Remotes.Replication.Fighter then
                return
            end
            
            local useItemRemote = rs.Remotes.Replication.Fighter.UseItem
            if not useItemRemote then return end
            
            useItemRemote:FireServer(itemObjectID, shootEnum, cameradata, nil)
            fired = true
        end)

        return success and fired
    end

    local function Void(hrp)
        if not hrp then return end

        pcall(function()
            State.rageBotClientC = hrp.CFrame
            State.rageBotClientV = hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            State.rageBotClientVA = hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity
            scheduleRageBotDesync()

            local targetCFrame, linearVelocity, angularVelocity = computeRageVoidTarget(hrp)
            if targetCFrame then
                hrp.CFrame = targetCFrame
                hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = linearVelocity or Vector3.zero
                hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = angularVelocity or Vector3.zero
            end
        end)
    end

    local function HideRagePosition(hrp)
        resetRageAttackTurnState()
        Void(hrp)
        return true
    end

    local reloadVoidConn = nil
    local function startReloadVoidWatcher()
        if reloadVoidConn then return end
        reloadVoidConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not State.rageBotEnabled or not State.voidSpamEnabled then return end
            if not fighterReq or not fighterReq.LocalFighter then return end
            local ok, reloading = pcall(function()
                return fighterReq.LocalFighter:Get("Reloading")
            end)
            if not ok then reloading = false end
            if not reloading then

                local ok2, ammo = pcall(function()
                    local it = fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem
                    return (it and it.Get and it:Get("Ammo"))
                end)
                if ok2 and type(ammo) == "number" and ammo <= 0 then
                    reloading = true
                end
            end

            if reloading then
                local char = lplr.Character
                if not char then return end
                local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if not hrp then return end
                local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
                if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                    pcall(function() HideRagePosition(hrp) end)
                else

                    State.rageBotClientC = nil
                    State.rageBotClientV = nil
                    State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                    State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive = false
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function stopReloadVoidWatcher()
        if reloadVoidConn then
            reloadVoidConn:Disconnect()
            reloadVoidConn = nil
        end
        State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive = false
    end

    startReloadVoidWatcher()

    local attackHeartbeatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(deltaTime)
        if not State.rageBotEnabled then return end
        rageHeartbeatSequence = rageHeartbeatSequence + 1

        fpsUpdateElapsed = fpsUpdateElapsed + deltaTime
        fpsUpdateFrameCount = fpsUpdateFrameCount + 1
        if fpsUpdateElapsed >= fpsUpdateInterval then
            cachedFps = fpsUpdateFrameCount / fpsUpdateElapsed
            fpsUpdateElapsed = 0
            fpsUpdateFrameCount = 0
        end

        local dynamicInterval = math.max(0.001, 1 / (cachedFps + 50))
        local now = tick()
        if lastHeartbeat and (now - lastHeartbeat) < dynamicInterval then return end
        lastHeartbeat = now

        if now - lastLogTime > 5 then
            lastLogTime = now
        end

        local char = lplr.Character
        if not char then return end

        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end

        if not fighterReq or not fighterReq.LocalFighter then return end
        local item = fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem
        if not item then return end

        local function hasAmmoByName(name)
            if not name or name == "" then return false end
            for _, it in ipairs(fighterReq.LocalFighter.Items or {}) do
                if it and it.Name == name then
                    local a = (it.Get and it:Get("Ammo"))
                    local r = (it.Get and it:Get("AmmoReserve"))
                    if (type(a) == "number" and a > 0) or (type(r) == "number" and r > 0) then
                        return true
                    end
                    if type(a) ~= "number" and type(r) ~= "number" then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
            return false
        end

        local function getRagePreferredWeaponNames()
            local pref = State.ragePreferredWeapon or "Primary"
            if isRageMeleeMode() then
                return getRageMeleeWeaponName(), State.autoLoadoutWeapon1, State.autoLoadoutWeapon2
            end
            if pref == "Primary" then
                return State.autoLoadoutWeapon1, State.autoLoadoutWeapon2, State.autoLoadoutWeapon3
            end
            return State.autoLoadoutWeapon2, State.autoLoadoutWeapon1, State.autoLoadoutWeapon3
        end

        local function equipRagePreferredWeapon()
            if not fighterReq.LocalFighter then return false end
            local item = fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem
            local preferred, alternate, third = getRagePreferredWeaponNames()
            if not preferred or preferred == "" then return false end
            if item and item.Name == preferred and hasAnyAmmo(item) then return false end
            if not hasAmmoByName(preferred) then
                return isRageMeleeMode()
            end
            if tick() - lastPreferredEquip < 0.25 then return false end

            lastPreferredEquip = tick()
            if isRageMeleeMode() then
                pcall(function()
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Three, false, game)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Three, false, game)
                end)
            else
                pcall(function()
                    firePickWeaponsLoadout(preferred, alternate, third, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
                end)
            end

            State.rageBotClientC = nil
            State.rageBotClientV = nil
            State.rageBotClientVA = nil
            task.wait(0.1)
            return true
        end

        local function pressRageWeaponHotkey()
            local pref = State.ragePreferredWeapon or "Primary"
            local desiredName = isRageMeleeMode()
                and getRageMeleeWeaponName()
                or ((pref == "Primary") and State.autoLoadoutWeapon1 or State.autoLoadoutWeapon2)
            local equipped = fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem
            if equipped and equipped.Name == desiredName then
                return
            end
            if tick() - lastAutoSwitchInput < 0.2 then
                return
            end
            lastAutoSwitchInput = tick()

            if isRageMeleeMode() then
                task.defer(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Three, false, game)
                        task.wait(0.1)
                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Three, false, game)
                    end)
                end)
            else
                local key = (pref == "Primary" and Enum.KeyCode.One) or Enum.KeyCode.Two
                task.defer(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, key, false, game)
                        task.wait(0.1)
                        VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, key, false, game)
                    end)
                end)
            end
        end

        if equipRagePreferredWeapon() then
            return
        end

        local targetReady = updateTarget(now)
        local targetHrp = currentTarget and currentTarget.Character and currentTarget.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if shouldVoidForReload(item) then

            local pref = State.ragePreferredWeapon or "Primary"
            local pName, oName
            if isRageMeleeMode() then
                pName = getRageMeleeWeaponName()
                oName = (pref == "Primary") and State.autoLoadoutWeapon1 or State.autoLoadoutWeapon2
            else
                pName = (pref == "Primary") and State.autoLoadoutWeapon1 or State.autoLoadoutWeapon2
                oName = (pref == "Primary") and State.autoLoadoutWeapon2 or State.autoLoadoutWeapon1
            end

            if State.voidSpamEnabled then
                local hrpRef = hrp
                local itemRef = item
                local fighterLocal = fighterReq.LocalFighter

                if not State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive then
                    State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive = true
                    task.spawn(function()
                        local startT = tick()
                        while State.rageBotEnabled and State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive do
                            local reloading = false
                            pcall(function()
                                if fighterLocal and fighterLocal.Get then
                                    reloading = fighterLocal:Get("Reloading")
                                end
                                if not reloading and itemRef and itemRef.Get then
                                    local a = itemRef:Get("Ammo")
                                    if type(a) == "number" and a <= 0 then
                                        reloading = true
                                    end
                                end
                            end)

                            if not reloading then break end

                            if hasAnyAmmo(itemRef) then
                                pcall(function() Void(hrpRef) end)
                            else

                                State.rageBotClientC = nil
                                State.rageBotClientV = nil
                                State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                                State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive = false
                                break
                            end
                            task.wait(math.clamp((State.rageAttackTime or 0.3) * 0.25, 0.01, 0.2))

                            if tick() - startT > 10 then break end
                        end
                        State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive = false
                    end)
                end

                if pName and pName ~= "" then
                    local curAmmo = (item.Get and item:Get("Ammo"))
                    if type(curAmmo) == "number" and curAmmo <= 0 then
                        if oName and oName ~= "" and hasAmmoByName(oName) then
                            pcall(function()
                                firePickWeaponsLoadout(oName, pName, State.autoLoadoutWeapon3, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
                            end)

                            State.rageBotClientC = nil
                            State.rageBotClientV = nil
                            State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            return
                        else
                            pcall(function()
                                firePickWeaponsLoadout(pName, oName or "", State.autoLoadoutWeapon3, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
                            end)

                            State.rageBotClientC = nil
                            State.rageBotClientV = nil
                            State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                            return
                        end
                    else
                        return
                    end
                else
                    return
                end
            else
                if pName and pName ~= "" then
                    local curAmmo = (item.Get and item:Get("Ammo"))
                    if type(curAmmo) == "number" and curAmmo <= 0 then
                        if oName and oName ~= "" and hasAmmoByName(oName) then
                            pcall(function()
                                firePickWeaponsLoadout(oName, pName, State.autoLoadoutWeapon3, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
                            end)

                            State.rageBotClientC = nil
                            State.rageBotClientV = nil
                            State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                            
                            task.spawn(function()
                                local startTime = tick()
                                while State.rageBotEnabled and tick() - startTime < 10 do
                                    local fighter = fighterReq.LocalFighter
                                    local equippedItem = fighter and fighter.EquippedItem
                                    if not shouldVoidForReload(equippedItem) then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    if hasAnyAmmo(equippedItem) then
                                        pcall(function() HideRagePosition(hrp) end)
                                    else
                                        State.rageBotClientC = nil
                                        State.rageBotClientV = nil
                                        State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                                        break
                                    end
                                    task.wait(0.05)
                                end
                            end)
                            
                            task.wait(0.1)
                            return
                        else
                            pcall(function()
                                firePickWeaponsLoadout(pName, oName or "", State.autoLoadoutWeapon3, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
                            end)

                            State.rageBotClientC = nil
                            State.rageBotClientV = nil
                            State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                            local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
                            if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                                HideRagePosition(hrp)
                            else
                                State.rageBotClientC = nil
                                State.rageBotClientV = nil
                                State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                            end
                            return
                        end
                    else
                        local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
                        if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                            HideRagePosition(hrp)
                        else
                            State.rageBotClientC = nil
                            State.rageBotClientV = nil
                            State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                        end
                        return
                    end
                else
                    local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
                    if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                        HideRagePosition(hrp)
                    else
                        State.rageBotClientC = nil
                        State.rageBotClientV = nil
                        State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
        end

        if not targetReady then
            resetRageAttackTurnState()
            return
        end
        State.rageVisualTargetRoot = (not isRageKnifeMode() and State.rageAttackTpMode == "Orbit" and targetHrp) or nil

        local attackDuration = math.clamp(State.rageAttackTime or 0.01, 0.01, 1.0)
        local hideDuration = math.clamp(State.rageVoidTime or 0.01, 0.01, 1.0)

        if State.voidSpamEnabled then
            if rageBotPhaseExpire == 0 then
                rageBotPhase = "Hide"
                rageBotPhaseExpire = now + hideDuration
                resetRageAttackTurnState()
            elseif now >= rageBotPhaseExpire then
                if rageBotPhase == "Hide" then
                    rageBotPhase = "Attack"
                    rageBotPhaseExpire = now + attackDuration
                    resetRageAttackTurnState()
                elseif rageAttackPendingHeartbeat and not rageAttackFiredThisTurn then
                    rageBotPhaseExpire = now + attackDuration
                elseif not rageAttackTurnEndHeartbeat then
                    rageAttackTurnEndHeartbeat = rageHeartbeatSequence
                    return
                elseif rageHeartbeatSequence <= rageAttackTurnEndHeartbeat then
                    return
                else
                    rageBotPhase = "Hide"
                    rageBotPhaseExpire = now + hideDuration
                    resetRageAttackTurnState()
                end
            end
        else
            rageBotPhase = "Attack"
            rageBotPhaseExpire = now + attackDuration
            rageAttackTurnEndHeartbeat = nil
            rageAttackFiredThisTurn = false
        end

        local inHidePhase = State.voidSpamEnabled and rageBotPhase == "Hide"
        local inAttackPhase = not State.voidSpamEnabled or rageBotPhase == "Attack"

        if inHidePhase or not targetHrp then
            resetRageAttackTurnState()
            local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
            if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                HideRagePosition(hrp)
            else
                State.rageBotClientC = nil
                State.rageBotClientV = nil
                State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                State._rageVoidReloadSpamActive = false
            end
            return
        end

        if State.voidSpamEnabled and rageAttackFiredThisTurn then
            return
        end

        local antiWeaponAction = nil
        if State.antiWeaponEnabled and currentTarget and AntiWeapon:IsAntiWeapon(currentTarget) then
            if AntiWeapon:IsKatana(currentTarget) then
                antiWeaponAction = "Void"
            elseif AntiWeapon:IsShield(currentTarget) then
                antiWeaponAction = "AttackOnBehind"
            else
                antiWeaponAction = "Void"
            end
        end
        local shouldAvoidAttack = (antiWeaponAction ~= nil)

        pressRageWeaponHotkey()
        local attackInterval = isRageKnifeMode() and 1.2 or (isRageMeleeMode() and 0.03 or 0.0001)

        if targetHrp then
            if shouldAvoidAttack and antiWeaponAction == "AttackOnBehind" then
                resetRageAttackWait()
                local targetCFrame = targetHrp.CFrame
                local behind = targetCFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0.15, 3)
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(behind.Position, targetHrp.Position)
                if now - lastAttackFire > attackInterval then
                    lastAttackFire = now

                    if currentTarget
                        and currentTarget.Character
                        and getRageTargetHitbox(currentTarget)
                        and Shoot(currentTarget)
                    then
                        markRageAttackFired()
                    end
                end
            elseif shouldAvoidAttack and antiWeaponAction == "Void" then
                resetRageAttackWait()
                local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
                if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                    HideRagePosition(hrp)
                else
                    State.rageBotClientC = nil
                    State.rageBotClientV = nil
                    State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                end
            else
                local attackPositionReady = TpToAttackpos(hrp, targetHrp, deltaTime)
                if not attackPositionReady then
                    return
                end
                if now - lastAttackFire > attackInterval then
                    local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
                    if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                        lastAttackFire = now

                        if currentTarget
                            and currentTarget.Character
                            and getRageTargetHitbox(currentTarget)
                            and Shoot(currentTarget)
                        then
                            markRageAttackFired()
                        end
                    else
                        resetRageAttackWait()
                        State.rageBotClientC = nil
                        State.rageBotClientV = nil
                        State.rageBotClientVA = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            resetRageAttackWait()
            local equipped = (fighterReq.LocalFighter and fighterReq.LocalFighter.EquippedItem)
            if hasAnyAmmo(equipped) then
                HideRagePosition(hrp)
            else
                State.rageBotClientC = nil
                State.rageBotClientV = nil
                State.rageBotClientVA = nil
            end
        end
    end)

    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("DesyncSync")
    end)
     RunService:BindToRenderStep("DesyncSync", Enum.RenderPriority.First.Value, function()
        if not State.rageBotEnabled then return end
        if rageBotDesyncLocalCFrame and lplr.Character then
            local hrp = lplr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                pcall(function()
                    hrp.CFrame = rageBotDesyncLocalCFrame
                    hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = rageBotDesyncLocalV or Vector3.zero
                    hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = rageBotDesyncLocalAV or Vector3.zero
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

    startRageBotDesyncSync()
    State.rageBotConnections = {attackHeartbeatConn}
    return true
end

State.rageBotMaxAttempts = 50
State.rageBotAttempt = 0
State.rageBotRetryConnection = nil

function State.startRageBot()
    if State.rageBotEnabled then
        return false, "Already running"
    end
    
    State.rageBotEnabled = true
    State.rageBotAttempt = 0
    pcall(applyRageLoadoutSelection)
    
    local success, err = initRageBot()
    
    if success then
		return true
    end
    
    if State.rageBotRetryConnection then
        State.rageBotRetryConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    
    local lastRageRetry = 0
    State.rageBotRetryConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if not State.rageBotEnabled then
            State.rageBotRetryConnection:Disconnect()
            return
        end
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastRageRetry < 0.2 then return end
        lastRageRetry = now
        
        State.rageBotAttempt = State.rageBotAttempt + 1
        if State.rageBotAttempt >= State.rageBotMaxAttempts then
            State.rageBotRetryConnection:Disconnect()
            State.rageBotRetryConnection = nil
            State.rageBotEnabled = false
            return
        end
        
        local success2, err2 = initRageBot()
        if success2 then
            pcall(applyRageLoadoutSelection)
            State.rageBotRetryConnection:Disconnect()
            State.rageBotRetryConnection = nil
        end
    end)
    
    return false, err
end

function State.stopRageBot()
    State.rageBotEnabled = false

    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("DesyncSync") end)

    if State.rageBotRetryConnection then
        State.rageBotRetryConnection:Disconnect()
        State.rageBotRetryConnection = nil
    end

    if State.rageBotDesyncConnection then
        State.rageBotDesyncConnection:Disconnect()
        State.rageBotDesyncConnection = nil
    end

    if State.rageBotConnections then
        for _, conn in ipairs(State.rageBotConnections) do
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
        end
        State.rageBotConnections = {}
    end

    State.rageBotClientC = nil
    State.rageBotClientV = nil
    State.rageBotClientVA = nil
    State.rageBotCurrentTarget = nil
    State.rageVisualTargetRoot = nil

    task.wait(0.3)

    pcall(function()
        local char = Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        if char then
            char:PivotTo(char:GetPivot())
        end
    end)
end

function State.cleanupRageBot()
    State.stopRageBot()
    State.rageBotEnabled = false
    State.rageBotConnections = {}
    State.rageBotClientC = nil
    State.rageBotClientV = nil
    State.rageBotClientVA = nil
    State.rageBotCurrentTarget = nil
    State.rageVisualTargetRoot = nil
end

function State.enablePerfectBlock()
    if State.perfectBlockEnabled then return end
    State.perfectBlockEnabled = true
    
    local Players = game:GetService("Players")
    local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local lplr = Players.LocalPlayer
    local char = lplr and lplr.Character
    if not char then 
        State.perfectBlockEnabled = false
        return
    end
    
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then 
        State.perfectBlockEnabled = false
        return
    end
    
    if State.perfectBlockConnection then
        State.perfectBlockConnection:Disconnect()
    end
    
    local lastPerfectBlockUpdate = 0
    State.perfectBlockConnection = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local now = tick()
        if now - lastPerfectBlockUpdate < 0.05 then return end
        lastPerfectBlockUpdate = now

        if not State.perfectBlockEnabled then
            State.perfectBlockConnection:Disconnect()
            State.perfectBlockConnection = nil
            return
        end
        
        local char = lplr and lplr.Character
        if not char then return end
        
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return end
        
        local target = State.getClosest()
        if not target or not target.Character then return end
        
        local targetHrp = target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not targetHrp then return end
        
        local directionToTarget = (targetHrp.Position - hrp.Position).Unit
        local backDirection = -directionToTarget
        
        local newCFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position) * CFrame.fromMatrix(
            Vector3.new(0, 0, 0),
            Vector3.new(1, 0, 0),
            Vector3.new(0, 1, 0),
            -backDirection
        )
        
        hrp.CFrame = State.applyAntiAimToCFrame(newCFrame)
        
        pcall(function()
            for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                    part.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    part.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
end

function State.disablePerfectBlock()
    State.perfectBlockEnabled = false
    if State.perfectBlockConnection then
        State.perfectBlockConnection:Disconnect()
        State.perfectBlockConnection = nil
    end
end

State.inputBeganConnection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then return end
    local k = input.KeyCode
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.W then State.flyKeys.W = true; State.speedKeys.W = true end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.A then State.flyKeys.A = true; State.speedKeys.A = true end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.S then State.flyKeys.S = true; State.speedKeys.S = true end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.D then State.flyKeys.D = true; State.speedKeys.D = true end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.Space then State.flyKeys.Space = true end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift then State.flyKeys.LeftShift = true end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.One then
        State.equipSlot1Held = true
        pcall(function()
            firePickWeaponsLoadout(State.autoLoadoutWeapon1, State.autoLoadoutWeapon2, State.autoLoadoutWeapon3, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
        end)
    end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.Two then
        State.equipSlot2Held = true
        pcall(function()
            firePickWeaponsLoadout(State.autoLoadoutWeapon2, State.autoLoadoutWeapon1, State.autoLoadoutWeapon3, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
        end)
    end
end)

State.inputEndedConnection = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
    if gameProcessed then return end
    local k = input.KeyCode
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.W then State.flyKeys.W = false; State.speedKeys.W = false end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.A then State.flyKeys.A = false; State.speedKeys.A = false end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.S then State.flyKeys.S = false; State.speedKeys.S = false end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.D then State.flyKeys.D = false; State.speedKeys.D = false end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.Space then State.flyKeys.Space = false end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift then State.flyKeys.LeftShift = false end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.One then State.equipSlot1Held = false end
    if k == Enum.KeyCode.Two then State.equipSlot2Held = false end
end)

State.equipSlotThreadRunning = true
State.equipSlotThread = task.spawn(function()
    while State.equipSlotThreadRunning and task.wait(0.1) do
        if State.equipSlot1Held or State.equipSlot2Held then
            local prefWeapon = State.equipSlot1Held and State.autoLoadoutWeapon1 or State.autoLoadoutWeapon2
            local fallbackWeapon = State.equipSlot1Held and State.autoLoadoutWeapon2 or State.autoLoadoutWeapon1
            if prefWeapon and prefWeapon ~= "" then
                firePickWeaponsLoadout(prefWeapon, fallbackWeapon or "", State.autoLoadoutWeapon3, State.autoLoadoutWeapon4)
            end
        end
    end
end)

local function updateFOVCircle(camera, delta)
    if not FOVCircle then return end

    local showAimFOV = State.aimbotEnabled and State.fovCircleEnabled and State.showFOVCircle
    if not showAimFOV then
        FOVCircle.Visible = false
        if FOVFillCircle then FOVFillCircle.Visible = false end
        State.fovCircleSmoothedCenter = nil
        hideFOVFillUI(false)
        return
    end

    local targetCenter = State.getFOVCenter(camera, State.fovPositionMode, State.currentTarget or State.cachedAimbotTarget, State.cachedAimbotTargetPart, "Aimbot")
    local center = State.getSmoothedFOVCenter("fovCircleSmoothedCenter", targetCenter, delta)
    if not center then return end

    FOVCircle.Position = center
    FOVCircle.Radius = State.fov
    FOVCircle.Filled = false
    FOVCircle.Color = State.fovCircleColor
    FOVCircle.Transparency = State.fovCircleTransparency
    FOVCircle.Thickness = State.fovCircleThickness
    FOVCircle.Visible = true

    if State.fovRotationEnabled and State.fovAutoRotate and State.fovRotationSpeed then
        State.fovRotationAngle = (State.fovRotationAngle + State.fovRotationSpeed * delta) % 360
    end

    local fillUIVisible = updateFOVFillUI(
        false,
        center,
        State.fov,
        State.fovCircleFilled,
        State.fovCircleFillStartColor,
        State.fovCircleFillMiddleColor,
        State.fovCircleFillEndColor,
        State.fovCircleTransparency,
        State.fovRotationEnabled and State.fovRotationAngle or 0
    )

    State.updateFOVFillDrawing(
        FOVFillCircle,
        center,
        State.fov,
        State.fovCircleFilled and not fillUIVisible,
        State.fovCircleFillMiddleColor or State.fovCircleColor,
        State.fovCircleTransparency
    )
end

local function updateSilentFOVCircle(camera, delta)
    if not SilentFOVCircle then return end

    local showSilentFOV = State.SilentAimState.FOVEnabled and State.SilentAimState.FOVVisible
    if not showSilentFOV then
        SilentFOVCircle.Visible = false
        if SilentFOVFillCircle then SilentFOVFillCircle.Visible = false end
        State.silentFOVSmoothedCenter = nil
        hideFOVFillUI(true)
        return
    end

    local targetCenter = State.getFOVCenter(camera, State.SilentAimState.FOVPositionMode, State.cachedSilentTarget, State.cachedSilentTargetPart, "Silent")
    local center = State.getSmoothedFOVCenter("silentFOVSmoothedCenter", targetCenter, delta)
    if not center then return end

    SilentFOVCircle.Position = center
    SilentFOVCircle.Radius = State.SilentAimState.FOV
    SilentFOVCircle.Filled = false
    SilentFOVCircle.Color = State.SilentAimState.FOVColor
    SilentFOVCircle.Transparency = State.SilentAimState.FOVTransparency
    SilentFOVCircle.Thickness = State.SilentAimState.FOVThickness
    SilentFOVCircle.Visible = true

    if State.SilentAimState.FOVRotationEnabled and State.SilentAimState.FOVAutoRotate and State.SilentAimState.FOVRotationSpeed then
        State.SilentAimState.FOVRotationAngle = (State.SilentAimState.FOVRotationAngle + State.SilentAimState.FOVRotationSpeed * delta) % 360
    end

    local silentFillUIVisible = updateFOVFillUI(
        true,
        center,
        State.SilentAimState.FOV,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVFilled,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVFillStartColor,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVFillMiddleColor,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVFillEndColor,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVTransparency,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVRotationEnabled and State.SilentAimState.FOVRotationAngle or 0
    )

    State.updateFOVFillDrawing(
        SilentFOVFillCircle,
        center,
        State.SilentAimState.FOV,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVFilled and not silentFillUIVisible,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVFillMiddleColor or State.SilentAimState.FOVColor,
        State.SilentAimState.FOVTransparency
    )
end

local function updateFlickbotFOVCircle(camera, delta)
    if not FlickbotFOVCircle then return end

    local showFlickbotFOV = State.flickbotEnabled and State.flickbotFOVEnabled and State.flickbotFOVVisible
    if not showFlickbotFOV then
        FlickbotFOVCircle.Visible = false
        State.flickbotFOVSmoothedCenter = nil
        return
    end

    local targetCenter = State.getFOVCenter(camera, State.flickbotFOVPositionMode, State.flickbotCurrentTarget, State.flickbotCurrentTargetPart, "Flickbot")
    local center = State.getSmoothedFOVCenter("flickbotFOVSmoothedCenter", targetCenter, delta)
    if not center then return end
    FlickbotFOVCircle.Position = center
    FlickbotFOVCircle.Radius = State.flickbotFOV
    FlickbotFOVCircle.Filled = false
    FlickbotFOVCircle.Color = State.flickbotFOVColor
    FlickbotFOVCircle.Transparency = State.flickbotFOVTransparency
    FlickbotFOVCircle.Thickness = State.flickbotFOVThickness
    FlickbotFOVCircle.Visible = true
end

function State.updateGameCrosshairVisibility()
    if State.disableGameCrosshair then
        if State.gameCrosshairOriginalMouseIconEnabled == nil then
            State.gameCrosshairOriginalMouseIconEnabled = UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled
        end
        UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = false
    elseif State.gameCrosshairOriginalMouseIconEnabled ~= nil then
        UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = State.gameCrosshairOriginalMouseIconEnabled
        State.gameCrosshairOriginalMouseIconEnabled = nil
    end
end

local function updateOrbitVisuals(camera)
    local orbitTarget = State.rageVisualTargetRoot
    local showOrbitVisuals = State.rageBotEnabled
        and State.rageAttackTpMode == "Orbit"
        and State.rageOrbitVirtualize
        and orbitTarget
        and orbitTarget.Parent

    if RageOrbitVisualLines then
        if showOrbitVisuals and #RageOrbitVisualLines > 0 then
            State.rageOrbitVisualsWereVisible = true
            local segments = #RageOrbitVisualLines
            local baseRadius = State.rageOrbitDistance or 50
            local radius = baseRadius
            if isRageMeleeMode() then
                radius = math.max(0.5, baseRadius - 0.5)
            else
                radius = math.max(1, baseRadius)
            end
            local base = orbitTarget.Position
            for i, line in ipairs(RageOrbitVisualLines) do
                if line then
                    local angle1 = (i - 1) * (math.pi * 2 / segments)
                    local angle2 = i * (math.pi * 2 / segments)
                    local point1 = base + Vector3.new(math.cos(angle1) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle1) * radius)
                    local point2 = base + Vector3.new(math.cos(angle2) * radius, 0, math.sin(angle2) * radius)
                    local screen1, onScreen1 = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(point1)
                    local screen2, onScreen2 = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(point2)
                    local visible = State.rageOrbitAlwaysOnTop
                        and screen1.Z > 0
                        and screen2.Z > 0
                        or (onScreen1 and onScreen2)

                    if visible then
                        line.From = Vector2.new(screen1.X, screen1.Y)
                        line.To = Vector2.new(screen2.X, screen2.Y)
                        line.Color = State.rageOrbitVirtualizeColor
                        line.Thickness = 2
                        line.Transparency = 1
                        line.ZIndex = 8
                        line.Visible = true
                    else
                        line.Visible = false
                    end
                end
            end
        elseif State.rageOrbitVisualsWereVisible then
            State.rageOrbitVisualsWereVisible = false
            for _, line in ipairs(RageOrbitVisualLines) do
                if line then line.Visible = false end
            end
        end
    end

    if RageOrbitTargetDot then
        if showOrbitVisuals and State.rageOrbitTargetDot then
            local screen, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(orbitTarget.Position)
            local visible = (State.rageOrbitAlwaysOnTop and screen.Z > 0) or onScreen
            if visible then
                RageOrbitTargetDot.Position = Vector2.new(screen.X, screen.Y)
                RageOrbitTargetDot.Radius = State.rageOrbitTargetDotSize or 6
                RageOrbitTargetDot.Color = State.rageOrbitTargetDotColor
                RageOrbitTargetDot.Transparency = 1
                RageOrbitTargetDot.ZIndex = 9
                RageOrbitTargetDot.Visible = true
            end
        elseif RageOrbitTargetDot.Visible then
            RageOrbitTargetDot.Visible = false
        end
    end
end

local function updateCustomCrosshair(camera, delta)
    if not CustomCrosshair then return end

    local enabled = State.customCrosshairEnabled
    local outlineEnabled = enabled and State.customCrosshairOutline

    if CustomCrosshairOutline then
        for _, line in pairs(CustomCrosshairOutline) do
            if line then
                line.Visible = outlineEnabled
                if outlineEnabled then
                    line.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                    line.Thickness = (State.customCrosshairThickness or 2) + 2
                    line.Transparency = 0.6
                end
            end
        end
    end

    for _, line in pairs(CustomCrosshair) do
        if line then
            line.Visible = enabled
            if enabled then
                line.Color = State.customCrosshairColor
                line.Thickness = State.customCrosshairThickness
                line.Transparency = 1
            end
        end
    end

    if not enabled then
        State.customCrosshairSmoothedCenter = nil
        return
    end

    local crosshairTarget = State.currentTarget or State.cachedAimbotTarget
    local crosshairKind = "Aimbot"
    if not crosshairTarget and State.cachedSilentTarget then
        crosshairTarget = State.cachedSilentTarget
        crosshairKind = "Silent"
    end
    if not crosshairTarget and State.flickbotCurrentTarget then
        crosshairTarget = State.flickbotCurrentTarget
        crosshairKind = "Flickbot"
    end

    local targetCenter = State.getFOVCenter(camera, State.customCrosshairPositionMode, crosshairTarget, nil, crosshairKind)
    local center = State.getSmoothedFOVCenter("customCrosshairSmoothedCenter", targetCenter, delta)
    if not center then return end
    local size = State.customCrosshairSize or 12
    local gap = State.customCrosshairGap or 6
    local rotation = State.customCrosshairRotation or 0

    if State.customCrosshairAutoRotate and State.customCrosshairRotateSpeed > 0 then
        rotation = (rotation + State.customCrosshairRotateSpeed * delta) % 360
        State.customCrosshairRotation = rotation
    end

    local angle = math.rad(rotation)
    local cosA = math.cos(angle)
    local sinA = math.sin(angle)
    local x = center.X
    local y = center.Y

    local function rotatePoint(px, py)
        local dx = px - x
        local dy = py - y
        return Vector2.new(
            x + dx * cosA - dy * sinA,
            y + dx * sinA + dy * cosA
        )
    end

    local topFrom = rotatePoint(x, y - gap - size)
    local topTo = rotatePoint(x, y - gap)
    local bottomFrom = rotatePoint(x, y + gap)
    local bottomTo = rotatePoint(x, y + gap + size)
    local leftFrom = rotatePoint(x - gap - size, y)
    local leftTo = rotatePoint(x - gap, y)
    local rightFrom = rotatePoint(x + gap, y)
    local rightTo = rotatePoint(x + gap + size, y)

    if CustomCrosshairOutline then
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Top.From = topFrom
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Top.To = topTo
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Bottom.From = bottomFrom
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Bottom.To = bottomTo
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Left.From = leftFrom
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Left.To = leftTo
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Right.From = rightFrom
        CustomCrosshairOutline.Right.To = rightTo
    end

    CustomCrosshair.Top.From = topFrom
    CustomCrosshair.Top.To = topTo
    CustomCrosshair.Bottom.From = bottomFrom
    CustomCrosshair.Bottom.To = bottomTo
    CustomCrosshair.Left.From = leftFrom
    CustomCrosshair.Left.To = leftTo
    CustomCrosshair.Right.From = rightFrom
    CustomCrosshair.Right.To = rightTo
end

State.mainRenderConnection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(delta)
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not camera then return end

    State.updateAdaptiveFrameTiming(delta)
    local players = nil

    if State.customFOVEnabled then
        if not State.customFOVOriginal then
            State.customFOVOriginal = camera.FieldOfView
        end
        camera.FieldOfView = State.customFOVValue
    else
        if State.customFOVOriginal then
            camera.FieldOfView = State.customFOVOriginal
            State.customFOVOriginal = nil
        end
    end

    if State.blurEnabled and VisualBlurEffect then
        VisualBlurEffect.Size = State.blurSize
    end

    if State.motionBlurEnabled and VisualMotionBlurEffect then
        VisualMotionBlurEffect.Intensity = State.motionBlurIntensity
    end

    State.updateWorldVisualEffects(delta)

    updateFOVCircle(camera, delta)
    updateSilentFOVCircle(camera, delta)
    updateFlickbotFOVCircle(camera, delta)
    State.updateGameCrosshairVisibility()
    updateOrbitVisuals(camera)
    updateCustomCrosshair(camera, delta)
    if State.targetHUDEnabled and State.shouldRunAdaptiveTask("targetHUD", delta, nil, 5) then
        State.updateTargetHUD()
    end

    local espEnabled = State.espBoxEnabled
        or State.espHealthEnabled
        or State.espNameEnabled
        or State.espDistanceEnabled
        or State.espCharmEnabled
        or State.espSkeletonEnabled
        or State.espWeaponEnabled
        or State.espLineEnabled
        or State.espArrowEnabled
        or State.espHeadDotEnabled
        or State.espGlowCharmEnabled
    if State.updateESP and espEnabled then
        players = players or Players:GetPlayers()
        State.updateESP(camera, players, delta)
    elseif State.espData and next(State.espData) then
        State.clearESP()
    end

    if (State.espPreviewEnabled or State.espPreviewGui) and State.shouldRunAdaptiveTask("espPreview", delta, nil, 2) then
        State.updateESPPreview()
    end

    if State.updateAuraESP and (State.auraESPEnabled or (State.auraObjects and next(State.auraObjects))) and State.shouldRunAdaptiveTask("auraESP", delta, nil, 2) then
        players = players or Players:GetPlayers()
        State.updateAuraESP(players)
    end

    local hasLegacyAuraColor = State.auraFireEnabled
        or State.auraIceEnabled
        or State.auraLightningEnabled
        or State.auraWindEnabled
        or State.auraWaterEnabled
        or State.auraEarthEnabled
        or State.auraLightEnabled
        or State.auraDarkEnabled
    local activeAuraColor = hasLegacyAuraColor and ESPUtil.getActiveAuraColor() or nil
    if not Core.isLocalPlayerInMatch() then
        if activeAuraColor or State.auraColorEffectsWereActive then
            if State.shouldRunAdaptiveTask("playerAuraColor", delta, nil, 12) then
                players = players or Players:GetPlayers()
                for _, player in ipairs(players) do
                    if player and player.Character then
                        ESPUtil.removePlayerAura(player.Character)
                    end
                end
                State.auraColorEffectsWereActive = false
            end
        end
    elseif activeAuraColor or State.auraColorEffectsWereActive then
        local auraMultiplier = activeAuraColor and 6 or 12
        if State.shouldRunAdaptiveTask("playerAuraColor", delta, nil, auraMultiplier) then
            players = players or Players:GetPlayers()
            for _, player in ipairs(players) do
                if player and player.Character then
                    ESPUtil.updatePlayerAura(player.Character, activeAuraColor)
                end
            end
            State.auraColorEffectsWereActive = activeAuraColor ~= nil
        end
    end

    local worldESPenabled = State.worldESPProjectileEnabled
    if State.updateWorldESP and worldESPenabled then
        State.updateWorldESP(camera, delta)
    elseif State.worldESPData and next(State.worldESPData) then
        State.clearWorldESP()
    end

    if State.aimbotEnabled then
        local aimActive = false

        if State.noKeybindEnabled then
            aimActive = true
        elseif Options and Options.AimbotKeybind and typeof(Options.AimbotKeybind.GetState) == "function" then
            aimActive = Options.AimbotKeybind:GetState()
        else
            local aimKey = State.aimbotKeybind or "Q"
            local aimKeyCode = nil
            if typeof(aimKey) == "string" then
                aimKeyCode = Enum.KeyCode[aimKey] or Enum.KeyCode[aimKey:gsub("%s+", "")]
            end
            aimActive = aimKeyCode and UserInputService:IsKeyDown(aimKeyCode)
        end

        if aimActive then
            State.currentTarget = State.getClosest()
        else
            State.currentTarget = nil
        end

        if State.currentTarget and State.currentTarget.Character then
            local targetPart = State.getCachedAimbotTargetPart(State.currentTarget, camera)
            if targetPart and (not State.wallCheckEnabled or Core.isTargetVisible(targetPart)) then
                State.lockOntoTarget(State.currentTarget)
            else
                State.currentTarget = nil
            end
        end
    end
end)

function removeDrawingObject(obj)
    if obj and type(obj.Remove) == "function" then
        pcall(function()
            obj:Remove()
        end)
    end
end

function State.cleanupAll()
    if State.cleanupComplete then return end
    State.cleanupComplete = true
    State.equipSlotThreadRunning = false

    local cleanupFns = {
        "disableAspectRatio",
        "disablePerformanceMode",
        "disableBulletTracers",
        "disableHitNotifier",
        "disableHitEffect",
        "disableLocalAura",
        "disableHitsoundChanger",
        "disableShootSoundChanger",
        "disableFFAAutoGrab",
        "disableFFAAutoRespawn",
        "disableFlickbot",
        "disableRapidFire",
        "disableNoSpread",
        "disableInstantADS",
        "disableNoRecoil",
        "disableAlwaysBackstab",
        "disableShader",
        "disableSnowEffect",
        "disableWorldFog",
        "disableWorldAtmosphere",
        "disableWorldSunRays",
        "disableWorldDepthOfField",
        "disableWorldClouds",
        "disableWorldTime",
        "disableWorldExposure",
        "disableWorldTechnology",
        "disableWorldMotionBlur",
        "stopAnimatePlayer",
        "disableAntiOutOfBounds",
        "stopAutoBanWeapons",
        "stopAutoMatchmaking",
        "disableReal3DText",
        "stopAntiAim",
        "stopMovementHeartbeat",
        "disableWeaponStateSpoof",
        "disableDeviceSpoof",
        "disableRankCharmSpoofer",
        "disableThirdPerson",
        "stopRageBot",
        "cleanupRageBot",
        "disablePerfectBlock",
        "destroyTargetHUD",
        "destroyESPPreview",
        "clearAuraESP",
    }

    for _, fnName in ipairs(cleanupFns) do
        local fn = State[fnName]
        if type(fn) == "function" then
            pcall(fn)
        end
    end

    pcall(disableViewModelCharm)
    if State.clearESP then pcall(State.clearESP) end
    if State.resetNameSpoof then pcall(State.resetNameSpoof) end
    if State.releaseFrozenRageTarget then pcall(State.releaseFrozenRageTarget) end
    if State.restoreEloSpoofLeaderboard then pcall(State.restoreEloSpoofLeaderboard) end
    State.playerDataSpoofEnabled = false

    if State.nameSpoofSyncConnection then
        pcall(function() State.nameSpoofSyncConnection:Disconnect() end)
        State.nameSpoofSyncConnection = nil
    end
    if State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection then
        pcall(function() State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection:Disconnect() end)
        State.playerDataSpoofSyncConnection = nil
    end
    if State.inputBeganConnection then
        pcall(function() State.inputBeganConnection:Disconnect() end)
        State.inputBeganConnection = nil
    end
    if State.inputEndedConnection then
        pcall(function() State.inputEndedConnection:Disconnect() end)
        State.inputEndedConnection = nil
    end
    if State.mainRenderConnection then
        pcall(function() State.mainRenderConnection:Disconnect() end)
        State.mainRenderConnection = nil
    end
    if weaponModRetryConn then
        pcall(function() weaponModRetryConn:Disconnect() end)
        weaponModRetryConn = nil
    end
    if State.rageBotTrackingConnections then
        for _, conn in ipairs(State.rageBotTrackingConnections) do
            if conn and conn.Disconnect then
                pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
            end
        end
        State.rageBotTrackingConnections = {}
    end

    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Noclip") end)
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("Triggerbot") end)
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("DesyncSync") end)

    removeDrawingObject(FOVFillCircle)
    removeDrawingObject(FOVCircle)
    removeDrawingObject(SilentFOVFillCircle)
    removeDrawingObject(SilentFOVCircle)
    removeDrawingObject(FlickbotFOVCircle)
    removeDrawingObject(RageOrbitTargetDot)
    FOVFillCircle = nil
    FOVCircle = nil
    SilentFOVFillCircle = nil
    SilentFOVCircle = nil
    FlickbotFOVCircle = nil
    RageOrbitTargetDot = nil
    if FOVFillGui then pcall(function() FOVFillGui:Destroy() end) end
    if SilentFOVFillGui then pcall(function() SilentFOVFillGui:Destroy() end) end
    FOVFillGui = nil
    FOVFillFrame = nil
    FOVFillGradient = nil
    SilentFOVFillGui = nil
    SilentFOVFillFrame = nil
    SilentFOVFillGradient = nil
    State.disableGameCrosshair = false
    State.updateGameCrosshairVisibility()

    for _, line in ipairs(RageOrbitVisualLines) do
        removeDrawingObject(line)
    end
    RageOrbitVisualLines = {}

    if CustomCrosshair then
        for _, line in pairs(CustomCrosshair) do
            removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
        CustomCrosshair = nil
    end
    if CustomCrosshairOutline then
        for _, line in pairs(CustomCrosshairOutline) do
            removeDrawingObject(line)
        end
        CustomCrosshairOutline = nil
    end
end

if type(Library.OnUnload) == "function" then
    Library:OnUnload(function()
        State.cleanupAll()
    end)
end

local CosmeticChangerGroup = Pages.Visual.Viewmodel:AddRightGroupbox("Cosmetic Changer")

local CosmeticTypes = {"Skin", "Wrap", "Charm", "Finisher"}
local AvailableWeapons = {}
local AvailableCosmeticNames = {
    Skin = {},
    Wrap = {},
    Charm = {},
    Finisher = {}
}
local AvailableCosmetics = {
    Skin = {},
    Wrap = {},
    Charm = {},
    Finisher = {}
}
local NoCosmeticResults = "No cosmetics found"

local UnrestrictedMode = false
local CosmeticLibraryModule = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("CosmeticLibrary")
local CosmeticLib = require(CosmeticLibraryModule)
local ShopLib
pcall(function()
	ShopLib = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("ShopLibrary"))
end)
local ItemLib
pcall(function()
	ItemLib = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Modules"):WaitForChild("ItemLibrary"))
end)

local AddSkinCaseOptions = {}
local AddSkinCaseOptionData = {}

local function addSkinCaseOption(label, rewardData)
	if not label or not rewardData or not rewardData.Name then
		return
	end
	if AddSkinCaseOptionData[label] then
		return
	end
	table.insert(AddSkinCaseOptions, label)
	AddSkinCaseOptionData[label] = rewardData
end

local function cosmeticHasLimitedMarker(data)
	for key, value in pairs(data) do
		local k = tostring(key):lower()
		local v = tostring(value):lower()
		if k:find("limited") or k:find("exclusive") or k:find("event") or k:find("season") then
			return true
		end
		if v:find("limited") or v:find("exclusive") or v:find("limited time") then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function populateAddSkinCaseData()
	table.clear(AddSkinCaseOptions)
	table.clear(AddSkinCaseOptionData)

	for name, info in pairs(CosmeticLib.Rewards or {}) do
		if type(info) == "table" and info.Type == "Lootbox" then
			local lower = name:lower()
			local prefix
			if lower:find("skin case", 1, true) then
				prefix = "Skin Case"
			elseif lower:find("wrap box", 1, true) then
				prefix = "Wrap Box"
			elseif lower:find("finisher pack", 1, true) then
				prefix = "Finisher Pack"
			elseif lower:find("case", 1, true) or lower:find("box", 1, true) or lower:find("pack", 1, true) or lower:find("chest", 1, true) or lower:find("crate", 1, true) or lower:find("capsule", 1, true) then
				prefix = "Limited Lootbox"
			end
			if prefix then
				addSkinCaseOption(prefix .. " - " .. name, {
					Name = name,
					Quantity = 1
				})
			end
		end
	end

	if ShopLib and type(ShopLib.Entries) == "table" then
		for _, entry in pairs(ShopLib.Entries) do
			local isLimited = type(entry) == "table" and (entry.IsLimited or entry.Limited or entry.IsLimitedTime or entry.LimitedTime)
			if isLimited and type(entry.Rewards) == "table" then
				for _, rewardData in pairs(entry.Rewards) do
					local cosmetic = rewardData and CosmeticLib.Cosmetics and CosmeticLib.Cosmetics[rewardData.Name]
					if cosmetic and cosmetic.Type == "Skin" then
						local weapon = rewardData.Weapon or cosmetic.ItemName
						local label = "Limited Skin - " .. rewardData.Name .. (weapon and (" (" .. weapon .. ")") or "")
						addSkinCaseOption(label, {
							Name = rewardData.Name,
							Weapon = weapon,
							Quantity = 1
						})
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end

	for name, data in pairs(CosmeticLib.Cosmetics or {}) do
		if type(data) == "table" and data.Type == "Skin" and cosmeticHasLimitedMarker(data) then
			local weapon = data.ItemName
			local label = "Limited Skin - " .. name .. (weapon and (" (" .. weapon .. ")") or "")
			addSkinCaseOption(label, {
				Name = name,
				Weapon = weapon,
				Quantity = 1
			})
		end
	end

	table.sort(AddSkinCaseOptions)
	if #AddSkinCaseOptions == 0 then
		table.insert(AddSkinCaseOptions, "No reward candidates found")
	end
end

local function populateCosmeticsData()
    AvailableWeapons = {}
    AvailableCosmeticNames = {
        Skin = {},
        Wrap = {},
        Charm = {},
        Finisher = {}
    }
    AvailableCosmetics = {
        Skin = {},
        Wrap = { Global = {} },
        Charm = {},
        Finisher = {}
    }

    local seenWeapons = {}
    local seenCosmetics = {}
    local function addWeapon(name)
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" or seenWeapons[name] then
            return
        end
        seenWeapons[name] = true
        table.insert(AvailableWeapons, name)
    end

    local function addCosmetic(cType, weapon, name)
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" or not AvailableCosmetics[cType] or bannedCosmetic(name) then
            return
        end

        seenCosmetics[cType] = seenCosmetics[cType] or {}
        if not seenCosmetics[cType][name] then
            seenCosmetics[cType][name] = true
            table.insert(AvailableCosmeticNames[cType], name)
        end

        weapon = weapon or "Global"
        AvailableCosmetics[cType][weapon] = AvailableCosmetics[cType][weapon] or {}
        if not table.find(AvailableCosmetics[cType][weapon], name) then
            table.insert(AvailableCosmetics[cType][weapon], name)
        end
    end

    if ShopLib and ShopLib.GetReleasedOwnableWeapons then
        pcall(function()
            for _, weapon in pairs(ShopLib:GetReleasedOwnableWeapons(nil, ShopLib.OwnableWeaponsAlphabetized)) do
                addWeapon(weapon)
            end
        end)
    end
    if ShopLib then
        for _, source in pairs({ ShopLib.OwnableWeaponsAlphabetized, ShopLib.OwnableWeapons }) do
            if type(source) == "table" then
                for _, weapon in pairs(source) do
                    addWeapon(weapon)
                end
            end
        end
    end
    if ItemLib and type(ItemLib.Items) == "table" then
        for weapon, info in pairs(ItemLib.Items) do
            if type(info) == "table" and info.Class then
                addWeapon(weapon)
            end
        end
    end

    for name, data in pairs(CosmeticLib.Cosmetics or {}) do
        local cType = type(data) == "table" and data.Type
        if AvailableCosmetics[cType] then
            if cType == "Wrap" then
                addCosmetic(cType, "Global", name)
            else
                local weapon = data.ItemName
                if weapon then
                    addWeapon(weapon)
                    addCosmetic(cType, weapon, name)
                else
                    addCosmetic(cType, "Global", name)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    table.sort(AvailableWeapons)
    for cType, names in pairs(AvailableCosmeticNames) do
        table.sort(names)
    end
    for _, wepData in pairs(AvailableCosmetics) do
        for _, names in pairs(wepData) do
            table.sort(names)
        end
    end
end

pcall(populateCosmeticsData)
pcall(populateAddSkinCaseData)

local UpdateCosmeticNames
local function getCosmeticNameOptions(cType, weapon)
    local names
    if cType == "Wrap" then
        names = AvailableCosmetics.Wrap and AvailableCosmetics.Wrap.Global
    elseif cType == "Skin" and UnrestrictedMode then
        names = AvailableCosmeticNames.Skin
    else
        names = weapon and AvailableCosmetics[cType] and AvailableCosmetics[cType][weapon]
        if (not names or #names == 0) and AvailableCosmetics[cType] then
            names = AvailableCosmetics[cType].Global
        end
    end

    local result = {}
    for _, name in pairs(names or {}) do
        table.insert(result, name)
    end
    table.sort(result)
    if #result == 0 then
        table.insert(result, NoCosmeticResults)
    end
    return result
end

local function isCosmeticPlaceholder(name)
    return name == NoCosmeticResults
end

local function setDropdownValues(dropdown, values)
    if not dropdown then
        return
    end
    dropdown:SetValues(values)
    dropdown:SetValue(values[1])
end

local function refreshCosmeticNameDropdown()
    if not CosmeticNameDropdown then
        return
    end
    local cType = CosmeticTypeDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value or "Skin"
    local weapon = CosmeticWeaponDropdown and CosmeticWeaponDropdown.Value or AvailableWeapons[1]
    setDropdownValues(CosmeticNameDropdown, getCosmeticNameOptions(cType, weapon))
end

local function refreshCosmeticWeaponDropdown(cType)
    cType = cType or (CosmeticTypeDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value) or "Skin"
    local weapons = {}
    for _, weapon in pairs(AvailableWeapons) do
        table.insert(weapons, weapon)
    end
    if #weapons == 0 then
        table.insert(weapons, "Global")
    end

    setDropdownValues(CosmeticWeaponDropdown, weapons)
    refreshCosmeticNameDropdown()
end

    Multi = false,
    Search = true,

            if CosmeticPreview then
                CosmeticPreview:SetImage('')
            end
            return
        end
        local ct = CosmeticTypeDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value or 'Skin'
        if CosmeticPreview and type(CosmeticPreview.SetImageFromLocal) == 'function' then
            CosmeticPreview:SetImageFromLocal(ct, Value)
        end
    end,
})

local function getPreviewImageFromTable(data, depth)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	depth = depth or 0
	if depth > 2 then
		return nil
	end

	local directKeys = {
		"ImageHighResolution",
		"Image",
		"Icon",
		"IconImage",
		"Thumbnail",
		"ThumbnailImage",
		"Texture",
		"TextureId",
		"TextureID",
		"Decal",
		"DecalId",
		"DecalID",
		"AssetId",
		"AssetID",
	}

	for _, key in ipairs(directKeys) do
		local value = data[key]
		if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
			return value
		end
		if type(value) == "number" then
			return tostring(value)
		end
	end

	for key, value in pairs(data) do
		local keyName = tostring(key):lower()
		local looksLikeImage = keyName:find("image", 1, true)
			or keyName:find("icon", 1, true)
			or keyName:find("thumb", 1, true)
			or keyName:find("texture", 1, true)
			or keyName:find("decal", 1, true)
			or keyName:find("asset", 1, true)

		if looksLikeImage then
			if type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" then
				return value
			end
			if type(value) == "number" then
				return tostring(value)
			end
		end
	end

	for _, value in pairs(data) do
		if type(value) == "table" then
			local nested = getPreviewImageFromTable(value, depth + 1)
			if nested then
				return nested
			end
		end
	end

	return nil
end

local function clonePreviewWeaponData(data)
	if type(data) ~= "table" then
		return nil
	end

	local clone = {}
	for key, value in pairs(data) do
		clone[key] = value
	end
	return clone
end

local function resolveCosmeticPreviewImage(cType, name)
	if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" or isCosmeticPlaceholder(name) then
		return ""
	end

	local cosmeticInfo = CosmeticLib and CosmeticLib.Cosmetics and CosmeticLib.Cosmetics[name]
	local imageSource = getPreviewImageFromTable(cosmeticInfo)
	if imageSource then
		return imageSource
	end

	if ItemLib and type(ItemLib.ViewModels) == "table" then
		imageSource = getPreviewImageFromTable(ItemLib.ViewModels[name])
		if imageSource then
			return imageSource
		end
	end

	if cType == "Skin" and ItemLib and type(ItemLib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData) == "function" then
		local weapon = CosmeticWeaponDropdown and CosmeticWeaponDropdown.Value
		local weaponData = weapon and ItemLib.Items and ItemLib.Items[weapon]
		local previewData = clonePreviewWeaponData(weaponData)

		if previewData then
			previewData.Name = previewData.Name or weapon
			if type(cosmeticInfo) == "table" then
				local skinInfo = clonePreviewWeaponData(cosmeticInfo) or cosmeticInfo
				skinInfo.Name = skinInfo.Name or name
				previewData.Skin = skinInfo
			else
				previewData.Skin = { Name = name }
			end

			local ok, resolved = pcall(ItemLib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData, ItemLib, previewData, true)
			if ok and resolved and resolved ~= "" then
				return resolved
			end

			ok, resolved = pcall(ItemLib.GetViewModelImageFromWeaponData, ItemLib, previewData, false)
			if ok and resolved and resolved ~= "" then
				return resolved
			end
		end
	end

	return ""
end

CosmeticPreview = CosmeticChangerGroup:AddImageLabel("CosmeticPreview", {
	Text = "Preview",
	Size = UDim2.new(0, 128, 0, 128),
	ImageResolver = resolveCosmeticPreviewImage,
})

CosmeticNameDropdown:OnChanged(function(val)
	if not val or isCosmeticPlaceholder(val) then
		if CosmeticPreview and type(CosmeticPreview.SetImage) == 'function' then
			CosmeticPreview:SetImage('')
		end
		return
	end
	local ct = CosmeticTypeDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value or 'Skin'
	if CosmeticPreview and type(CosmeticPreview.SetImageFromLocal) == 'function' then
		CosmeticPreview:SetImageFromLocal(ct, val)
	end
end)

if CosmeticTypeDropdown then
	CosmeticTypeDropdown:OnChanged(function(_)
		local val = CosmeticNameDropdown and CosmeticNameDropdown.Value
		if not val or isCosmeticPlaceholder(val) then
			if CosmeticPreview and type(CosmeticPreview.SetImage) == 'function' then
				CosmeticPreview:SetImage('')
			end
			return
		end
		local ct = CosmeticTypeDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value or 'Skin'
		if CosmeticPreview and type(CosmeticPreview.SetImageFromLocal) == 'function' then
			CosmeticPreview:SetImageFromLocal(ct, val)
		end
	end)
end

local function initializeCosmeticPreview()
	if not CosmeticPreview then
		return
	end
	
	local val = CosmeticNameDropdown and CosmeticNameDropdown.Value
	local ct = CosmeticTypeDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value or 'Skin'
	
	if not val or isCosmeticPlaceholder(val) then
		if type(CosmeticPreview.SetImage) == 'function' then
			CosmeticPreview:SetImage('')
		end
		return
	end
	
	if type(CosmeticPreview.SetImageFromLocal) == 'function' then
		CosmeticPreview:SetImageFromLocal(ct, val)
	else

		if CosmeticLib and CosmeticLib.Cosmetics and CosmeticLib.Cosmetics[val] then
			local cosmeticInfo = CosmeticLib.Cosmetics[val]
			local imageSource = cosmeticInfo.ImageHighResolution or cosmeticInfo.Image or ''
			if imageSource ~= '' and type(CosmeticPreview.SetImage) == 'function' then
				CosmeticPreview:SetImage(imageSource)
			end
		end
	end
end

end

    populateCosmeticsData()
    refreshCosmeticWeaponDropdown(CosmeticTypeDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value)
    initializeCosmeticPreview()
end

	if CosmeticPreview and CosmeticNameDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown then
		initializeCosmeticPreview()
	end
end)
task.delay(0.2, function()
	if CosmeticPreview and CosmeticNameDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown then
		initializeCosmeticPreview()
	end
end)
task.delay(0.25, function()
	if CosmeticPreview and CosmeticNameDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown then
		initializeCosmeticPreview()
	end
end)

	if CosmeticPreview and CosmeticNameDropdown and CosmeticTypeDropdown then
		initializeCosmeticPreview()
	end

CosmeticChangerGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "Equip Cosmetic",
    Func = function()
        local wep = CosmeticWeaponDropdown.Value
        local ct = CosmeticTypeDropdown.Value
        local cdName = CosmeticNameDropdown.Value
        
        if wep and ct and cdName and cdName:gsub(" ", "") ~= "" and not isCosmeticPlaceholder(cdName) then
            local http = game:GetService("HttpService")
            
            local cfg = { equipped = {}, favorites = {} }
            if readfile and isfile and isfile(unlockAllSaveFile) then
                pcall(function()
                    cfg = http:JSONDecode(readfile(unlockAllSaveFile))
                end)
            end
            
            if not cfg.equipped[wep] then
                cfg.equipped[wep] = {}
            end
            cfg.equipped[wep][ct] = { name = cdName, seed = math.random(1, 1000000), inverted = false }
            
            if writefile then
                pcall(function()
                    makefolder("PhantomRivals")
                    writefile(unlockAllSaveFile, http:JSONEncode(cfg))
                    notify("Cosmetic Changer", "Saved " .. cdName .. " (" .. ct .. ") to " .. wep)
                end)

                if not bannedCosmetic(cdName) then
                    local cl = clonecos(cdName, ct, false, false)
                    if cl then
                        UnlockAllState.equip[wep] = UnlockAllState.equip[wep] or {}
                        UnlockAllState.equip[wep][ct] = cl
                        if ct == "Finisher" then unlockAllFinisherCache[wep] = cdName end
                        rebuildinv()
                        refreshEquippedCosmeticVisuals(wep, ct)
                        task.defer(function()
                            saferep("WeaponInventory")
                            task.wait(0.2)
                            savecfg()
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end

        else
            notify("Cosmetic Changer", "Please select weapon, type and cosmetic")
        end
    end,
    DoubleClick = false,
})

CosmeticChangerGroup:AddButton({
    Text = "Reload Cosmetic Lists",
    Func = function()
        UpdateCosmeticNames()
        refreshAllEquippedCosmeticVisuals()
        notify("Cosmetic Changer", "Reloaded cosmetics and refreshed visuals")
    end,
    DoubleClick = false,
})

task.defer(function()
	local autoloadOk, autoloadError = pcall(DirectSettings.LoadDefaults, DirectSettings)
	if not autoloadOk then
		print("[Phantom] Direct settings autoload failed: " .. tostring(autoloadError))
	end
end)

notify("Phantom Rivals", "Loaded successfully!")
